    -- ============================================================================
-- KINETIC OS v2.5 — CIRUGÍA 1: LIMPIEZA QUIRÚRGICA Y CUENTA ÚNICA DE ADMIN
-- Archivo: sql/cirugia1_limpieza_y_admin_oficial.sql
-- Destino: Supabase SQL Editor (Proyecto gcppxrlzxlgkuapktxvl)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. PROTECCIÓN ESTRICTA DE public.afiliados (CONSERVANDO CLUBES REALES)
-- ============================================================================
DELETE FROM public.afiliados
WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(nombre, ''))) IN (
        'kinetic oficial',
        'sede kinetic',
        'sede oficial kinetic'
      )
   OR LOWER(COALESCE(nombre, '')) LIKE '%atleta 5975%'
   OR LOWER(COALESCE(nombre, '')) LIKE '%atleta kinetic%';

UPDATE public.afiliados
SET propietario_id = NULL,
    email_contacto = CASE
      WHEN LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(email_contacto, ''))) IN (
        'club@kinetic.com',
        'especialista@kinetic.com',
        'kinetic@kinetic.com',
        'atleta@kinetic.com'
      ) THEN NULL
      ELSE email_contacto
    END;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. VACIADO TOTAL DE DATOS DE PRUEBA (EVENTOS, RESERVAS, CHATS, HISTORIAS, WAITLIST)
-- ============================================================================
DELETE FROM public.reservas;
DELETE FROM public.servicios;
DELETE FROM public.equipo_mensajes;
DELETE FROM public.equipo_miembros;
DELETE FROM public.equipos_kinetic;
DELETE FROM public.admin_audit_logs;

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_tbl TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH v_tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY[
    'comunidad_comentarios',
    'comunidad_posts',
    'historias_24h',
    'kinetic_stories',
    'mensajes_directos',
    'chats_privados',
    'negocios'
  ]
  LOOP
    IF EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM information_schema.tables
      WHERE table_schema = 'public'
        AND table_name = v_tbl
    ) THEN
      EXECUTE format('DELETE FROM public.%I', v_tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;

ALTER TABLE public.fundadores_waitlist
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS estado TEXT DEFAULT 'pendiente';

DELETE FROM public.fundadores_waitlist;

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. CUENTA ÚNICA DE ADMINISTRADOR ('administrador@kinetic.com.mx' -> 'Kinetic Oficial')
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.perfiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS email TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_membresia NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_topup NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS saldo_kc NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS xp_total INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS racha_dias INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recompensas_reclamadas TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}';

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_admin_id UUID;
  v_has_tipo_cuenta BOOLEAN;
  r_fk RECORD;
BEGIN
  -- Localizar la cuenta base kinetic@kinetic.com o administrador@kinetic.com.mx conservando su encrypted_password
  SELECT u.id
    INTO v_admin_id
  FROM auth.users u
  WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
    'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
    'kinetic@kinetic.com',
    'administrador@kinetic.com'
  )
  ORDER BY
    (LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx') DESC,
    (LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = 'kinetic@kinetic.com') DESC,
    u.created_at ASC
  LIMIT 1;

  IF v_admin_id IS NULL THEN
    SELECT p.id
      INTO v_admin_id
    FROM public.perfiles p
    JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = p.id
    WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
    ORDER BY p.created_at ASC NULLS LAST
    LIMIT 1;
  END IF;

  IF v_admin_id IS NULL THEN
    v_admin_id := gen_random_uuid();
    INSERT INTO auth.users (
      id,
      instance_id,
      email,
      encrypted_password,
      email_confirmed_at,
      created_at,
      updated_at,
      raw_app_meta_data,
      raw_user_meta_data,
      is_super_admin,
      role,
      aud
    ) VALUES (
      v_admin_id,
      '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
      'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      crypt('KineticAdmin2026!', gen_salt('bf')),
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      '{"provider":"email","providers":["email"]}'::jsonb,
      '{"nombre":"Kinetic Oficial","rol":"admin","tipo_cuenta":"admin"}'::jsonb,
      FALSE,
      'authenticated',
      'authenticated'
    );
  END IF;

  -- Limpiar referencias residuales en tablas satélite públicas que apunten a perfiles/users de prueba
  FOR r_fk IN
    SELECT DISTINCT
      kcu.table_name,
      kcu.column_name
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
    JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
      ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
     AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
    JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
      ON ccu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name
    WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
      AND tc.table_schema = 'public'
      AND tc.table_name NOT IN ('perfiles', 'afiliados')
      AND (
        (ccu.table_schema = 'auth' AND ccu.table_name = 'users')
        OR (ccu.table_schema = 'public' AND ccu.table_name = 'perfiles')
      )
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format(
      'DELETE FROM public.%I WHERE %I IS NOT NULL AND %I <> $1',
      r_fk.table_name,
      r_fk.column_name,
      r_fk.column_name
    ) USING v_admin_id;
  END LOOP;

  -- Eliminar TODAS las cuentas de prueba en public.perfiles, auth.identities y auth.users
  DELETE FROM public.perfiles WHERE id <> v_admin_id;
  DELETE FROM auth.identities WHERE user_id <> v_admin_id;
  DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id <> v_admin_id;

  -- Transformar la cuenta Admin a 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx' conservando su encrypted_password actual
  UPDATE auth.users
  SET email = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      email_confirmed_at = NOW(),
      updated_at = NOW(),
      raw_app_meta_data = COALESCE(raw_app_meta_data, '{"provider":"email","providers":["email"]}'::jsonb),
      raw_user_meta_data = '{"nombre":"Kinetic Oficial","rol":"admin","tipo_cuenta":"admin"}'::jsonb
  WHERE id = v_admin_id;

  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM auth.identities WHERE user_id = v_admin_id AND provider = 'email'
  ) THEN
    UPDATE auth.identities
    SET identity_data = COALESCE(identity_data, '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
          'sub', v_admin_id::text,
          'email', 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'email_verified', true
        ),
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE user_id = v_admin_id
      AND provider = 'email';
  ELSE
    INSERT INTO auth.identities (
      id,
      user_id,
      identity_data,
      provider,
      provider_id,
      last_sign_in_at,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    ) VALUES (
      v_admin_id,
      v_admin_id,
      jsonb_build_object(
        'sub', v_admin_id::text,
        'email', 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
        'email_verified', true
      ),
      'email',
      v_admin_id::text,
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      NOW()
    );
  END IF;

  -- Asegurar registro único en public.perfiles con saldos y contadores en 0
  INSERT INTO public.perfiles (
    id,
    email,
    nombre,
    rol,
    nivel,
    kc_membresia,
    kc_topup,
    saldo_kc,
    xp_total,
    racha_dias,
    recompensas_reclamadas
  ) VALUES (
    v_admin_id,
    'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
    'Kinetic Oficial',
    'admin',
    'Black',
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0,
    '{}'
  )
  ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
  SET email = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      nombre = 'Kinetic Oficial',
      rol = 'admin',
      nivel = 'Black',
      kc_membresia = 0,
      kc_topup = 0,
      saldo_kc = 0,
      xp_total = 0,
      racha_dias = 0,
      recompensas_reclamadas = '{}';

  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_schema = 'public'
      AND table_name = 'perfiles'
      AND column_name = 'tipo_cuenta'
  ) INTO v_has_tipo_cuenta;

  IF v_has_tipo_cuenta THEN
    EXECUTE 'UPDATE public.perfiles SET tipo_cuenta = ''admin'' WHERE id = $1' USING v_admin_id;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. FUNCIÓN Y POLÍTICAS RLS DE ADMIN MAESTRO + RPC INYECCIÓN BÓVEDA
-- ============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com'
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  );
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin() TO authenticated, anon;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_is_admin BOOLEAN;
  v_mem NUMERIC;
  v_top NUMERIC;
  v_nivel TEXT;
BEGIN
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com'
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  ) INTO v_is_admin;

  IF NOT COALESCE(v_is_admin, FALSE) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado: requiere privilegios de Admin Maestro Kinetic.';
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base')
    INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = p_user_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN
    v_mem := GREATEST(0, v_mem + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  ELSE
    v_top := GREATEST(0, v_top + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  END IF;

  IF p_nuevo_nivel IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel)) > 0 THEN
    v_nivel := TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel);
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET kc_membresia = v_mem,
      kc_topup = v_top,
      saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top,
      nivel = v_nivel
  WHERE id = p_user_id;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'kc_membresia', v_mem,
    'kc_topup', v_top,
    'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top,
    'nivel', v_nivel
  );
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_fondos_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(p_user_id, p_cantidad, p_billetera, p_nuevo_nivel);
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_fondos_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;

-- Políticas RLS de Admin Maestro sobre tablas operativas y fundadores_waitlist
ALTER TABLE public.fundadores_waitlist ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Insercion publica fundadores_waitlist" ON public.fundadores_waitlist;
CREATE POLICY "Insercion publica fundadores_waitlist"
ON public.fundadores_waitlist
FOR INSERT
TO anon, authenticated
WITH CHECK (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona fundadores_waitlist" ON public.fundadores_waitlist;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona fundadores_waitlist"
ON public.fundadores_waitlist
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona afiliados" ON public.afiliados;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona afiliados"
ON public.afiliados
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona servicios" ON public.servicios;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona servicios"
ON public.servicios
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona perfiles" ON public.perfiles;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona perfiles"
ON public.perfiles
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. VERIFICACIÓN FINAL DE CONTEOS OFICIALES
-- ============================================================================
SELECT
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM auth.users) AS total_auth_users,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.perfiles) AS total_perfiles,
  (SELECT email || ' — ' || nombre FROM public.perfiles LIMIT 1) AS admin_unico,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.servicios) AS total_servicios,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.fundadores_waitlist) AS total_fundadores_waitlist,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.afiliados) AS total_afiliados_reales_conservados;

    FILE: src/__tests__/adversarial/adminHubStage7.adversarial.test.ts

    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/adminHubStage7.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { buildPartnerSpeiWhatsappUrl, buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload, computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation, sanitizePendingB2BChanges } from '../../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import { calcularCorteSemanalB2B, isAdminOrB2BAccount } from '../../services/adminB2BCutEngine';

declare const describe: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;
declare const it: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;

const expectTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string): void => { if (!cond) throw new Error(msg); };
const expectEq = <T>(actual: T, expected: T, msg: string): void => {
  if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(`${msg} (expected=${String(expected)}, actual=${String(actual)})`);
};
const ALLOWED_KEYS = new Set(['nombre', 'ubicacion', 'whatsapp', 'horarios', 'disciplina', 'portada_url', 'avatar_url']);

describe('Admin Hub CEO v2.6.8 — Allowlist B2B, Tesorería ACID, Consolidación SPEI & Ficha/WhatsApp (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  it('Propiedad 1: sanitizePendingB2BChanges bloquea 100% claves sensibles/__proto__ y URLs javascript:/data:', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.record({
          nombre: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 320 }),
          ubicacion: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 320 }),
          whatsapp: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 40 }),
          horarios: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 320 }),
          disciplina: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 80 }),
          portada_url: fc.oneof(
            fc.constant('https://cdn.kineticapp.com.mx/cover.jpg'),
            fc.constant('javascript:alert(1)'),
            fc.constant('data:text/html;base64,PHNjcmlwdD4='),
            fc.constant('http://insecure.example.com/a.jpg'),
            fc.string({ maxLength: 100 })
          ),
          avatar_url: fc.oneof(
            fc.constant('https://cdn.kineticapp.com.mx/avatar.jpg'),
            fc.constant('javascript:void(0)'),
            fc.constant('data:image/svg+xml;utf8,<svg/>')
          ),
          id: fc.uuid(),
          propietario_id: fc.uuid(),
          estado: fc.constantFrom('activo', 'aprobado', 'admin'),
          verificado: fc.boolean(),
          strikes_operativos: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 3 }),
          estrellas_exclusividad: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
          rfc: fc.constant('EKU9003173C9'),
          regimen_fiscal: fc.constant('persona_moral'),
          clabe_bancaria: fc.constant('012345678901234567'),
          csf_validada: fc.constant(true),
          email_contacto: fc.constant('attacker@evil.com'),
        }),
        fc.boolean(),
        (rawObj, asJson) => {
          const payload = asJson
            ? JSON.stringify({ ...rawObj, ['__proto__']: { isAdmin: true }, constructor: 'hack', prototype: 'hack' })
            : Object.assign(Object.create(null), rawObj, { constructor: 'hack', prototype: 'hack' });

          const safe = sanitizePendingB2BChanges(payload);
          for (const k of Object.keys(safe)) {
            expectTrue(ALLOWED_KEYS.has(k), `forbidden key leaked: ${k}`);
            expectTrue(typeof safe[k] === 'string' && safe[k].length > 0, `empty or non-string value in ${k}`);
            if (k === 'portada_url' || k === 'avatar_url') {
              expectTrue(safe[k].startsWith('https://') && safe[k].length <= 600, `unsafe URL in ${k}: ${safe[k]}`);
            } else {
              expectTrue(safe[k].length <= 240, `unbounded text length in ${k}`);
            }
          }
          expectEq(({} as any).isAdmin, undefined, 'zero prototype pollution');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 2: computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation garantiza saldos >= 0, enteros exactos y mem + top === total en secuencias', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.integer({ min: -500, max: 250_000 }),
        fc.integer({ min: -500, max: 250_000 }),
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            mode: fc.constantFrom<'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset'>('inyectar', 'retirar', 'reset'),
            wallet: fc.constantFrom<'membresia' | 'topup'>('membresia', 'topup'),
            rawDelta: fc.oneof(
              fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 100_000 }),
              fc.integer({ min: -50_000, max: -1 }),
              fc.integer({ min: 100_001, max: 999_999 }),
              fc.constant(NaN),
              fc.constant(Infinity),
              fc.constant(-Infinity)
            ),
          }),
          { minLength: 1, maxLength: 15 }
        ),
        (initMem, initTop, ops) => {
          let mem = Math.max(0, initMem);
          let top = Math.max(0, initTop);
          for (const op of ops) {
            const res = computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation({
              currentMem: mem, currentTop: top, rawDelta: op.rawDelta, wallet: op.wallet, mode: op.mode,
            });
            expectTrue(Number.isSafeInteger(res.effectiveDelta), 'effectiveDelta must be integer');
            expectTrue(Number.isSafeInteger(res.projectedMem) && res.projectedMem >= 0, 'projectedMem >= 0');
            expectTrue(Number.isSafeInteger(res.projectedTop) && res.projectedTop >= 0, 'projectedTop >= 0');
            expectEq(res.projectedMem + res.projectedTop, res.projectedTotal, 'mem + top === total');

            if (!Number.isFinite(op.rawDelta) || op.rawDelta < 0 || op.rawDelta > 100_000) {
              expectTrue(!res.ok && res.effectiveDelta === 0, 'invalid delta must fail closed');

              expectTrue(res.ok, 'valid bounded delta must succeed');
              mem = res.projectedMem;
              top = res.projectedTop;
            }
          }
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

const parseRgbaAlpha = (rgba: string): number => {
  const m = String(rgba || '').match(/rgba?\([^,]+,[^,]+,[^,]+,\s*([0-9.]+)\s*\)/i);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : 1;
};

import fc from 'fast-check';
import ts from 'typescript';
import { TOPUP_PACKAGES } from '../../components/boveda/TopUpPackagesSection';
import { buildAdminSpeiBatchCsvPayload, sanitizeSpeiCsvCell } from '../../services/admin/adminSpeiBatchCsvHelper';

  resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan,
  simulateBovedaSandboxPurchase,
  simulateMonthlyStreakShieldActivation,
} from '../../services/kineticFintechSecurityGuards';

interface NodeFs { existsSync(p: string): boolean; statSync(p: string): { isFile(): boolean; size: number }; readdirSync(p: string, o: { withFileTypes: true }): { name: string; isDirectory(): boolean }[]; readFileSync(p: string, e: 'utf8'): string; }
interface NodePath { resolve(...a: string[]): string; join(...a: string[]): string; dirname(p: string): string; relative(a: string, b: string): string; }
declare const require: (id: string) => unknown;
declare const __dirname: string;
const fs = require('node:fs') as NodeFs;
const path = require('node:path') as NodePath;

const g = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
const describeSuite = typeof g.describe === 'function' ? (g.describe as (n: string, fn: () => void) => void) : (_n: string, fn: () => void) => fn();
const itCase = typeof g.it === 'function' ? (g.it as (n: string, fn: () => unknown) => void) : (_n: string, fn: () => unknown) => fn();
const assertTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string): void => { if (!cond) throw new Error(msg); };
const assertEq = <T>(actual: T, expected: T, msg: string): void => {
  if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(`${msg} (expected=${String(expected)}, actual=${String(actual)})`);
};

const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..');
const SRC_ROOT = path.join(ROOT, 'src');
const NUM_RUNS = 500;
const PLATFORM_EXTS = ['', '.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.web.ts', '.web.tsx', '.native.ts', '.native.tsx', '.ios.ts', '.ios.tsx', '.android.ts', '.android.tsx'];
const V284_TOUCHED_FILES = [

  'src/components/kineticlub/StreakModal.tsx',

  'supabase/functions/stripe-create-payment/index.ts',
  'supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/index.ts',
];

function walkFiles(dir: string, exts: RegExp, skipTests = false): string[] {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const ent of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, ent.name);
    if (ent.isDirectory()) {
      if (ent.name === 'node_modules' || (skipTests && ent.name === '__tests__')) continue;
      out.push(...walkFiles(full, exts, skipTests));
    } else if (exts.test(ent.name) && !ent.name.endsWith('.d.ts')) {
      out.push(path.resolve(full));
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function resolveMatches(fromFile: string, spec: string): string[] {
  if (!spec || (!spec.startsWith('.') && !spec.startsWith('@/'))) return [];
  const base = spec.startsWith('@/') ? path.join(SRC_ROOT, spec.slice(2)) : path.resolve(path.dirname(fromFile), spec);
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const ext of PLATFORM_EXTS) {
    const cand = ext ? `${base}${ext}` : base;
    if (fs.existsSync(cand) && fs.statSync(cand).isFile()) out.push(path.resolve(cand));
  }
  for (const idx of ['.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.web.ts', '.web.tsx']) {
    const cand = path.join(base, `index${idx}`);
    if (fs.existsSync(cand) && fs.statSync(cand).isFile()) out.push(path.resolve(cand));
  }
  return [...new Set(out)];
}

function extractSpecs(filePath: string, content: string, includeTypes: boolean): string[] {
  const kind = filePath.endsWith('.tsx') || filePath.endsWith('.jsx') ? ts.ScriptKind.TSX : ts.ScriptKind.TS;
  const sf = ts.createSourceFile(filePath, content, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true, kind);
  const specs: string[] = [];
  const visit = (node: ts.Node) => {
    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(node) && ts.isStringLiteral(node.moduleSpecifier)) {
      const cl = node.importClause;
      if (!includeTypes && cl && (cl.isTypeOnly || (!cl.name && cl.namedBindings && ts.isNamedImports(cl.namedBindings) && !cl.namedBindings.elements.some((e) => !e.isTypeOnly)))) return;
      specs.push(node.moduleSpecifier.text);
    } else if (ts.isExportDeclaration(node) && node.moduleSpecifier && ts.isStringLiteral(node.moduleSpecifier)) {
      if (!includeTypes && (node.isTypeOnly || (node.exportClause && ts.isNamedExports(node.exportClause) && node.exportClause.elements.every((e) => e.isTypeOnly)))) return;
      specs.push(node.moduleSpecifier.text);
    } else if (ts.isCallExpression(node) && node.arguments.length >= 1 && ts.isStringLiteral(node.arguments[0]) && ((ts.isIdentifier(node.expression) && node.expression.text === 'require') || node.expression.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ImportKeyword)) {
      specs.push(node.arguments[0].text);
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, visit);
  };
  visit(sf);
  return specs;
}

function scanGraph() {
  const srcFiles = walkFiles(SRC_ROOT, /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, true);
  const astAdj = new Map<string, string[]>(), rtAdj = new Map<string, string[]>(), fullAdj = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const f of srcFiles) {
    const content = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
    const astDeps = new Set<string>(), fullDeps = new Set<string>(), rtDeps = new Set<string>();
    for (const s of extractSpecs(f, content, false)) { const m = resolveMatches(f, s); if (m[0]) astDeps.add(m[0]); }
    for (const s of extractSpecs(f, content, true)) for (const m of resolveMatches(f, s)) fullDeps.add(m);
    const cjs = ts.transpileModule(content, { compilerOptions: { module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2020, jsx: ts.JsxEmit.React, esModuleInterop: true }, fileName: f }).outputText;
    for (const s of extractSpecs(`${f}.js`, cjs, true)) { const m = resolveMatches(f, s); if (m[0]) rtDeps.add(m[0]); }
    astAdj.set(f, [...astDeps]); fullAdj.set(f, [...fullDeps]); rtAdj.set(f, [...rtDeps]);
  }
  const findSccs = (adj: Map<string, string[]>): string[][] => {
    let idx = 0;
    const ind = new Map<string, number>(), low = new Map<string, number>(), st: string[] = [], on = new Set<string>(), sccs: string[][] = [];
    const sc = (v: string) => {
      ind.set(v, idx); low.set(v, idx); idx += 1; st.push(v); on.add(v);
      for (const w of adj.get(v) || []) {
        if (!ind.has(w)) { sc(w); low.set(v, Math.min(low.get(v)!, low.get(w)!)); }
        else if (on.has(w)) low.set(v, Math.min(low.get(v)!, ind.get(w)!));
      }
      if (low.get(v) === ind.get(v)) {
        const comp: string[] = [];
        while (true) { const w = st.pop()!; on.delete(w); comp.push(w); if (w === v) break; }
        if (comp.length > 1 || (adj.get(v) || []).includes(v)) sccs.push(comp.map((p) => path.relative(SRC_ROOT, p)));
      }
    };
    for (const v of adj.keys()) if (!ind.has(v)) sc(v);
    return sccs;
  };
  const testsRoot = path.join(SRC_ROOT, '__tests__');
  const seeds = [...walkFiles(path.join(SRC_ROOT, 'app'), /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, false), ...walkFiles(testsRoot, /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, false), ...walkFiles(path.join(ROOT, 'scripts'), /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs)$/, false), ...walkFiles(path.join(ROOT, 'supabase', 'functions'), /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, false)];
  const visited = new Set<string>(), q: string[] = [];
  for (const s of seeds) {
    const abs = path.resolve(s);
    if (abs.startsWith(SRC_ROOT) && !abs.startsWith(testsRoot)) { if (!visited.has(abs)) { visited.add(abs); q.push(abs); } }
    else if (fs.existsSync(abs)) for (const sp of extractSpecs(abs, fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8'), true)) for (const m of resolveMatches(abs, sp)) if (!visited.has(m)) { visited.add(m); q.push(m); }
  }
  while (q.length > 0) for (const nxt of fullAdj.get(q.shift()!) || []) if (!visited.has(nxt)) { visited.add(nxt); q.push(nxt); }
  return { astCycles: findSccs(astAdj), rtCycles: findSccs(rtAdj), orphans: srcFiles.filter((f) => !visited.has(f)).map((f) => path.relative(SRC_ROOT, f)) };
}

function parseRfc4180CsvRows(csvWithoutBom: string): string[][] {
  const rows: string[][] = [];
  let row: string[] = [], cell = '', inQuotes = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < csvWithoutBom.length; i += 1) {
    const ch = csvWithoutBom[i], next = csvWithoutBom[i + 1];
    if (inQuotes) {
      if (ch === '"' && next === '"') { cell += '"'; i += 1; }
      else if (ch === '"') inQuotes = false;
      else cell += ch;
    } else if (ch === '"') inQuotes = true;
    else if (ch === ',') { row.push(cell); cell = ''; }
    else if (ch === '\r' && next === '\n') { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); row = []; cell = ''; i += 1; }
    else if (ch === '\n') { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); row = []; cell = ''; }
    else cell += ch;
  }
  if (cell.length > 0 || row.length > 0) { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); }
  return rows;
}

describeSuite('KINETIC OS v2.8.4 — Piso Top-Up 100 KC, Escudo Mensual & Layout Bancario SPEI .CSV (500x)', () => {
  itCase('G1: 0 ciclos AST/RT, 0 huerfanos, <450L, 0 any, .env.example 887B, topup_50 purgado y >=33 suites', () => {
    const { astCycles, rtCycles, orphans } = scanGraph();
    assertEq(astCycles.length, 0, `AST cycles detected: ${JSON.stringify(astCycles)}`);
    assertEq(rtCycles.length, 0, `RT cycles detected: ${JSON.stringify(rtCycles)}`);
    assertEq(orphans.length, 0, `Orphans detected: ${JSON.stringify(orphans)}`);
    for (const f of [...walkFiles(SRC_ROOT, /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, true), ...walkFiles(path.join(ROOT, 'supabase', 'functions'), /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, false)]) {
      assertTrue(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).length < 500, `Global <500L violated in ${path.relative(ROOT, f)}`);
    }
    assertEq(walkFiles(path.join(SRC_ROOT, 'app'), /\.styles\.(ts|tsx|js)$/, false).length, 0, 'Styles forbidden in src/app');
    const anyRe = new RegExp([':\\s*an' + 'y\\b', '\\bas\\s+an' + 'y\\b', '<an' + 'y>'].join('|'));
    for (const rel of V284_TOUCHED_FILES) {
      const txt = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), 'utf8');
      const lines = txt.split(/\r?\n/).length;
      assertTrue(lines < 450, `<450L violated in ${rel} (${lines})`);
      assertTrue(!anyRe.test(txt), `Forbidden any in ${rel}`);
      assertTrue(!/@ts-(ignore|expect-error)|\bconsole\.log\s*\(|\beval\s*\(/.test(txt), `Hygiene violation in ${rel}`);
    }
    const envFiles = fs.readdirSync(ROOT, { withFileTypes: true }).map((e) => e.name).filter((n) => n.startsWith('.env'));
    assertEq(envFiles.join(','), '.env.example', 'Only .env.example allowed in root');
    assertEq(fs.statSync(path.join(ROOT, '.env.example')).size, 887, '.env.example must remain 887B');

    assertEq(TOPUP_PACKAGES.map((p) => p.id).join(','), 'topup_100,topup_250,topup_500,topup_1000', 'TOPUP_PACKAGES must start at 100 KC');
    assertEq(Object.keys(CANONICAL_TOPUP_KC_BY_ID).join(','), 'topup_100,topup_250,topup_500,topup_1000', 'CANONICAL_TOPUP_KC_BY_ID must omit topup_50');

    const sqlRel = 'supabase/migrations/20261123010000_migration_v284_topup_min100_and_monthly_shield.sql';
    const sqlTxt = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, sqlRel), 'utf8');
    assertTrue(!/\b(?:topup_50|pkg_50)\b/i.test(sqlTxt), 'v2.8.4 SQL migration must not include topup_50');
    assertTrue(sqlTxt.includes('BEGIN;') && sqlTxt.includes('COMMIT;'), 'SQL migration must be transactional');
    assertTrue(sqlTxt.includes('SET search_path = pg_catalog, public'), 'SQL RPCs must pin search_path = pg_catalog, public');
    assertTrue(sqlTxt.includes('COALESCE(v_sandbox_ok, FALSE)') && sqlTxt.includes('fn_read_claimed_rewards_text_arr'), 'SQL must fail closed on missing sandbox flag and enforce daily shield idempotency');
    assertTrue(sqlTxt.includes('escudos_usados_mes') && sqlTxt.includes('escudo_mes_iso'), 'SQL migration must add & protect monthly shield columns');
    for (const edgeRel of ['supabase/functions/stripe-create-payment/index.ts', 'supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/index.ts']) {
      assertTrue(!/\btopup_50\b/.test(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, edgeRel), 'utf8')), `${edgeRel} must not accept topup_50`);
    }
    const streakTxt = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'src/components/kineticlub/StreakModal.tsx'), 'utf8');
    const rewardsTxt = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'src/hooks/kineticlub/useKinetiClubRewards.ts'), 'utf8');
    assertTrue(streakTxt.includes('1 GRATIS/MES') && streakTxt.includes('1 FREE/MO'), 'StreakModal CTA must reflect monthly shield');
    assertTrue(!/GRATIS\/SEM|FREE\/WK|semanal/i.test(streakTxt) && !/semanal|weekly shield/i.test(rewardsTxt), 'Weekly shield copy must be replaced with monthly');
    assertTrue(fs.readdirSync(path.join(SRC_ROOT, '__tests__', 'adversarial'), { withFileTypes: true }).filter((e) => e.name.endsWith('.adversarial.test.ts')).length >= 33, 'At least 33 adversarial suites required');
  });

  itCase('P1 (500x): Rechazo topup_50/50/pkg_50/prototipos y paridad exacta 1.0x VIP vs 1.2x Base para [100,250,500,1000]', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.constantFrom('topup_50', '50', 'pkg_50', '__proto__', 'constructor', 'toString', '', 'topup_999'),
        fc.constantFrom(100, 250, 500, 1000),
        fc.constantFrom<'prefix' | 'bare' | 'pkg'>('prefix', 'bare', 'pkg'),
        fc.constantFrom('Acceso Base', 'Bronze', 'Silver', 'Gold', 'Platinum'),
        (badPlan, validKc, fmt, lvl) => {
          let checkoutThrew = false;
          try { resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan({ tipo: 'topup', planId: badPlan, currentLevel: lvl }); } catch { checkoutThrew = true; }
          assertTrue(checkoutThrew, `resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan must reject ${badPlan}`);
          const badSim = simulateBovedaSandboxPurchase({ planId: badPlan, tipo: 'topup', currentLevel: lvl, kcTopup: 200 });
          assertEq(badSim.ok, false, `Sandbox must reject ${badPlan}`);
          assertEq(badSim.kcInyectados, 0, 'Rejected purchase must inject 0 KC');
          assertEq(badSim.kcTopup, 200, 'Rejected purchase must not mutate balance');

          const validId = fmt === 'prefix' ? `topup_${validKc}` : fmt === 'bare' ? String(validKc) : `pkg_${validKc}`;
          const sim = simulateBovedaSandboxPurchase({ planId: validId, tipo: 'topup', currentLevel: lvl, kcMembresia: 500, kcTopup: 100 });
          assertTrue(sim.ok, `Valid topup ${validId} must succeed`);
          assertEq(sim.kcInyectados, validKc, 'Injected KC must match package');
          assertEq(sim.kcTopup, 100 + validKc, 'Topup wallet must increment');
          const expectedMxn = lvl === 'Acceso Base' ? Number((validKc * 1.2).toFixed(2)) : validKc;
          assertEq(sim.montoMxn, expectedMxn, 'VIP 1.0x vs Base 1.2x pricing invariant violated');
          if (fmt !== 'pkg') {
            const plan = resolveCanonicalCheckoutPlan({ tipo: 'topup', planId: validId, currentLevel: lvl });
            assertEq(plan.precioMxn, expectedMxn, 'Canonical checkout price must match 1.0x vs 1.2x');
          }
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: NUM_RUNS }
    );
  });

  itCase('P2 (500x): Escudo de Racha mensual (1 gratis/mes = 0 KC, 2do+ = 25 KC mem->topup, rechazo sin saldo y reset mensual)', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 60 }),
        fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 60 }),
        fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 12 }),
        fc.integer({ min: 2026, max: 2028 }),
        (initMem, initTop, monthNum, yearNum) => {
          const m1 = `${yearNum}-${String(monthNum).padStart(2, '0')}`;
          const m2 = monthNum === 12 ? `${yearNum + 1}-01` : `${yearNum}-${String(monthNum + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
          const first = simulateMonthlyStreakShieldActivation({ currentMonthKey: m1, escudoMesIso: null, escudosUsadosMes: 0, kcMembresia: initMem, kcTopup: initTop });
          assertTrue(first.ok && first.gratis && first.costoKc === 0 && first.escudosUsadosMes === 1, '1st monthly shield must be free');
          assertEq(first.kcMembresia, initMem, 'Free shield must not touch kc_membresia');
          assertEq(first.kcTopup, initTop, 'Free shield must not touch kc_topup');

          const second = simulateMonthlyStreakShieldActivation({ currentMonthKey: m1, escudoMesIso: first.escudoMesIso, escudosUsadosMes: first.escudosUsadosMes, kcMembresia: first.kcMembresia, kcTopup: first.kcTopup });
          if (initMem + initTop < STREAK_SHIELD_EXTRA_KC_COST) {
            assertTrue(!second.ok && second.errorCode === 'SALDO_INSUFICIENTE_ESCUDO', 'Must reject 2nd shield when balance < 25');
            assertEq(second.kcMembresia, initMem, 'Rejected shield must keep kcMembresia unchanged');
            assertEq(second.kcTopup, initTop, 'Rejected shield must keep kcTopup unchanged');
            assertEq(second.escudosUsadosMes, 1, 'Rejected shield must not increment monthly counter');
          } else {
            assertTrue(second.ok && !second.gratis && second.costoKc === 25 && second.escudosUsadosMes === 2, '2nd shield in same month must cost 25 KC');
            const expectedUseMem = Math.min(initMem, 25);
            assertEq(second.kcMembresia, initMem - expectedUseMem, 'Priority 1 must deduct kcMembresia first');
            assertEq(second.kcTopup, initTop - (25 - expectedUseMem), 'Priority 2 must deduct remainder from kcTopup');
            assertTrue(second.kcMembresia >= 0 && second.kcTopup >= 0, 'Wallets can never go negative');
          }

          const nextMonth = simulateMonthlyStreakShieldActivation({ currentMonthKey: m2, escudoMesIso: second.escudoMesIso, escudosUsadosMes: second.escudosUsadosMes, kcMembresia: second.kcMembresia, kcTopup: second.kcTopup });
          assertTrue(nextMonth.ok && nextMonth.gratis && nextMonth.costoKc === 0 && nextMonth.escudosUsadosMes === 1, 'New calendar month must reset free shield quota');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: NUM_RUNS }
    );
  });

  itCase('P3 (500x): Layout SPEI Masivo .CSV excluye sin CLABE/neto<=0, suma exacta al centavo y neutraliza CSV Injection', () => {
    const validClabeArb = fc.array(fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 9 }), { minLength: 18, maxLength: 18 }).map((a) => a.join(''));
    const formulaPrefixArb = fc.constantFrom('=CMD|"/C calc"!A0', '+2+3', '-10', '@SUM(A1:A2)', '\t=1+1', '\r=2+2', 'Club "Pro", Elite');
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            id: fc.uuid(),
            nombre: fc.oneof(formulaPrefixArb, fc.string({ minLength: 1, maxLength: 35 })),
            clabeValid: fc.boolean(),
            clabe: validClabeArb,
            netoCents: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 500_000 }),
          }),
          { minLength: 1, maxLength: 12 }
        ),
        (items) => {
          const cortes = items.map((it) => ({
            afiliado_id: it.id, afiliado_nombre: it.nombre, disciplina: 'Pilates', clabe_bancaria: it.clabeValid ? it.clabe : '12345',
            rfc: 'KIN250101AB1', regimen_fiscal: 'persona_moral', categoria_operador: 'club',
            periodo_inicio: '2026-11-16', periodo_fin: '2026-11-22', sesiones_completadas: 5,
            volumen_bruto_mxn: Number((it.netoCents / 80).toFixed(2)), comision_kinetic_total_mxn: Number((it.netoCents / 400).toFixed(2)),
            comision_kinetic_base_mxn: 0, comision_kinetic_iva_mxn: 0, subtotal_socio_80_mxn: Number((it.netoCents / 100).toFixed(2)),
            tasa_retencion_isr_pct: 0, tasa_retencion_iva_pct: 0, retencion_isr_mxn: 0, retencion_iva_mxn: 0, total_retenciones_sat_mxn: 0,
            neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: Number((it.netoCents / 100).toFixed(2)), en_custodia_escrow_kc: 0,
          })) as unknown as ICorteSemanalAfiliado[];
          const reporte: Partial<IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO> = { periodo_inicio: '2026-11-16', periodo_fin: '2026-11-22', cortes_por_afiliado: cortes };
          const payload = buildAdminSpeiBatchCsvPayload(reporte, { referenceDateIso: '2026-11-22' });

          const expectedReady = items.filter((it) => it.netoCents > 0 && it.clabeValid);
          const expectedSkipped = items.filter((it) => it.netoCents > 0 && !it.clabeValid);
          assertEq(payload.fileName, 'Kinetic_Layout_SPEI_Masivo_2026-11-22.csv', 'Clean deterministic CSV filename required');
          assertEq(payload.readyCount, expectedReady.length, 'Ready row count mismatch');
          assertEq(payload.skippedCount, expectedSkipped.length, 'Skipped invalid CLABE count mismatch');
          assertTrue(payload.csvContent.startsWith('\uFEFF'), 'UTF-8 BOM required');

          const parsed = parseRfc4180CsvRows(payload.csvContent.slice(1));
          assertEq(parsed.length, 1 + expectedReady.length, 'CSV line count mismatch');
          assertEq(parsed[0].length, 12, 'CSV header must have 12 columns');
          let parsedNetCents = 0;
          for (let r = 1; r < parsed.length; r += 1) {
            const cols = parsed[r];
            assertEq(cols.length, 12, `Row ${r} must have exactly 12 RFC-4180 columns`);
            assertTrue(/^\d{18}$/.test(cols[1]), 'CLABE_18_DIGITOS must be 18 digits');
            assertTrue(/^\d{7}$/.test(cols[4]), 'REFERENCIA_NUMERICA must be 7 digits');
            assertTrue(cols[3].length <= 40, 'CONCEPTO_SPEI max 40 chars');
            assertTrue(!/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(cols[0]), `CSV formula injection leaked in BENEFICIARIO: ${cols[0]}`);
            parsedNetCents += Math.round(Number(cols[2]) * 100);
          }
          const expectedTotalCents = expectedReady.reduce((acc, it) => acc + it.netoCents, 0);
          assertEq(parsedNetCents, expectedTotalCents, 'CSV total neto must match to exact centavo');
          assertEq(Math.round(payload.totalNetoSpeiMxn * 100), expectedTotalCents, 'Payload totalNetoSpeiMxn centavo precision mismatch');
          assertTrue(!/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(sanitizeSpeiCsvCell('=1+1').replace(/^"/, '')), 'sanitizeSpeiCsvCell must neutralize leading =');
          const infRep = buildAdminSpeiBatchCsvPayload({
            periodo_fin: '2026-11-22',
            cortes_por_afiliado: [{ afiliado_id: 'a1', afiliado_nombre: 'Club Inf', disciplina: 'Box', clabe_bancaria: '012345678901234567', rfc: 'KIN250101AB1', regimen_fiscal: 'persona_moral', categoria_operador: 'club', periodo_inicio: '2026-11-16', periodo_fin: '2026-11-22', sesiones_completadas: 1, volumen_bruto_mxn: Infinity, comision_kinetic_total_mxn: NaN, comision_kinetic_base_mxn: 0, comision_kinetic_iva_mxn: 0, subtotal_socio_80_mxn: 100, tasa_retencion_isr_pct: 0, tasa_retencion_iva_pct: 0, retencion_isr_mxn: -5, retencion_iva_mxn: Infinity, total_retenciones_sat_mxn: 0, neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: 100, en_custodia_escrow_kc: 0 }],
          });
          assertTrue(!/Infinity|NaN/i.test(infRep.csvContent), 'CSV must never emit Infinity or NaN');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: NUM_RUNS }
    );
  });
});

    FILE: src/app/(tabs)/admin.tsx

    // === src/app/(tabs)/admin.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Platform,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  StatusBar,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import {
  AdminHubDashboardCards,
  baseAdm,
} from '../../components/admin/AdminHubDashboardCards';
import { AdminModuleModal, AdminModuleType } from '../../components/AdminModuleModal';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from '../../components/BovedaModals';
import { EventDetailsModal } from '../../components/EventDetailsModal';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useAdmin } from '../../hooks/useAdmin';
import {
  buildDefaultAdminEvent,
  buildEditableAdminEvent,
  normalizeAdminTopDisciplinas,
} from '../../services/admin/adminScreenHelpers';
import { isMasterAdminAccount } from '../../services/adminService';
import { isRealSupabaseJwt } from '../../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import { COLORS, SPACING, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { subscribeKineticRootRefresh } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';

export type WalletContextFilter = 'all' | 'membresia' | 'topup';

export default function AdminHubScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user, session, profile } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, t, tx, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const adm = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseAdm, resolvedTheme),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<{
    visible: boolean;
    title?: string;
    message?: string;
    type?: string;
    confirmText?: string;
    cancelText?: string;
    onConfirm?: (() => void) | null;
  }>({ visible: false });
  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback(
    (
      title: string,
      message: string,
      type = 'alert',
      onConfirm: (() => void) | null = null,
      confirmText = tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
      cancelText = tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL')
    ) => {
      setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type, confirmText, cancelText, onConfirm });
    },
    [tx]
  );

  const canAdminDelete = useMemo(() => {
    if (!user?.id || !isRealSupabaseJwt(session?.access_token)) return false;
    const emailConfirmed = Boolean((user as { email_confirmed_at?: unknown } | null)?.email_confirmed_at);
    return emailConfirmed && isMasterAdminAccount(user.email);
  }, [user, session?.access_token]);

  const {
    hasHydratedAdminKpis, refreshing, isSyncing, lastUpdatedLabel, kpis, solicitudes, preRegistrosWeb,
    preRegistrosPendientesCount, ugcPendingCount, experienciasActivas, sociosReales, atletasList,
    reservasEscrowActivas, reservasHistoricas, auditLogs, distribucionNiveles,
    topDisciplinas, reporteCorteSemanal, isInjectingKC, isUpdatingFiscal, isExportingPDF,
    isSubmitting, togglingAffiliateName, isSavingManualAffiliate, isSweepingNoShow, cargarDatos, onRefresh,
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado, crearAfiliadoManual, eliminarSocioB2B, cambiarEstadoServicio,
    gestionarPreRegistroWeb, guardarEvento, eliminarEvento, eliminarCuentaAtleta, inyectarKCAtleta,
    actualizarFiscalSocio, exportarReporteMaestroPDF, exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF, copiarDatoBancario,
    ejecutarBarridoNoShow40,
  } = useAdmin(showTacticalAlert, { enabled: canAdminDelete, userId: user?.id });

  const [activeModule, setActiveModule] = useState<AdminModuleType>(null);
  const [moduleHistory, setModuleHistory] = useState<AdminModuleType[]>([]);
  const [walletContextFilter, setWalletContextFilter] = useState<WalletContextFilter>('all');
  const [isCreateModalOpen, setIsCreateModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isEditingEvent, setIsEditingEvent] = useState(false);
  const [hasScrolledHeader, setHasScrolledHeader] = useState(false);
  const hasScrolledRef = useRef(false);
  const returnModuleAfterEditRef = useRef<AdminModuleType>(null);
  const modalSwitchTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const scrollYRef = useRef(0);
  const pullStartYRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const handlePullToRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
    setWalletContextFilter('all');
    await onRefresh();
  }, [onRefresh]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  const prevUserIdRef = useRef<string | undefined>(user?.id);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (prevUserIdRef.current === user?.id) return;
    prevUserIdRef.current = user?.id;
    setActiveModule(null);
    setModuleHistory([]);
    setWalletContextFilter('all');
    setIsCreateModalOpen(false);
    setIsEditingEvent(false);
  }, [user?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return subscribeKineticRootRefresh(() => {
      cargarDatos(true, true);
    });
  }, [cargarDatos]);

  const [nuevoEvento, setNuevoEvento] = useState<Record<string, unknown>>(() => buildDefaultAdminEvent(tx) as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);

  const normalizedTopDisciplinas = useMemo(
    () =>
      normalizeAdminTopDisciplinas(
        topDisciplinas,
        experienciasActivas,
        solicitudes,
        sociosReales
      ),
    [topDisciplinas, experienciasActivas, solicitudes, sociosReales]
  );

  const openRootModule = useCallback(
    (mod: AdminModuleType, walletFilter: WalletContextFilter = 'all') => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      const normalizedMod: AdminModuleType = mod === 'TESORERIA' ? 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS' : mod;
      setModuleHistory([]);
      setWalletContextFilter(walletFilter);
      setActiveModule(normalizedMod);
    },
    []
  );

  const navigateToSubModule = useCallback(
    (targetMod: AdminModuleType, walletFilter: WalletContextFilter = 'all') => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      const normalizedTarget: AdminModuleType =
        targetMod === 'TESORERIA' ? 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS' : targetMod;
      setWalletContextFilter(walletFilter);
      setActiveModule((currentMod) => {
        if (currentMod && currentMod !== normalizedTarget) {
          setModuleHistory((prev) => [...prev, currentMod]);
        }
        return normalizedTarget;
      });
    },
    []
  );

  const handleStepBackModule = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setModuleHistory((prevStack) => {
      if (prevStack.length > 0) {
        const nextStack = [...prevStack];
        const prevMod = nextStack.pop() || null;
        setWalletContextFilter('all');
        setActiveModule(prevMod);
        return nextStack;
      }
      setWalletContextFilter('all');
      setActiveModule(null);
      return [];
    });
  }, []);

  const handleCloseAllModules = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setModuleHistory([]);
    setWalletContextFilter('all');
    setActiveModule(null);
  }, []);

  const abrirCreadorNuevo = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    returnModuleAfterEditRef.current = null;
    setIsEditingEvent(false);
    setNuevoEvento(buildDefaultAdminEvent(tx));
    setIsCreateModalOpen(true);
  }, [tx]);

  const abrirEditorOnTheGo = useCallback(
    (item: Record<string, unknown> | null) => {
      if (!item) {
        const hadModule = Boolean(activeModule);
        setModuleHistory([]);
        setWalletContextFilter('all');
        setActiveModule(null);
        if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
        if (hadModule) {
          modalSwitchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => abrirCreadorNuevo(), 190);
        } else {
          abrirCreadorNuevo();
        }
        return;
      }
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      returnModuleAfterEditRef.current = activeModule;
      setActiveModule(null);
      setIsEditingEvent(true);
      setNuevoEvento(buildEditableAdminEvent(item, tx));
      if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
      modalSwitchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsCreateModalOpen(true), 190);
    },
    [activeModule, abrirCreadorNuevo, tx]
  );

  const handleCloseEditorModal = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setIsCreateModalOpen(false);
    const prevMod = returnModuleAfterEditRef.current;
    if (prevMod) {
      returnModuleAfterEditRef.current = null;
      if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
      modalSwitchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setActiveModule(prevMod), 190);
    }
  }, []);

  const tokenomicsReales = useMemo(() => {
    const sumaMembresia = Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.sumaMembresia) || 0);
    const sumaTopup = Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.sumaTopup) || 0);
    const saldoDisponibleUsuarios = Math.max(
      0,
      Number(kpis?.saldoDisponibleUsuarios) || sumaMembresia + sumaTopup
    );
    const escrowTotal = Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.escrowTotal) || 0);
    const masaMonetariaTotal = Math.max(
      0,
      Number(kpis?.masaMonetariaTotal) || saldoDisponibleUsuarios + escrowTotal
    );
    const liveTotalClubs =
      Array.isArray(sociosReales) && sociosReales.length > 0
        ? sociosReales.length
        : Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.clubesTotales) || 0);
    const liveActiveClubs =
      Array.isArray(sociosReales) && sociosReales.length > 0
        ? sociosReales.filter((s) => Boolean((s as Record<string, unknown>)?.esClubActivo)).length
        : Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.clubesActivos) || 0);
    const liveProspectClubs = Math.max(0, liveTotalClubs - liveActiveClubs);
    return {
      sumaMembresia,
      sumaTopup,
      saldoDisponibleUsuarios,
      escrowTotal,
      masaMonetariaTotal,
      reservasActivasCount: Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.reservasActivasCount) || 0),
      atletasCount: Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.atletasCount) || 0),
      clubesTotales: liveTotalClubs,
      clubesActivos: liveActiveClubs,
      clubesProspectos: liveProspectClubs,
    };
  }, [kpis, sociosReales]);

  const netoSpeiSemanalFormatted = useMemo(() => {
    const val = Math.max(0, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) || 0);
    return val.toLocaleString(language === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 2,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    });
  }, [reporteCorteSemanal?.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn, language]);

  if (!canAdminDelete) {
    return <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]} />;
  }

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={themeColors.background} />
      <View
        style={[
          adm.header,
          {
            paddingTop: insets.top + 14,
            borderBottomWidth: hasScrolledHeader ? 1 : 0,
            borderBottomColor: isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
          <Text style={[adm.headerSub, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('admin.sub')}</Text>
          <Text style={[adm.headerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('admin.title')}</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              adm.refreshBtn,
              isLightMode ? baseAdm.refreshBtnLight : baseAdm.refreshBtnDark,
              {
                elevation: 0,
                minWidth: 122,
                height: 32,
                flexDirection: 'row',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                paddingVertical: 0,
                paddingHorizontal: 10,
                borderRadius: 20,
              },
            ]}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
            onPress={handlePullToRefresh}
            disabled={refreshing}
          >
            {refreshing || (!hasHydratedAdminKpis && isSyncing) ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            ) : (
              <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            )}
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[
                adm.refreshBtnText,
                { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
              ]}
            >
              {refreshing || (!hasHydratedAdminKpis && isSyncing) ? tx('Sincronizando...', 'Syncing...') : lastUpdatedLabel}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={scrollViewRef}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        removeClippedSubviews={false}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        onScroll={(e) => {
          const y = e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
          scrollYRef.current = y;
          const nextScrolled = y > 4;
          if (nextScrolled !== hasScrolledRef.current) {
            hasScrolledRef.current = nextScrolled;
            setHasScrolledHeader(nextScrolled);
          }
        }}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onTouchStart={(e) => {
          if (Platform.OS === 'web' && scrollYRef.current <= 2) {
            const ne = e.nativeEvent as unknown as { pageY?: number; clientY?: number };
            pullStartYRef.current = ne.pageY ?? ne.clientY ?? 0;
          } else {
            pullStartYRef.current = null;
          }
        }}
        onTouchEnd={(e) => {
          if (Platform.OS === 'web' && pullStartYRef.current !== null && !refreshing) {
            const ne = e.nativeEvent as unknown as { pageY?: number; clientY?: number };
            const currentY = ne.pageY ?? ne.clientY ?? 0;
            const deltaY = currentY - pullStartYRef.current;
            pullStartYRef.current = null;
            if (deltaY > 68) {
              handlePullToRefresh();
            }
          }
        }}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg, paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 120 }}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={handlePullToRefresh}
            tintColor="#00F0FF"
            colors={['#00F0FF']}
          />
        }
      >
        <AdminHubDashboardCards
          hasHydratedAdminKpis={hasHydratedAdminKpis}
          tokenomicsReales={tokenomicsReales}
          netoSpeiSemanalFormatted={netoSpeiSemanalFormatted}
          solicitudesCount={solicitudes.length}
          preRegistrosPendientesCount={preRegistrosPendientesCount}
          ugcPendingCount={ugcPendingCount}
          experienciasCount={experienciasActivas.length}
          isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF}
          openRootModule={openRootModule}
          abrirCreadorNuevo={abrirCreadorNuevo}
          exportarReporteMaestroPDF={exportarReporteMaestroPDF}
        />
      </ScrollView>

      <AdminModuleModal
        activeModule={activeModule}
        isLoading={!hasHydratedAdminKpis}
        hasPreviousModule={moduleHistory.length > 0}
        walletContextFilter={walletContextFilter}
        onStepBack={handleStepBackModule}
        onCloseAll={handleCloseAllModules}
        onNavigateModule={navigateToSubModule}
        tokenomicsReales={tokenomicsReales}
        solicitudes={solicitudes} preRegistrosWeb={preRegistrosWeb} ugcPendingCount={ugcPendingCount} experienciasActivas={experienciasActivas}
        sociosReales={sociosReales} atletasList={atletasList} reservasEscrowActivas={reservasEscrowActivas}
        reservasHistoricas={reservasHistoricas} auditLogs={auditLogs} distribucionNiveles={distribucionNiveles}
        topDisciplinas={normalizedTopDisciplinas} reporteCorteSemanal={reporteCorteSemanal}
        cambiarEstadoAfiliado={cambiarEstadoAfiliado} crearAfiliadoManual={crearAfiliadoManual}
        eliminarSocioB2B={eliminarSocioB2B} canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
        togglingAffiliateName={togglingAffiliateName} isSavingManualAffiliate={isSavingManualAffiliate}
        isSubmitting={isSubmitting} cambiarEstadoServicio={cambiarEstadoServicio}
        gestionarPreRegistroWeb={gestionarPreRegistroWeb} guardarEvento={guardarEvento}
        eliminarEvento={eliminarEvento} eliminarCuentaAtleta={eliminarCuentaAtleta} user={user}
        abrirEditorOnTheGo={abrirEditorOnTheGo} isInjectingKC={isInjectingKC}
        inyectarKCAtleta={(atletaId: string, cantidad: string, billetera: 'membresia' | 'topup', nuevoNivel?: string | null, modo?: 'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset', cb?: () => void) => inyectarKCAtleta(atletaId, cantidad, billetera, nuevoNivel, modo, user?.id, cb)}
        actualizarFiscalSocio={actualizarFiscalSocio} isUpdatingFiscal={isUpdatingFiscal}
        exportarReporteMaestroPDF={exportarReporteMaestroPDF} exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF={exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF}
        isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF} copiarDatoBancario={copiarDatoBancario}
        ejecutarBarridoNoShow40={ejecutarBarridoNoShow40} isSweepingNoShow={isSweepingNoShow}
      />

      <EventDetailsModal
        visible={isCreateModalOpen}
        isCreateMode={!isEditingEvent}
        defaultClubName={String(nuevoEvento?.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial')}
        event={nuevoEvento}
        onClose={handleCloseEditorModal}
        onRefreshEvents={() => cargarDatos(true, true)}
      />

      <TacticalAlertModal
        alertConfig={alertConfig}
        setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

interface AdminAffiliateDirectoryCardProps {
  af: any;

  isTogglingThis: boolean;

                    {isSpec ? tx('ESPECIALISTA', 'SPECIALIST') : 'CLUB'} • {String(tDisc(af.disciplina || 'Fuerza')).toUpperCase()}

                </View>

              <Text style={styles.affiliateName} numberOfLines={1}>
                {af.nombre}
              </Text>

              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.locationLinkRow} activeOpacity={0.75} onPress={() => onOpenMaps(af)}>

                  {isValidB2BWhatsapp(af.whatsapp)
                    ? `WA: ${af.whatsapp} • `

                      borderWidth: 1,

                      ⏳ {tx('Sin agenda +14d', 'No schedule +14d')}

              <Text style={styles.statLabel} numberOfLines={1}>Check-ins</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.statCell, { minWidth: 0 }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {af.ingresosGenerados ?? 0} KC
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.statLabel} numberOfLines={1}>{tx('Generado', 'Generated')}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.statCell, { minWidth: 0 }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.green }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {af.enCustodia ?? 0} KC
              </Text>

                {isTogglingThis

                  : isActive

            <View style={[styles.switchActionChip, isActive ? styles.switchActionChipOff : styles.switchActionChipOn]}>
              <Text style={[styles.switchActionChipText, { color: isActive ? themeColors.textPrimary : '#050505' }]}>
