    // === src/components/admin/AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Keyboard, Linking, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { cleanLocationDisplay } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { normalizeAffiliateKey } from '../../services/adminB2BCutEngine';
import {
  adminService,
  extractInstagramFromAffiliate,
  isValidB2BWhatsapp,
} from '../../services/adminService';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated, extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../AdminOperationsViews.styles';
import type { BovedaAlertPayload } from '../boveda/BovedaTacticalAlertModal';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from '../BovedaPassAndReceiptModals';
import { ClubDetailsModal } from '../ClubDetailsModal';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { AdminAffiliateDirectoryCard } from './AdminAffiliateDirectoryCard';
import { AffiliateQuickActivateModal } from './AdminAffiliateSubModals';

type AffRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
  id?: string;
  nombre?: string | null;
  ubicacion?: string | null;
  disciplina?: string | null;
  categoria?: string | null;
  whatsapp?: string | null;
  instagram?: string | null;
  estado?: string | null;
  esClubActivo?: boolean;
  verificado?: boolean;
  es_especialista?: boolean;
  ingresosGenerados?: number;
  strikesOperativos?: number;
  strikes_operativos?: number;
  sinAgenda14d?: boolean;
  latitud?: number | string | null;
  latitude?: number | string | null;
  longitud?: number | string | null;
  longitude?: number | string | null;
};

const openMapsForAffiliate = (af: AffRow) => {
  triggerHaptic('Light');
  const parsed = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(af?.ubicacion as string | undefined);
  if (parsed.url) { Linking.openURL(parsed.url).catch((e) => { void e; }); return; }
  const query = encodeURIComponent(`${String(af?.nombre || '').trim()} ${cleanLocationDisplay(af?.ubicacion as string | undefined, '')}`.trim() || 'Kinetic');
  const lat = Number(af?.latitud ?? af?.latitude ?? parsed.lat);
  const lon = Number(af?.longitud ?? af?.longitude ?? parsed.lng);
  const url = Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) && Math.abs(lat) > 1
    ? Platform.select({ ios: `maps:0,0?q=${query}@${lat},${lon}`, android: `geo:${lat},${lon}?q=${query}`, default: `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${query}` })
    : `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${query}`;
  if (url) Linking.openURL(url).catch((e) => { void e; });
};

export const AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView = memo(
  ({
    sociosReales = [], cambiarEstadoAfiliado, crearAfiliadoManual, eliminarSocioB2B,
    canAdminDelete = true, isAdmin = true, togglingAffiliateName = null,
    isModuleActive = true, onMountSubOverlay, onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler, onRequestAlert,
  }: {
    sociosReales: AffRow[];
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado?: (afiliado: AffRow, nuevoEstado?: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'prospecto', cb?: () => void) => Promise<unknown>;
    crearAfiliadoManual?: (payload: Parameters<typeof adminService.crearNuevoAfiliado>[0], cb?: () => void) => Promise<unknown>;
    eliminarSocioB2B?: (afiliado: Record<string, unknown> & { id?: string; nombre?: string }, cb?: () => void) => Promise<unknown>;
    canAdminDelete?: boolean;
    isAdmin?: boolean;
    togglingAffiliateName?: string | null;
    isSavingManualAffiliate?: boolean;
    isModuleActive?: boolean;
    onMountSubOverlay?: (node: React.ReactNode) => void;
    onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler?: (handler: (() => boolean) | null) => void;
    onRequestAlert?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<BovedaAlertPayload>>;
  }) => {
    void crearAfiliadoManual;
    const isAdminOperator = Boolean(isAdmin ?? canAdminDelete);
    const { resolvedTheme, tx, tDisc } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({ search: '', status: 'todos', sort: 'active_first' });
    const [overrides, setOverrides] = useState<{
      stateMap: Record<string, 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'prospecto'>;
      contactMap: Record<string, { whatsapp?: string; instagram?: string }>;
      added: AffRow[];
      deleted: string[];
      busyKey: string | null;
    }>({ stateMap: {}, contactMap: {}, added: [], deleted: [], busyKey: null });
    const [modals, setModals] = useState<{
      creating: boolean;
      openEdit: boolean;
      quickTarget: AffRow | null;
      quickBusy: boolean;
      selected: AffRow | null;
    }>({ creating: false, openEdit: false, quickTarget: null, quickBusy: false, selected: null });
    const [localAlertConfig, setLocalAlertConfig] = useState<BovedaAlertPayload>({ visible: false });
    const rankSnapshotRef = React.useRef<Record<string, number>>({});
    const lastSortKeyRef = React.useRef('');
    const prevSociosRef = React.useRef(sociosReales);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (prevSociosRef.current === sociosReales || overrides.busyKey || modals.quickBusy) return;
      prevSociosRef.current = sociosReales;
      setOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, stateMap: {}, contactMap: {} }));
    }, [sociosReales, overrides.busyKey, modals.quickBusy]);

    const dispatchAlert = onRequestAlert || setLocalAlertConfig;
    const toggleBusySetRef = React.useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());

    const showLocalTacticalAlert = useCallback(
      (title: string, message: string, type: 'warning' | 'error' | 'success' | 'alert' = 'warning') =>
        dispatchAlert({ visible: true, title, message, type, confirmText: tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT') }),
      [dispatchAlert, tx]
    );

    const setAffiliateStateOverride = useCallback((key: string, rawK: string, nextSt: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'prospecto') => {
      setOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, stateMap: { ...prev.stateMap, [key]: nextSt, [rawK]: nextSt } }));
    }, []);

    const executeConfirmedDelete = useCallback(async (af: AffRow) => {
      if (!canAdminDelete || !af) return;
      const nombre = String(af?.nombre || '').trim();
      const keyLower = nombre.toLowerCase();
      setOverrides((prev) => {
        const nextMap = { ...prev.stateMap };
        delete nextMap[keyLower];
        return {
          ...prev,
          deleted: [...prev.deleted, keyLower, String(af?.id || '')],
          added: prev.added.filter((item) => item.id !== af.id && String(item.nombre || '').toLowerCase() !== keyLower),
          stateMap: nextMap,
        };
      });
      if (eliminarSocioB2B) {
        await eliminarSocioB2B({ ...af, id: af.id || undefined, nombre: af.nombre || undefined });
      } else {
        await adminService.eliminarAfiliado(String(af.id || ''), nombre);
        showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('AFILIADO ELIMINADO', 'AFFILIATE DELETED'), tx(`"${nombre}" fue eliminado correctamente.`, `"${nombre}" was deleted.`), 'success');
      }
    }, [canAdminDelete, eliminarSocioB2B, tx, showLocalTacticalAlert]);

    const handleDeleteAffiliatePrompt = useCallback((af: AffRow) => {
      if (!canAdminDelete) return;
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      const nombre = String(af?.nombre || '').trim();
      dispatchAlert({
        visible: true, title: tx('PASO 1/2 • ¿ELIMINAR AFILIADO?', 'STEP 1/2 • DELETE AFFILIATE?'),
        message: tx(`Vas a iniciar la baja permanente de "${nombre}". Confirma para pasar a la verificación final de Administrador.`, `You are initiating permanent removal of "${nombre}". Confirm to proceed to final Admin verification.`),
        type: 'warning', confirmText: tx('CONTINUAR (1/2)', 'CONTINUE (1/2)'), cancelText: tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel'),
        onConfirm: () => {
          setTimeout(() => {
            dispatchAlert({
              visible: true, title: tx('CONFIRMACIÓN DEFINITIVA (2/2)', 'FINAL CONFIRMATION (2/2)'),
              message: tx(`¿Confirmas eliminar definitivamente a "${nombre}" de public.afiliados y del Radar GPS?`, `Confirm permanently deleting "${nombre}" from public.afiliados and GPS Radar?`),
              type: 'error', confirmText: tx('ELIMINAR DEFINITIVO', 'DELETE PERMANENTLY'), cancelText: tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel'),
              onConfirm: async () => {
                triggerHaptic('Heavy');
                try { await executeConfirmedDelete(af); } catch (err: unknown) { showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('ERROR AL ELIMINAR', 'DELETION ERROR'), err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Error', 'error'); }
              },
            });
          }, 120);
        },
      });
    }, [canAdminDelete, tx, executeConfirmedDelete, showLocalTacticalAlert]);

    const mergedSocios = useMemo(() => {
      const byName = new Map<string, AffRow>();
      overrides.added.forEach((item) => {
        const k = normalizeAffiliateKey(item?.nombre as string) || String(item?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (k && !overrides.deleted.includes(k) && !overrides.deleted.includes(String(item?.id || ''))) byName.set(k, item);
      });
      (sociosReales || []).forEach((s) => {
        const k = normalizeAffiliateKey(s?.nombre as string) || String(s?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!k || overrides.deleted.includes(k) || overrides.deleted.includes(String(s?.id || ''))) return;
        const rawK = String(s?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const ov = overrides.stateMap[k] ?? overrides.stateMap[rawK];
        const cOv = overrides.contactMap[k] ?? overrides.contactMap[rawK];
        const isCurrentlyActive = ov !== undefined ? ov === 'activo' : Boolean(s?.esClubActivo ?? (String(s?.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'activo' || s?.verificado));
        byName.set(k, {
          ...s,
          ...(cOv?.whatsapp ? { whatsapp: cOv.whatsapp } : {}),
          ...(cOv?.instagram ? { instagram: cOv.instagram } : {}),
          esClubActivo: isCurrentlyActive, verificado: isCurrentlyActive, estado: isCurrentlyActive ? 'activo' : 'inactivo',
        });
      });
      overrides.added.forEach((item) => {
        const k = normalizeAffiliateKey(item?.nombre as string) || String(item?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (k && !byName.has(k)) byName.set(k, item);
      });
      return Array.from(byName.values());
    }, [sociosReales, overrides]);

    const counts = useMemo(() => {
      let activos = 0, prospectos = 0, clubes = 0, especialistas = 0, alertas = 0;
      mergedSocios.forEach((s) => {
        if (s.esClubActivo) activos += 1; else prospectos += 1;
        if (s.es_especialista) especialistas += 1; else clubes += 1;
        if ((Number(s.strikesOperativos ?? s.strikes_operativos) || 0) > 0 || Boolean(s.sinAgenda14d)) alertas += 1;
      });
      return { total: mergedSocios.length, activos, prospectos, clubes, especialistas, alertas };
    }, [mergedSocios]);

    const filteredSocios = useMemo(() => {
      const q = filters.search.trim().toLowerCase();
      const base = mergedSocios.filter((s) => {
        if (filters.status === 'activos' && !s.esClubActivo) return false;
        if (filters.status === 'prospectos' && s.esClubActivo) return false;
        if (filters.status === 'clubes' && s.es_especialista) return false;
        if (filters.status === 'especialistas' && !s.es_especialista) return false;
        if (filters.status === 'alertas' && (Number(s.strikesOperativos ?? s.strikes_operativos) || 0) === 0 && !s.sinAgenda14d) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        const ig = extractInstagramFromAffiliate(s);
        return `${s.nombre || ''} ${s.disciplina || ''} ${tDisc(String(s.disciplina || ''))} ${s.categoria || ''} ${s.ubicacion || ''} ${ig}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      });
      const sortKey = `${filters.sort}_${filters.status}_${q}`;
      if (lastSortKeyRef.current !== sortKey) {
        lastSortKeyRef.current = sortKey;
        const sorted = [...base].sort((a, b) => {
          if (filters.sort === 'revenue_desc') return (Number(b.ingresosGenerados) || 0) - (Number(a.ingresosGenerados) || 0);
          if (filters.sort === 'name_az') return String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''));
          return Number(b.esClubActivo) - Number(a.esClubActivo) || (Number(b.ingresosGenerados) || 0) - (Number(a.ingresosGenerados) || 0) || String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''));
        });
        const snap: Record<string, number> = {};
        sorted.forEach((item, idx) => { snap[normalizeAffiliateKey(item.nombre as string) || String(item.id || item.nombre)] = idx; });
        rankSnapshotRef.current = snap;
        return sorted;
      }
      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        const kA = normalizeAffiliateKey(a.nombre as string) || String(a.id || a.nombre);
        const kB = normalizeAffiliateKey(b.nombre as string) || String(b.id || b.nombre);
        return (rankSnapshotRef.current[kA] ?? 9999) - (rankSnapshotRef.current[kB] ?? 9999);
      });
    }, [mergedSocios, filters, tDisc]);

    const handleToggleAffiliate = useCallback(async (af: AffRow) => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      const nombre = String(af?.nombre || '').trim();
      const rawKey = nombre.toLowerCase();
      const key = normalizeAffiliateKey(nombre) || rawKey;
      if (!key || toggleBusySetRef.current.has(key)) return;
      const nextEstado: 'activo' | 'inactivo' = af.esClubActivo ? 'inactivo' : 'activo';
      if (nextEstado === 'activo') {
        const hasWa = String(af?.whatsapp || '').trim().length > 0;
        const waValid = isValidB2BWhatsapp(af?.whatsapp as string | null | undefined);
        const igCore = extractInstagramFromAffiliate(af).replace(/^@+/, '').trim();
        if (isAdminOperator ? (hasWa && !waValid) : (!waValid || igCore.length < 2)) {
          triggerHaptic('Warning');
          setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, quickTarget: af }));
          return;
        }
      }
      toggleBusySetRef.current.add(key);
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      setOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, busyKey: rawKey, stateMap: { ...prev.stateMap, [key]: nextEstado, [rawKey]: nextEstado } }));
      try {
        if (cambiarEstadoAfiliado) {
          const ok = await cambiarEstadoAfiliado(af, nextEstado);
          if (ok === false) setAffiliateStateOverride(key, rawKey, af.esClubActivo ? 'activo' : 'inactivo');
        } else {
          await adminService.toggleEstadoAfiliado(af, nextEstado === 'activo' ? 'activo' : 'prospecto');
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        setAffiliateStateOverride(key, rawKey, af.esClubActivo ? 'activo' : 'inactivo');
        showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('ERROR AL ACTUALIZAR ESTADO', 'STATUS UPDATE ERROR'), err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('No se pudo persistir el estado.', 'Could not persist status.'), 'error');
      } finally {
        toggleBusySetRef.current.delete(key);
        setOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, busyKey: null }));
      }
    }, [cambiarEstadoAfiliado, isAdminOperator, setAffiliateStateOverride, showLocalTacticalAlert, tx]);

    const handleConfirmQuickActivate = useCallback(async (whatsapp: string, instagram: string) => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      const target = modals.quickTarget;
      if (!target) return;
      const nombre = String(target.nombre || '').trim();
      const rawKey = nombre.toLowerCase();
      const key = normalizeAffiliateKey(nombre) || rawKey;
      setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, quickBusy: true }));
      setOverrides((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        busyKey: rawKey,
        contactMap: { ...prev.contactMap, [key]: { whatsapp, instagram }, [rawKey]: { whatsapp, instagram } },
        stateMap: { ...prev.stateMap, [key]: 'activo', [rawKey]: 'activo' },
      }));
      try {
        const enriched = { ...target, whatsapp, instagram };
        if (cambiarEstadoAfiliado) {
          const ok = await cambiarEstadoAfiliado(enriched, 'activo');
          if (ok === false) { setAffiliateStateOverride(key, rawKey, 'inactivo'); return; }
        } else {
          await adminService.toggleEstadoAfiliado(enriched, 'activo');
          showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('ACTIVO EN RADAR GPS', 'ACTIVE ON GPS RADAR'), tx(`${nombre} ya aparece activo en el Radar GPS.`, `${nombre} is now live on the GPS Radar.`), 'success');
        }
        setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, quickTarget: null }));
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        setAffiliateStateOverride(key, rawKey, 'inactivo');
        showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('ERROR AL ACTIVAR', 'ACTIVATION ERROR'), err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('No se pudo activar el operador.', 'Could not activate operator.'), 'error');
      } finally {
        setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, quickBusy: false }));
        setOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, busyKey: null }));
      }
    }, [cambiarEstadoAfiliado, modals.quickTarget, setAffiliateStateOverride, showLocalTacticalAlert, tx]);

    const handlePromptStrike = useCallback((af: AffRow) => {
      const curStrikes = Number(af.strikesOperativos ?? af.strikes_operativos) || 0;
      const nombre = String(af.nombre || 'Operador');
      const canReset = curStrikes > 0 && curStrikes < 3;
      const rawK = nombre.trim().toLowerCase();
      const k = normalizeAffiliateKey(nombre) || rawK;
      dispatchAlert({
        visible: true, title: tx('GESTIÓN DE STRIKES B2B', 'B2B STRIKES MANAGEMENT'),
        message: tx(`"${nombre}" (${curStrikes}/3 Strikes).\n\nAl acumular 3 Strikes se suspende automáticamente del Radar GPS.`, `"${nombre}" (${curStrikes}/3 Strikes).\n\nAccumulating 3 Strikes automatically suspends the operator from GPS Radar.`),
        type: curStrikes >= 2 ? 'error' : 'warning',
        confirmText: curStrikes < 3 ? tx('+1 STRIKE CALIDAD', '+1 QUALITY STRIKE') : tx('LIMPIAR A 0 STRIKES', 'RESET TO 0 STRIKES'),
        cancelText: canReset ? tx('LIMPIAR A 0', 'RESET TO 0') : undefined,
        onConfirm: async () => {
          triggerHaptic('Warning');
          const next = curStrikes >= 3 ? 0 : curStrikes + 1;
          try {
            await adminService.actualizarStrikesAfiliado(af, next);
            setAffiliateStateOverride(k, rawK, next >= 3 ? 'inactivo' : (af.esClubActivo ? 'activo' : 'inactivo'));
            showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('STRIKES ACTUALIZADOS', 'STRIKES UPDATED'), tx(`"${nombre}" tiene ${next}/3 strikes.`, `"${nombre}" has ${next}/3 strikes.`), 'alert');
          } catch (err: unknown) {
            showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('ERROR AL ACTUALIZAR STRIKES', 'STRIKES UPDATE ERROR'), err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('No se pudieron actualizar.', 'Could not update strikes.'), 'error');
          }
        },
        onCancel: canReset ? async () => {
          triggerHaptic('Medium');
          try {
            await adminService.actualizarStrikesAfiliado(af, 0);
            setAffiliateStateOverride(k, rawK, af.esClubActivo ? 'activo' : 'inactivo');
            showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('STRIKES LIMPIOS', 'STRIKES CLEARED'), tx(`"${nombre}" cuenta con 0/3 strikes.`, `"${nombre}" has 0/3 strikes.`), 'success');
          } catch (err: unknown) {
            showLocalTacticalAlert(tx('ERROR AL LIMPIAR STRIKES', 'STRIKES RESET ERROR'), err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('No se pudieron limpiar.', 'Could not reset strikes.'), 'error');
          }
        } : undefined,
      });
    }, [dispatchAlert, tx, showLocalTacticalAlert, setAffiliateStateOverride]);

    const handleSelectAffiliateForModal = useCallback((af: AffRow, editMode: boolean) => {
      setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, selected: af, openEdit: editMode }));
    }, []);

    const listHeader = useMemo(() => (
      <View>
        <Text style={[styles.sectionTag, { marginBottom: 4 }]} numberOfLines={1}>{tx('DIRECTORIO MAESTRO B2B', 'B2B MASTER DIRECTORY')}</Text>
        <View style={styles.headerBanner}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>{tx('Clubes y Especialistas', 'Clubs & Specialists')} ({counts.total})</Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionSub}>{tx(`${counts.activos} Activos en Radar • ${counts.prospectos} Prospectos Ocultos`, `${counts.activos} Active on Radar • ${counts.prospectos} Hidden Prospects`)}</Text>
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.addAffiliateTopBtn} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, creating: true })); }}>
            <Feather name="plus-circle" size={14} color="#050505" />
            <Text style={styles.addAffiliateTopBtnText}>{tx('+ NUEVO AFILIADO', '+ NEW AFFILIATE')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        <KineticListFilterSortBar
          searchQuery={filters.search}
          onChangeSearch={(search) => setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, search }))}
          searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar por nombre, disciplina o ubicación...', 'Search by name, discipline, or location...')}
          filterChips={[
            { key: 'todos', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: counts.total },
            { key: 'activos', label: tx('Activos', 'Active'), count: counts.activos },
            { key: 'alertas', label: tx('Alertas Calidad', 'Quality Alerts'), count: counts.alertas },
            { key: 'prospectos', label: tx('Prospectos', 'Prospects'), count: counts.prospectos },
            { key: 'clubes', label: tx('Clubes', 'Clubs'), count: counts.clubes },
            { key: 'especialistas', label: tx('Especialistas', 'Specialists'), count: counts.especialistas },
          ]}
          activeFilter={filters.status}
          onSelectFilter={(status) => setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, status }))}
          sortOptions={[
            { key: 'active_first', label: tx('Activos ↑', 'Active ↑') },
            { key: 'revenue_desc', label: tx('Mayor KC', 'Top KC') },
            { key: 'name_az', label: 'A-Z' },
          ]}
          activeSort={filters.sort}
          onSelectSort={(sort) => setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, sort }))}
        />
      </View>
    ), [counts, filters, styles, tx]);

    const renderAffiliateItem = useCallback(({ item: af }: { item: AffRow }) => {
      const keyLower = String(af?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const isTogglingThis = overrides.busyKey === keyLower || String(togglingAffiliateName || '').trim().toLowerCase() === keyLower;
      return (
        <AdminAffiliateDirectoryCard
          af={af} styles={styles} isTogglingThis={isTogglingThis} isAdminOperator={isAdminOperator} canAdminDelete={Boolean(canAdminDelete)}
          onSelectAffiliate={handleSelectAffiliateForModal} onOpenMaps={openMapsForAffiliate} onPromptStrike={handlePromptStrike}
          onToggleAffiliate={handleToggleAffiliate} onDeleteAffiliate={handleDeleteAffiliatePrompt}
        />
      );
    }, [overrides.busyKey, canAdminDelete, handleDeleteAffiliatePrompt, handlePromptStrike, handleSelectAffiliateForModal, handleToggleAffiliate, isAdminOperator, styles, togglingAffiliateName]);

    const hasAnySubOverlay = Boolean(modals.selected || modals.creating || modals.quickTarget || (!onRequestAlert && localAlertConfig?.visible));
    const subOverlaysNode = useMemo(() => hasAnySubOverlay ? (
      <>
        <ClubDetailsModal inlineOverlay portalOnWeb={false} visible={Boolean(modals.selected)} affiliate={modals.selected} isAdmin openInEditMode={modals.openEdit} onClose={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, selected: null, openEdit: false })); }} onRefreshRadar={() => emitKineticRadarUpdated()} />
        <ClubDetailsModal inlineOverlay portalOnWeb={false} visible={modals.creating} isCreateMode isAdmin onClose={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, creating: false })); }} onCreatedAffiliate={(created: AffRow) => { if (created) setOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, added: [created, ...prev.added] })); emitKineticRadarUpdated(); }} onRefreshRadar={() => emitKineticRadarUpdated()} />
        <AffiliateQuickActivateModal inlineOverlay visible={Boolean(modals.quickTarget)} affiliate={modals.quickTarget} isAdmin={isAdminOperator} isSubmitting={modals.quickBusy} onConfirmActivate={handleConfirmQuickActivate} onClose={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, quickTarget: null })); }} />
      </>
    ) : null, [hasAnySubOverlay, modals, isAdminOperator, handleConfirmQuickActivate]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!isModuleActive) {
        setModals({ creating: false, openEdit: false, quickTarget: null, quickBusy: false, selected: null });
        setLocalAlertConfig({ visible: false });
      }
    }, [isModuleActive]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler) return;
      onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler(() => {
        if (localAlertConfig?.visible) { setLocalAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false })); return true; }
        if (modals.quickTarget) { Keyboard.dismiss(); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, quickTarget: null })); return true; }
        if (modals.creating) { Keyboard.dismiss(); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, creating: false })); return true; }
        if (modals.selected) { Keyboard.dismiss(); setModals((prev) => ({ ...prev, selected: null, openEdit: false })); return true; }
        return false;
      });
      return () => onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler(null);
    }, [onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler, localAlertConfig?.visible, modals.quickTarget, modals.creating, modals.selected]);

    useEffect(() => { if (onMountSubOverlay) onMountSubOverlay(isModuleActive ? subOverlaysNode : null); }, [onMountSubOverlay, isModuleActive, subOverlaysNode]);
    useEffect(() => () => { onMountSubOverlay?.(null); }, [onMountSubOverlay]);

    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <FlatList
          data={filteredSocios} keyExtractor={(item, idx) => String(item?.id || item?.nombre || idx)} renderItem={renderAffiliateItem}
          ListHeaderComponent={listHeader} contentContainerStyle={[styles.container, { paddingBottom: 24 }]}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" initialNumToRender={8} maxToRenderPerBatch={8} windowSize={5} removeClippedSubviews={false}
        />
        {!onMountSubOverlay && subOverlaysNode}
        {!onRequestAlert && <TacticalAlertModal inlineOverlay alertConfig={localAlertConfig} setAlertConfig={setLocalAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAffiliateSubModals.styles.ts

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAffiliateSubModals.styles.ts === //
import { StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';

export const baseSubStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  sheetSurface: { backgroundColor: '#090D16', borderTopWidth: 2, borderColor: '#00B8D4', paddingHorizontal: 18, paddingBottom: 24 },
  sheetTag: { color: COLORS.cyan, fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1.3, marginBottom: 2 },
  sheetTitle: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '900', marginBottom: 12 },
  discRow3D: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', backgroundColor: '#0E131F', borderRadius: 12, paddingHorizontal: 13, paddingVertical: 11, marginBottom: 8, borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)', borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#03060B' },
  medallionBase: { width: 26, height: 26, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1.2, borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 3.4, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 8 },
  medallionCyanDark: { backgroundColor: '#06222E', borderColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.45)', borderTopColor: '#99FFFF', borderBottomColor: '#006B78' },
  medallionCyanLight: { backgroundColor: '#E0F2FE', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
  medallionPurpleDark: { backgroundColor: '#220B38', borderColor: 'rgba(199, 125, 255, 0.48)', borderTopColor: '#E0AAFF', borderBottomColor: '#5A189A' },
  medallionPurpleLight: { backgroundColor: '#F3E8FF', borderColor: '#C4B5FD', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED' },
  discRowSelected: { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)', borderColor: '#00B8D4', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' },
  discRowTitle: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: '800' },
  discDivisionTag: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.8, marginTop: 2 },
  customDaysCard: { backgroundColor: '#060A12', borderRadius: 14, padding: 13, marginTop: 6, borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#00B8D4', borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' },
  subLabel: { color: COLORS.textMuted, fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1, marginBottom: 6, marginTop: 6 },
  weekdayWrap: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 },
  dayChip3D: { paddingHorizontal: 11, paddingVertical: 8, borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: '#0E131F', borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)', borderBottomWidth: 3.5, borderBottomColor: '#03060B' },
  dayChipActive: { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan, borderColor: '#5CEEFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' },
  dayChipText: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900' },
  hoursColsWrap: { flex: 1, flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 },
  hourColBox: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#060A12', borderRadius: 14, padding: 10, borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)', borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' },
  slotItem3D: { paddingVertical: 9, paddingHorizontal: 10, borderRadius: 9, backgroundColor: '#0E131F', marginBottom: 6, alignItems: 'center', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)', borderBottomWidth: 3.5, borderBottomColor: '#03060B' },
  slotItemCyan: { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan, borderColor: '#5CEEFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4' },
  slotItemPurple: { backgroundColor: COLORS.purple, borderColor: '#C77DFF', borderBottomColor: '#6D28D9' },
  slotText: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: '900' },

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAffiliateSubModals.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAffiliateSubModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { SORTED_CATEGORIES, isWellnessCategory } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { KINETIC_OPERATING_DAYS_OPTIONS, KINETIC_OPERATING_HOURS_30M } from '../../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setDisciplinePickerOpen } from '../../utils/kineticOverlayStack';
import { translateOperatingHours } from '../../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../utils/kineticHapticsEngine';
import { baseSubStyles } from './AdminAffiliateSubModals.styles';

export { baseSubStyles };

export const CUSTOM_WEEKDAYS: { es: string; en: string; shortEs: string; shortEn: string }[] = [
  { es: 'Lunes', en: 'Monday', shortEs: 'Lun', shortEn: 'Mon' },
  { es: 'Martes', en: 'Tuesday', shortEs: 'Mar', shortEn: 'Tue' },
  { es: 'Miércoles', en: 'Wednesday', shortEs: 'Mié', shortEn: 'Wed' },
  { es: 'Jueves', en: 'Thursday', shortEs: 'Jue', shortEn: 'Thu' },
  { es: 'Viernes', en: 'Friday', shortEs: 'Vie', shortEn: 'Fri' },
  { es: 'Sábado', en: 'Saturday', shortEs: 'Sáb', shortEn: 'Sat' },
  { es: 'Domingo', en: 'Sunday', shortEs: 'Dom', shortEn: 'Sun' },
];

export const resolveCanonicalDaysEs = (daysKey: string, customDays: string[]): string => {
  if (daysKey === 'custom') return customDays.length > 0 ? customDays.join(', ') : 'Lunes a Viernes';
  return KINETIC_OPERATING_DAYS_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.key === daysKey)?.es || 'Lunes a Sábado';
};

export const resolveShortDaysDisplay = (daysKey: string, customDays: string[], isEn: boolean): string => {
  if (daysKey === 'custom') {
    const joined = customDays.length > 0 ? customDays.join(', ') : 'Lunes a Viernes';
    return isEn ? translateOperatingHours(joined, 'en') : joined;
  }
  const found = KINETIC_OPERATING_DAYS_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.key === daysKey);
  if (!found) return isEn ? 'Mon–Sat' : 'Lunes a Sábado';
  return isEn ? found.shortEn || found.en : found.es;
};

export const AffiliateDisciplinePickerModal = memo(({
  visible, selectedDiscipline, onSelectDiscipline, onClose,
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  selectedDiscipline: string;
  onSelectDiscipline: (disc: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, tDisc } = useKineticPreferences();
  const s = useMemo(
    () =>
      adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSubStyles, resolvedTheme, [
        'medallionCyanDark',
        'medallionCyanLight',
        'medallionPurpleDark',
        'medallionPurpleLight',
      ]),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [divFilter, setDivFilter] = useState<'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness'>('all');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState<'az' | 'za'>('az');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setDisciplinePickerOpen(true);
    return () => setDisciplinePickerOpen(false);
  }, [visible]);

  const { fitnessCount, wellnessCount } = useMemo(() => {
    let fit = 0;
    let well = 0;
    SORTED_CATEGORIES.forEach((c) => { if (isWellnessCategory(c)) well += 1; else fit += 1; });
    return { fitnessCount: fit, wellnessCount: well };
  }, []);

  const filteredDisciplines = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    const base = SORTED_CATEGORIES.filter((disc) => {
      const isWell = isWellnessCategory(disc);
      if (divFilter === 'fitness' && isWell) return false;
      if (divFilter === 'wellness' && !isWell) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return disc.toLowerCase().includes(q) || tDisc(disc).toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
    return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
      const cmp = tDisc(a).localeCompare(tDisc(b));
      return sortOrder === 'za' ? -cmp : cmp;
    });
  }, [searchQuery, divFilter, sortOrder, tDisc]);

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={visible} onBackdropPress={onClose} onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end', zIndex: 9999, elevation: 20 }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      avoidKeyboard
    >
      <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, s.sheetSurface, { zIndex: 9999, elevation: 20, opacity: 1, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0A0D14' }]}>
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />
        <Text style={s.sheetTag}>
          {tx(`CATÁLOGO OFICIAL • ${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} DISCIPLINAS`, `OFFICIAL CATALOG • ${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} DISCIPLINES`)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={s.sheetTitle}>{tx('Selecciona Disciplina Principal', 'Select Primary Discipline')}</Text>

        <KineticListFilterSortBar
          searchQuery={searchQuery}
          onChangeSearch={setSearchQuery}
          searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar disciplina...', 'Search discipline...')}
          filterChips={[
            { key: 'all', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), count: SORTED_CATEGORIES.length },
            { key: 'fitness', label: tx('Fitness & Deporte', 'Fitness & Sports'), count: fitnessCount },
            { key: 'wellness', label: tx('Bienestar & Spa', 'Wellness & Spa'), count: wellnessCount },
          ]}
          activeFilter={divFilter}
          onSelectFilter={(k) => setDivFilter(k as 'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness')}
          sortOptions={[{ key: 'az', label: 'A-Z' }, { key: 'za', label: 'Z-A' }]}
          activeSort={sortOrder}
          onSelectSort={(k) => setSortOrder(k as 'az' | 'za')}
        />

        <FlatList
          data={filteredDisciplines}
          keyExtractor={(item) => item}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          initialNumToRender={24}
          maxToRenderPerBatch={32} windowSize={7} removeClippedSubviews={false} style={{ flex: 1 }}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 24 }}
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const selected = item === selectedDiscipline;
            const isWell = isWellnessCategory(item);
            const low = item.toLowerCase();
            const iconName: 'heart' | 'sun' | 'zap' | 'activity' = isWell
              ? low.includes('fisio') || low.includes('masaje') || low.includes('rehab') ? 'heart' : 'sun'
              : low.includes('cross') || low.includes('hiit') || low.includes('box') ? 'zap' : 'activity';
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                style={[s.discRow3D, selected && s.discRowSelected]}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onSelectDiscipline(item); onClose(); }}
              >
                <View style={[s.medallionBase, isWell ? (isLightMode ? s.medallionPurpleLight : s.medallionPurpleDark) : (isLightMode ? s.medallionCyanLight : s.medallionCyanDark)]}>
                  <Feather name={iconName} size={13} color={isWell ? (isLightMode ? '#7C3AED' : '#E0AAFF') : (isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan)} />
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: 10 }}>
                  <Text style={[s.discRowTitle, selected && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tDisc(item)}</Text>
                  <Text style={[s.discDivisionTag, { color: isWell ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                    {isWell ? tx('BIENESTAR & SPA', 'WELLNESS & SPA') : tx('FITNESS & DEPORTE', 'FITNESS & SPORTS')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <Feather name={selected ? 'check-circle' : 'chevron-right'} size={16} color={selected ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          }}
        />
      </View>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

export const AffiliateOperatingHoursModal = memo(({
  mode, selectedDaysKey, customDaysEs, openHour, closeHour, onApplyDays, onApplyHours, onClose,
}: {
  mode: 'days' | 'hours' | null;
  selectedDaysKey: string;
  customDaysEs: string[];
  openHour: string;
  closeHour: string;
  onApplyDays: (daysKey: string, customDays: string[]) => void;
  onApplyHours: (openH: string, closeH: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, language, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const s = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSubStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const [draftDaysKey, setDraftDaysKey] = useState(selectedDaysKey);
  const [draftCustomDays, setDraftCustomDays] = useState<string[]>(customDaysEs);
  const [draftOpen, setDraftOpen] = useState(openHour);
  const [draftClose, setDraftClose] = useState(closeHour);
  const lastModeRef = useRef<'days' | 'hours'>('days');
  if (mode) lastModeRef.current = mode;
  const displayMode = mode || lastModeRef.current || 'days';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode) {
      setDraftDaysKey(selectedDaysKey);
      setDraftCustomDays(customDaysEs.length > 0 ? customDaysEs : ['Lunes', 'Miércoles', 'Viernes']);
      setDraftOpen(openHour);
      setDraftClose(closeHour > openHour ? closeHour : '21:00');
    }
  }, [mode, selectedDaysKey, customDaysEs, openHour, closeHour]);

  const openingSlots = useMemo(() => KINETIC_OPERATING_HOURS_30M.slice(0, -1), []);
  const closingSlots = useMemo(() => KINETIC_OPERATING_HOURS_30M.filter((slot) => slot > draftOpen), [draftOpen]);

  const handleSelectOpenSlot = (slot: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setDraftOpen(slot);
    if (draftClose <= slot) {
      const idx = KINETIC_OPERATING_HOURS_30M.indexOf(slot);
      setDraftClose(KINETIC_OPERATING_HOURS_30M[idx + 1] || '22:00');
    }
  };

  const toggleCustomDay = (dayEs: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setDraftCustomDays((prev) => {
      if (prev.includes(dayEs)) return prev.length > 1 ? prev.filter((d) => d !== dayEs) : prev;
      return CUSTOM_WEEKDAYS.map((d) => d.es).filter((d) => prev.includes(d) || d === dayEs);
    });
  };

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(mode)} onBackdropPress={onClose} onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, s.sheetSurface]}>
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />
        <Text style={s.sheetTag}>
          {displayMode === 'days'
            ? tx('DÍAS DE OPERACIÓN B2B', 'B2B OPERATING DAYS')
            : tx('RANGO DE HORARIO • INTERVALOS 30 MIN', 'HOURS RANGE • 30-MIN INTERVALS')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={s.sheetTitle}>
          {displayMode === 'days' ? tx('Selecciona Días de Atención', 'Select Operating Days') : tx('Horario de Apertura y Cierre', 'Opening & Closing Hours')}
        </Text>

        {displayMode === 'days' ? (
          <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 24 }}>
            {KINETIC_OPERATING_DAYS_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
              const active = draftDaysKey === opt.key;
              const label = isEn ? `${opt.en} (${opt.shortEn || opt.en})` : opt.es;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.key}
                  activeOpacity={0.84}
                  style={[s.discRow3D, active && s.discRowSelected]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    setDraftDaysKey(opt.key);
                    if (opt.key !== 'custom') { onApplyDays(opt.key, draftCustomDays); onClose(); }
                  }}
                >
                  <Text style={[s.discRowTitle, active && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{label}</Text>
                  <Feather name={active ? 'check-circle' : 'circle'} size={16} color={active ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}

            {draftDaysKey === 'custom' && (
              <View style={s.customDaysCard}>
                <Text style={s.subLabel}>{tx('ACTIVA LOS DÍAS DE ATENCIÓN', 'TOGGLE ACTIVE OPERATING DAYS')}</Text>
                <View style={s.weekdayWrap}>
                  {CUSTOM_WEEKDAYS.map((d) => {
                    const on = draftCustomDays.includes(d.es);
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity key={d.es} style={[s.dayChip3D, on && s.dayChipActive]} onPress={() => toggleCustomDay(d.es)}>
                        <Text style={[s.dayChipText, on && { color: '#050505' }]}>{isEn ? d.en : d.es}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={s.primaryCta3D}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Medium'); onApplyDays('custom', draftCustomDays); onClose(); }}
                >
                  <Feather name="check-circle" size={15} color="#050505" />
                  <Text style={s.primaryCtaText}>{tx('CONFIRMAR DÍAS PERSONALIZADOS', 'CONFIRM CUSTOM DAYS')}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}
          </ScrollView>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <View style={s.hoursColsWrap}>
              <View style={s.hourColBox}>
                <Text style={[s.subLabel, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('APERTURA (06:00+)', 'OPENING (06:00+)')}</Text>
                <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                  {openingSlots.map((slot) => {
                    const active = slot === draftOpen;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity key={`open_${slot}`} style={[s.slotItem3D, active && s.slotItemCyan]} onPress={() => handleSelectOpenSlot(slot)}>
                        <Text style={[s.slotText, active && { color: '#050505' }]}>{slot} hrs</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </ScrollView>
              </View>

              <View style={s.hourColBox}>
                <Text style={[s.subLabel, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>{tx('CIERRE (HASTA 22:00)', 'CLOSING (UP TO 22:00)')}</Text>
                <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                  {closingSlots.map((slot) => {
                    const active = slot === draftClose;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={`close_${slot}`}
                        style={[s.slotItem3D, active && s.slotItemPurple]}
                        onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setDraftClose(slot); }}
                      >
                        <Text style={[s.slotText, active && { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>{slot} hrs</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </ScrollView>
              </View>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[s.primaryCta3D, { marginTop: 12 }]}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Medium');
                onApplyHours(draftOpen, draftClose > draftOpen ? draftClose : '22:00');
                onClose();
              }}
            >
              <Feather name="clock" size={15} color="#050505" />
              <Text style={s.primaryCtaText}>
                {tx(`CONFIRMAR HORARIO (${draftOpen} a ${draftClose})`, `CONFIRM HOURS (${draftOpen} to ${draftClose})`)}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

export { AffiliateQuickActivateModal } from './AffiliateQuickActivateModal';

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAthletesDirectoryView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAthletesDirectoryView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { isMasterAdminAccount } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel, localizeVipTierLabel } from './AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const resolveAthleteCode = (atl: any): string =>
  String(
    atl?.codigo_referido ||
      atl?.codigo_amigo_squad ||
      `#${String(atl?.id || '').slice(0, 6).toUpperCase()}`
  ).trim();

export const AdminAthletesDirectoryView = memo(
  ({
    atletasList = [],
    walletContextFilter = 'all',
    styles,
    inyectarKCAtleta,
    isInjectingKC = false,
    eliminarCuentaAtleta,
    canAdminDelete = false,
  }: {
    atletasList: any[];
    walletContextFilter?: 'all' | 'membresia' | 'topup';
    styles: any;
    onNavigateModule?: (mod: any, filter?: any) => void;
    onOpenInTreasury?: (athleteId: string) => void;
    inyectarKCAtleta?: any;
    isInjectingKC?: boolean;
    eliminarCuentaAtleta?: (atleta: any) => void;
    canAdminDelete?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const { isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const [dirSearch, setDirSearch] = useState('');
    const [filterTab, setFilterTab] = useState<string>(
      walletContextFilter === 'membresia'
        ? 'membresia'
        : walletContextFilter === 'topup'
        ? 'topup'
        : 'todos'
    );
    const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<string>('kc_desc');
    const [selectedAthleteId, setSelectedAthleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (walletContextFilter === 'membresia') setFilterTab('membresia');
      else if (walletContextFilter === 'topup') setFilterTab('topup');
      else setFilterTab('todos');
    }, [walletContextFilter]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (selectedAthleteId && !atletasList.some((a) => String(a.id) === String(selectedAthleteId))) {
        setSelectedAthleteId(null);
      }
    }, [atletasList, selectedAthleteId]);

    const filteredDirAthletes = useMemo(() => {
      const q = dirSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
      const base = (atletasList || []).filter((a: any) => {
        if (q) {
          const code = resolveAthleteCode(a);
          const blob = `${a?.nombre || ''} ${a?.codigo_referido || ''} ${a?.codigo_amigo_squad || ''} ${code} ${a?.email || ''} ${a?.id || ''}`.toLowerCase();
          if (!blob.includes(q)) return false;
        }
        if (filterTab === 'con_saldo') return (a.saldo_total_kc || 0) > 0;
        if (filterTab === 'membresia') return (a.kc_membresia || 0) > 0;
        if (filterTab === 'topup') return (a.kc_topup || 0) > 0;
        return true;
      });
      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        if (sortBy === 'streak_desc') return (Number(b.racha_dias) || 0) - (Number(a.racha_dias) || 0);
        if (sortBy === 'name_az') return String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''));
        return (Number(b.saldo_total_kc) || 0) - (Number(a.saldo_total_kc) || 0);
      });
    }, [atletasList, dirSearch, filterTab, sortBy]);

    const dirFilterChips = useMemo(
      () => [
        { key: 'todos', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: atletasList.length },
        { key: 'con_saldo', label: tx('Con Saldo KC', 'With KC Balance') },
        { key: 'membresia', label: tx('Membresía', 'Membership') },
        ...(walletContextFilter === 'topup' || filterTab === 'topup'
          ? [{ key: 'topup', label: 'Top-Up' }]
          : []),
      ],
      [atletasList.length, walletContextFilter, filterTab, tx]
    );

    const listHeader = useMemo(
      () => (
        <View style={{ paddingTop: 4 }}>
          <Text style={[styles.moduleTitle, { lineHeight: 27, paddingTop: 2, marginBottom: 4 }]}>
            {tx('Directorio y Tesorería KC', 'Athletes & KC Treasury')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.moduleSub}>
            {tx(
              `${atletasList.length} atletas registrados. Toca un perfil para gestionar sus Kineticoins o rango VIP in-situ.`,
              `${atletasList.length} registered athletes. Tap a profile to manage Kineticoins or VIP tier inline.`
            )}
          </Text>
          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={dirSearch}
            onChangeSearch={setDirSearch}
            searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar atleta por nombre o código...', 'Search athlete by name or code...')}
            filterChips={dirFilterChips}
            activeFilter={filterTab}
            onSelectFilter={setFilterTab}
            sortOptions={[
              { key: 'kc_desc', label: tx('Mayor KC', 'Top KC') },
              { key: 'streak_desc', label: tx('Racha 🔥', 'Streak 🔥') },
              { key: 'name_az', label: 'A-Z' },
            ]}
            activeSort={sortBy}
            onSelectSort={setSortBy}
          />
        </View>
      ),
      [atletasList.length, dirFilterChips, dirSearch, filterTab, sortBy, styles, tx]
    );

    const renderAthleteItem = useCallback(
      ({ item: atl }: { item: any }) => {
        const isExpanded = String(selectedAthleteId) === String(atl.id);
        const codeStr = resolveAthleteCode(atl);
        return (
          <View style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.athleteDirCard,
                isExpanded && { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                setSelectedAthleteId(isExpanded ? null : String(atl.id));
              }}
            >
              <View style={styles.avatarCircle}>
                <Feather name="user" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: 10 }}>
                <Text style={styles.whiteBold}>{atl.nombre}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.simSub}>
                  {localizeVipTierLabel(atl.nivel, isEn).toUpperCase()} • 🔥 {atl.racha_dias || 0}D • {codeStr}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <Text style={[styles.whiteBold, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  {atl.saldo_total_kc || 0} KC
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.simSub}>
                  M: {atl.kc_membresia || 0} • T: {atl.kc_topup || 0}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {isExpanded && (
              <View style={styles.dirInfoStrip3D}>
                <AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel
                  athlete={atl}
                  inyectarKCAtleta={inyectarKCAtleta}
                  isInjectingKC={Boolean(isInjectingKC)}
                  onClosePanel={() => setSelectedAthleteId(null)}
                />

                {canAdminDelete && !isMasterAdminAccount(atl.email) && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    style={{
                      flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                      gap: 6, marginTop: 8, paddingVertical: 7, paddingHorizontal: 12,
                      borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)',
                      borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35)', elevation: 0,
                    }}
                    onPress={() => eliminarCuentaAtleta?.(atl)}
                  >
                    <Feather name="trash-2" size={12} color={COLORS.red} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', color: COLORS.red, letterSpacing: 0.8 }}>
                      {tx('ELIMINAR CUENTA DE ATLETA', 'DELETE ATHLETE ACCOUNT')}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        );
      },
      [canAdminDelete, eliminarCuentaAtleta, inyectarKCAtleta, isEn, isInjectingKC, isLightMode, selectedAthleteId, styles, themeColors.cyanText, tx]
    );

    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <FlatList
          data={filteredDirAthletes}
          keyExtractor={(item, idx) => String(item?.id || idx)}
          renderItem={renderAthleteItem}
          ListHeaderComponent={listHeader}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 4, paddingTop: 10, paddingBottom: 84 }}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          initialNumToRender={8}
          maxToRenderPerBatch={8}
          windowSize={5}
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Animated,
  Keyboard,
  Platform,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { VIP_GEM_CONFIGS, baseStyles, localizeVipTierLabel } from '../AdminTreasuryViews.styles';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export { VIP_GEM_CONFIGS, localizeVipTierLabel };

const QUICK_AMOUNTS = ['50', '100', '250', '500', '1000'] as const;
type QuickAmountPreset = (typeof QUICK_AMOUNTS)[number];

const AnimatedScaleTouchable = memo(
  ({
    onPress,
    disabled,
    wrapperStyle,
    style,
    children,
  }: {
    onPress: () => void;
    disabled?: boolean;
    wrapperStyle?: any;
    style?: any;
    children: React.ReactNode;
  }) => {
    const scaleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
    const animateTo = (val: number, bounciness: number) =>
      Animated.spring(scaleAnim, {
        toValue: val,
        speed: 30,
        bounciness,
        useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
      }).start();
    return (
      <Animated.View style={[wrapperStyle, { transform: [{ scale: scaleAnim }] }]}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.9}
          disabled={disabled}
          onPressIn={() => animateTo(0.94, 8)}
          onPressOut={() => animateTo(1, 12)}
          onPress={onPress}
          style={style}
        >
          {children}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </Animated.View>
    );
  }
);

export const AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel = memo(
  ({
    athlete,
    inyectarKCAtleta,
    isInjectingKC,
    onClosePanel,
  }: {
    athlete: any;
    inyectarKCAtleta: any;
    isInjectingKC: boolean;
    onClosePanel?: () => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState<'fondos' | 'rango'>('fondos');
    const [opMode, setOpMode] = useState<'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset'>('inyectar');
    const [targetWallet, setTargetWallet] = useState<'membresia' | 'topup'>('topup');
    const [amountStr, setAmountStr] = useState<QuickAmountPreset>('100');
    const [selectedRank, setSelectedRank] = useState<string>(athlete?.nivel || 'Acceso Base');
    const [liveFeedback, setLiveFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      setSelectedRank(athlete?.nivel || 'Acceso Base');
      setLiveFeedback(null);
    }, [athlete?.id, athlete?.nivel]);

    const safeAmountStr: QuickAmountPreset = (QUICK_AMOUNTS as readonly string[]).includes(amountStr)
      ? amountStr
      : '100';
    const parsedInput = parseInt(safeAmountStr, 10);

    const currentMem = Math.max(0, Number(athlete?.kc_membresia) || 0);
    const currentTop = Math.max(0, Number(athlete?.kc_topup) || 0);
    const currentTotal = currentMem + currentTop;
    const activeWalletBalance = targetWallet === 'membresia' ? currentMem : currentTop;
    const isWalletZero = activeWalletBalance <= 0;

    useEffect(() => {
      if (isWalletZero && (opMode === 'retirar' || opMode === 'reset')) {
        setOpMode('inyectar');
      }
    }, [isWalletZero, opMode]);

    if (!athlete) return null;
    const simulatedMem =
      opMode === 'reset'
        ? targetWallet === 'membresia' ? 0 : currentMem
        : targetWallet === 'membresia'
        ? opMode === 'retirar' ? Math.max(0, currentMem - parsedInput) : currentMem + parsedInput
        : currentMem;
    const simulatedTop =
      opMode === 'reset'
        ? targetWallet === 'topup' ? 0 : currentTop
        : targetWallet === 'topup'
        ? opMode === 'retirar' ? Math.max(0, currentTop - parsedInput) : currentTop + parsedInput
        : currentTop;
    const simulatedTotal = simulatedMem + simulatedTop;

    const selectedGem =
      VIP_GEM_CONFIGS.find((g) => g.dbValue.toLowerCase() === String(selectedRank).toLowerCase()) ||
      VIP_GEM_CONFIGS[0];
    const walletLabelUpper = targetWallet === 'membresia' ? tx('MEMBRESÍA', 'MEMBERSHIP') : 'TOP-UP';
    const currentRankDisplay = localizeVipTierLabel(athlete.nivel, isEn).toUpperCase();
    const selectedRankDisplay = localizeVipTierLabel(selectedRank, isEn).toUpperCase();
    const athleteUpper = String(athlete.nombre || '').toUpperCase();
    const withdrawAccent = isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52';

    const handleExecuteFunds = () => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      setLiveFeedback(null);
      inyectarKCAtleta?.(
        athlete.id,
        opMode === 'reset' ? '0' : String(parsedInput),
        targetWallet,
        null,
        opMode,
        () => {
          setLiveFeedback(
            opMode === 'reset'
              ? tx(`✓ Billetera ${walletLabelUpper} vaciada a 0 KC en Supabase`, `✓ ${walletLabelUpper} wallet reset to 0 KC in Supabase`)
              : opMode === 'retirar'
              ? tx(`✓ Se retiraron -${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) a ${athlete.nombre}`, `✓ Withdrew -${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) from ${athlete.nombre}`)
              : tx(`✓ Se inyectaron +${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) a ${athlete.nombre}`, `✓ Injected +${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) to ${athlete.nombre}`)
          );
        }
      );
    };

    const handleExecuteRankChange = () => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      setLiveFeedback(null);
      inyectarKCAtleta?.(athlete.id, '0', 'membresia', selectedRank, 'inyectar', () => {
        setLiveFeedback(
          tx(
            `✓ Rango de ${athlete.nombre} actualizado a ${selectedRankDisplay} en Supabase`,
            `✓ ${athlete.nombre}’s VIP tier updated to ${selectedRankDisplay} in Supabase`
          )
        );
      });
    };

    return (
      <View style={styles.treasuryEditorBox}>
        {onClosePanel && (
          <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
            <Text style={styles.cyanMicroTitle}>
              {tx('GESTIONAR BÓVEDA:', 'MANAGE VAULT:')} {athleteUpper}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClosePanel}>
              <Text style={styles.closeMiniLink}>{tx('CERRAR ✕', 'CLOSE ✕')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}

        {Boolean(liveFeedback) && (
          <View style={styles.liveSuccessBanner}>
            <Feather name="check-circle" size={14} color={themeColors.green} />
            <Text style={styles.liveSuccessText}>{liveFeedback}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={[styles.rowGap8, { marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 12 }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.modeSwitchTab, subTab === 'fondos' && styles.modeSwitchTabCyan]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSubTab('fondos'); }}
          >
            <Feather name="sliders" size={13} color={subTab === 'fondos' ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.modeSwitchText, subTab === 'fondos' && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {tx('MOVER FONDOS (KC)', 'MOVE FUNDS (KC)')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.modeSwitchTab, subTab === 'rango' && styles.modeSwitchTabPurple]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSubTab('rango'); }}
          >
            <Feather name="award" size={13} color={subTab === 'rango' ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.modeSwitchText, subTab === 'rango' && { color: themeColors.purpleText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {tx('RANGO VIP', 'VIP TIER')} ({currentRankDisplay})
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {subTab === 'fondos' ? (
          <>
            <Text style={styles.stepLabel}>
              {tx('1. OPERACIÓN EN BÓVEDA (SIN ALTERAR RANGO VIP)', '1. VAULT OPERATION (VIP TIER UNCHANGED)')}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.rowGap8}>
              {[
                { key: 'inyectar', label: tx('+ INYECTAR', '+ INJECT') },
                { key: 'retirar', label: tx('− RETIRAR', '− WITHDRAW') },
                { key: 'reset', label: tx('Ø VACIAR A 0', 'Ø RESET TO 0') },
              ].map((op: any) => {
                const active = opMode === op.key;
                const isOpDisabled = (op.key === 'retirar' || op.key === 'reset') && isWalletZero;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={op.key}
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    disabled={isOpDisabled}
                    style={[
                      styles.pillOptionBtn,
                      active && (op.key === 'reset' ? styles.quickAmtChipActiveRed : styles.pillOptionBtnActiveCyan),
                      isOpDisabled && { opacity: 0.4 },
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setOpMode(op.key); }}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.pillOptionText,
                        active && { color: op.key === 'reset' ? withdrawAccent : themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {op.label}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.stepLabel}>
              {tx('2. SELECCIONA LA BILLETERA OBJETIVO', '2. SELECT TARGET WALLET')}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.rowGap8}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                style={[styles.pillOptionBtn, targetWallet === 'membresia' && styles.pillOptionBtnActiveCyan]}

            </Text>

            <View style={styles.vipGrid}>
              {VIP_GEM_CONFIGS.map((gem) => {
                const active = gem.dbValue.toLowerCase() === String(selectedRank).toLowerCase();
                const bg = isLightMode
                  ? active ? gem.lightBgActive : gem.lightBg
                  : active ? gem.darkBgActive : gem.darkBg;
                const txtColor = isLightMode ? gem.lightText : gem.darkText;
                return (
                  <AnimatedScaleTouchable
                    key={gem.dbValue}
                    wrapperStyle={{ width: '48.4%' }}
                    onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSelectedRank(gem.dbValue); }}
                    style={[
                      styles.gemRankBtn,

                        shadowOpacity: active ? 0.52 : 0.24,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <View style={styles.gemRankLeft}>
                      <Feather name={gem.icon} size={15} color={txtColor} />
                      <Text style={[styles.gemRankText, { color: txtColor, fontWeight: active ? '900' : '800' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {isEn ? gem.labelEn : gem.labelEs}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    {active && <Feather name="check-circle" size={14} color={txtColor} />}
                  </AnimatedScaleTouchable>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            <AnimatedScaleTouchable
              wrapperStyle={{ marginTop: 14 }}
              disabled={isInjectingKC}
              onPress={handleExecuteRankChange}
              style={[
                styles.confirmActionBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: selectedGem.ctaBg,
                  borderColor: selectedGem.border,
                  borderTopColor: selectedGem.ctaTop,
                  borderBottomColor: selectedGem.ctaBottom,
                  shadowColor: selectedGem.shadow,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {isInjectingKC ? (
                <KineticLoader size={16} color={selectedGem.ctaText} />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Feather name={selectedGem.icon} size={15} color={selectedGem.ctaText} />
                  <Text style={[styles.confirmActionText, { color: selectedGem.ctaText }]}>
                    {tx(`ACTUALIZAR RANGO A ${selectedRankDisplay}`, `UPDATE VIP TIER TO ${selectedRankDisplay}`)}
                  </Text>
                </>
              )}
            </AnimatedScaleTouchable>
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
