  periodo_inicio?: string;
  periodo_fin?: string;
};

interface SpeiPeriodDropdownSelectorProps {
  periodOptions: ISpeiPeriodOption[];
  selectedPeriodRange: ISpeiPeriodOption;
  isPeriodMenuOpen: boolean;
  onToggleMenu: () => void;
  onSelectPeriod: (key: string) => void;
  pStyles: typeof speiPeriodStyles;
  isLightMode: boolean;
  cyanInk: string;
  themeColors: { textPrimary: string; textMuted: string };
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
}

export const SpeiPeriodDropdownSelector = memo(
  ({
    periodOptions,
    selectedPeriodRange,
    isPeriodMenuOpen,
    onToggleMenu,
    onSelectPeriod,
    pStyles,
    isLightMode,
    cyanInk,
    themeColors,
    tx,
  }: SpeiPeriodDropdownSelectorProps) => (
    <>
      <TouchableOpacity
        activeOpacity={0.86}
        style={[pStyles.periodBtn3D, isLightMode ? pStyles.periodBtnLight : pStyles.periodBtnDark]}
        onPress={onToggleMenu}
      >
        <View style={pStyles.periodBtnLeft}>
          <Feather name="calendar" size={14} color={cyanInk} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={[pStyles.periodMicroTag, { color: cyanInk }]}>
              {tx('PERIODO / HISTÓRICO DE CORTE B2B', 'SETTLEMENT PERIOD / HISTORY')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[pStyles.periodMainText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {selectedPeriodRange.title} • {selectedPeriodRange.rangeLabel}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <Text style={[pStyles.periodChevron, { color: cyanInk }]}>{isPeriodMenuOpen ? '▲' : '▼'}</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {isPeriodMenuOpen && (
        <View style={[pStyles.periodDropdown3D, isLightMode ? pStyles.periodDropdownLight : pStyles.periodDropdownDark]}>
          <ScrollView nestedScrollEnabled showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" style={{ maxHeight: 245 }}>
            {periodOptions.map((opt: ISpeiPeriodOption) => {
              const isSel = opt.key === selectedPeriodRange.key;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.key}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[pStyles.periodOptionRow, isSel && (isLightMode ? pStyles.periodOptionSelLight : pStyles.periodOptionSelDark)]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    onSelectPeriod(opt.key);
                  }}
                >
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                    <Text style={[pStyles.periodOptionTitle, { color: isSel ? cyanInk : themeColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: isSel ? '900' : '700' }]}>
                      {opt.title}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[pStyles.periodOptionRange, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{opt.rangeLabel}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Feather name={isSel ? 'check-circle' : 'circle'} size={14} color={isSel ? cyanInk : themeColors.textMuted} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      )}
    </>
  )
);

interface SpeiPartnerFiscalInlineEditorProps {
  styles: AdminOpsStylesMap;
  themeColors: { textMuted: string };
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  rfcDraft: string;
  setRfcDraft: (v: string) => void;
  clabeDraft: string;
  setClabeDraft: (v: string) => void;
  regimenDraft: RegimenFiscalSAT;
  setRegimenDraft: (v: RegimenFiscalSAT) => void;
  isUpdatingFiscal: boolean;
  onSave: () => void;
}

export const SpeiPartnerFiscalInlineEditor = memo(
  ({
    styles,
    themeColors,
    tx,
    rfcDraft,
    setRfcDraft,
    clabeDraft,
    setClabeDraft,
    regimenDraft,
    setRegimenDraft,
    isUpdatingFiscal,
    onSave,
  }: SpeiPartnerFiscalInlineEditorProps) => (
    <View style={styles.fiscalEditorBox}>
      <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{tx('RFC DEL OPERADOR', 'OPERATOR TAX ID (RFC)')}</Text>
      <TextInput
        style={[styles.textInput, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }]}
        placeholder={tx('Ej. EKU9003173C9', 'E.g. EKU9003173C9')}
        placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
        autoCapitalize="characters"
        value={rfcDraft}
        onChangeText={setRfcDraft}
      />
      <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{tx('CLABE INTERBANCARIA (18 DÍGITOS)', 'INTERBANK CLABE (18 DIGITS)')}</Text>
      <TextInput
        style={[styles.textInput, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }]}
        placeholder={tx('18 dígitos SPEI', '18-digit SPEI CLABE')}
        placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
        keyboardType="numeric"
        maxLength={18}
        value={clabeDraft}
        onChangeText={(txt) => setClabeDraft(String(txt || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 18))}
      />
      <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{tx('RÉGIMEN FISCAL SAT', 'SAT TAX REGIME')}</Text>
      <View style={styles.regimenRow}>
        {[
          { key: 'persona_moral', label: tx('P. Moral (0% Ret)', 'Corp. Entity (0% Tax)') },
          { key: 'persona_fisica_con_rfc', label: tx('P. Física (2.1% ISR / 8% IVA)', 'Individual w/ Tax ID (2.1% / 8%)') },
          { key: 'sin_rfc', label: tx('Sin RFC (20% ISR / 16% IVA)', 'No Tax ID (20% / 16%)') },
        ].map((rg) => {
          const active = regimenDraft === rg.key;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={rg.key}
              style={[styles.regimenChip, active && styles.regimenChipActive]}
              onPress={() => setRegimenDraft(rg.key as RegimenFiscalSAT)}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.regimenChipText, active && { color: '#050505' }]}>{rg.label}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>
      <TouchableOpacity style={styles.saveNewAffiliateBtn} disabled={isUpdatingFiscal} onPress={onSave}>
        <Text style={styles.saveNewAffiliateBtnText}>
          {isUpdatingFiscal
            ? tx('ACTUALIZANDO...', 'UPDATING...')
            : tx('GUARDAR EXPEDIENTE SAT Y RECALCULAR', 'SAVE TAX RECORD & RECALCULATE')}
        </Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    </View>
  )
);

const formatSignedDeduction = (val: number) => {
  const n = Number(val) || 0;
  return Math.abs(n) < 0.005 ? formatMxn(0) : `-${formatMxn(Math.abs(n))}`;
};

interface SpeiAffiliateCorteCardProps {
  c: SpeiCorteItem;
  matchedSocio?: { whatsapp?: string | null } | null;
  activeReport?: Partial<IReporteMaestroConPases> | null;
  isEditing: boolean;
  revealedClabe: boolean;
  isLightMode: boolean;
  themeColors: { cyanText: string; textMuted: string; green: string; purpleText: string };
  styles: AdminOpsStylesMap;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  td: (v: string) => string;
  isEn: boolean;
  rfcDraft: string;
  setRfcDraft: (v: string) => void;
  clabeDraft: string;
  setClabeDraft: (v: string) => void;
  regimenDraft: RegimenFiscalSAT;
  setRegimenDraft: (v: RegimenFiscalSAT) => void;
  isUpdatingFiscal: boolean;
  isExportingPDF: boolean;
  onExportPdf?: (c: SpeiCorteItem) => void;
  onCopyClabe?: (value: string, label: string) => void;
  onOpenFiscalEditor: (c: SpeiCorteItem) => void;
  onToggleRevealClabe: (afiliadoId: string) => void;
  onCopySpeiSlip: (c: SpeiCorteItem) => void;
  onSaveFiscal: (afiliadoId: string) => void;
}

export const SpeiAffiliateCorteCard = memo(
  ({
    c,
    matchedSocio,
    activeReport,
    isEditing,
    revealedClabe,
    isLightMode,
    themeColors,
    styles,
    tx,
    td,
    isEn,
    rfcDraft,
    setRfcDraft,
    clabeDraft,
    setClabeDraft,
    regimenDraft,
    setRegimenDraft,
    isUpdatingFiscal,
    isExportingPDF,
    onExportPdf,
    onCopyClabe,
    onOpenFiscalEditor,
    onToggleRevealClabe,
    onCopySpeiSlip,
    onSaveFiscal,
  }: SpeiAffiliateCorteCardProps) => {
    const catOp =
      String(c.categoria_operador || '').toLowerCase() === 'especialista'
        ? tx('Especialista', 'Specialist')
        : 'Club';
    const waUrl = buildPartnerSpeiWhatsappUrl({
      whatsappRaw: matchedSocio?.whatsapp || c.whatsapp,
      partnerName: c.afiliado_nombre,
      periodoInicio: c.periodo_inicio || activeReport?.periodo_inicio,
      periodoFin: c.periodo_fin || activeReport?.periodo_fin,
      brutoMxn: c.volumen_bruto_mxn,
      retencionSatMxn: Number(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn ?? c.retenciones_sat_mxn) || 0,
      netoSpeiMxn: c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn,
      isEn,
    });
    const pmDia = Number(c.pases_maestros_dia) || 0;
    const pmSem = Number(c.pases_maestros_semana) || 0;
    const pmMes = Number(c.pases_maestros_mes) || 0;

    return (
      <View style={styles.speiAffiliateCard}>
        <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
            <Text style={styles.affiliateName}>{c.afiliado_nombre}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.contactMetaText}>
              {catOp} • {td(c.disciplina)} • {tx('RFC:', 'Tax ID:')}{' '}
              {c.rfc || tx('SIN RFC (Ret. Máx)', 'NO TAX ID (Max Rate)')}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.pdfMiniBtn, isExportingPDF && { opacity: 0.55 }]} disabled={Boolean(isExportingPDF)} onPress={() => onExportPdf?.(c)}>
            <Feather name="download" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={styles.pdfMiniBtnText}>{tx('ESTADO PDF', 'PDF STATEMENT')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.speiNumbersRow}>
          <View style={styles.statCell}>
            <Text style={styles.statValue}>{formatMxn(c.volumen_bruto_mxn)}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{tx('Bruto 100%', 'Gross 100%')}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statCell}>
            <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {formatMxn(c.comision_kinetic_total_mxn)}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>Kinetic 20%</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statCell}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.statValue,
                {
                  color:
                    Math.abs(Number(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn ?? c.retenciones_sat_mxn) || 0) >= 0.005
                      ? '#D97706'
                      : themeColors.textMuted,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {formatSignedDeduction(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn ?? c.retenciones_sat_mxn ?? 0)}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{tx('Ret. SAT', 'Tax Withheld')}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statCell}>
            <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.green }]}>
              {formatMxn(c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn)}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{tx('Neto SPEI', 'Net SPEI')}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View
          style={{
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            alignItems: 'center',
            marginTop: 8,
            paddingHorizontal: 10,
            paddingVertical: 6,
            borderRadius: 8,
            backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)',
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderColor: isLightMode ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
            elevation: 0,
          }}
        >
          <Text style={[styles.contactMetaText, { marginTop: 0, flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }]}>
            {tx('Pases Maestros 0 KC:', '0 KC Master Passes:')} {pmDia} {tx('Día', 'Day')} • {pmSem}{' '}
            {tx('Sem', 'Wk')} • {pmMes} {tx('Mes', 'Mo')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textMuted }}>
            {tx('Exento SPEI $0.00', 'Exempt SPEI $0.00')}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.speiActionsRow}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.copyClabeBtn}
              onPress={() =>
                c.clabe_bancaria
                  ? onCopyClabe?.(c.clabe_bancaria, `CLABE • ${c.afiliado_nombre}`)
                  : onOpenFiscalEditor(c)
              }
            >
              <Feather name="copy" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text style={styles.copyClabeBtnText}>
                {c.clabe_bancaria
                  ? `CLABE: ${revealedClabe ? c.clabe_bancaria : maskClabeAccount(c.clabe_bancaria)}`
                  : tx('SIN CLABE (EDITAR)', 'NO CLABE (EDIT)')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            {!!c.clabe_bancaria && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.copyClabeBtn, { paddingHorizontal: 8 }]}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  onToggleRevealClabe(c.afiliado_id);
                }}
              >
                <Feather name={revealedClabe ? 'eye-off' : 'eye'} size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.fichaSpeiBtn} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => onCopySpeiSlip(c)}>
            <Feather name="clipboard" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={styles.fichaSpeiBtnText}>{tx('FICHA SPEI', 'SPEI SLIP')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {Boolean(waUrl) && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.whatsappSpeiBtn}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => {
                if (!waUrl) return;
                triggerHaptic('Light');
                Linking.openURL(waUrl).catch(() => {});
              }}
            >
              <Feather name="message-circle" size={12} color={themeColors.green} />
              <Text style={styles.whatsappSpeiBtnText}>WHATSAPP SPEI</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.fiscalEditToggleBtn} onPress={() => onOpenFiscalEditor(c)}>
            <Feather name="sliders" size={12} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
            <Text style={styles.fiscalEditToggleText}>
              {isEditing ? tx('CERRAR SAT', 'CLOSE TAX') : tx('DATOS SAT', 'TAX INFO')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {isEditing && (
          <SpeiPartnerFiscalInlineEditor
            styles={styles}
            themeColors={themeColors}
            tx={tx}
            rfcDraft={rfcDraft}
            setRfcDraft={setRfcDraft}
            clabeDraft={clabeDraft}
            setClabeDraft={setClabeDraft}
            regimenDraft={regimenDraft}
            setRegimenDraft={setRegimenDraft}
            isUpdatingFiscal={isUpdatingFiscal}
            onSave={() => onSaveFiscal(c.afiliado_id)}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

export const speiPeriodStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  periodBtn3D: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', borderRadius: 13, paddingHorizontal: 13, paddingVertical: 10, marginBottom: 10, borderWidth: 1.2, borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.2, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 }, shadowRadius: 8, elevation: 9 },
  periodBtnDark: { backgroundColor: '#08101C', borderColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.45)', borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF', borderBottomColor: '#006B78', shadowColor: COLORS.cyan, shadowOpacity: 0.34 },
  periodBtnLight: { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4', shadowColor: '#00B8D4', shadowOpacity: 0.22 },
  periodBtnLeft: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 },
  periodMicroTag: { fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.8, marginBottom: 1 },
  periodMainText: { fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: '900' },
  periodChevron: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' },
  periodDropdown3D: { borderRadius: 13, padding: 6, marginBottom: 12, borderWidth: 1.2, borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.2, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 5 }, shadowRadius: 10, elevation: 10 },
  periodDropdownDark: { backgroundColor: '#070C15', borderColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.45)', borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF', borderBottomColor: '#006B78', shadowColor: COLORS.cyan, shadowOpacity: 0.34 },
  periodDropdownLight: { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4', shadowColor: '#00B8D4', shadowOpacity: 0.2 },
  periodOptionRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: 11, paddingVertical: 9, borderRadius: 9, marginBottom: 2, elevation: 0 },
  periodOptionSelDark: { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)', elevation: 0 },
  periodOptionSelLight: { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', elevation: 0 },
  periodOptionTitle: { fontSize: 12 },
  periodOptionRange: { fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '700', marginTop: 1 },
});

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminDispersionSpeiView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminDispersionSpeiView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload,
  fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc,
} from '../../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import { buildSpeiBatchToastSummary, exportarLayoutSpeiMasivoCsv } from '../../services/admin/adminSpeiBatchCsvHelper';
import { calcularCorteSemanalB2B, type IReporteMaestroConPases, normalizeAffiliateKey } from '../../services/adminB2BCutEngine';
import { formatMxn } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { ICorteSemanalAfiliado, IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO, RegimenFiscalSAT } from '../../types';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { yieldNextUiFrame } from '../../utils/kineticVolumetric3dPureLeaf';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../AdminOperationsViews.styles';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import {
  buildAdminSpeiPeriodOptions,
  SpeiAffiliateCorteCard,
  SpeiPeriodDropdownSelector,
  speiPeriodStyles,
} from './AdminDispersionSpeiSubViews';

type AdminRecordRow = Record<string, unknown> & { id?: string; nombre?: string | null; whatsapp?: string | null };
type SpeiCorteItem = Partial<ICorteSemanalAfiliado> & {
  afiliado_id: string; afiliado_nombre: string; categoria_operador?: string; disciplina: string;
  rfc?: string; clabe_bancaria?: string; whatsapp?: string; retenciones_sat_mxn?: number;
  total_retenciones_sat_mxn?: number; volumen_bruto_mxn: number; comision_kinetic_total_mxn: number;
  neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: number; periodo_inicio?: string; periodo_fin?: string;
  pases_maestros_dia?: number; pases_maestros_semana?: number; pases_maestros_mes?: number;
};

export interface AdminDispersionSpeiViewProps {
  reporteCorteSemanal?: Partial<IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO> | null;
  sociosReales?: AdminRecordRow[]; reservasHistoricas?: AdminRecordRow[];
  actualizarFiscalSocio?: (afiliadoId: string, rfc: string, regimen: RegimenFiscalSAT, clabe: string, csf: boolean, onSuccess?: () => void) => Promise<boolean>;
  isUpdatingFiscal?: boolean;
  exportarReporteMaestroPDF?: (customReporte?: unknown, pasesResumen?: { dia?: number; semana?: number; mes?: number }) => Promise<void> | void;
  exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF?: (corteSocio: ICorteSemanalAfiliado, pasesResumen?: { dia?: number; semana?: number; mes?: number }) => Promise<void> | void;
  isExportingPDF?: boolean; copiarDatoBancario?: (value: string, label: string) => void;
}

export const AdminDispersionSpeiView = memo(({
  reporteCorteSemanal, sociosReales = [], reservasHistoricas = [], actualizarFiscalSocio,
  isUpdatingFiscal = false, exportarReporteMaestroPDF, exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF,
  isExportingPDF = false, copiarDatoBancario,
}: AdminDispersionSpeiViewProps) => {
    const [editingAffiliateId, setEditingAffiliateId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [rfcDraft, setRfcDraft] = useState('');
    const [clabeDraft, setClabeDraft] = useState('');
    const [regimenDraft, setRegimenDraft] = useState<RegimenFiscalSAT>('persona_moral');
    const [csfDraft, setCsfDraft] = useState(true);
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
    const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState('activos_o_movimiento');
    const [activeSort, setActiveSort] = useState('spei_desc');
    const [selectedPeriodKey, setSelectedPeriodKey] = useState<string>('week_0');
    const [isPeriodMenuOpen, setIsPeriodMenuOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
    const [speiToastMsg, setSpeiToastMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isExportingSpeiCsv, setIsExportingSpeiCsv] = useState(false);
    const [revealedClabeIds, setRevealedClabeIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [rpcMasterPasses, setRpcMasterPasses] = useState<{ dia: number; semana: number; mes: number }>({
      dia: 0,
      semana: 0,
      mes: 0,
    });
    const mountedRef = useRef(true);
    const speiCsvBusyRef = useRef(false);
    const exportLockRef = useRef(false);
    const toastTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      mountedRef.current = true;
      return () => {
        mountedRef.current = false;
        if (toastTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(toastTimerRef.current);
      };
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      let cancelled = false;
      fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc(null)
        .then((res) => {
          if (!cancelled && mountedRef.current && res) {
            setRpcMasterPasses({ dia: res.dia, semana: res.semana, mes: res.mes });
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
      };
    }, []);

    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx, td, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, ['masterPdfBtnText', 'speiBatchCsvBtnText']), [resolvedTheme]);
    const pStyles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(speiPeriodStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

    const showSpeiToast = useCallback((msg: string) => {
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setSpeiToastMsg(msg);
      if (toastTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(toastTimerRef.current);
      toastTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (mountedRef.current) setSpeiToastMsg(null); }, 3400);
    }, []);

    const periodOptions = useMemo(() => buildAdminSpeiPeriodOptions(isEn, tx), [isEn, tx]);
    const selectedPeriodRange = useMemo(
      () => periodOptions.find((p) => p.key === selectedPeriodKey) || periodOptions[0],
      [periodOptions, selectedPeriodKey]
    );

    const activeReport = useMemo<Partial<IReporteMaestroConPases> | null>(
      () =>
        (Array.isArray(sociosReales) && sociosReales.length > 0
          ? calcularCorteSemanalB2B(sociosReales, reservasHistoricas, selectedPeriodRange.override)
          : reporteCorteSemanal) as Partial<IReporteMaestroConPases> | null,
      [sociosReales, reservasHistoricas, selectedPeriodRange, reporteCorteSemanal]
    );

    const socioLookup = useMemo(() => {
      const byId = new Map<string, AdminRecordRow>();
      const byKey = new Map<string, AdminRecordRow>();
      (Array.isArray(sociosReales) ? sociosReales : []).forEach((s: AdminRecordRow) => {
        if (!s) return;
        if (s.id) byId.set(String(s.id), s);
        const nk = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(s.nombre || ''));
        if (nk && !byKey.has(nk)) byKey.set(nk, s);
      });
      return { byId, byKey };
    }, [sociosReales]);

    const cortes = useMemo(() => (activeReport?.cortes_por_afiliado || []) as SpeiCorteItem[], [activeReport?.cortes_por_afiliado]);
    const periodoStartStr = activeReport?.periodo_inicio ? fmtDate(activeReport.periodo_inicio) || activeReport.periodo_inicio : tx('Lunes', 'Monday');
    const periodoEndStr = activeReport?.periodo_fin ? fmtDate(activeReport.periodo_fin) || activeReport.periodo_fin : tx('Domingo', 'Sunday');

    const filteredCortes = useMemo(() => {
      const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
      const base = cortes.filter((c: SpeiCorteItem) => {
        if (activeFilter === 'activos_o_movimiento') {
          const hasMovement =
            (Number(c.volumen_bruto_mxn) || 0) > 0 ||
            (Number(c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) || 0) > 0 ||
            (Number(c.en_custodia_escrow_kc) || 0) > 0 ||
            (Number(c.sesiones_completadas) || 0) > 0 ||
            (Number(c.compensaciones_noshow) || 0) > 0 ||
            (Number(c.pases_maestros_semana) || 0) > 0;
          if (!hasMovement) return false;
        }
        if (activeFilter === 'con_pago' && (Number(c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) || 0) <= 0) return false;
        if (activeFilter === 'con_clabe' && !c.clabe_bancaria) return false;
        if (activeFilter === 'sin_rfc' && c.regimen_fiscal !== 'sin_rfc' && Boolean(c.rfc)) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        return `${c.afiliado_nombre || ''} ${c.disciplina || ''} ${c.rfc || ''} ${c.clabe_bancaria || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      });
      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        if (activeSort === 'bruto_desc') return (Number(b.volumen_bruto_mxn) || 0) - (Number(a.volumen_bruto_mxn) || 0);
        if (activeSort === 'ret_desc') return (Number(b.total_retenciones_sat_mxn) || 0) - (Number(a.total_retenciones_sat_mxn) || 0);
        if (activeSort === 'name_az') return String(a.afiliado_nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.afiliado_nombre || ''));
        return (Number(b.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) || 0) - (Number(a.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) || 0);
      });
    }, [cortes, searchQuery, activeFilter, activeSort]);

    const openFiscalEditor = useCallback((c: SpeiCorteItem) => {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      setEditingAffiliateId((prev) => {
        if (prev === c.afiliado_id) return null;
        setRfcDraft(c.rfc || '');
        setClabeDraft(c.clabe_bancaria || '');
        setRegimenDraft(c.regimen_fiscal || 'persona_moral');
        setCsfDraft(Boolean(c.csf_validada));
        return c.afiliado_id;
      });
    }, []);

    const handleCopySpeiSlip = useCallback(
      (c: SpeiCorteItem) => {
        const slip = buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload(c as ICorteSemanalAfiliado, isEn);
        if (!slip.ok) {
          triggerHaptic('Warning');
          setEditingAffiliateId(c.afiliado_id);
          setRfcDraft(c.rfc || '');
          setClabeDraft(c.clabe_bancaria || '');
          setRegimenDraft(c.regimen_fiscal || 'persona_moral');
          setCsfDraft(Boolean(c.csf_validada));
          showSpeiToast(
            tx(
              `Registra una CLABE válida de 18 dígitos para "${c.afiliado_nombre}" antes de copiar la Ficha SPEI.`,
              `Register a valid 18-digit CLABE for "${c.afiliado_nombre}" before copying the SPEI slip.`
            )
          );
          return;
        }
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        copiarDatoBancario?.(slip.clipboardText, tx(`FICHA SPEI • ${c.afiliado_nombre}`, `SPEI SLIP • ${c.afiliado_nombre}`));
        showSpeiToast(
          tx(
            `Ficha SPEI copiada ($${slip.netAmountFixed} MXN • CLABE ${slip.clabeClean.slice(-4)}).`,
            `SPEI slip copied ($${slip.netAmountFixed} MXN • CLABE ${slip.clabeClean.slice(-4)}).`
          )
        );
      },
      [copiarDatoBancario, isEn, showSpeiToast, tx]
    );

    const handleSaveFiscal = useCallback(
      (afiliadoId: string) =>
        actualizarFiscalSocio?.(afiliadoId, rfcDraft, regimenDraft, clabeDraft, csfDraft, () => setEditingAffiliateId(null)),
      [actualizarFiscalSocio, rfcDraft, regimenDraft, clabeDraft, csfDraft]
    );

    const toggleRevealClabe = useCallback((afiliadoId: string) => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      setRevealedClabeIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [afiliadoId]: !prev[afiliadoId] }));
    }, []);

    const handleExportPartnerPdf = useCallback(
      (partnerCorte: SpeiCorteItem) => {
        if (exportLockRef.current || speiCsvBusyRef.current || isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv) return;
        exportLockRef.current = true;
        Promise.resolve(
          exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF?.(partnerCorte as ICorteSemanalAfiliado, {
            dia: Number(partnerCorte?.pases_maestros_dia) || 0,
            semana: Number(partnerCorte?.pases_maestros_semana) || 0,
            mes: Number(partnerCorte?.pases_maestros_mes) || 0,
          })
        ).finally(() => { exportLockRef.current = false; });
      },
      [exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF, isExportingPDF, isExportingSpeiCsv]
    );

    const cyanInk = isLightMode ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan;
    const isCurrentPeriod = selectedPeriodKey === 'week_0';
    const globalDia = isCurrentPeriod ? Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.dia, Number(activeReport?.total_pases_maestros_dia) || 0) : Number(activeReport?.total_pases_maestros_dia) || 0;
    const globalSemana = isCurrentPeriod ? Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.semana, Number(activeReport?.total_pases_maestros_semana) || 0) : Number(activeReport?.total_pases_maestros_semana) || 0;
    const globalMes = isCurrentPeriod ? Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.mes, Number(activeReport?.total_pases_maestros_mes) || 0) : Number(activeReport?.total_pases_maestros_mes) || 0;
    const masterPassResumen = useMemo(() => ({ dia: globalDia, semana: globalSemana, mes: globalMes }), [globalDia, globalSemana, globalMes]);

    const handleExportMasterPdf = useCallback(() => {
      if (exportLockRef.current || speiCsvBusyRef.current || isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv) return;
      exportLockRef.current = true;
      Promise.resolve(exportarReporteMaestroPDF?.(activeReport, masterPassResumen)).finally(() => { exportLockRef.current = false; });
    }, [exportarReporteMaestroPDF, activeReport, masterPassResumen, isExportingPDF, isExportingSpeiCsv]);

    const handleExportSpeiBatchCsv = useCallback(async () => {
      if (exportLockRef.current || speiCsvBusyRef.current || isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv) return;
      exportLockRef.current = true;
      speiCsvBusyRef.current = true;
      setIsExportingSpeiCsv(true);
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      await yieldNextUiFrame();
      try {
        const res = await exportarLayoutSpeiMasivoCsv(activeReport as Partial<IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO>);
        triggerHaptic(res.exported ? 'Success' : 'Warning');
        showSpeiToast(buildSpeiBatchToastSummary(res, formatMxn, tx));
      } catch {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showSpeiToast(tx('No se pudo exportar el Layout SPEI Masivo (.CSV).', 'Could not export the Massive SPEI Layout (.CSV).'));
      } finally {
        exportLockRef.current = false;
        speiCsvBusyRef.current = false;
        if (mountedRef.current) setIsExportingSpeiCsv(false);
      }
    }, [activeReport, isExportingPDF, isExportingSpeiCsv, showSpeiToast, tx]);

    const listHeader = useMemo(
      () => (
        <View>
          <SpeiPeriodDropdownSelector
            periodOptions={periodOptions} selectedPeriodRange={selectedPeriodRange} isPeriodMenuOpen={isPeriodMenuOpen}
            onToggleMenu={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsPeriodMenuOpen((prev) => !prev); }}
            onSelectPeriod={(k: string) => { setSelectedPeriodKey(k); setIsPeriodMenuOpen(false); }}
            pStyles={pStyles} isLightMode={isLightMode} cyanInk={cyanInk} themeColors={themeColors} tx={tx}
          />

          {Boolean(speiToastMsg) && (
            <View style={styles.feedbackToast}>
              <Feather name="info" size={13} color={cyanInk} />
              <Text style={styles.feedbackToastText}>{speiToastMsg}</Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <View style={styles.speiHeroBox}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTag}>{tx('CORTE SEMANAL B2B • MANDATO MERCANTIL 80/20', 'WEEKLY B2B SETTLEMENT • 80/20 COMMERCIAL MANDATE')}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.speiHeroAmount}>{formatMxn(activeReport?.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn || 0)} MXN</Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionSub}>{tx(`Neto total a dispersar vía SPEI • Periodo: ${periodoStartStr} al ${periodoEndStr}`, `Total net SPEI payout • Period: ${periodoStartStr} to ${periodoEndStr}`)}</Text>

            <View style={styles.speiSummaryGrid}>
              <View style={styles.speiSummaryItem}>
                <Text style={styles.speiSummaryLabel}>{tx('BRUTO 100%', 'GROSS 100%')}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.speiSummaryValue}>{formatMxn(activeReport?.total_volumen_bruto_mxn || 0)}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.speiSummaryItem}>
                <Text style={styles.speiSummaryLabel}>{tx('COMISIÓN KINETIC 20%', 'KINETIC 20% FEE')}</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.speiSummaryValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{formatMxn(activeReport?.total_comision_kinetic_20_mxn || 0)}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.speiSummaryItem}>
                <Text style={styles.speiSummaryLabel}>{tx('RETENCIÓN SAT', 'SAT TAX WITHHELD')}</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.speiSummaryValue, { color: '#D97706' }]}>{formatMxn(activeReport?.total_retenciones_sat_mxn || 0)}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.masterPassRow, isLightMode && { borderColor: '#CBD5E1', backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC' }]}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.textPrimary, letterSpacing: 0.4 }}>{tx('Pases Maestros 0 KC', '0 KC Master Passes')}</Text>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700', color: themeColors.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }}>{tx(`${globalDia} Día / ${globalSemana} Semana / ${globalMes} Mes • Exento SPEI $0.00`, `${globalDia} Day / ${globalSemana} Week / ${globalMes} Month • Exempt SPEI $0.00`)}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.textMuted }}>$0.00</Text>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textMuted }}>SPEI</Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.masterPdfBtn, (isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv) && { opacity: 0.85 }]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              disabled={isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv}
              onPress={handleExportMasterPdf}
            >
              <Feather name={isExportingPDF ? 'loader' : 'file-text'} size={15} color="#050505" />
              <Text style={styles.masterPdfBtnText}>
                {isExportingPDF
                  ? tx('GENERANDO REPORTE PDF...', 'GENERATING PDF REPORT...')
                  : tx('DESCARGAR REPORTE MAESTRO CEO (.PDF)', 'DOWNLOAD CEO MASTER REPORT (.PDF)')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.speiBatchCsvBtn, isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#047857', borderColor: '#059669', borderTopColor: '#6EE7B7', borderBottomColor: '#064E3B' }, (isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv) && { opacity: 0.85 }]}
              activeOpacity={0.85} disabled={isExportingPDF || isExportingSpeiCsv} onPress={handleExportSpeiBatchCsv}
            >
              {isExportingSpeiCsv ? <KineticLoader size={15} color={isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#6EE7B7'} /> : <Feather name="download" size={15} color={isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#6EE7B7'} />}
              <Text style={[styles.speiBatchCsvBtnText, isLightMode && { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
                {isExportingSpeiCsv ? tx('GENERANDO LAYOUT SPEI...', 'GENERATING SPEI LAYOUT...') : tx('EXPORTAR LAYOUT SPEI MASIVO (.CSV)', 'EXPORT MASSIVE SPEI LAYOUT (.CSV)')}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={searchQuery} onChangeSearch={setSearchQuery}
            searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar socio, RFC o CLABE...', 'Search partner, Tax ID or CLABE...')}
            filterChips={[
              { key: 'activos_o_movimiento', label: tx('Activas / Con Operación', 'Active / With Volume') },
              { key: 'all', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), count: cortes.length },
              { key: 'con_pago', label: tx('Con Dispersión', 'With Payout') },
              { key: 'con_clabe', label: tx('CLABE Lista', 'CLABE Ready') },
              { key: 'sin_rfc', label: tx('Sin RFC', 'No Tax ID') },
            ]}
            activeFilter={activeFilter} onSelectFilter={setActiveFilter}
            sortOptions={[
              { key: 'spei_desc', label: tx('Mayor SPEI', 'Top SPEI') },
              { key: 'bruto_desc', label: tx('Mayor Bruto', 'Top Gross') },
              { key: 'ret_desc', label: tx('Mayor Ret.', 'Top Tax') },
              { key: 'name_az', label: 'A-Z' },
            ]}
            activeSort={activeSort} onSelectSort={setActiveSort}
          />
        </View>
      ),
      [
        periodOptions, selectedPeriodRange, isPeriodMenuOpen, pStyles, isLightMode, cyanInk,
        themeColors, tx, speiToastMsg, styles, activeReport, periodoStartStr, periodoEndStr,
        globalDia, globalSemana, globalMes, isExportingPDF, isExportingSpeiCsv,
        handleExportMasterPdf, handleExportSpeiBatchCsv, searchQuery, cortes.length, activeFilter, activeSort,
      ]
    );

    const renderCorteItem = useCallback(
      ({ item: c }: { item: SpeiCorteItem }) => {
        const matchedSocio =
          socioLookup.byId.get(String(c.afiliado_id || '')) ||
          socioLookup.byKey.get(normalizeAffiliateKey(c.afiliado_nombre));
        const hasRevealedClabe =
          Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(revealedClabeIds, c.afiliado_id) && Boolean(revealedClabeIds[c.afiliado_id]);
        return (
          <SpeiAffiliateCorteCard
            c={c} matchedSocio={matchedSocio} activeReport={activeReport}
            isEditing={editingAffiliateId === c.afiliado_id} revealedClabe={hasRevealedClabe}
            isLightMode={isLightMode} themeColors={themeColors} styles={styles} tx={tx} td={td} isEn={isEn}
            rfcDraft={rfcDraft} setRfcDraft={setRfcDraft} clabeDraft={clabeDraft} setClabeDraft={setClabeDraft}
            regimenDraft={regimenDraft} setRegimenDraft={setRegimenDraft}
            isUpdatingFiscal={isUpdatingFiscal} isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF}
            onExportPdf={handleExportPartnerPdf} onCopyClabe={copiarDatoBancario}
            onOpenFiscalEditor={openFiscalEditor} onToggleRevealClabe={toggleRevealClabe}
            onCopySpeiSlip={handleCopySpeiSlip} onSaveFiscal={handleSaveFiscal}
          />
        );
      },
      [
        socioLookup, activeReport, editingAffiliateId, revealedClabeIds, isLightMode, themeColors, styles, tx, td, isEn,
        rfcDraft, clabeDraft, regimenDraft, isUpdatingFiscal, isExportingPDF, handleExportPartnerPdf, copiarDatoBancario,
        openFiscalEditor, toggleRevealClabe, handleCopySpeiSlip, handleSaveFiscal,
      ]
    );

    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <FlatList
          style={{ flex: 1 }} data={filteredCortes}
          keyExtractor={(c: SpeiCorteItem, idx: number) => String(c.afiliado_id || c.afiliado_nombre || idx)}
          renderItem={renderCorteItem} ListHeaderComponent={listHeader}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
              <Feather name="briefcase" size={24} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
              <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>{tx('SIN CORTES COINCIDENTES', 'NO MATCHING SETTLEMENTS')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.emptyDesc}>{tx('Ajusta tu búsqueda o filtro para consultar otros socios B2B en este periodo.', 'Adjust your search or filter to inspect other B2B partners in this period.')}</Text>
            </View>
          }
          contentContainerStyle={[styles.container, { paddingBottom: 28 }]}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          initialNumToRender={8} maxToRenderPerBatch={8} windowSize={5} removeClippedSubviews={Platform.OS === 'android'}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminEscrowActiveView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminEscrowActiveView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { isOrphanEscrowReservation } from '../../services/adminB2BCutEngine';
import {
  formatNoShowSweepResultMessage,
  isReservationEligibleForNoShow40,
  summarizePendingNoShowContracts,
} from '../../services/kineticNoShowEngine';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../AdminOperationsViews.styles';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { SkeletonPulse } from '../SharedUI';
import { AdminNoShowsAuditListLeaf } from './AdminNoShowsAuditListLeaf';

type EscrowAlertPayload = { visible: boolean; title: string; message: string; type: 'success' | 'warning' | 'alert' | 'error' };
type EscrowSweepResult =
  | false
  | { ok?: boolean; error?: string; res?: { ok?: boolean; procesadas: number; escrow_retenido_kc: number } }
  | null
  | undefined;
interface EscrowContractRow extends Record<string, unknown> {
  id?: string | number | null;
  estado?: string | null;
  servicio?: string | null;
  nombre_servicio?: string | null;
  negocio_nombre?: string | null;
  atleta_nombre?: string | null;
  fecha_clase?: string | null;
  hora_inicio?: string | null;
  folio_corto?: string | null;
  monto_escrow?: number | string | null;
  monto_puerta?: number | string | null;
  escrow_membresia?: number | string | null;
  precio_total_kc?: number | string | null;
  costo_total?: number | string | null;
  es_pase_maestro?: boolean | null;
  created_at?: string | null;
  updated_at?: string | null;
}

export const AdminEscrowActiveView = memo(
  ({
    reservasEscrowActivas = [],
    reservasHistoricas = [],
    ejecutarBarridoNoShow40,
    isSweepingNoShow = false,
    isLoading = false,
    onRequestAlert,
  }: {
    reservasEscrowActivas: EscrowContractRow[];
    reservasHistoricas?: EscrowContractRow[];
    ejecutarBarridoNoShow40?: (reservaId?: string | null) => Promise<EscrowSweepResult> | EscrowSweepResult;
    isSweepingNoShow?: boolean;
    isLoading?: boolean;
    onRequestAlert?: (cfg: EscrowAlertPayload) => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, language, tx, fmtDate, fmtDateTime } =
      useKineticPreferences();
    const isLightMode = resolvedTheme === 'light';
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const styles = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const [viewTab, setViewTab] = useState<'activos' | 'noshows'>('activos');
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
    const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState('all');
    const [activeSort, setActiveSort] = useState('recent');
    const [sweepingId, setSweepingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const sweepBusyRef = useRef(false);

    const triggerSweep = async (reservaId?: string | null) => {
      if (!ejecutarBarridoNoShow40 || sweepBusyRef.current) return;
      sweepBusyRef.current = true;
      setSweepingId(reservaId ? String(reservaId) : '__ALL__');
      try {
        const result = await ejecutarBarridoNoShow40(reservaId || null);
        if (result && result.ok && result.res && onRequestAlert) {
          onRequestAlert({
            visible: true,
            title: tx('BARRIDO NO-SHOW 40% EJECUTADO', '40% NO-SHOW SWEEP COMPLETE'),
            message: formatNoShowSweepResultMessage(result.res, isEn),
            type: result.res.procesadas > 0 ? 'success' : 'warning',
          });
        }
      } finally {
        sweepBusyRef.current = false;
        setSweepingId(null);
      }
    };

    const safeEscrowList = useMemo(
      () => (reservasEscrowActivas || []).filter((r) => !isOrphanEscrowReservation(r)),
      [reservasEscrowActivas]
    );

    const totalEscrowKc = useMemo(
      () =>
        safeEscrowList.reduce(
          (acc, r) => acc + (Number(r?.monto_escrow) || 0),
          0
        ),
      [safeEscrowList]
    );

    const noShowSummary = useMemo(
      () => summarizePendingNoShowContracts(safeEscrowList),
      [safeEscrowList]
    );
    const eligibleSet = useMemo(
      () => new Set(noShowSummary.eligibleIds),
      [noShowSummary.eligibleIds]
    );

    const filterCounts = useMemo(() => {
      let clubes = 0;
      let oficial = 0;
      let highKc = 0;
      safeEscrowList.forEach((res) => {
        const clubLower = String(res?.negocio_nombre || '').toLowerCase();
        const isOfficial =
          clubLower.includes('kinetic oficial') ||
          clubLower.includes('kinetic sports club');
        if (isOfficial) oficial += 1;
        else clubes += 1;
        if ((Number(res?.precio_total_kc ?? res?.costo_total) || 0) >= 150) {
          highKc += 1;
        }
      });
      return { clubes, oficial, highKc };
    }, [safeEscrowList]);

    const formatContractDate = (res: EscrowContractRow) => {
      const raw = res?.fecha_clase || res?.created_at;
      if (!raw) return tx('Fecha programada', 'Scheduled date');
      const s = String(raw).trim();
      if (
        s.includes('T') &&
        !s.endsWith('T00:00:00') &&
        !s.endsWith('T00:00:00.000Z')
      ) {
        return fmtDateTime(s) || s;
      }
      return fmtDate(s) || s;
    };

    const filteredContracts = useMemo(() => {
      const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
      const base = safeEscrowList.filter((res) => {
        const clubLower = String(res?.negocio_nombre || '').toLowerCase();
        const isOfficial =
          clubLower.includes('kinetic oficial') ||
          clubLower.includes('kinetic sports club');
        if (activeFilter === 'oficial' && !isOfficial) return false;
        if (activeFilter === 'clubes' && isOfficial) return false;
        if (
          activeFilter === 'high_kc' &&
          (Number(res?.precio_total_kc ?? res?.costo_total) || 0) < 150
        ) {
          return false;
        }
        if (!q) return true;
        const folio =
          res?.folio_corto || `#KNT-${String(res?.id || '').slice(0, 8)}`;
        const blob = `${folio} ${res?.servicio || ''} ${res?.nombre_servicio || ''} ${
          res?.negocio_nombre || ''
        } ${res?.atleta_nombre || ''}`.toLowerCase();
        return blob.includes(q);
      });

      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        if (activeSort === 'escrow_desc') {
          return (Number(b?.monto_escrow) || 0) - (Number(a?.monto_escrow) || 0);
        }
        if (activeSort === 'total_desc') {
          return (
            (Number(b?.precio_total_kc ?? b?.costo_total) || 0) -
            (Number(a?.precio_total_kc ?? a?.costo_total) || 0)
          );
        }
        if (activeSort === 'athlete_az') {
          return String(a?.atleta_nombre || '').localeCompare(
            String(b?.atleta_nombre || '')
          );
        }
        const tsB = new Date(String(b?.fecha_clase || b?.created_at || 0)).getTime() || 0;
        const tsA = new Date(String(a?.fecha_clase || a?.created_at || 0)).getTime() || 0;
        return tsB - tsA;
      });
    }, [safeEscrowList, searchQuery, activeFilter, activeSort]);

    return (
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerBanner}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTag}>
              {tx('BÓVEDA CENTRAL ESCROW 40%', 'CENTRAL 40% ESCROW VAULT')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
              {tx('Contratos en Custodia Activa', 'Contracts in Active Escrow')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionSub}>
              {tx(
                'Capital retenido al 40% esperando validación Pase QR en recepción.',
                '40% capital held in custody awaiting front-desk QR Pass validation.'
              )}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.escrowTotalBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.escrowTotalValue}>
              {isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 ? '...' : `${totalEscrowKc} KC`}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.escrowTotalLabel}>
              {isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 ? tx('CARGANDO', 'LOADING') : `${safeEscrowList.length} ${tx('RESERVAS', 'BOOKINGS')}`}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <KineticListFilterSortBar
          searchQuery={searchQuery}
          onChangeSearch={setSearchQuery}
          searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar atleta, sede, evento o #folio...', 'Search athlete, venue, event or folio...')}
          filterChips={[
            { key: 'all', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 ? undefined : safeEscrowList.length },
            { key: 'clubes', label: tx('Clubes B2B', 'B2B Clubs'), count: isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 ? undefined : filterCounts.clubes },
            { key: 'oficial', label: tx('Oficiales', 'Official'), count: isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 ? undefined : filterCounts.oficial },
            { key: 'high_kc', label: '≥ 150 KC', count: isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 ? undefined : filterCounts.highKc },
            {
              key: 'noshows',
              label: tx('No-Shows', 'No-Shows'),
              count: isLoading && (reservasHistoricas || []).length === 0 ? undefined : (reservasHistoricas || []).filter((r) => ['no_show', 'penalizada', 'penalizado'].includes(String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim())).length,
            },
          ]}
          activeFilter={viewTab === 'noshows' ? 'noshows' : activeFilter}
          onSelectFilter={(k) => {
            if (k === 'noshows') { setViewTab('noshows'); }
            else { setViewTab('activos'); setActiveFilter(k); }
          }}
          sortOptions={[
            { key: 'recent', label: tx('Recientes', 'Newest') },
            { key: 'escrow_desc', label: tx('Mayor Custodia', 'Top Escrow') },
            { key: 'total_desc', label: tx('Mayor Total', 'Top Total') },
            { key: 'athlete_az', label: tx('Atleta A-Z', 'Athlete A-Z') },
          ]}
          activeSort={activeSort}
          onSelectSort={setActiveSort}
        />

        {isLoading && safeEscrowList.length === 0 && (reservasHistoricas || []).length === 0 ? (
          <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 92, borderRadius: 16 }} />
            <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 92, borderRadius: 16 }} />
          </View>
        ) : viewTab === 'noshows' ? (
          <AdminNoShowsAuditListLeaf reservasHistoricas={reservasHistoricas} searchQuery={searchQuery} />
        ) : (
          <>

        {noShowSummary.eligibleCount > 0 && (
          <View
            style={{
              marginBottom: 12,
              padding: 12,
              borderRadius: 14,
              borderWidth: 1.2,
              borderColor: isLightMode ? '#D97706' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.52)',
              backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFBEB' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12)',
              elevation: 0,
            }}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8, marginBottom: 6 }}>
              <Text style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24' }}>
                {tx(
                  `⏱ ${noShowSummary.eligibleCount} VENCIDA(S) PARA BARRIDO NO-SHOW 40%`,
                  `⏱ ${noShowSummary.eligibleCount} EXPIRED FOR 40% NO-SHOW SWEEP`
                )}
              </Text>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.textPrimary }}>
                {noShowSummary.totalEscrow40Kc} KC
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: themeColors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 10 }}>
              {tx(
                `Retiene 40% (${noShowSummary.venueNet80Mxn} KC sede / ${noShowSummary.kineticFee20Mxn} KC Kinetic) sin cobrar el 60% de puerta.`,
                `Retains 40% (${noShowSummary.venueNet80Mxn} KC venue / ${noShowSummary.kineticFee20Mxn} KC Kinetic) without charging 60% door fee.`
              )}
            </Text>
            {ejecutarBarridoNoShow40 && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                disabled={isSweepingNoShow || sweepingId !== null}
                onPress={() => triggerSweep(null)}
                style={{
                  minHeight: 40,
                  borderRadius: 10,
                  paddingHorizontal: 12,
                  backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#D97706' : '#F59E0B',
                  flexDirection: 'row',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: 6,
                }}
              >
                {isSweepingNoShow || sweepingId === '__ALL__' ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" />
                ) : (
                  <Feather name="zap" size={14} color="#050505" />
                )}
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: '#050505' }}>
                  {isSweepingNoShow || sweepingId === '__ALL__'
                    ? tx('LIQUIDANDO NO-SHOW 40%...', 'SETTLING 40% NO-SHOW...')
                    : tx('⚡ EJECUTAR BARRIDO NO-SHOW 40%', '⚡ RUN 40% NO-SHOW SWEEP')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        )}

        {filteredContracts.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={26} color={COLORS.green} />
            <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>
              {reservasEscrowActivas.length === 0
                ? tx(
                    'SIN CONTRATOS PENDIENTES EN CUSTODIA',
                    'NO PENDING ESCROW CONTRACTS'
                  )
                : tx('SIN RESULTADOS EN FILTRO', 'NO MATCHING CONTRACTS')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.emptyDesc}>
              {reservasEscrowActivas.length === 0
                ? tx(
                    'Todas las reservas recientes han sido liquidadas o no existen bloqueos activos en este momento.',
                    'All recent bookings have been settled or there are no active escrow holds right now.'
                  )
                : tx(
                    'Ajusta tu búsqueda o filtro para ver otros contratos en custodia.',
                    'Adjust your search or filter to view other active escrow contracts.'
                  )}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          filteredContracts.map((res, idx: number) => {
            const isNoShowReady =
              eligibleSet.has(String(res.id || '').trim()) ||
              isReservationEligibleForNoShow40(res);
            return (
              <View key={String(res.id || idx)} style={styles.escrowContractCard}>
                <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
                  <View style={styles.folioBadge}>
                    <Feather name="lock" size={11} color={themeColors.green} />
                    <Text style={styles.folioText}>{res.folio_corto || `#KNT-${String(res.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    {(Number(res.escrow_membresia) > 0 || Number(res.monto_escrow) > 0) && (
                      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3, paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 2, borderRadius: 6, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(8,145,178,0.08)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.12)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(8,145,178,0.2)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.3)', elevation: 0 }}>
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '700', color: themeColors.cyanText }}>{`🛡️ ${tx('Ciclo 30d Blindado', '30d Cycle Protected')}`}</Text>
                      </View>
                    )}
                    <Text style={styles.escrowDateText}>{formatContractDate(res)}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.contractServiceTitle}>{res.servicio || res.nombre_servicio || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event')}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.contractClubSub}>
                  {tx('Sede:', 'Venue:')} {res.negocio_nombre || tx('Kinetic Oficial', 'Kinetic Official')} • {tx('Atleta:', 'Athlete:')} {res.atleta_nombre || tx('Atleta Kinetic', 'Kinetic Athlete')}
                </Text>
                <View style={[styles.escrowSplitRow, { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8 }]}>
                  <View style={[styles.escrowMetricBox, { flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.escrowMetricLabel} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>{tx('RETENIDO 40%', '40% ESCROW')}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.escrowMetricValue, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{res.monto_escrow ?? 0} KC</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.escrowMetricBox, { flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.escrowMetricLabel} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>{tx('PUERTA 60% (QR)', '60% DOOR (QR)')}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.escrowMetricValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{res.monto_puerta ?? 0} KC</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.escrowMetricBox, { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, alignItems: 'flex-end' }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.escrowMetricLabel, { textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>{tx('TOTAL 100%', 'TOTAL 100%')}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.escrowMetricValue, { textAlign: 'right' }]}>{res.precio_total_kc ?? res.costo_total ?? 0} KC</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                {isNoShowReady && (
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8, marginTop: 10 }}>
                    <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#FEF3C7' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.16)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLightMode ? '#D97706' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45)', elevation: 0 }}>
                      <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24' }}>
                        {tx('⏱ VENCIDA • NO-SHOW 40%', '⏱ EXPIRED • 40% NO-SHOW')}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    {ejecutarBarridoNoShow40 && (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        activeOpacity={0.85}
                        disabled={isSweepingNoShow || sweepingId !== null}
                        onPress={() => triggerSweep(String(res.id))}
                        style={{ paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#D97706' : '#F59E0B', flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}
                      >
                        {sweepingId === String(res.id) && <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" />}
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: '#050505' }}>
                          {sweepingId === String(res.id) ? tx('Liquidando...', 'Settling...') : tx('Liquidar 40%', 'Settle 40%')}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            );
          })
        )}
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    // === src/components/admin/AdminHubDashboardCards.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, RADIUS, SPACING, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticPhysicsPressable } from '../../utils/kineticPhysicsEngine';
import { AdminModuleType } from '../AdminModuleModal';
import { PulseBadge, SkeletonPulse, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export const AdminScannerSkeleton = memo(({ topInset }: { topInset: number }) => {
  const { themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <View style={[baseAdm.header, { paddingTop: topInset + 14, backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
        <View style={{ gap: 6 }}>
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 115, height: 11, borderRadius: 6 }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 160, height: 26, borderRadius: 8 }} />
        </View>
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 132, height: 34, borderRadius: 12 }} />
      </View>
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg }}>
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 128, borderRadius: RADIUS.xxl, marginBottom: 14 }} />
        <View style={baseAdm.kpiGrid}>
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 102, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 102, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 102, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 102, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
        </View>
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 54, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl, marginBottom: 22 }} />
        <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 210, height: 12, borderRadius: 6, marginBottom: 12 }} />
        <View style={baseAdm.modulesGrid}>
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 92, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 92, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 92, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
          <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '48.2%', height: 92, borderRadius: RADIUS.xl }} />
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
});

export const AdminHubDashboardCards = memo(
  ({
    hasHydratedAdminKpis = true,
    tokenomicsReales,
    netoSpeiSemanalFormatted,
    solicitudesCount,
    preRegistrosPendientesCount = 0,
    ugcPendingCount = 0,
    experienciasCount,
    isExportingPDF,
    openRootModule,
    abrirCreadorNuevo,
    exportarReporteMaestroPDF,
  }: {
    hasHydratedAdminKpis?: boolean;
    tokenomicsReales: {
      masaMonetariaTotal?: number; saldoDisponibleUsuarios?: number; escrowTotal?: number;
      atletasCount?: number; clubesTotales?: number; clubesActivos?: number; clubesProspectos?: number;
      reservasActivasCount?: number; [k: string]: unknown;
    };
    netoSpeiSemanalFormatted: string;
    solicitudesCount: number;
    preRegistrosPendientesCount?: number;
    ugcPendingCount?: number;
    experienciasCount: number;
    isExportingPDF: boolean;
    openRootModule: (mod: AdminModuleType, filter?: 'all' | 'membresia' | 'topup') => void;
    abrirCreadorNuevo: () => void;
    exportarReporteMaestroPDF: () => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, t, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const adm = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseAdm, resolvedTheme),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const totalPendingReview = solicitudesCount + preRegistrosPendientesCount;
    const isKpiLoading = !hasHydratedAdminKpis;

    return (
      <>
        <KineticPhysicsPressable
          preset="cardFloat"
          style={adm.heroCard}
          onPress={() => openRootModule('LIQUIDEZ')}
        >
          <View style={adm.heroTopRow}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <Feather name="zap" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text style={[adm.heroLabel, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {t('admin.total_circulation')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={adm.desglosePill}>
              <Text style={adm.desglosePillText}>{t('admin.breakdown')}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          {isKpiLoading ? (
            <>
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 176, height: 42, borderRadius: 10, marginVertical: 3 }} />
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '86%', height: 14, borderRadius: 6, marginTop: 6 }} />
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Text style={adm.heroAmount} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>
                {tokenomicsReales.masaMonetariaTotal}{' '}
                <Text style={[adm.heroUnit, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>KC</Text>
              </Text>
              <Text style={[adm.heroSubtext, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                {tx(
                  `Liquidez en bóvedas de atletas (${tokenomicsReales.saldoDisponibleUsuarios} KC) + Reserva Protegida 40/60 (${tokenomicsReales.escrowTotal} KC)`,
                  `Athlete vault liquidity (${tokenomicsReales.saldoDisponibleUsuarios} KC) + 40/60 Protected Escrow (${tokenomicsReales.escrowTotal} KC)`
                )}
              </Text>
            </>
          )}
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>

        <View style={adm.kpiGrid}>
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.kpiCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS', 'all')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.14)' }]}>
                <Feather name="users" size={15} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
              </View>
              <Feather name="arrow-up-right" size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </View>
            {isKpiLoading ? (
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 92, height: 24, borderRadius: 7, marginBottom: 4 }} />
            ) : (
              <Text style={adm.kpiValue}>{tokenomicsReales.atletasCount}</Text>
            )}
            <Text style={adm.kpiTitle}>{t('admin.active_athletes')}</Text>
            <Text style={adm.kpiSub}>
              {tx('Ver directorio y saldos', 'View directory & balances')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.kpiCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('SOCIOS')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="briefcase" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              <Feather name="arrow-up-right" size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </View>
            {isKpiLoading ? (
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 92, height: 24, borderRadius: 7, marginBottom: 4 }} />
            ) : (
              <Text style={adm.kpiValue}>{tokenomicsReales.clubesTotales}</Text>
            )}
            <Text style={adm.kpiTitle}>{t('admin.network_affiliates')}</Text>
            {isKpiLoading ? (
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 118, height: 12, borderRadius: 5, marginTop: 3 }} />
            ) : (
              <Text style={adm.kpiSub}>
                {tx(
                  `${tokenomicsReales.clubesActivos} Activos • ${tokenomicsReales.clubesProspectos} Prospectos`,
                  `${tokenomicsReales.clubesActivos} Active • ${tokenomicsReales.clubesProspectos} Prospects`
                )}
              </Text>
            )}
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.kpiCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('ESCROW_ACTIVO')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="lock" size={15} color={themeColors.green} />
              </View>
              <Feather name="arrow-up-right" size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </View>
            {isKpiLoading ? (
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 92, height: 24, borderRadius: 7, marginBottom: 4 }} />
            ) : (
              <Text style={adm.kpiValue}>
                {tokenomicsReales.reservasActivasCount}{' '}
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 13, color: themeColors.green }}>
                  ({tokenomicsReales.escrowTotal} KC)
                </Text>
              </Text>
            )}
            <Text style={adm.kpiTitle}>{t('admin.protected_bookings')}</Text>
            <Text style={adm.kpiSub}>
              {tx('40% en custodia activa', '40% in active escrow')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.kpiCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('DISPERSION_B2B')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="credit-card" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              <Feather name="arrow-up-right" size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </View>
            {isKpiLoading ? (
              <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: 92, height: 24, borderRadius: 7, marginBottom: 4 }} />
            ) : (
              <Text style={adm.kpiValue} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>
                ${netoSpeiSemanalFormatted}
              </Text>
            )}
            <Text style={adm.kpiTitle}>{t('admin.weekly_spei')}</Text>
            <Text style={adm.kpiSub}>
              {tx('Dispersión B2B y SAT', 'B2B & Tax Settlement')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        </View>


        {preRegistrosPendientesCount > 0 && (
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={adm.waitlistAlertBanner}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('SOLICITUDES')}
          >
            <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)' }]}>
              <Feather name="bell" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[adm.waitlistAlertTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {tx(
                  `⚡ ${preRegistrosPendientesCount} NUEVOS PRE-REGISTROS WEB`,
                  `⚡ ${preRegistrosPendientesCount} NEW WEB PRE-REGISTRATIONS`
                )}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[adm.waitlistAlertSub, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                {tx(
                  'Toca aquí para revisar y aceptar solicitudes desde kineticapp.com.mx',
                  'Tap here to review and accept leads from kineticapp.com.mx'
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        )}

        <Text style={[adm.sectionHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
          {t('admin.ops_audit_modules')}
        </Text>

        <View style={adm.modulesGrid}>
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={[
              adm.moduleCard,
              totalPendingReview > 0 && { borderColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.45)' },
            ]}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('SOLICITUDES')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="inbox" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              {totalPendingReview > 0 && <PulseBadge color={COLORS.green} size={8} />}
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('Revisión', 'Review')}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                adm.moduleSub,
                totalPendingReview > 0 && { color: themeColors.green, fontWeight: '800' },
              ]}
            >
              {totalPendingReview} {tx('Pendientes', 'Pending')}
              {preRegistrosPendientesCount > 0
                ? tx(` • ${preRegistrosPendientesCount} Web`, ` • ${preRegistrosPendientesCount} Web`)
                : ''}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.moduleCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('EVENTOS')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="calendar" size={16} color={themeColors.green} />
              </View>
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('Cartelera', 'Schedule')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {experienciasCount}{' '}
              {Number(experienciasCount) === 1
                ? tx('evento • Ocupación', 'event • Occupancy')
                : tx('eventos • Ocupación', 'events • Occupancy')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.moduleCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('ATLETAS')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.14)' }]}>
                <Feather name="bar-chart-2" size={16} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
              </View>
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Métricas', 'Metrics')}</Text>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('Niveles VIP y demanda', 'VIP tiers & demand')}</Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="cardFloat" style={adm.moduleCard} onPress={() => openRootModule('AUDIT_LOGS')}>
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="shield" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Bitácora de Auditoría', 'Audit Logs')}</Text>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('Movimientos KC y Rango VIP', 'KC & VIP Tier History')}</Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={[adm.moduleCard, ugcPendingCount > 0 && { borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.55)' }]}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('KINETI_CLUB')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 0, 127, 0.14)' }]}>
                <Feather name="users" size={16} color="#FF007F" />
              </View>
              {ugcPendingCount > 0 && (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
                  <PulseBadge color="#F59E0B" size={8} />
                  <View style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.18)', borderRadius: 8, paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 2, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45)' }}>
                    <Text style={{ color: '#F59E0B', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900' }}>{ugcPendingCount}</Text>

              )}

            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('KinetiClub', 'KinetiClub')}</Text>

              {ugcPendingCount > 0
                ? tx(`${ugcPendingCount} reporte(s) de comunidad pendiente(s)`, `${ugcPendingCount} pending community report(s)`)
                : tx('Moderación de Muro, Historias y Equipos', 'Feed, Stories & Teams Moderation')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={[adm.moduleCard, { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#7C3AED' : 'rgba(123, 97, 255, 0.42)' }]}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('ACCESOS_WEB')}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(123, 97, 255, 0.14)' }]}>
                <Feather name="key" size={16} color="#A78BFA" />
              </View>
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Accesos Web', 'Web Passes')}</Text>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('Llaves privadas y revocación 1-Tap', 'Private keys & 1-Tap revoke')}</Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); abrirCreadorNuevo(); }}
            style={[adm.moduleCard, { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.42)' }]}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)' }]}>
                <Feather name="plus-circle" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              <Feather name="arrow-up-right" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('Crear Evento', 'Create Event')}</Text>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('Publicar en cartelera oficial', 'Publish to official lineup')}</Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        </View>
      </>
    );
  }
);
