  dismissBtnText: { color: '#F87171', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 },
  acceptedPill: {
    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6, paddingVertical: 7, borderRadius: 10,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.1)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.35)', elevation: 0,
  },
  acceptedPillTxt: { fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 },
  b2bPendingBanner: {
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 171, 0, 0.08)', borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#FFAB00',
    borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: 'rgba(255, 224, 130, 0.65)', borderBottomWidth: 3.5, borderBottomColor: '#B45309',
    borderRadius: 12, padding: 10, marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 8, elevation: 0,
  },
  b2bPendingBadge: { backgroundColor: '#FFAB00', paddingHorizontal: 7, paddingVertical: 2, borderRadius: 6, alignSelf: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 6 },
  b2bPendingBadgeTxt: { color: '#050505', fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 },
  b2bPendingDetails: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700', marginBottom: 8, lineHeight: 15 },
  b2bActionsRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 },
});

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Linking, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { escapeLikePattern, UUID_ANY_RE } from '../../services/admin/adminAffiliateCrudHelper';
import { sanitizePendingB2BChanges } from '../../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import type { IWaitlistPreRegistro } from '../../services/admin/adminWaitlistService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { baseWaitlistStyles } from './AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection.styles';

interface AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosProps {
  preRegistrosWeb?: IWaitlistPreRegistro[];
  isSubmitting?: boolean;
  onGestionarPreRegistro?: (registro: IWaitlistPreRegistro, accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar') => void;
  onCopyText?: (value: string, label: string) => void;
}

export const AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection = memo(
  ({ preRegistrosWeb = [], isSubmitting = false, onGestionarPreRegistro, onCopyText }: AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
    const s = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseWaitlistStyles, resolvedTheme, ['acceptBtn3D', 'acceptBtnText']),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const [tab, setTab] = useState<'todos' | 'pendientes' | 'aceptados'>('todos');
    const [clearedPendingIds, setClearedPendingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [busyLeadId, setBusyLeadId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [feedbackMsg, setFeedbackMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const mountedRef = useRef(true);
    const busyLeadRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    const feedbackTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      mountedRef.current = true;
      return () => {
        mountedRef.current = false;
        if (feedbackTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      };
    }, []);

    const showFeedback = useCallback((msg: string) => {
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setFeedbackMsg(msg);
      if (feedbackTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (mountedRef.current) setFeedbackMsg(null); }, 3600);
    }, []);

    const { pendientes, aceptados } = useMemo(() => {
      const pend: IWaitlistPreRegistro[] = [];
      const acep: IWaitlistPreRegistro[] = [];
      (preRegistrosWeb || []).forEach((item) => {
        const st = String(item?.estado || item?.estadoRevision || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (st === 'aceptado' || st === 'aprobado') acep.push(item);
        else if (st !== 'descartado' && st !== 'rechazado') pend.push(item);
      });
      return { pendientes: pend, aceptados: acep };
    }, [preRegistrosWeb]);

    const visibleList = useMemo(
      () => (tab === 'todos' ? preRegistrosWeb : tab === 'pendientes' ? pendientes : aceptados),
      [tab, preRegistrosWeb, pendientes, aceptados]
    );

    const handleOpenEmail = (email: string) => {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      const clean = String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!clean || !clean.includes('@')) return;
      Linking.openURL(`mailto:${clean}`).catch(() => onCopyText?.(clean, tx('Correo', 'Email')));
    };

    const handleOpenWhatsapp = (phone: string) => {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      const digits = String(phone || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
      if (digits.length >= 10) {
        const waDigits = digits.length === 10 ? `52${digits}` : digits;
        Linking.openURL(`https://wa.me/${waDigits}`).catch(() => onCopyText?.(phone, tx('Teléfono', 'Phone')));
      } else {
        onCopyText?.(phone, tx('Teléfono', 'Phone'));
      }
    };

    const resolveLeadKey = (leadItem: any) =>
      String(leadItem?.id || leadItem?.email || leadItem?.nombre || '').trim();

    const handleApproveB2BChanges = useCallback(
      async (leadItem: any) => {
        const leadKey = resolveLeadKey(leadItem);
        const rawDbId = String(leadItem?.id || '').trim();
        if (!leadKey || busyLeadRef.current) return;
        busyLeadRef.current = leadKey;
        triggerHaptic('Heavy');
        setBusyLeadId(leadKey);
        try {
          const safeChanges = sanitizePendingB2BChanges(leadItem?.cambios_pendientes_b2b);
          const safeKeys = Object.keys(safeChanges);

          if (safeKeys.length > 0) {
            let updatedAny = false;
            const targetAfId = String(leadItem?.afiliado_id || rawDbId).trim();
            if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(targetAfId)) {
              const { data: byIdRows, error: byIdErr } = await supabase
                .from('afiliados').update(safeChanges).eq('id', targetAfId).select('id');
              if (!byIdErr && Array.isArray(byIdRows) && byIdRows.length > 0) updatedAny = true;
            }
            const cleanLeadName = String(leadItem?.nombre || '').trim();
            if (!updatedAny && cleanLeadName.length >= 3 && !/[%_]/.test(cleanLeadName) && cleanLeadName.toLowerCase() !== 'kinetic oficial') {
              const { error: byNameErr } = await supabase
                .from('afiliados').update(safeChanges).ilike('nombre', escapeLikePattern(cleanLeadName));
              if (byNameErr) throw byNameErr;
            }
          }

          if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(rawDbId)) {
            const { error: wlErr } = await supabase
              .from('fundadores_waitlist').update({ cambios_pendientes_b2b: null }).eq('id', rawDbId);
            if (wlErr) throw wlErr;
          }
          if (mountedRef.current) setClearedPendingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [leadKey]: true }));

          if (safeKeys.length > 0) {
            emitKineticRadarUpdated();
            showFeedback(tx(`Escaparate B2B actualizado (${safeKeys.join(', ')}).`, `B2B storefront updated (${safeKeys.join(', ')}).`));
          } else {
            showFeedback(tx('Solicitud depurada: sin campos editables permitidos.', 'Request cleared: no allowed editable storefront fields.'));
          }
        } catch {
          showFeedback(tx('No se pudo autorizar el cambio B2B. Verifica permisos RLS.', 'Could not authorize B2B change. Verify RLS permissions.'));
        } finally {
          busyLeadRef.current = null;
          if (mountedRef.current) setBusyLeadId(null);
        }
      },
      [showFeedback, tx]
    );

    const handleRejectB2BChanges = useCallback(
      async (leadItem: any) => {
        const leadKey = resolveLeadKey(leadItem);
        const rawDbId = String(leadItem?.id || '').trim();
        if (!leadKey || busyLeadRef.current) return;
        busyLeadRef.current = leadKey;
        triggerHaptic('Medium');
        setBusyLeadId(leadKey);
        try {
          if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(rawDbId)) {
            const { error: wlErr } = await supabase
              .from('fundadores_waitlist').update({ cambios_pendientes_b2b: null }).eq('id', rawDbId);
            if (wlErr) throw wlErr;
          }
          if (mountedRef.current) setClearedPendingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [leadKey]: true }));
          showFeedback(tx('Cambios pendientes B2B descartados.', 'Pending B2B changes dismissed.'));
        } catch {
          showFeedback(tx('Error al descartar solicitud B2B.', 'Error dismissing B2B request.'));
        } finally {
          busyLeadRef.current = null;
          if (mountedRef.current) setBusyLeadId(null);
        }
      },
      [showFeedback, tx]
    );

    const formatLeadDate = (iso?: string | null) => {
      if (!iso) return tx('Reciente', 'Recent');
      try {
        return fmtDate(iso, { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }) || String(iso).slice(0, 10);
      } catch {
        return String(iso).slice(0, 10);
      }
    };

    return (
      <View style={s.sectionWrap}>
        {pendientes.length > 0 && (
          <View
            style={[
              s.alertBanner3D,
              isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={s.alertIconCircle}>
              <Feather name="bell" size={15} color={isLightMode ? '#0369A1' : COLORS.cyan} />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[s.alertTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {tx(`NUEVOS PRE-REGISTROS WEB (${pendientes.length})`, `NEW WEB PRE-REGISTRATIONS (${pendientes.length})`)}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[s.alertSub, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                {tx('Solicitudes recibidas desde kineticapp.com.mx listas para revisión y aceptación.', 'Leads received from kineticapp.com.mx ready for review and approval.')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {Boolean(feedbackMsg) && (
          <View style={s.feedbackBanner3D}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={13} color={themeColors.green} />
            <Text style={[s.feedbackBannerTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{feedbackMsg}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={s.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ width: '100%' }}>
            <Text style={[s.subTag, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
              {tx('LISTA DE ACCESO PRIORITARIO WEB', 'PRIORITY WEB WAITLIST')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[s.subTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
              {tx('Pre-Registros kineticapp.com.mx', 'Web Pre-Registrations')} ({preRegistrosWeb.length})
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={s.filterPillsRow}>
            {[
              { k: 'todos', lbl: tx('Todos', 'All'), n: preRegistrosWeb.length },
              { k: 'pendientes', lbl: tx('Pendientes', 'Pending'), n: pendientes.length },
              { k: 'aceptados', lbl: tx('Aceptados', 'Accepted'), n: aceptados.length },
            ].map((p) => {
              const active = tab === p.k;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={p.k}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[s.filterPill, active && s.filterPillActive]}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setTab(p.k as any); }}
                >
                  <Text style={[s.filterPillTxt, active && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{p.lbl} ({p.n})</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>

        {visibleList.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={s.emptyBox}>
            <Feather name="inbox" size={18} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[s.emptyTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('No hay pre-registros en esta categoría.', 'No pre-registrations in this category.')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          visibleList.map((lead, idx) => {
            const leadKey = resolveLeadKey(lead) || `lead_${idx}`;
            const isB2B = lead.esSocioB2B;
            const badgeColor = isB2B ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText;
            const hasRawPending = !clearedPendingIds[leadKey] && Boolean(lead.cambios_pendientes_b2b);
            const previewEntries = hasRawPending ? Object.entries(sanitizePendingB2BChanges(lead.cambios_pendientes_b2b)) : [];
            const isRowBusy = isSubmitting || busyLeadId === leadKey;

            return (
              <View key={leadKey} style={[s.leadCard3D, isB2B && { borderColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.45)' }]}>
                <View style={s.topBadgeRow}>
                  <View style={[s.roleBadge, { borderColor: badgeColor, backgroundColor: isB2B ? 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.14)' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                    <Text style={[s.roleBadgeTxt, { color: badgeColor }]}>{isB2B ? tx('SOCIO B2B', 'B2B PARTNER') : tx('ATLETA B2C', 'B2C ATHLETE')}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={[s.dateMetaTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{lead.origen} • {formatLeadDate(lead.created_at)}</Text>
                </View>

                <Text style={[s.leadName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{lead.nombre}</Text>
                <Text style={[s.profileDetailTxt, { color: badgeColor }]}>{lead.tipo_perfil}</Text>

                <View style={s.metaGrid}>
                  {Boolean(lead.email) && (
                    <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.8} style={s.metaChip} onPress={() => handleOpenEmail(lead.email)}>
                      <Feather name="mail" size={11} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                      <Text numberOfLines={1} style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{lead.email}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(lead.telefono) && (
                    <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.8} style={s.metaChip} onPress={() => handleOpenWhatsapp(lead.telefono)}>
                      <Feather name="phone" size={11} color={themeColors.green} />
                      <Text style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{lead.telefono}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(lead.ciudad_zona) && (
                    <View style={s.metaChip}>
                      <Feather name="map-pin" size={11} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
                      <Text style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{lead.ciudad_zona}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(lead.disciplina_interes) && (
                    <View style={s.metaChip}>
                      <Feather name="award" size={11} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                      <Text style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{lead.disciplina_interes}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>

                {hasRawPending && (
                  <View style={s.b2bPendingBanner}>
                    <View style={s.b2bPendingBadge}>
                      <Text style={s.b2bPendingBadgeTxt}>{tx('CAMBIOS PENDIENTES B2B', 'PENDING B2B CHANGES')}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <Text style={[s.b2bPendingDetails, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {previewEntries.length > 0
                        ? previewEntries.map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join(' • ')
                        : tx('Sin campos de escaparate válidos (se depurará al autorizar/rechazar).', 'No valid storefront fields (will clear on action).')}
                    </Text>
                    <View style={s.b2bActionsRow}>
                      <TouchableOpacity disabled={isRowBusy} activeOpacity={0.85} style={[s.acceptBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={() => handleApproveB2BChanges(lead)}>
                        <Feather name="check" size={13} color="#050505" />
                        <Text style={s.acceptBtnText}>{tx('AUTORIZAR', 'AUTHORIZE')}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                      <TouchableOpacity disabled={isRowBusy} activeOpacity={0.85} style={[s.dismissBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={() => handleRejectB2BChanges(lead)}>
                        <Text style={s.dismissBtnText}>{tx('RECHAZAR', 'REJECT')}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                )}

                {lead.estadoRevision === 'pendiente' ? (
                  <View style={s.actionsRow}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      disabled={isRowBusy}
                      activeOpacity={0.86}
                      style={[s.acceptBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                      onPress={() => onGestionarPreRegistro?.(lead, 'aceptar')}
                      {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any)}
                    >
                      <Feather name="check-circle" size={13} color="#050505" />
                      <Text style={s.acceptBtnText}>{isB2B ? tx('ACEPTAR SOCIO', 'ACCEPT PARTNER') : tx('ACEPTAR REGISTRO', 'ACCEPT SIGNUP')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <TouchableOpacity disabled={isRowBusy} activeOpacity={0.86} style={[s.dismissBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={() => onGestionarPreRegistro?.(lead, 'descartar')}>
                      <Text style={s.dismissBtnText}>{tx('DESCARTAR', 'DISMISS')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <View style={s.acceptedPill}>
                    <Feather name="check" size={12} color={themeColors.green} />
                    <Text style={[s.acceptedPillTxt, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{tx('PRE-REGISTRO REVISADO Y ACEPTADO', 'PRE-REGISTRATION ACCEPTED')}</Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            );
          })
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminWebPrivatePassesView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminWebPrivatePassesView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Switch, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers } from '../../services/bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers';
import {
  type AdminWebInviteRow, type PrivateWebTargetPath,
  adminCreateWebInviteCloud, adminListWebInvitesCloud, adminRevokeWebInviteCloud,
} from '../../services/kineticWebPrivatePassLeaf';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const TTL_OPTS = [24, 168, 720] as const;
const USE_OPTS = [1, 3, 10] as const;

export const AdminWebPrivatePassesView = ({ onRequestAlert }: { onRequestAlert?: (a: { visible: boolean; title: string; message: string }) => void }) => {
  const { tx, themeColors, isLightMode } = useKineticPreferences();
  const [form, setForm] = useState<{ label: string; ttl: number; uses: number; target: PrivateWebTargetPath; sandbox: boolean }>({
    label: '', ttl: 24, uses: 1, target: '/login', sandbox: false,
  });
  const [listState, setListState] = useState<{ loading: boolean; busy: boolean; rows: AdminWebInviteRow[] }>({ loading: true, busy: false, rows: [] });
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<{ code: string; url: string } | null>(null);
  const [revokingId, setRevokingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const actionBusyRef = useRef(false);

  const loadRows = useCallback(async () => {
    setListState((p) => ({ ...p, loading: true }));
    const rows = await adminListWebInvitesCloud();
    if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
    setListState((p) => ({ ...p, loading: false, rows }));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    void loadRows();
    return () => { isMountedRef.current = false; };
  }, [loadRows]);

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    if (actionBusyRef.current || listState.busy) return;
    actionBusyRef.current = true;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setListState((p) => ({ ...p, busy: true }));
    try {
      const res = await adminCreateWebInviteCloud({
        label: form.label || 'Tester Privado Kinetic', ttlHours: form.ttl, maxUses: form.uses, targetPath: form.target, allowSandboxBeta: form.sandbox,
      });
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      if (!res.ok || !res.code || !res.url) {
        onRequestAlert?.({ visible: true, title: tx('Error al crear pase', 'Create Error'), message: res.error || 'ADMIN_ONLY' });
        return;
      }
      setCreated({ code: res.code, url: res.url });
      setForm((p) => ({ ...p, label: '' }));
      await loadRows();
    } finally {
      actionBusyRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setListState((p) => ({ ...p, busy: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = async (txt: string, label: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Success');
    await bovedaDataAndSettingsHelpers.copiarAlPortapapelesSilencioso(txt);
    onRequestAlert?.({ visible: true, title: tx('Copiado', 'Copied'), message: `${label}: ${txt}` });
  };

  const handleRevoke = async (id: string) => {
    if (actionBusyRef.current || revokingId || listState.busy) return;
    actionBusyRef.current = true;
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    setRevokingId(id);
    try {
      const ok = await adminRevokeWebInviteCloud(id);
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      if (!ok) {
        onRequestAlert?.({ visible: true, title: tx('Error al revocar', 'Revoke Error'), message: 'ADMIN_REVOKE_FAILED' });
        return;
      }
      await loadRows();
    } finally {
      actionBusyRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setRevokingId(null);
    }
  };

  const cardBg = isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0D131D';
  const borderCol = isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 12 }}>
      <View style={[s.card, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: borderCol }]}>
        <Text style={[s.heading, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Nuevo Acceso Web Privado', 'New Private Web Pass')}</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={[s.input, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, borderColor: borderCol, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F8FAFC' : '#05070B' }]}
          placeholder={tx('Etiqueta (ej. Coach Cabo / Beta Atleta)', 'Label (e.g. Coach Cabo / Beta Tester)')}
          placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
          value={form.label}
          onChangeText={(label) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, label }))}
        />
        <Text style={[s.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('Duración / Usos / Destino', 'TTL / Max Uses / Target')}</Text>
        <View style={s.row}>
          {TTL_OPTS.map((h) => (
            <TouchableOpacity key={h} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, ttl: h }))} style={[s.pill, form.ttl === h && s.pillOn]}>
              <Text style={[s.pillTxt, form.ttl === h && s.pillTxtOn]}>{h === 24 ? '24h' : h === 168 ? '7d' : '30d'}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
          {USE_OPTS.map((u) => (
            <TouchableOpacity key={u} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, uses: u }))} style={[s.pill, form.uses === u && s.pillOn]}>
              <Text style={[s.pillTxt, form.uses === u && s.pillTxtOn]}>{u}x</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </View>
        <View style={s.row}>
          {(['/login', '/socios'] as const).map((t) => (
            <TouchableOpacity key={t} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, target: t }))} style={[s.pill, form.target === t && s.pillOn]}>
              <Text style={[s.pillTxt, form.target === t && s.pillTxtOn]}>{t}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, marginLeft: 'auto' }}>
            <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' }}>Sandbox</Text>
            <Switch value={form.sandbox} onValueChange={(sandbox) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, sandbox }))} trackColor={{ true: '#00F0FF' }} />
          </View>
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[s.primaryBtn, listState.busy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} disabled={listState.busy} onPress={handleCreate}>
          <Feather name="key" size={14} color="#050505" />
          <Text style={s.primaryTxt}>{listState.busy ? '...' : tx('Generar Llave Única', 'Mint Private Key')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {created && (
        <View style={[s.card, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.08)', borderColor: '#00F0FF', elevation: 0 }]}>
          <Text style={{ color: '#00F0FF', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('LLAVE ACTIVA (COPIA UNICA)', 'ACTIVE KEY (COPY NOW)')}</Text>
          <Text selectable style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1.5 }}>{created.code}</Text>
          <View style={s.row}>
            <TouchableOpacity style={s.pillOn} onPress={() => handleCopy(created.url, tx('Link WhatsApp', 'Link'))}><Text style={s.pillTxtOn}>{tx('Copiar Link', 'Copy Link')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity style={s.pillOn} onPress={() => handleCopy(created.code, tx('Clave', 'Key'))}><Text style={s.pillTxtOn}>{tx('Copiar Clave', 'Copy Key')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      <View style={[s.card, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: borderCol }]}>
        <Text style={[s.heading, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('Invitaciones Emitidas', 'Issued Invitations')}</Text>
        {listState.loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#00F0FF" /> : listState.rows.map((r) => {
          const isRevoked = Boolean(r.revoked_at);
          const isExp = !isRevoked && Date.parse(r.expires_at) <= Date.now();
          const stTxt = isRevoked ? tx('REVOCADA', 'REVOKED') : isExp ? tx('EXPIRADA', 'EXPIRED') : tx('ACTIVA', 'ACTIVE');
          return (
            <View key={r.id} style={[s.itemRow, { borderColor: borderCol }]}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800' }}>{r.label}</Text>
                <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 11 }}>{r.code_hint} • {r.used_count}/{r.max_uses} • {r.target_path} • {stTxt}</Text>
              </View>
              {!isRevoked && !isExp && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[s.revokeBtn, Boolean(revokingId) && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
                  disabled={Boolean(revokingId)}
                  onPress={() => handleRevoke(r.id)}
                >
                  <Text style={{ color: '#FF3B30', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                    {revokingId === r.id ? '...' : tx('Revocar', 'Revoke')}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  card: { borderRadius: 16, borderWidth: 1, padding: 14, gap: 10, elevation: 0 },
  heading: { fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '900' },
  lbl: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' },
  input: { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 12, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 10, fontSize: 16 },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' },
  pill: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(148,163,184,0.3)' },
  pillOn: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: '#00F0FF', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#00F0FF' },
  pillTxt: { color: '#94A3B8', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' },
  pillTxtOn: { color: '#050505', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' },
  primaryBtn: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, backgroundColor: '#00F0FF', paddingVertical: 12, borderRadius: 12 },
  primaryTxt: { color: '#050505', fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: '900' },
  itemRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: 10, borderTopWidth: 1, gap: 8 },
  revokeBtn: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,59,48,0.45)', backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,59,48,0.1)', elevation: 0 },
});

    FILE: src/components/admin/AffiliateQuickActivateModal.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AffiliateQuickActivateModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { extractInstagramFromAffiliate, normalizeInstagramHandle } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { isStrictValidWhatsappInput } from '../../utils/kineticPhoneValidatorLeaf';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from '../shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../utils/kineticHapticsEngine';
import { baseSubStyles } from './AdminAffiliateSubModals.styles';

interface IQuickActivateAffiliate {
  nombre?: string | null;
  whatsapp?: string | null;
  instagram?: string | null;
  redes?: { instagram?: string | null } | null;
  email_contacto?: string | null;
}

export const AffiliateQuickActivateModal = memo(({
  visible, affiliate, isAdmin = true, isSubmitting = false, onConfirmActivate, onClose, inlineOverlay = false,
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  affiliate: IQuickActivateAffiliate | null;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  isSubmitting?: boolean;
  onConfirmActivate: (whatsapp: string, instagram: string) => Promise<void>;
  onClose: () => void;
  inlineOverlay?: boolean;
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const s = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSubStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const [waInput, setWaInput] = useState('');
  const [igInput, setIgInput] = useState('');
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const lastAffiliateRef = useRef<IQuickActivateAffiliate | null>(null);
  if (affiliate) lastAffiliateRef.current = affiliate;
  const displayAffiliate = affiliate || lastAffiliateRef.current;

  useEffect(() => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    if (visible && affiliate) {
      setWaInput(String(affiliate.whatsapp || '').replace(/\D/g, ''));
      setIgInput(extractInstagramFromAffiliate(affiliate));
      setErrorMsg(null);
    }
  }, [visible, affiliate]);

  const handleClose = () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    onClose();
  };

  const handleActivatePress = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    const digits = waInput.replace(/\D/g, '');
    const cleanIg = igInput.trim() ? normalizeInstagramHandle(igInput) : '';
    const igCore = cleanIg.replace(/^@+/, '').trim();
    const validWa = isStrictValidWhatsappInput(waInput, isAdmin);
    if (!validWa || (!isAdmin && igCore.length < 2)) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      setErrorMsg(isAdmin
        ? tx('Si capturas WhatsApp debe ser válido (sin dígitos repetidos o secuencias), o déjalo vacío.', 'If entering WhatsApp it must be valid (no repeated digits or sequences), or leave empty.')
        : tx('Captura un WhatsApp real (sin secuencias/repetidos) y el usuario oficial de Instagram (@cluboficial).', 'Enter a valid WhatsApp (no sequences/repeats) and official Instagram handle (@officialclub).'));
      return;
    }
    setErrorMsg(null);
    await onConfirmActivate(digits, cleanIg);
  };

  const modalBody = (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
      <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, s.sheetSurface]}>
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={handleClose} />
        <ScrollView keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" bounces={false} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
          <Text style={s.sheetTag}>{tx('PROTOCOLO DE ACTIVACIÓN EN RADAR GPS', 'GPS RADAR ACTIVATION PROTOCOL')}</Text>
          <Text style={s.sheetTitle}>{displayAffiliate?.nombre || tx('Operador B2B', 'B2B Operator')}</Text>

          <View style={s.reqNotice3D}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={16} color={isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FFAB00'} style={{ marginTop: 1 }} />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[s.reqNoticeTitle, { color: isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FFAB00' }]}>
                {isAdmin ? tx('ACTIVACIÓN DIRECTA DE ADMINISTRADOR', 'ADMIN 1-TAP ACTIVATION') : tx('REQUISITO OBLIGATORIO DE RADAR', 'MANDATORY RADAR REQUIREMENT')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={s.reqNoticeText}>
                {isAdmin
                  ? tx('Como Administrador puedes activar en 1 toque sin WhatsApp obligatorio, o registrarlo opcionalmente ahora.', 'As Admin you can activate in 1 tap without mandatory WhatsApp, or optionally save it now.')
                  : tx('Para publicar un prospecto oculto como ACTIVO EN RADAR GPS, registra su WhatsApp (10 dígitos) e Instagram oficial (@usuario).', 'To publish a hidden prospect as ACTIVE ON GPS RADAR, enter its 10-digit WhatsApp and official Instagram (@handle).')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <Text style={s.subLabel}>{isAdmin ? tx('WHATSAPP (10 DÍGITOS — OPCIONAL)', 'WHATSAPP (10 DIGITS — OPTIONAL)') : tx('WHATSAPP (10 DÍGITOS) *', 'WHATSAPP (10 DIGITS) *')}</Text>
          <TextInput style={[s.modalInput3D, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} placeholder={tx('Opcional — 10 dígitos', 'Optional — 10 digits')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} keyboardType="phone-pad" maxLength={15} value={waInput} onChangeText={setWaInput} />
          <Text style={s.subLabel}>{isAdmin ? tx('INSTAGRAM OFICIAL (OPCIONAL)', 'OFFICIAL INSTAGRAM (OPTIONAL)') : tx('INSTAGRAM OFICIAL *', 'OFFICIAL INSTAGRAM *')}</Text>
          <TextInput style={[s.modalInput3D, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} placeholder={tx('@cluboficial', '@officialclub')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect={false} value={igInput} onChangeText={setIgInput} />

          {Boolean(errorMsg) && (
            <View style={s.errorBox3D}>
              <Feather name="alert-triangle" size={14} color="#FF3B30" />
              <Text style={s.errorBoxText}>{errorMsg}</Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </ScrollView>
        <KineticErgonomicFooter
          onBack={handleClose}
          rightAction={{
            label: isSubmitting ? tx('ACTIVANDO...', 'ACTIVATING...') : tx('GUARDAR Y ACTIVAR', 'SAVE & ACTIVATE'),
            icon: 'zap',
            tone: 'green',
            loading: isSubmitting,
            onPress: handleActivatePress,
          }}
          style={{ marginHorizontal: -16 }}
        />
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    if (!visible) return null;
    const webFixedStyle: ViewStyle = Platform.OS === 'web'
      ? ({ position: 'fixed', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, width: '100vw', height: '100dvh', zIndex: 9940 } as unknown as ViewStyle)
      : { position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, zIndex: 9940 };
    const inlineWrap = (
      <View
        style={{
          ...webFixedStyle,
          backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)' : 'rgba(5, 7, 12, 0.92)',
          justifyContent: 'flex-end',
          elevation: 0,
        }}
      >
        <Pressable style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }} onPress={handleClose} />
        {modalBody}
      </View>
    );
    return Platform.OS === 'web' ? renderInWebPortal(inlineWrap) : inlineWrap;
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={visible} onBackdropPress={handleClose} onBackButtonPress={handleClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      avoidKeyboard
    >
      {modalBody}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/AdminModuleModal.tsx

    // === src/components/AdminModuleModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  InteractionManager,
  Keyboard,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView } from './admin/AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView';
import { AdminAuditLogsView } from './admin/AdminAuditLogsView';
import { AdminCatalogAndMetricsView } from './admin/AdminCatalogAndMetricsView';
import { AdminDispersionSpeiView } from './admin/AdminDispersionSpeiView';
import { AdminEscrowActiveView } from './admin/AdminEscrowActiveView';
import { AdminKinetiClubModerationView } from './admin/AdminKinetiClubModerationView';
import {
  AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView,
  AdminLiquidityView,
} from './AdminTreasuryViews';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from './shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import {
  KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
  KineticSwipeCloseHeader,
} from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import type { BovedaAlertPayload } from './boveda/BovedaTacticalAlertModal';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from './BovedaPassAndReceiptModals';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import { AdminWebPrivatePassesView } from './admin/AdminWebPrivatePassesView';

export type AdminModuleType =
  | null
  | 'LIQUIDEZ'
  | 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS'
  | 'SOCIOS'
  | 'ESCROW_ACTIVO'
  | 'DISPERSION_B2B'
  | 'SOLICITUDES'
  | 'EVENTOS'
  | 'ATLETAS'
  | 'TESORERIA'
  | 'AUDIT_LOGS'
  | 'ACCESOS_WEB'
  | 'KINETI_CLUB'
  | 'KINETICLUB';

const VIRTUALIZED_MODULES = new Set<AdminModuleType>([
  'DISPERSION_B2B',
  'SOCIOS',
  'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS',
  'AUDIT_LOGS',
]);

export const AdminModuleModal = React.memo(
  ({
    activeModule,
    hasPreviousModule = false,
    walletContextFilter = 'all',
    onStepBack,
    onCloseAll,
    onNavigateModule,
    tokenomicsReales,
    solicitudes = [],
    preRegistrosWeb = [],
    ugcPendingCount = 0,
    experienciasActivas = [],
    sociosReales = [],
    atletasList = [],
    reservasEscrowActivas = [],
    reservasHistoricas = [], auditLogs = [], distribucionNiveles = [], topDisciplinas = [],
    reporteCorteSemanal, cambiarEstadoAfiliado, crearAfiliadoManual, eliminarSocioB2B,
    canAdminDelete = true, togglingAffiliateName = null, isSavingManualAffiliate = false,
    isSubmitting = false, isLoading = false, cambiarEstadoServicio, gestionarPreRegistroWeb,
    guardarEvento, eliminarEvento, user, abrirEditorOnTheGo, inyectarKCAtleta, isInjectingKC = false,
    actualizarFiscalSocio, isUpdatingFiscal = false, exportarReporteMaestroPDF,
    exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF, isExportingPDF = false, copiarDatoBancario,
    eliminarCuentaAtleta, ejecutarBarridoNoShow40, isSweepingNoShow = false,
  }: {
    activeModule: AdminModuleType;
    isLoading?: boolean;
    hasPreviousModule?: boolean;
    walletContextFilter?: 'membresia' | 'topup' | 'all';
    onStepBack?: () => void;
    onCloseAll: () => void;
    onNavigateModule?: (mod: AdminModuleType) => void;
    tokenomicsReales?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminLiquidityView>['tokenomicsReales'];
    solicitudes?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['solicitudes'];
    preRegistrosWeb?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['preRegistrosWeb'];
    ugcPendingCount?: number;
    experienciasActivas?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['experienciasActivas'];
    sociosReales?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView>['sociosReales'] & React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['sociosReales'];
    atletasList?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView>['atletasList'];
    reservasEscrowActivas?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminEscrowActiveView>['reservasEscrowActivas'];
    reservasHistoricas?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['reservasHistoricas'];
    auditLogs?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAuditLogsView>['auditLogs'];
    distribucionNiveles?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['distribucionNiveles'];
    topDisciplinas?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['topDisciplinas'];
    reporteCorteSemanal?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['reporteCorteSemanal'];
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView>['cambiarEstadoAfiliado'];
    crearAfiliadoManual?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView>['crearAfiliadoManual'];
    eliminarSocioB2B?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView>['eliminarSocioB2B'];
    canAdminDelete?: boolean;
    togglingAffiliateName?: string | null;
    isSavingManualAffiliate?: boolean;
    isSubmitting?: boolean;
    cambiarEstadoServicio?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['cambiarEstadoServicio'];
    gestionarPreRegistroWeb?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['gestionarPreRegistroWeb'];
    guardarEvento?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['guardarEvento'];
    eliminarEvento?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['eliminarEvento'];
    user?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['user'];
    abrirEditorOnTheGo?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminCatalogAndMetricsView>['abrirEditorOnTheGo'];
    inyectarKCAtleta?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView>['inyectarKCAtleta'];
    isInjectingKC?: boolean;
    actualizarFiscalSocio?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['actualizarFiscalSocio'];
    isUpdatingFiscal?: boolean;
    exportarReporteMaestroPDF?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['exportarReporteMaestroPDF'];
    exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF'];
    isExportingPDF?: boolean;
    copiarDatoBancario?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminDispersionSpeiView>['copiarDatoBancario'];
    eliminarCuentaAtleta?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView>['eliminarCuentaAtleta'];
    ejecutarBarridoNoShow40?: React.ComponentProps<typeof AdminEscrowActiveView>['ejecutarBarridoNoShow40'];
    isSweepingNoShow?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const { isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);

    const [isKeyboardVisible, setIsKeyboardVisible] = useState(false);
    const [inlineAlertConfig, setInlineAlertConfig] = useState<BovedaAlertPayload>({ visible: false });
    const [moduleSubOverlay, setModuleSubOverlay] = useState<React.ReactNode>(null);
    const subOverlayBackHandlerRef = useRef<(() => boolean) | null>(null);
    const registerSubOverlayBackHandler = useCallback((fn: (() => boolean) | null) => {
      subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current = fn;
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      const subs = [
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardWillShow', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(true)),
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardDidShow', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(true)),
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardWillHide', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(false)),
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardDidHide', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(false)),
      ];
      const onWebFocusIn = (e: FocusEvent) => {
        const targetEl = e?.target as { tagName?: string } | null;
        const tag = String(targetEl?.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') setIsKeyboardVisible(true);
      };
      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
        document.addEventListener('focusin', onWebFocusIn);
      }
      return () => {
        subs.forEach((s) => s.remove());
        if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
          document.removeEventListener('focusin', onWebFocusIn);
        }
      };
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!activeModule) {
        setIsKeyboardVisible(false);
        setInlineAlertConfig({ visible: false });
        setModuleSubOverlay(null);
        subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current = null;
      }
    }, [activeModule]);

    const cachedModuleRef = useRef<AdminModuleType>('LIQUIDEZ');
    const resolvedInputModule: AdminModuleType =
      activeModule === 'TESORERIA' ? 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS' : activeModule;
    if (resolvedInputModule) {
      cachedModuleRef.current = resolvedInputModule;
    }
    const displayModule = resolvedInputModule || cachedModuleRef.current;
    const isVirtualizedModule = VIRTUALIZED_MODULES.has(displayModule);
    const [readyModule, setReadyModule] = useState<AdminModuleType>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!resolvedInputModule) return;
      if (!VIRTUALIZED_MODULES.has(resolvedInputModule)) {
        setReadyModule(resolvedInputModule);
        return;
      }
      let cancelled = false;
      const task = InteractionManager.runAfterInteractions(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setReadyModule(resolvedInputModule);
      });
      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
        task?.cancel?.();
      };
    }, [resolvedInputModule]);

    const canRenderVirtualized = !isVirtualizedModule || readyModule === displayModule;

    useEffect(() => {
      if (activeModule) {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
          scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
        }, 10);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }, [activeModule]);

    const closeHandler = onCloseAll || onStepBack;
    const handleStepBackLayer = () => {
      if (inlineAlertConfig?.visible) {
        setInlineAlertConfig((prev: BovedaAlertPayload) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
        return;
      }
      if (subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current?.()) {
        return;
      }
      (onStepBack || onCloseAll)?.();
    };

    const moduleBody = (
      <>
        {displayModule === 'LIQUIDEZ' && (
          <AdminLiquidityView
            tokenomicsReales={tokenomicsReales}
            reporteCorteSemanal={reporteCorteSemanal}
            onNavigateModule={onNavigateModule}
          />
        )}

        {(displayModule === 'TESORERIA' ||
          displayModule === 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS') && (
          <AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView
            mode={displayModule}
            atletasList={atletasList}
            walletContextFilter={walletContextFilter}
            inyectarKCAtleta={inyectarKCAtleta}
            isInjectingKC={isInjectingKC}
            onNavigateModule={onNavigateModule}
            eliminarCuentaAtleta={eliminarCuentaAtleta}
            canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'SOCIOS' && (
          <AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView
            sociosReales={sociosReales}
            cambiarEstadoAfiliado={cambiarEstadoAfiliado}
            crearAfiliadoManual={crearAfiliadoManual}
            eliminarSocioB2B={eliminarSocioB2B}
            canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
            togglingAffiliateName={togglingAffiliateName}
            isSavingManualAffiliate={isSavingManualAffiliate}
            isModuleActive={activeModule === 'SOCIOS'}
            onMountSubOverlay={setModuleSubOverlay}
            onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler={registerSubOverlayBackHandler}
            onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'ESCROW_ACTIVO' && (
          <AdminEscrowActiveView
            reservasEscrowActivas={reservasEscrowActivas}
            reservasHistoricas={reservasHistoricas}
            ejecutarBarridoNoShow40={ejecutarBarridoNoShow40}
            isSweepingNoShow={isSweepingNoShow}
            isLoading={isLoading}
            onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'DISPERSION_B2B' && (
          <AdminDispersionSpeiView
            reporteCorteSemanal={reporteCorteSemanal}
            sociosReales={sociosReales}
            reservasHistoricas={reservasHistoricas}
            actualizarFiscalSocio={actualizarFiscalSocio}
            isUpdatingFiscal={isUpdatingFiscal}
            exportarReporteMaestroPDF={exportarReporteMaestroPDF}
            exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF={exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF}
            isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF}
            copiarDatoBancario={copiarDatoBancario}
          />
        )}

        {(displayModule === 'SOLICITUDES' ||
          displayModule === 'EVENTOS' ||
          displayModule === 'ATLETAS') && (
          <AdminCatalogAndMetricsView
            mode={displayModule}
            solicitudes={solicitudes}
            preRegistrosWeb={preRegistrosWeb}
            experienciasActivas={experienciasActivas}
            distribucionNiveles={distribucionNiveles}
            topDisciplinas={topDisciplinas}
            cambiarEstadoServicio={cambiarEstadoServicio}
            gestionarPreRegistroWeb={gestionarPreRegistroWeb}
            copiarDatoBancario={copiarDatoBancario}
            guardarEvento={guardarEvento}
            eliminarEvento={eliminarEvento}
            user={user}
            isSubmitting={isSubmitting}
            abrirEditorOnTheGo={abrirEditorOnTheGo}
            onNavigateModule={onNavigateModule}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'AUDIT_LOGS' && (
          <AdminAuditLogsView auditLogs={auditLogs} />
        )}

        {(displayModule === 'KINETI_CLUB' || displayModule === 'KINETICLUB') && (
          <AdminKinetiClubModerationView ugcPendingCount={ugcPendingCount} onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig} />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'ACCESOS_WEB' && (
          <AdminWebPrivatePassesView onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig} />
        )}
      </>
    );

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(activeModule)}
        onBackdropPress={() => {
          if (inlineAlertConfig?.visible) {
            setInlineAlertConfig((prev: BovedaAlertPayload) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
            return;
          }
          if (subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current?.()) {
            return;
          }
          closeHandler?.();
        }}
        onBackButtonPress={() => {
          if (inlineAlertConfig?.visible) {
            setInlineAlertConfig((prev: BovedaAlertPayload) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
            return;
          }
          if (subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current?.()) {
            return;
          }
          (onStepBack || onCloseAll)?.();
        }}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
        backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.52 : 0.93}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        animationIn="slideInUp"
        animationOut="slideOutDown"
        animationInTiming={200}
        avoidKeyboard={true}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          pointerEvents="box-none"
          behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
          style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        >
          <View
            data-kinetic-sheet-open="true"
            pointerEvents={moduleSubOverlay ? 'none' : 'auto'}
            style={[
              globalStyles.bottomSheet,
              KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
              isLightMode && {
                backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderTopColor: '#0284C7',
              },
              {
                paddingBottom: 0,
                ...(moduleSubOverlay ? { elevation: 0 } : null),
              },
            ]}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader visible={Boolean(activeModule)} onClose={handleStepBackLayer} />

            {isVirtualizedModule ? (
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                {canRenderVirtualized ? (
                  moduleBody
                ) : (
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 48 }}>
                    <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            ) : (
              <ScrollView
                ref={scrollViewRef}
                style={{ flex: 1 }}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
                nestedScrollEnabled={true}
                bounces={false}
                overScrollMode="never"
                contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
              >
                {moduleBody}
              </ScrollView>
            )}

            {!isKeyboardVisible && (
              <KineticErgonomicFooter
                onBack={onStepBack || onCloseAll}
                backLabel={hasPreviousModule ? tx('← ATRÁS', '← BACK') : undefined}
                style={{ marginHorizontal: -16 }}
              />
            )}
          </View>
          {moduleSubOverlay}
          <TacticalAlertModal inlineOverlay alertConfig={inlineAlertConfig} setAlertConfig={setInlineAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);

export default AdminModuleModal;

export const localizeVipTierLabel = (rawTier: string | undefined, isEn: boolean): string => {
  const found = VIP_GEM_CONFIGS.find(
    (g) => g.dbValue.toLowerCase() === String(rawTier || '').trim().toLowerCase()
  );
  if (found) return isEn ? found.labelEn : found.labelEs;
  return rawTier || (isEn ? 'Base Access' : 'Acceso Base');
};

    // === src/components/AdminTreasuryViews.tsx === //
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc } from '../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { AdminAthletesDirectoryView } from './admin/AdminAthletesDirectoryView';
import { AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel } from './admin/AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel';
import { baseStyles } from './AdminTreasuryViews.styles';

export { AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel };

// ============================================================================
// 1. MÓDULO: RADIOGRAFÍA FINANCIERA (LIQUIDEZ — ARQUEO CONTABLE LIMPIO)
// ============================================================================
export const AdminLiquidityView = memo(
  ({
    tokenomicsReales,
    reporteCorteSemanal,
  }: {
    tokenomicsReales: any;
    reporteCorteSemanal?: any;
    onNavigateModule?: (mod: any, filter?: any) => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [rpcMasterPasses, setRpcMasterPasses] = useState<{ dia: number; semana: number; mes: number }>({
      dia: 0,
      semana: 0,
      mes: 0,
    });

    useEffect(() => {
      let cancelled = false;
      fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc(null).then((res) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setRpcMasterPasses({ dia: res.dia, semana: res.semana, mes: res.mes });
        }
      });
      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
      };
    }, []);

    const pasesDia = Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.dia, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_pases_maestros_dia) || 0);
    const pasesSemana = Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.semana, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_pases_maestros_semana) || 0);
    const pasesMes = Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.mes, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_pases_maestros_mes) || 0);

    const totalRaw = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.masaMonetariaTotal) || 0);
    const totalDivisor = Math.max(1, totalRaw);
    const mem = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.sumaMembresia) || 0);
    const top = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.sumaTopup) || 0);
    const esc = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.escrowTotal) || 0);
    const bookingsCount = tokenomicsReales?.reservasActivasCount || 0;
    const memPct = Math.round((mem / totalDivisor) * 100);
    const topPct = Math.round((top / totalDivisor) * 100);
    const escPct = Math.round((esc / totalDivisor) * 100);

    return (
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 18, paddingTop: 4 }}>
        <Text style={[styles.moduleTitle, { lineHeight: 26, paddingTop: 2 }]}>
          {tx('Radiografía Financiera', 'Financial X-Ray')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.moduleSub}>
          {tx(
            'Arqueo contable del circulante Kineticoins respaldado en billeteras y custodia activa.',

export const AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView = memo(
  ({
    atletasList = [],
    walletContextFilter = 'all',
    inyectarKCAtleta,
    isInjectingKC = false,
    onNavigateModule,
    eliminarCuentaAtleta,
    canAdminDelete = false,
  }: {
    mode?: 'TESORERIA' | 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS';
    atletasList: any[];
    walletContextFilter?: 'all' | 'membresia' | 'topup';
    inyectarKCAtleta: any;
    isInjectingKC: boolean;
    onNavigateModule?: (mod: any, filter?: any) => void;
    eliminarCuentaAtleta?: (atleta: any) => void;
    canAdminDelete?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

    return (
      <AdminAthletesDirectoryView
        atletasList={atletasList}
        walletContextFilter={walletContextFilter}
        styles={styles}

        isInjectingKC={isInjectingKC}
        eliminarCuentaAtleta={eliminarCuentaAtleta}
        canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
      />
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/partner-web/PartnerWebAdminDirectoryView.tsx

    // === src/components/partner-web/PartnerWebAdminDirectoryView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { maskClabeAccount } from '../../services/kineticSecurityMfaService';
import {
  buildSpeiBatchToastSummary,
  createAdminDirectoryAffiliate,
  exportAdminCeoPdfReport,
  exportAdminMassiveSpeiCsv,
  toggleAdminDirectoryAffiliateState,
} from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebDataService';
import { filterAdminAffiliatesDirectory, sanitizePartnerTextAntiXss } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebGuards';
import type { AdminDirectoryFilterKey, IPartnerWebAffiliateRow } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebTypes';
import type { IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../../types';
import { PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, type IPartnerWebPalette, partnerWebSharedStyles } from './partnerWebThemeStyles';

interface PartnerWebAdminDirectoryViewProps {
  pal: IPartnerWebPalette;
  affiliates: IPartnerWebAffiliateRow[];
  reporteCorteSemanal: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO | null;
  loading: boolean;
  supervisedAffiliateId?: string | null;
  onSelectSupervisedAffiliate: (af: IPartnerWebAffiliateRow) => void;
  onRefreshDirectory: () => Promise<void>;
}

const formatMxnInline = (n: number): string =>
  `$${(Number(n) || 0).toLocaleString('es-MX', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}`;

export function PartnerWebAdminDirectoryView({
  pal,
  affiliates,
  reporteCorteSemanal,
  loading,
  supervisedAffiliateId,
  onSelectSupervisedAffiliate,
  onRefreshDirectory,
}: PartnerWebAdminDirectoryViewProps) {
  const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterKey, setFilterKey] = useState<AdminDirectoryFilterKey>('all');
  const [createDraft, setCreateDraft] = useState({
    open: false,
    nombre: '',
    disciplina: 'Fuerza',
    ubicacion: 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.',
    esEspecialista: false,
  });
  const [actionBusyId, setActionBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [exportBusy, setExportBusy] = useState<'csv' | 'pdf' | null>(null);
  const [bannerMsg, setBannerMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const actionBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const exportBusyRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => { mountedRef.current = false; };
  }, []);

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => filterAdminAffiliatesDirectory(affiliates, searchQuery, filterKey),
    [affiliates, searchQuery, filterKey]
  );

  const handleExportSpeiCsv = async () => {
    if (!reporteCorteSemanal || exportBusyRef.current || exportBusy) return;
    exportBusyRef.current = true;
    setExportBusy('csv');
    setBannerMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await exportAdminMassiveSpeiCsv(reporteCorteSemanal);
      if (mountedRef.current) setBannerMsg({ ok: res.exported, text: buildSpeiBatchToastSummary(res, formatMxnInline, tx) });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (mountedRef.current) setBannerMsg({ ok: false, text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('Error exportando Layout SPEI .CSV.', 'SPEI .CSV export failed.') });
    } finally {
      exportBusyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setExportBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const handleExportCeoPdf = async () => {
    if (!reporteCorteSemanal || exportBusyRef.current || exportBusy) return;
    exportBusyRef.current = true;
    setExportBusy('pdf');
    setBannerMsg(null);
    try {
      await exportAdminCeoPdfReport(reporteCorteSemanal);
      if (mountedRef.current) setBannerMsg({ ok: true, text: tx('Reporte Maestro CEO PDF A4 generado.', 'CEO Master A4 PDF Report generated.') });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (mountedRef.current) setBannerMsg({ ok: false, text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('Error generando Reporte CEO.', 'Failed generating CEO Report.') });
    } finally {
      exportBusyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setExportBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const handleCreateAffiliate = async () => {
    const cleanName = sanitizePartnerTextAntiXss(createDraft.nombre, 120, true);
    if (cleanName.length < 2 || actionBusyRef.current || actionBusyId) return;
    actionBusyRef.current = true;
    setActionBusyId('__create__');
    setBannerMsg(null);
    try {
      await createAdminDirectoryAffiliate({
        nombre: cleanName,
        disciplina: sanitizePartnerTextAntiXss(createDraft.disciplina, 60, true) || 'Fuerza',
        ubicacion: sanitizePartnerTextAntiXss(createDraft.ubicacion, 240, true) || 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.',
        es_especialista: createDraft.esEspecialista,
        activo: true,
      });
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setCreateDraft((p) => ({ ...p, nombre: '', open: false }));
        setBannerMsg({ ok: true, text: tx('Afiliado creado y activado en la red Kinetic.', 'Affiliate created and activated in Kinetic network.') });
      }
      await onRefreshDirectory();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (mountedRef.current) setBannerMsg({ ok: false, text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('No fue posible crear el afiliado.', 'Failed creating affiliate.') });
    } finally {
      actionBusyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setActionBusyId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleEstado = async (af: IPartnerWebAffiliateRow) => {
    const rowBusyKey = String(af.id || af.nombre || '').trim();
    if (!rowBusyKey || actionBusyRef.current || actionBusyId) return;
    actionBusyRef.current = true;
    const currentlyActive = String(af.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'activo';
    const nextState: 'activo' | 'prospecto' = currentlyActive ? 'prospecto' : 'activo';
    setActionBusyId(rowBusyKey);
    setBannerMsg(null);
    try {
      await toggleAdminDirectoryAffiliateState(af, nextState);
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setBannerMsg({
          ok: true,
          text: currentlyActive ? tx(`Sede pausada a Prospecto: ${af.nombre}`, `Venue paused to Prospect: ${af.nombre}`) : tx(`Sede activada: ${af.nombre}`, `Venue activated: ${af.nombre}`),
        });
      }
      await onRefreshDirectory();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (mountedRef.current) setBannerMsg({ ok: false, text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : tx('No fue posible actualizar la sede.', 'Could not update venue.') });
    } finally {
      actionBusyRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setActionBusyId(null);
    }
  };

  const filterPills: Array<{ key: AdminDirectoryFilterKey; label: string }> = [
    { key: 'all', label: `${tx('Todos', 'All')} (${affiliates.length})` },
    { key: 'active', label: tx('Activos', 'Active') },
    { key: 'pending', label: tx('Pendientes/Prospectos', 'Pending/Prospects') },
    { key: 'clubs', label: tx('Clubes', 'Clubs') },
    { key: 'specialists', label: tx('Especialistas', 'Specialists') },
  ];

  return (
    <View testID="partner-web-admin-directory" style={{ gap: 18 }}>
      <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, borderTopColor: pal.purple, borderBottomColor: pal.borderBottom3D, gap: 14 }]}>
        <View style={s.headerRow}>
          <View>
            <Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('Directorio Global de Afiliados & Tesorería SPEI', 'Global Affiliates Directory & SPEI Treasury')}</Text>
            <Text style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 13 }}>
              {tx('Supervisa cualquier Club o Especialista, activa o pausa sedes y exporta layouts bancarios SPEI.', 'Supervise any Club or Specialist, activate or pause venues, and export massive SPEI bank layouts.')}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={s.wrapRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity testID="admin-export-spei-csv-btn" disabled={Boolean(exportBusy) || !reporteCorteSemanal} onPress={handleExportSpeiCsv} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryCyan, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE]}>
              <Feather name="file-text" size={15} color="#050505" />
              <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900' }}>{exportBusy === 'csv' ? tx('Generando...', 'Exporting...') : tx('Layout SPEI Masivo .CSV', 'Massive SPEI .CSV')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity testID="admin-export-ceo-pdf-btn" disabled={Boolean(exportBusy) || !reporteCorteSemanal} onPress={handleExportCeoPdf} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle }]}>
              <Feather name="download" size={15} color={pal.cyanText} />
              <Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800' }}>{exportBusy === 'pdf' ? tx('Generando...', 'Exporting...') : tx('Reporte Maestro CEO PDF A4', 'CEO Master PDF A4')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity testID="admin-toggle-create-affiliate-btn" onPress={() => setCreateDraft((p) => ({ ...p, open: !p.open }))} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryPurple, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE]}>
              <Feather name="plus" size={15} color="#FFFFFF" />
              <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('Nuevo Afiliado', 'New Affiliate')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        <TextInput
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          placeholder={tx('Buscar por nombre, disciplina, ubicación o correo...', 'Search by name, discipline, location or email...')}
          placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted}
          style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { fontSize: 16, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, color: pal.textPrimary }]}
        />

        <View style={s.wrapRow}>
          {filterPills.map((fp) => {
            const sel = filterKey === fp.key;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={fp.key}
                onPress={() => setFilterKey(fp.key)}
                style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { backgroundColor: sel ? pal.translucentPurpleBg : pal.recessedBg, borderColor: sel ? pal.purple : pal.borderSubtle, paddingVertical: 7 }]}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: sel ? pal.textPrimary : pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' }}>{fp.label}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {bannerMsg && (
          <View style={[s.bannerBox, { borderColor: bannerMsg.ok ? pal.greenText : pal.redText, backgroundColor: bannerMsg.ok ? pal.translucentCyanBg : pal.translucentPurpleBg }]}>
            <Text style={{ color: bannerMsg.ok ? pal.greenText : pal.redText, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '700' }}>{bannerMsg.text}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        {createDraft.open && (
          <View style={[s.createBox, { backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, borderColor: pal.purple }]}>
            <Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Alta Rápida de Sede / Especialista', 'Quick Affiliate Creation')}</Text>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
              <TextInput value={createDraft.nombre} onChangeText={(v) => setCreateDraft((p) => ({ ...p, nombre: v }))} placeholder={tx('Nombre comercial', 'Commercial name')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { flex: 1, minWidth: 220, fontSize: 16, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, color: pal.textPrimary }]} />
              <TextInput value={createDraft.disciplina} onChangeText={(v) => setCreateDraft((p) => ({ ...p, disciplina: v }))} placeholder={tx('Disciplina', 'Discipline')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { flex: 1, minWidth: 180, fontSize: 16, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, color: pal.textPrimary }]} />
              <TextInput value={createDraft.ubicacion} onChangeText={(v) => setCreateDraft((p) => ({ ...p, ubicacion: v }))} placeholder={tx('Ubicación', 'Location')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { flex: 1, minWidth: 220, fontSize: 16, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, color: pal.textPrimary }]} />
            </View>
            <View style={s.headerRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setCreateDraft((p) => ({ ...p, esEspecialista: !p.esEspecialista }))} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '700' }}>{createDraft.esEspecialista ? tx('Tipo: Especialista Independiente', 'Type: Specialist') : tx('Tipo: Club / Estudio', 'Type: Club / Studio')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity disabled={actionBusyId === '__create__' || !createDraft.nombre.trim()} onPress={handleCreateAffiliate} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryCyan, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE]}>
                <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900' }}>{actionBusyId === '__create__' ? tx('Guardando...', 'Saving...') : tx('Crear y Activar', 'Create & Activate')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>

      {loading && affiliates.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={{ paddingVertical: 40, alignItems: 'center' }}><ActivityIndicator size="large" color={pal.cyanText} /></View>
      ) : (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 14 }}>
          {filtered.map((af) => {
            const isActive = String(af.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'activo';
            const isSup = supervisedAffiliateId === af.id;
            const isSpec = Boolean(af.es_especialista) || String(af.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes('especialista');
            return (
              <View
                key={af.id || af.nombre}
                style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, s.dirCard, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: isSup ? pal.amberText : pal.borderSubtle, borderTopColor: isSup ? pal.amberText : pal.borderTop3D, borderBottomColor: pal.borderBottom3D }]}
              >
                <View style={s.cardTop}>
                  <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '900', flex: 1 }}>{af.nombre}</Text>
                  <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.translucentPill, { backgroundColor: isActive ? pal.translucentCyanBg : pal.translucentAmberBg, borderColor: isActive ? pal.greenText : pal.amberText }]}>
                    <Text style={{ color: isActive ? pal.greenText : pal.amberText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{isActive ? tx('ACTIVO', 'ACTIVE') : String(af.estado || 'PROSPECTO').toUpperCase()}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 13 }}>{isSpec ? tx('Especialista', 'Specialist') : tx('Club', 'Club')} • {af.disciplina || 'Fitness'}</Text>
                <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 12 }}>{af.ubicacion || tx('Ubicación pendiente', 'Location pending')}</Text>
                <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 12 }}>
                  CLABE: {af.clabe_bancaria ? maskClabeAccount(af.clabe_bancaria) : tx('Sin CLABE', 'No CLABE')} • RFC: {af.rfc || tx('Sin RFC', 'No RFC')}
                </Text>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 4 }}>
                  <TouchableOpacity disabled={actionBusyId === (af.id || af.nombre)} onPress={() => handleToggleEstado(af)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle }]}>
                    <Text style={{ color: isActive ? pal.amberText : pal.greenText, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' }}>{isActive ? tx('Pausar', 'Pause') : tx('Activar', 'Activate')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity testID={`admin-manage-venue-${af.id}`} onPress={() => onSelectSupervisedAffiliate(af)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryCyan, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { flex: 2, paddingVertical: 10 }]}>
                    <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('Administrar Sede →', 'Manage Venue →')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  headerRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 },
  wrapRow: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 },
  bannerBox: { borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1, padding: 12, elevation: 0 },

});
