import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { useFocusEffect } from 'expo-router';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import { adminService, isAdminOrB2BAccount } from '../services/adminService';
import { isSandboxAccountOrReservation } from '../services/adminB2BCutEngine';
import { invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache } from '../services/adminDashboardFetcher';
import { isExplicitKineticSignOut, registerSessionCleanupHook } from '../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import {
  getAdminHubSwrSync,
  hydrateAdminHubSwrEncrypted,
  setAdminHubSwrSync,
  type SwrRecord,
} from '../services/kineticSwrMemoryHub';
import { IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../types';
import { createDebouncedEventRunner, subscribeKineticRadarUpdated } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { useAdminActions } from './useAdminActions';

const ADMIN_CACHE_KEY = '@kinetic_admin_hub_cache_v3';
const LEGACY_ADMIN_CACHE_KEYS = [
  '@kinetic_admin_hub_cache_v1',
  '@kinetic_admin_hub_cache_v2',
];
const ADMIN_EGRESS_TTL_MS = 120_000;
const ONE_HOUR_AUTO_REFRESH_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
type AdminRow = Record<string, unknown>;
type AdminKpisShape = {
  masaMonetariaTotal: number; sumaMembresia: number; sumaTopup: number; saldoDisponibleUsuarios: number;
  escrowTotal: number; reservasActivasCount: number; atletasCount: number; clubesTotales: number;
  clubesActivos: number; clubesProspectos: number; liquidezTotalCapturada: number; comisionKinetic20: number;
  ticketPromedio: number; checkInsTotales: number; tasaConversionQR: number; [k: string]: unknown;
};
type AdminSnapShape = {
  kpis?: AdminKpisShape; solicitudes?: AdminRow[]; preRegistrosWeb?: AdminRow[]; ugcPendingCount?: number;
  experienciasActivas?: AdminRow[]; sociosReales?: AdminRow[]; atletasList?: AdminRow[];
  reservasEscrowActivas?: AdminRow[]; reservasHistoricas?: AdminRow[]; auditLogs?: AdminRow[];
  distribucionNiveles?: AdminRow[]; topDisciplinas?: AdminRow[]; reporteCorteSemanal?: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO | null;
  [k: string]: unknown;
};
const ZERO_ADMIN_KPIS: AdminKpisShape = {
  masaMonetariaTotal: 0, sumaMembresia: 0, sumaTopup: 0, saldoDisponibleUsuarios: 0,
  escrowTotal: 0, reservasActivasCount: 0, atletasCount: 0, clubesTotales: 0,
  clubesActivos: 0, clubesProspectos: 0, liquidezTotalCapturada: 0, comisionKinetic20: 0,
  ticketPromedio: 0, checkInsTotales: 0, tasaConversionQR: 0,
};
let memoryAdminCache: AdminSnapShape | null = null;
let memoryAdminCacheUid: string | null = null;
let memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;

registerSessionCleanupHook(() => {
  memoryAdminCache = null;
  memoryAdminCacheUid = null;
  memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;
  if (typeof AsyncStorage?.getAllKeys === 'function' && typeof AsyncStorage?.multiRemove === 'function') {
    AsyncStorage.getAllKeys()
      .then((keys) => {
        const targets = (keys || []).filter(
          (k) => k.startsWith(ADMIN_CACHE_KEY) || LEGACY_ADMIN_CACHE_KEYS.includes(k)
        );
        if (targets.length > 0) return AsyncStorage.multiRemove(targets);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }
});

const formatTimeHHMM = (d: Date): string => {
  const hh = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
  const mm = String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${hh}:${mm}`;
};

export function useAdmin(
  showTacticalAlert?: (
    title: string,
    message: string,
    type?: string,
    onConfirm?: (() => void) | null
  ) => void,
  options?: { enabled?: boolean; userId?: string | null }
) {
  const adminEnabled = options?.enabled !== false && Boolean(options?.userId);
  const adminUid = String(options?.userId || '').trim();
  const hubCached = adminEnabled && !memoryAdminCache ? (getAdminHubSwrSync(adminUid) as AdminSnapShape | null) : null;
  if (hubCached && adminUid) {
    memoryAdminCache = hubCached;
    memoryAdminCacheUid = adminUid;
  }
  const hasScopedCache = adminEnabled && memoryAdminCacheUid === adminUid ? memoryAdminCache : null;
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [hasLoadedInitial, setHasLoadedInitial] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [hasHydratedAdminKpis, setHasHydratedAdminKpis] = useState<boolean>(() => Boolean(hasScopedCache));
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [lastUpdatedLabel, setLastUpdatedLabel] = useState<string>(() =>
    formatTimeHHMM(new Date())
  );
  const [kpis, setKpis] = useState<AdminKpisShape>(
    hasScopedCache?.kpis || {
      masaMonetariaTotal: 0,
      sumaMembresia: 0,
      sumaTopup: 0,
      saldoDisponibleUsuarios: 0,
      escrowTotal: 0,
      reservasActivasCount: 0,
      atletasCount: 0,
      clubesTotales: 0,
      clubesActivos: 0,
      clubesProspectos: 0,
      liquidezTotalCapturada: 0,
      comisionKinetic20: 0,
      ticketPromedio: 0,
      checkInsTotales: 0,
      tasaConversionQR: 0,
    }
  );
  const [solicitudes, setSolicitudes] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.solicitudes || []);
  const [preRegistrosWeb, setPreRegistrosWeb] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.preRegistrosWeb || []);
  const [ugcPendingCount, setUgcPendingCount] = useState<number>(Number(hasScopedCache?.ugcPendingCount) || 0);
  const [experienciasActivas, setExperienciasActivas] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.experienciasActivas || []);
  const [sociosReales, setSociosReales] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.sociosReales || []);
  const [atletasList, setAtletasList] = useState<AdminRow[]>(
    (hasScopedCache?.atletasList || []).filter((a) => !isAdminOrB2BAccount(a) && !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(a, a))
  );
  const [reservasEscrowActivas, setReservasEscrowActivas] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.reservasEscrowActivas || []);
  const [reservasHistoricas, setReservasHistoricas] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.reservasHistoricas || []);
  const [auditLogs, setAuditLogs] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.auditLogs || []);
  const [distribucionNiveles, setDistribucionNiveles] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.distribucionNiveles || []);
  const [topDisciplinas, setTopDisciplinas] = useState<AdminRow[]>(hasScopedCache?.topDisciplinas || []);
  const [reporteCorteSemanal, setReporteCorteSemanal] =
    useState<IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO | null>(hasScopedCache?.reporteCorteSemanal || null);

  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const isFetchingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const hasLoadedInitialRef = useRef<boolean>(Boolean(hasScopedCache));
  const lastValidDataRef = useRef<AdminSnapShape | null>(hasScopedCache);
  const prevAdminUidRef = useRef<string>(adminUid);
  const retryTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const applyDashboardSnapshot = useCallback((snapshot: AdminSnapShape | null | undefined) => {
    if (!snapshot || !isMountedRef.current) return;
    const cleanAthletes = Array.isArray(snapshot.atletasList)
      ? snapshot.atletasList.filter((a) => !isAdminOrB2BAccount(a) && !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(a, a))
      : null;
    if (snapshot.kpis) {
      setKpis({
        ...snapshot.kpis,
        ...(cleanAthletes ? { atletasCount: cleanAthletes.length } : {}),
      });
    }
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.solicitudes)) setSolicitudes(snapshot.solicitudes);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.preRegistrosWeb)) setPreRegistrosWeb(snapshot.preRegistrosWeb);
    if (typeof snapshot.ugcPendingCount === 'number') setUgcPendingCount(Math.max(0, snapshot.ugcPendingCount));
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.experienciasActivas)) setExperienciasActivas(snapshot.experienciasActivas);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.sociosReales)) setSociosReales(snapshot.sociosReales);
    if (cleanAthletes) setAtletasList(cleanAthletes);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.reservasEscrowActivas)) setReservasEscrowActivas(snapshot.reservasEscrowActivas);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.reservasHistoricas)) setReservasHistoricas(snapshot.reservasHistoricas);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.auditLogs)) setAuditLogs(snapshot.auditLogs);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.distribucionNiveles)) setDistribucionNiveles(snapshot.distribucionNiveles);
    if (Array.isArray(snapshot.topDisciplinas)) setTopDisciplinas(snapshot.topDisciplinas);
    if (snapshot.reporteCorteSemanal) setReporteCorteSemanal(snapshot.reporteCorteSemanal);
    setHasHydratedAdminKpis(true);
    setLastUpdatedLabel(formatTimeHHMM(new Date()));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (prevAdminUidRef.current === adminUid) return;
    prevAdminUidRef.current = adminUid;
    isFetchingRef.current = false;
    pendingForceRefreshRef.current = false;
    const hubHit = adminEnabled ? (getAdminHubSwrSync(adminUid) as AdminSnapShape | null) : null;
    if (hubHit) {
      memoryAdminCache = hubHit;
      memoryAdminCacheUid = adminUid;
    }
    const scoped = adminEnabled && memoryAdminCacheUid === adminUid ? memoryAdminCache : null;
    lastValidDataRef.current = scoped;
    hasLoadedInitialRef.current = Boolean(scoped);
    if (scoped) {
      applyDashboardSnapshot(scoped);
      return;
    }
    memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;
    setHasHydratedAdminKpis(false);
    setKpis(ZERO_ADMIN_KPIS);
    setSolicitudes([]); setPreRegistrosWeb([]); setUgcPendingCount(0);
    setExperienciasActivas([]); setSociosReales([]); setAtletasList([]);
    setReservasEscrowActivas([]); setReservasHistoricas([]); setAuditLogs([]);
    setDistribucionNiveles([]); setTopDisciplinas([]); setReporteCorteSemanal(null);
    if (adminEnabled && adminUid) {
      void hydrateAdminHubSwrEncrypted(adminUid).then((hydrated) => {
        if (!isMountedRef.current || prevAdminUidRef.current !== adminUid || !hydrated) return;
        const snap = hydrated as AdminSnapShape;
        memoryAdminCache = snap;
        memoryAdminCacheUid = adminUid;
        lastValidDataRef.current = snap;
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        applyDashboardSnapshot(snap);
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [adminEnabled, adminUid, applyDashboardSnapshot]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      if (retryTimerRef.current) { clearTimeout(retryTimerRef.current); retryTimerRef.current = null; }
    };
  }, []);

  const pendingForceRefreshRef = useRef(false);

  const cargarDatos = useCallback(
    async (_isSilent = false, force = false) => {
      const activeAdminUid = adminUid;
      if (!adminEnabled || !activeAdminUid || isExplicitKineticSignOut()) return;
      if (isFetchingRef.current) {
        if (force) pendingForceRefreshRef.current = true;
        return;
      }
      const scopedMem =
        (memoryAdminCacheUid === activeAdminUid ? memoryAdminCache : null) ||
        (getAdminHubSwrSync(activeAdminUid) as AdminSnapShape | null);
      const hasValidAthletes = Boolean((scopedMem?.atletasList?.length ?? 0) > 0 || (lastValidDataRef.current?.atletasList?.length ?? 0) > 0);
      const hasCached = Boolean(hasLoadedInitialRef.current || scopedMem || lastValidDataRef.current) && hasValidAthletes;
      if (!force && hasCached && Date.now() - memoryAdminLastFetchedAt < ADMIN_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setHasHydratedAdminKpis(true);
          setHasLoadedInitial((p) => (p ? p : true));
          setIsLoading((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setIsSyncing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        }
        return;
      }
      isFetchingRef.current = true;
      if (isMountedRef.current && !_isSilent && !hasCached) {
        setIsSyncing(true);
      }
      const scopedCacheKey = `${ADMIN_CACHE_KEY}_${activeAdminUid}`;
      try {
        if (force) {
          memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;
          invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
        }
        const freshData = (await adminService.fetchDashboardData(force)) as unknown as AdminSnapShape;
        if (!isMountedRef.current || isExplicitKineticSignOut() || prevAdminUidRef.current !== activeAdminUid) return;
        memoryAdminCache = freshData;
        memoryAdminCacheUid = activeAdminUid;
        lastValidDataRef.current = freshData;
        memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = Date.now();
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        setAdminHubSwrSync(activeAdminUid, freshData as SwrRecord);
        applyDashboardSnapshot(freshData);
        AsyncStorage.multiRemove([ADMIN_CACHE_KEY, ...LEGACY_ADMIN_CACHE_KEYS, scopedCacheKey]).catch((err: unknown) => { void err; });
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
        isFetchingRef.current = false;
        hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
        if (pendingForceRefreshRef.current && isMountedRef.current) {
          pendingForceRefreshRef.current = false;
          if (retryTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(retryTimerRef.current);
          retryTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (isMountedRef.current) cargarDatos(true, true); }, 10);
        } else if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setHasHydratedAdminKpis(true);
          setHasLoadedInitial((p) => (p ? p : true));
          setIsLoading((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
          setIsSyncing((p) => (p ? false : p));
        }
      }
    },
    [adminEnabled, adminUid, applyDashboardSnapshot]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!adminEnabled || !adminUid) {
      memoryAdminCache = null;
      memoryAdminCacheUid = null;
      memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;
      lastValidDataRef.current = null;
      setHasHydratedAdminKpis(false);
      AsyncStorage.getAllKeys()
        .then((keys) => {
          const targets = (keys || []).filter(
            (k) => k.startsWith(ADMIN_CACHE_KEY) || LEGACY_ADMIN_CACHE_KEYS.includes(k)
          );
          if (targets.length > 0) return AsyncStorage.multiRemove(targets);
        })
        .catch(() => {});
      return;
    }
    cargarDatos(true, false);
    const hourlyInterval = setInterval(() => {
      if (isMountedRef.current && (typeof document === 'undefined' || !document.hidden)) {
        cargarDatos(true, true);
      }
    }, ONE_HOUR_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
    const debouncedMutate = createDebouncedEventRunner(() => {
      memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;
      cargarDatos(true, true);
    }, 150);
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(debouncedMutate.trigger);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function') {
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
    }
    return () => {
      clearInterval(hourlyInterval);
      debouncedMutate.cancel();
      unsubRadar();
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.removeEventListener === 'function') {
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedMutate.trigger);
      }
    };
  }, [cargarDatos]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      cargarDatos(true, false);
    }, [cargarDatos])
  );

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setRefreshing(true);
    setIsSyncing(true);
    memoryAdminLastFetchedAt = 0;
    await cargarDatos(true, true);
  }, [cargarDatos]);

  const actions = useAdminActions({
    isMountedRef,
    cargarDatos,
    setSociosReales,
    setPreRegistrosWeb,
    setAtletasList,
    setKpis,
    setExperienciasActivas,
    setReservasEscrowActivas,
    sociosReales,
    atletasList,
    reservasEscrowActivas,
    reservasHistoricas,
    reporteCorteSemanal,
    showTacticalAlert,
  } as unknown as Parameters<typeof useAdminActions>[0]);

  const preRegistrosPendientesCount = preRegistrosWeb.filter(
    (item) => (item?.estado || item?.estadoRevision) === 'pendiente'
  ).length;

  return {
    isLoading,
    hasLoadedInitial,
    hasHydratedAdminKpis,
    refreshing,
    isSyncing,
    lastUpdatedLabel,
    kpis,
    solicitudes,
    preRegistrosWeb,
    preRegistrosPendientesCount,
    ugcPendingCount,
    experienciasActivas,
    sociosReales,
    atletasList,
    reservasEscrowActivas,
    reservasHistoricas,
    auditLogs,
    distribucionNiveles,
    topDisciplinas,
    reporteCorteSemanal,
    cargarDatos,
    onRefresh,
    ...actions,
  };
}

export default useAdmin;

    // === src/hooks/useAdminActions.ts === //
import { MutableRefObject, useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import {
  adminPick as pick, buildAffiliateToggleAlert, buildDeleteEventAlert, buildManualAffiliateAlert,
  buildPreRegistroAlert, buildSaveEventAlert, buildServiceModerationAlert, buildTreasuryMutationAlert,
  computeTreasuryPatchedAthletesAndKpis, patchAffiliateStatusInList, patchPreRegistroDecisionInList,
  removeAffiliateFromList, resolveTargetB2BCorteReport,
} from '../services/admin/adminScreenHelpers';
import { adminService } from '../services/adminService';
import { computeOptimisticNoShowState, executeNoShow40Sweep } from '../services/kineticNoShowEngine';
import { ICorteSemanalAfiliado, IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO, RegimenFiscalSAT } from '../types';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { yieldNextUiFrame } from '../utils/kineticVolumetric3dPureLeaf';

type AdminRow = Record<string, unknown>;
type AdminKpisMap = Record<string, unknown>;
const errMsg = (err: unknown, fallback: string): string => (err instanceof Error && err.message ? err.message : fallback);

export function useAdminActions({
  isMountedRef, cargarDatos, setSociosReales, setPreRegistrosWeb, setAtletasList, setKpis,
  setExperienciasActivas, setReservasEscrowActivas, sociosReales, atletasList, reservasEscrowActivas, reservasHistoricas, reporteCorteSemanal, showTacticalAlert,
}: {
  isMountedRef: MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  cargarDatos: (isSilent?: boolean, force?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  setSociosReales: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AdminRow[]>>;
  setPreRegistrosWeb?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AdminRow[]>>;
  setAtletasList?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AdminRow[]>>;
  setKpis?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AdminKpisMap>>;
  setExperienciasActivas?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AdminRow[]>>;
  setReservasEscrowActivas?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AdminRow[]>>;
  sociosReales?: AdminRow[];
  atletasList?: AdminRow[];
  reservasEscrowActivas?: AdminRow[];
  reservasHistoricas?: AdminRow[];
  reporteCorteSemanal: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO | null;
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, message: string, type?: string, onConfirm?: (() => void) | null, confirmText?: string, cancelText?: string) => void;
}) {
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [isInjectingKC, setIsInjectingKC] = useState(false);
  const [isUpdatingFiscal, setIsUpdatingFiscal] = useState(false);
  const [isExportingPDF, setIsExportingPDF] = useState(false);
  const [togglingAffiliateName, setTogglingAffiliateName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSavingManualAffiliate, setIsSavingManualAffiliate] = useState(false);
  const [isSweepingNoShow, setIsSweepingNoShow] = useState(false);
  const isModeratingServiceRef = useRef(false);
  const submitBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const toggleAfBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const manualAfBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const pdfBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const injectBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const fiscalBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const noShowBusyRef = useRef(false);
  const athletesRef = useRef<AdminRow[]>(atletasList || []);
  athletesRef.current = atletasList || [];
  const reservasHistRef = useRef<AdminRow[]>(reservasHistoricas || []);
  reservasHistRef.current = reservasHistoricas || [];
  const reservasActivasRef = useRef<AdminRow[]>(reservasEscrowActivas || []);
  reservasActivasRef.current = reservasEscrowActivas || [];

  const cambiarEstadoAfiliado = useCallback(
    async (afiliado: Record<string, unknown>, nuevoEstado?: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'prospecto', onSuccessCallback?: () => void) => {
      if (toggleAfBusyRef.current) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      const nombreAf = String(afiliado?.nombre || '').trim();
      if (!nombreAf) return false;
      const currentlyActive = Boolean(afiliado?.esClubActivo ?? (String(afiliado?.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'activo' || afiliado?.verificado));
      const targetEstado: 'activo' | 'prospecto' = (nuevoEstado || (currentlyActive ? 'prospecto' : 'activo')) === 'activo' ? 'activo' : 'prospecto';
      const isActivating = targetEstado === 'activo';
      toggleAfBusyRef.current = true;
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      setTogglingAffiliateName(nombreAf);
      setSociosReales((prev) => patchAffiliateStatusInList(prev, afiliado, nombreAf, isActivating, isActivating ? 'activo' : 'inactivo'));
      try {
        await adminService.toggleEstadoAfiliado(afiliado, targetEstado);
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        onSuccessCallback?.();
        const alertInfo = buildAffiliateToggleAlert(nombreAf, isActivating, isEn);
        showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
        return true;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
        setSociosReales((prev) => patchAffiliateStatusInList(prev, afiliado, nombreAf, currentlyActive, currentlyActive ? 'activo' : 'inactivo'));
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL ACTUALIZAR ESTADO', 'STATUS UPDATE ERROR'), message || pick(isEn, 'No se pudo cambiar el estado del operador.', 'Could not update operator status.'), 'error');
        return false;
      } finally {
        toggleAfBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setTogglingAffiliateName(null);
      }
    },
    [cargarDatos, isMountedRef, setSociosReales, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const crearAfiliadoManual = useCallback(
    async (payload: Parameters<typeof adminService.crearNuevoAfiliado>[0], onSuccessCallback?: () => void) => {
      if (manualAfBusyRef.current || isSavingManualAffiliate) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      const cleanName = String(payload?.nombre || '').trim();
      if (!cleanName) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'NOMBRE REQUERIDO', 'NAME REQUIRED'), pick(isEn, 'Ingresa el nombre comercial del Club o Especialista.', 'Enter the commercial name of the Club or Specialist.'), 'warning');
        return false;
      }
      manualAfBusyRef.current = true;
      setIsSavingManualAffiliate(true);
      try {
        await adminService.crearNuevoAfiliado(payload);
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn');
        onSuccessCallback?.();
        const alertInfo = buildManualAffiliateAlert(cleanName, Boolean(payload?.es_especialista), isEn);
        showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
        return true;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL REGISTRAR', 'REGISTRATION ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo guardar el nuevo afiliado.', 'Could not save the new affiliate.')), 'error');
        return false;
      } finally {
        manualAfBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsSavingManualAffiliate(false);
      }
    },
    [isSavingManualAffiliate, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const cambiarEstadoServicio = useCallback(
    async (id: string, nuevoEstado: 'aprobado' | 'rechazado' | string, onSuccessCallback?: () => void) => {
      if (isModeratingServiceRef.current || submitBusyRef.current || isSubmitting) return;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      isModeratingServiceRef.current = true; submitBusyRef.current = true; setIsSubmitting(true);
      try {
        triggerHaptic('Medium');
        await adminService.updateServiceStatus(id, nuevoEstado);
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerHaptic('Success'); onSuccessCallback?.();
        const alertInfo = buildServiceModerationAlert(nuevoEstado, isEn);
        showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'FRICCIÓN', 'MODERATION ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo actualizar el estado de la solicitud.', 'Could not update submission status.')), 'error');
      } finally {
        isModeratingServiceRef.current = false; submitBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsSubmitting(false);
      }
    },
    [isSubmitting, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const guardarEvento = useCallback(
    async (nuevoEvento: Parameters<typeof adminService.saveEvent>[0], isEditing: boolean, userId: string, negocioNombre: string, onSuccessCallback?: () => void) => {
      if (submitBusyRef.current || isSubmitting) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      const tituloClean = String(nuevoEvento?.titulo || '').trim();
      const descClean = String(nuevoEvento?.descripcion || '').trim();
      const ubicacionClean = String(nuevoEvento?.ubicacion || '').trim();
      if (!tituloClean || !ubicacionClean || !descClean) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'DATOS INVÁLIDOS', 'INVALID FIELDS'), pick(isEn, 'Completa el título, la ubicación y la descripción de la experiencia.', 'Complete the title, location, and description of the experience.'), 'error');
        return false;
      }
      submitBusyRef.current = true; setIsSubmitting(true);
      try {
        await adminService.saveEvent(nuevoEvento, isEditing, userId, negocioNombre);
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn'); onSuccessCallback?.();
        const alertInfo = buildSaveEventAlert(isEditing, isEn);
        showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
        return true;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL GUARDAR', 'SAVE ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo sincronizar la experiencia en Supabase.', 'Could not sync the experience in Supabase.')), 'error');
        return false;
      } finally {
        submitBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsSubmitting(false);
      }
    },
    [isSubmitting, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const eliminarEvento = useCallback(

        const res = (await adminService.deleteEvent(id)) as { refundedAthletes?: number } | null | undefined;
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerHaptic('Success'); onSuccessCallback?.();
        const alertInfo = buildDeleteEventAlert(Number(res?.refundedAthletes || 0), isEn);
        showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL ELIMINAR', 'DELETE ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo eliminar la experiencia.', 'Could not delete the experience.')), 'error');
      } finally {
        submitBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsSubmitting(false);
      }
    },
    [isSubmitting, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, setExperienciasActivas, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const eliminarCuentaAtleta = useCallback((atleta: AdminRow & { id?: string; nombre?: string; email?: string }) => {

      pick(isEn, `¿Confirmas eliminar permanentemente la cuenta de "${nombre}"? Esta acción borrará sus reservas y registros en cascada.`, `Are you sure you want to permanently delete the profile for "${nombre}"? This action will cascade delete all bookings and records.`),
      'confirm',

  const inyectarKCAtleta = useCallback(
    async (
      atletaId: string, cantidadInput: string, billetera: 'membresia' | 'topup' = 'topup',
      nuevoNivel?: string | null, modoOperacion: 'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset' = 'inyectar',
      adminId?: string, onSuccessCallback?: () => void
    ) => {
      if (!atletaId || isInjectingKC || injectBusyRef.current) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      const parsedAmount = Math.max(0, parseInt(String(cantidadInput), 10) || 0);
      if (!nuevoNivel && modoOperacion !== 'reset' && parsedAmount <= 0) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'CANTIDAD REQUERIDA', 'AMOUNT REQUIRED'), pick(isEn, 'Ingresa una cantidad válida de Kineticoins.', 'Enter a valid Kineticoins amount.'), 'warning');
        return false;
      }
      const signedDelta = modoOperacion === 'retirar' ? -parsedAmount : modoOperacion === 'reset' ? 0 : parsedAmount;
      injectBusyRef.current = true;
      setIsInjectingKC(true);
      try {
        const res = await adminService.inyectarFondosAtleta(atletaId, signedDelta, billetera, nuevoNivel, modoOperacion, adminId);
        if (res && setAtletasList) {
          let snapshotAthletes = athletesRef.current;
          setAtletasList((prev) => {
            const patched = computeTreasuryPatchedAthletesAndKpis(prev, null, atletaId, res).nextAthletes;
            snapshotAthletes = patched;
            athletesRef.current = patched;
            return patched;
          });
          setKpis?.((prevK: AdminKpisMap) => computeTreasuryPatchedAthletesAndKpis(snapshotAthletes, prevK, atletaId, res).nextKpis as AdminKpisMap);
        }
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerArcadeHaptic('levelUp');
        onSuccessCallback?.();
        const alertInfo = buildTreasuryMutationAlert({ nuevoNivel, modoOperacion, billetera, signedDelta, isEn });
        showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
        return true;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR EN TESORERÍA', 'TREASURY ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo procesar el movimiento en la bóveda.', 'Could not process the vault transaction.')), 'error');
        return false;
      } finally {
        injectBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsInjectingKC(false);
      }
    },
    [isInjectingKC, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, setAtletasList, setKpis, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const actualizarFiscalSocio = useCallback(
    async (afiliadoId: string, rfc: string, regimenFiscal: RegimenFiscalSAT, clabeBancaria: string, csfValidada: boolean, onSuccessCallback?: () => void) => {

        return true;
      } catch (err: unknown) {

        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL GUARDAR', 'SAVE ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudieron actualizar los datos fiscales.', 'Could not update tax details.')), 'error');
        return false;
      } finally {

      }
    },
    [isUpdatingFiscal, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  const exportarReporteMaestroPDF = useCallback(async (customReporte?: unknown, pasesResumen?: { dia?: number; semana?: number; mes?: number }) => {
    const target = resolveTargetB2BCorteReport(customReporte, reporteCorteSemanal, sociosReales, reservasHistoricas);
    if (!target || pdfBusyRef.current || isExportingPDF) return;
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    pdfBusyRef.current = true;
    setIsExportingPDF(true);
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    await yieldNextUiFrame();
    try {
      await adminService.exportarPDFReporteMaestroCEO(target, pasesResumen);
      triggerHaptic('Success');
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL EXPORTAR PDF', 'PDF EXPORT ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo generar el Reporte Maestro PDF.', 'Could not generate the CEO Master Report PDF.')), 'error');
    } finally {
      pdfBusyRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setIsExportingPDF(false);
    }
  }, [reporteCorteSemanal, sociosReales, reservasHistoricas, isExportingPDF, isMountedRef, showTacticalAlert]);

  const exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF = useCallback(async (corteSocio: ICorteSemanalAfiliado, pasesResumen?: { dia?: number; semana?: number; mes?: number }) => {
    if (!corteSocio || pdfBusyRef.current || isExportingPDF) return;
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    pdfBusyRef.current = true;
    setIsExportingPDF(true);
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    await yieldNextUiFrame();
    try {
      await adminService.exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado(corteSocio, corteSocio.periodo_inicio, corteSocio.periodo_fin, pasesResumen);
      triggerHaptic('Success');
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR AL EXPORTAR PDF', 'PDF EXPORT ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo generar el Estado de Cuenta PDF.', 'Could not generate the B2B Account Statement PDF.')), 'error');
    } finally {
      pdfBusyRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setIsExportingPDF(false);
    }
  }, [isExportingPDF, isMountedRef, showTacticalAlert]);

  const copiarDatoBancario = useCallback(async (valor: string, etiqueta?: string) => {
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    const cleanLabel = etiqueta || pick(isEn, 'Dato', 'Details');
    triggerHaptic('Success');
    await adminService.copiarAlPortapapeles(valor);
    showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'COPIADO AL PORTAPAPELES', 'COPIED TO CLIPBOARD'), pick(isEn, `${cleanLabel} (${valor}) copiado al portapapeles.`, `${cleanLabel} (${valor}) copied to clipboard.`), 'success');
  }, [showTacticalAlert]);

  const gestionarPreRegistroWeb = useCallback(async (registro: AdminRow & { id?: string; email?: string; negocio_nombre?: string }, accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar') => {
    if (!registro?.id || submitBusyRef.current || isSubmitting) return;
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    submitBusyRef.current = true;
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      setPreRegistrosWeb?.((prev) => patchPreRegistroDecisionInList(prev, String(registro.id), accion));
      await adminService.gestionarPreRegistroWeb(registro as never, accion);
      await cargarDatos(true, true);
      triggerHaptic('Success');
      const alertInfo = buildPreRegistroAlert(registro, accion, isEn);
      showTacticalAlert?.(alertInfo.title, alertInfo.message, 'success');
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR DE REVISIÓN', 'REVIEW ERROR'), errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo actualizar el pre-registro.', 'Could not update pre-registration.')), 'error');
    } finally {
      submitBusyRef.current = false;
      if (isMountedRef.current) setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }, [isSubmitting, cargarDatos, isMountedRef, setPreRegistrosWeb, showTacticalAlert]);

  const ejecutarBarridoNoShow40 = useCallback(
    async (reservaId?: string | null) => {
      if (noShowBusyRef.current) return false;
      const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
      noShowBusyRef.current = true;
      setIsSweepingNoShow(true);
      triggerHaptic('Medium');
      try {
        const res = await executeNoShow40Sweep(reservaId, 45);
        if (res.procesadas > 0) {
          setReservasEscrowActivas?.((prev) => computeOptimisticNoShowState(prev, {}, reservaId, res).nextReservasActivas);
          setKpis?.((prevKpis: AdminKpisMap) => computeOptimisticNoShowState(reservasActivasRef.current, prevKpis, reservaId, res).nextKpis as AdminKpisMap);
        }
        await cargarDatos(true, true);
        triggerHaptic('Success');
        return { ok: true, res };
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        const msg = errMsg(err, pick(isEn, 'No se pudo ejecutar la liquidación No-Show 40%.', 'Could not execute 40% No-Show sweep.'));
        showTacticalAlert?.(pick(isEn, 'ERROR EN MOTOR NO-SHOW', 'NO-SHOW ENGINE ERROR'), msg, 'error');
        return { ok: false, error: msg };
      } finally {
        noShowBusyRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsSweepingNoShow(false);
      }
    },
    [cargarDatos, isMountedRef, setKpis, setReservasEscrowActivas, showTacticalAlert]
  );

  return {
    isSubmitting, isInjectingKC, isUpdatingFiscal, isExportingPDF, togglingAffiliateName, isSavingManualAffiliate,
    isSweepingNoShow, cambiarEstadoAfiliado, crearAfiliadoManual, eliminarSocioB2B, cambiarEstadoServicio, gestionarPreRegistroWeb,
    guardarEvento, eliminarEvento, eliminarCuentaAtleta, inyectarKCAtleta, actualizarFiscalSocio, exportarReporteMaestroPDF,
    exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF, copiarDatoBancario, ejecutarBarridoNoShow40,
  };
}

    // === src/services/admin/adminAffiliateCrudHelper.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import {
  AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY,
  KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES,
  canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram,
  upsertRuntimeAffiliateState,
} from '../../constants/affiliatesCatalog';
import { SPECIALIST_DEFAULT_AVATAR, resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated, extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { validateStrictWhatsappPhone } from '../../utils/kineticPhoneValidatorLeaf';
import { invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache } from '../adminDashboardFetcher';
import { exploreService } from '../exploreService';
import { isValidGeoCoordPair, parseStrictCoord, resolveAffiliateWriteOnceGeoPatch } from '../kineticCloudAiAndGeoService';
import { markAffiliateDeleted, unmarkAffiliateDeleted } from './adminOpsHelpers';

export const UUID_ANY_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const UUID_V4_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

export const DEFAULT_MASTER_ADMIN_NAME = 'Kinetic Oficial';

export const escapeLikePattern = (val: string): string => val.replace(/[\\%_]/g, '\\$&');

export const isMasterAdminAccount = (rawEmail?: string | null): boolean => {
  const email = String(rawEmail || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return (
    email === 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx' ||
    email === 'administrador@kinetic.com' ||
    email === 'kinetic@kinetic.com' ||
    email === 'admin@kinetic.com'
  );
};

export const ensureAffiliateUuidV4 = (candidate?: string | null): string => {
  const clean = String(candidate || '').trim();
  if (UUID_V4_RE.test(clean) || UUID_ANY_RE.test(clean)) return clean;
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') return crypto.randomUUID();
  return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, (ch) => {
    const r = (Math.random() * 16) | 0;
    return (ch === 'x' ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8).toString(16);
  });
};

export const normalizeInstagramHandle = (raw?: string | null, clubName?: string | null): string =>
  canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(raw, clubName);

export const stripInstagramMetaFromHorarios = (rawHorarios?: string | null): string =>
  String(rawHorarios || '')
    .replace(/\s*\|\|\s*ig:@?[^\s|]+/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s*__ig:@?[^\s|]+/gi, '')
    .replace(/([a-zA-ZáéíóúÁÉÍÓÚñÑ])(\d{1,2}:\d{2})/g, '$1 • $2')
    .trim();

export const isValidB2BWhatsapp = (raw?: string | null): boolean => {
  const res = validateStrictWhatsappPhone(raw);
  return res.valid && res.normalized10 !== '6241234567';
};

export const extractInstagramFromAffiliate = (af: any): string => {
  if (!af) return '';
  const direct = normalizeInstagramHandle(af.instagram);
  if (direct) return direct;
  if (Array.isArray(af.amenidades)) {
    const hit = af.amenidades.find((item: any) => String(item || '').trim().toLowerCase().startsWith('__ig:'));
    if (hit) return normalizeInstagramHandle(String(hit).trim().slice(5));
  }
  const m = String(af.horarios || '').match(/(?:\|\|\s*ig:|__ig:)(@?[^\s|]+)/i);
  return m?.[1] ? normalizeInstagramHandle(m[1]) : '';
};

export const buildAmenidadesWithIg = (existingAmenidades: unknown, cleanIg: string): string[] => {
  const base = Array.isArray(existingAmenidades)
    ? existingAmenidades.map((x) => String(x || '').trim()).filter((x) => Boolean(x) && !x.toLowerCase().startsWith('__ig:'))
    : [];
  const seeded = base.length > 0 ? base : ['Acceso Controlado'];
  return cleanIg ? [...seeded, `__ig:${cleanIg}`] : seeded;
};

export interface ICreateAffiliatePayload {
  id?: string;
  nombre: string;
  disciplina?: string;
  es_especialista?: boolean;
  ubicacion?: string;
  whatsapp?: string;
  instagram?: string;
  horarios?: string;
  descripcion?: string;
  latitud?: number | string | null;
  longitud?: number | string | null;
  rating?: number | string;
  estrellas_exclusividad?: number;
  avatar_url?: string | null;
  portada_url?: string | null;
  galeria?: string[];
  amenidades?: string[];
  activo?: boolean;
}

export async function crearNuevoAfiliado(payload: ICreateAffiliatePayload) {
  const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
  const cleanNombre = String(payload?.nombre || '').trim();
  if (!cleanNombre) {
    throw new Error(isEn ? 'Enter the commercial name of the Club or Specialist.' : 'Ingresa el nombre comercial del Club o Especialista.');
  }
  const isSpec = Boolean(payload.es_especialista);
  const disciplina = String(payload.disciplina || 'Fuerza').trim() || 'Fuerza';
  const parsedGeo = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(payload.ubicacion);
  const ubicacion = parsedGeo.cleanValue || 'Ubicación Verificada';
  const rawWhatsapp = String(payload.whatsapp || '').trim();
  const waCheck = rawWhatsapp ? validateStrictWhatsappPhone(rawWhatsapp) : null;
  if (rawWhatsapp && (!waCheck?.valid || waCheck.normalized10 === '6241234567')) {
    throw new Error(isEn ? 'Enter a valid real WhatsApp number (10 to 15 digits).' : 'Ingresa un WhatsApp comercial real (10 a 15 dígitos).');
  }
  const cleanWhatsapp = waCheck?.valid ? waCheck.normalized10 : '';
  const cleanIg = normalizeInstagramHandle(payload.instagram) || extractInstagramFromAffiliate(payload);
  const cleanHorarios = stripInstagramMetaFromHorarios(payload.horarios) || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00';
  const isActivating = payload.activo !== false;
  const targetEstado: 'activo' | 'inactivo' = isActivating ? 'activo' : 'inactivo';
  const rawLat = parseStrictCoord(payload.latitud ?? parsedGeo.lat);
  const rawLon = parseStrictCoord(payload.longitud ?? parsedGeo.lng);
  const hasExplicitCoords = isValidGeoCoordPair(rawLat, rawLon);
  const lat = hasExplicitCoords ? (rawLat as number) : 22.8935;
  const lon = hasExplicitCoords ? (rawLon as number) : -109.9142;
  const rating = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Number(payload.rating) || 5.0));
  const estrellas = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(payload.estrellas_exclusividad) || (isSpec ? 5 : 4))));
  const fallbackCover = resolveCategoryImage(disciplina, cleanNombre);
  const avatarUrl = payload.avatar_url || (isSpec ? SPECIALIST_DEFAULT_AVATAR : fallbackCover);
  const portadaUrl = payload.portada_url || fallbackCover;
  const galeria = Array.isArray(payload.galeria) && payload.galeria.length > 0 ? payload.galeria : [portadaUrl];
  const amenidades = buildAmenidadesWithIg(payload.amenidades, cleanIg);

  let targetId = ensureAffiliateUuidV4(payload.id);
  await unmarkAffiliateDeleted(payload.id, cleanNombre);
  try {
    if (payload.id && UUID_ANY_RE.test(String(payload.id).trim())) {
      const { data: byId } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('id').eq('id', String(payload.id).trim()).maybeSingle();
      if (byId?.id) targetId = String(byId.id);
    } else if (!/[%_]/.test(cleanNombre)) {
      const { data: existingRows } = await supabase.from('afiliados').select('id').ilike('nombre', escapeLikePattern(cleanNombre)).limit(1);
      const existingId = Array.isArray(existingRows) ? existingRows[0]?.id : null;
      if (existingId && UUID_ANY_RE.test(String(existingId))) targetId = String(existingId);
    }
  } catch {}

  const geoPatch = await resolveAffiliateWriteOnceGeoPatch({ affiliateId: targetId, ubicacion });
  const finalLat = !hasExplicitCoords && geoPatch.latitud !== undefined ? geoPatch.latitud : lat;
  const finalLon = !hasExplicitCoords && geoPatch.longitud !== undefined ? geoPatch.longitud : lon;
  const runtimeBase = {
    id: targetId, nombre: cleanNombre, disciplina, categoria: isSpec ? 'Especialista' : 'Club',
    es_especialista: isSpec, ubicacion, direccion: parsedGeo.detectedZone || 'Ubicación Verificada',
    whatsapp: cleanWhatsapp, instagram: cleanIg, horarios: cleanHorarios, amenidades, galeria,
    avatar_url: avatarUrl, portada_url: portadaUrl,
    descripcion: String(payload.descripcion || '').trim() || `${isSpec ? 'Especialista certificado' : 'Sede deportiva'} de ${disciplina} en la red Kinetic.`,
    latitude: finalLat, longitude: finalLon, latitud: finalLat, longitud: finalLon, rating,
    verificado: isActivating, esClubActivo: isActivating, estado: targetEstado,
  };

  const dbPayload = {
    id: targetId, nombre: cleanNombre, disciplina, categoria: isSpec ? 'Especialista' : 'Club',
    ubicacion, whatsapp: cleanWhatsapp, horarios: cleanHorarios, es_especialista: isSpec,
    verificado: isActivating, estrellas_exclusividad: estrellas, latitud: finalLat, longitud: finalLon,
    avatar_url: avatarUrl, portada_url: portadaUrl, galeria, amenidades, rating, estado: targetEstado,
    ...geoPatch,
  };
  const { data: upserted, error: upsertErr } = await supabase.from('afiliados').upsert(dbPayload, { onConflict: 'id' }).select('id').maybeSingle();
  if (upsertErr) {
    const { amenidades: _am, galeria: _ga, place_id: _pi, formatted_address: _fa, geo_verified_at: _gv, ...fallbackBase } = dbPayload;
    const { error: fallbackErr } = await supabase.from('afiliados').upsert({ ...fallbackBase, horarios: cleanIg ? `${cleanHorarios} || ig:${cleanIg}` : cleanHorarios }, { onConflict: 'id' });
    if (fallbackErr) {
      throw new Error(fallbackErr.message || upsertErr.message || 'AFFILIATE_UPSERT_FAILED');
    }
  }
  const runtimeRecord = upsertRuntimeAffiliateState(
    upserted?.id && upserted.id !== targetId ? { ...runtimeBase, id: upserted.id } : runtimeBase
  );

  exploreService.clearRadarCache();
  invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return runtimeRecord;
}

export async function eliminarAfiliado(afiliadoId: string, nombre?: string) {
  const cleanId = String(afiliadoId || '').trim();
  const cleanNombre = String(nombre || '').trim();
  if (cleanNombre.toLowerCase() === 'kinetic oficial') {
    throw new Error('No se puede eliminar la sede oficial Kinetic.');
  }
  await markAffiliateDeleted(cleanId, cleanNombre);
  if (cleanId && UUID_ANY_RE.test(cleanId)) await supabase.from('afiliados').delete().eq('id', cleanId);
  if (cleanNombre && !/[%_]/.test(cleanNombre)) {
    await supabase.from('afiliados').delete().ilike('nombre', escapeLikePattern(cleanNombre));
  }
  for (let i = KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const item = KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES[i];
    if ((cleanId && item.id === cleanId) || (cleanNombre && item.nombre?.toLowerCase() === cleanNombre.toLowerCase())) {
      KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const map = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (cleanId && map[cleanId]) delete map[cleanId];
      if (cleanNombre) Object.keys(map).forEach((k) => { if (map[k]?.nombre?.toLowerCase() === cleanNombre.toLowerCase()) delete map[k]; });
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
    }
  } catch {}
  invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return true;
}

    FILE: src/services/admin/adminDashboardEnrichmentHelpers.ts

    // === src/services/admin/adminDashboardEnrichmentHelpers.ts === //
import {
  KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES,
  canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram,
  cleanLocationDisplay,
  resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline,
} from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { isMasterPass0Kc, normalizeAffiliateKey } from '../adminB2BCutEngine';
import { isSpecialistEventOrCategory } from './adminMetricsCalculator';
import { isAffiliateMarkedDeleted } from './adminOpsHelpers';

export type AdminDashRow = Record<string, unknown>;

export interface IAdminSocioRecord extends AdminDashRow {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;
  ubicacion: string;
  disciplina: string;
  es_especialista: boolean;
  estado: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'pendiente';
  verificado: boolean;
  esClubActivo: boolean;
  strikes_operativos: number;
  strikesOperativos: number;
  sinAgenda14d: boolean;
  experienciasCount: number;
  ingresosGenerados: number;
  enCustodia: number;
  sesionesCompletadas: number;
}

export function buildEnrichedAdminSociosList(params: {
  afiliadosDb: AdminDashRow[];
  serviciosRaw: AdminDashRow[];
  reservasHistoricas: AdminDashRow[];
  deletedAffSet: Set<string>;
  runtimeAffRaw: string | null;
}): {
  sociosReales: IAdminSocioRecord[];
  clubesActivos: number;
  clubesProspectos: number;
  socioByKey: Map<string, IAdminSocioRecord>;
} {
  const { afiliadosDb, serviciosRaw, reservasHistoricas, deletedAffSet, runtimeAffRaw } = params;
  let runtimeOverrides: Record<string, AdminDashRow> = {};
  if (runtimeAffRaw) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(runtimeAffRaw);
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
        runtimeOverrides = parsed as Record<string, AdminDashRow>;
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  const afilByName = new Map<string, AdminDashRow>();
  (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).forEach((rawAf) => {
    const stAf = rawAf as unknown as AdminDashRow;
    if (isAffiliateMarkedDeleted(deletedAffSet, stAf?.id as string | undefined, stAf?.nombre as string | undefined)) return;
    const key = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(stAf?.nombre || ''));
    if (!key) return;
    const rt = runtimeOverrides[key] || runtimeOverrides[String(stAf?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase()] || {};
    afilByName.set(key, { ...stAf, ...rt });
  });

  afiliadosDb.forEach((dbAf) => {
    if (isAffiliateMarkedDeleted(deletedAffSet, dbAf?.id as string | undefined, dbAf?.nombre as string | undefined)) return;
    const key = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(dbAf?.nombre || ''));
    if (!key) return;
    const prev = afilByName.get(key) || {};
    const rt = runtimeOverrides[key] || runtimeOverrides[String(dbAf?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase()] || {};
    const prevIg = canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram(
      String(dbAf.instagram || rt.instagram || prev.instagram || ''),
      String(dbAf.nombre || prev.nombre || '')
    );
    const resolvedDisc = resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline(
      String(dbAf.nombre || prev.nombre || ''),
      String(dbAf.disciplina || rt.disciplina || prev.disciplina || ''),
      Boolean(dbAf.es_especialista ?? prev.es_especialista)
    );
    afilByName.set(key, {
      ...prev,
      ...rt,
      ...dbAf,
      _fromDbRow: true,
      id: dbAf.id || prev.id || key,
      nombre:
        key === 'palesttra club'
          ? 'PALESTTRA CLUB'
          : key === 'baja fitness medano'
            ? 'Baja Fitness Médano'
            : dbAf.nombre || prev.nombre,
      disciplina: resolvedDisc,
      rfc: dbAf.rfc || prev.rfc || '',
      clabe_bancaria: dbAf.clabe_bancaria || prev.clabe_bancaria || '',
      whatsapp: dbAf.whatsapp || rt.whatsapp || prev.whatsapp || '',
      instagram: prevIg,
      horarios: dbAf.horarios || rt.horarios || prev.horarios || '',
      ubicacion: cleanLocationDisplay(String(dbAf.ubicacion || prev.ubicacion || ''), 'Ubicación Verificada'),
    });
  });

  const sociosReales: IAdminSocioRecord[] = Array.from(afilByName.values()).map((af) => {
    const nombreAf = String(af.nombre || '').trim();
    const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(nombreAf);
    const servsAf = serviciosRaw.filter(
      (s) => normalizeAffiliateKey(String(s.negocio_nombre || '')) === normKey && (s.estado === 'aprobado' || s.estado === 'activo')
    );
    const resAf = reservasHistoricas.filter(
      (r) => !isMasterPass0Kc(r) && normalizeAffiliateKey(String(r.negocio_nombre || '')) === normKey
    );

    let ingresosGenerados = 0;
    let enCustodia = 0;
    let sesionesCompletadas = 0;
    resAf.forEach((r) => {
      const st = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase();
      const total = Math.max(
        Number(r.costo_total) || 0,
        Number(r.precio_kc) || 0,
        (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
      );
      const esc = Number(r.monto_escrow) || Math.round(total * 0.4);
      if (st === 'completada') {
        ingresosGenerados += total;
        sesionesCompletadas += 1;
      } else if (st === 'no_show' || st === 'penalizada') {
        ingresosGenerados += esc;
      } else if (['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'].includes(st)) {
        enCustodia += esc;
      }
    });

    const rawEstado = String(af.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const explicitlyInactive = [
      'pendiente',
      'inactivo',
      'oculto',
      'prospecto',
      'suspendido',
      'bloqueado',
    ].includes(rawEstado);
    const hasDbRow = Boolean(af._fromDbRow);
    const esClubActivo = explicitlyInactive
      ? false
      : hasDbRow
        ? rawEstado === 'activo'
        : Boolean(
            rawEstado === 'activo' ||
              af.verificado ||
              af.propietario_id ||
              servsAf.length > 0 ||
              ingresosGenerados > 0 ||
              normKey.includes('kinetic') ||
              normKey.includes('cabo pilates')
          );

    const strikesOperativos = Math.max(0, Number(af.strikes_operativos) || 0);
    const nowMs = Date.now();
    const window14dMs = nowMs + 14 * 86_400_000;
    const hasUpcoming14d = servsAf.some((s) => {
      const freq = String(s.frecuencia || '').toLowerCase();
      if (/diario|semanal|mensual|daily|weekly|monthly/i.test(freq)) return true;
      const rawDate = s.fecha || s.fecha_evento;
      if (!rawDate) return false;
      const t = new Date(String(rawDate).slice(0, 10)).getTime();
      return !isNaN(t) && t >= nowMs - 86_400_000 && t <= window14dMs;
    });
    const isVerifiedOfficial =
      Boolean(af.es_cuenta_oficial) &&
      Boolean(af.verificado || rawEstado === 'activo') &&
      !explicitlyInactive;
    const sinAgenda14d = isVerifiedOfficial ? false : Boolean(esClubActivo && !hasUpcoming14d);
    const isSpec = Boolean(
      af.es_especialista ||
        String(af.categoria || af.tipo || '').toLowerCase() === 'especialista' ||
        normKey.includes('especialista')
    );

    return {
      ...af,
      id: String(af.id || nombreAf),
      nombre: nombreAf,
      ubicacion: cleanLocationDisplay(String(af.ubicacion || ''), 'Ubicación Verificada'),
      disciplina: resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline(nombreAf, String(af.disciplina || af.categoria || ''), isSpec),
      es_especialista: isSpec,
      estado: esClubActivo ? 'activo' : rawEstado === 'inactivo' || rawEstado === 'oculto' ? 'inactivo' : 'pendiente',
      verificado: esClubActivo,
      esClubActivo,
      strikes_operativos: strikesOperativos,
      strikesOperativos,
      sinAgenda14d,
      experienciasCount: servsAf.length,
      ingresosGenerados,
      enCustodia,
      sesionesCompletadas,
    };
  });

  sociosReales.sort(
    (a, b) =>
      b.ingresosGenerados - a.ingresosGenerados ||
      Number(b.esClubActivo) - Number(a.esClubActivo) ||
      a.nombre.localeCompare(b.nombre, 'es')
  );

  const clubesActivos = sociosReales.filter((s) => s.esClubActivo).length;
  const clubesProspectos = Math.max(0, sociosReales.length - clubesActivos);
  const socioByKey = new Map<string, IAdminSocioRecord>();
  sociosReales.forEach((soc) => {
    socioByKey.set(normalizeAffiliateKey(soc.nombre), soc);
  });

  return { sociosReales, clubesActivos, clubesProspectos, socioByKey };
}

export function buildEnrichedAdminExperiences(params: {
  serviciosRaw: AdminDashRow[];
  reservasHistoricas: AdminDashRow[];
  socioByKey: Map<string, IAdminSocioRecord>;
}): { solicitudes: AdminDashRow[]; experienciasActivas: AdminDashRow[] } {
  const { serviciosRaw, reservasHistoricas, socioByKey } = params;
  const solicitudes: AdminDashRow[] = [];
  const experienciasActivas: AdminDashRow[] = [];

  serviciosRaw.forEach((s) => {
    const rawSt = String(s.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (rawSt === 'pendiente') {
      solicitudes.push(s);
    } else if (rawSt !== 'rechazado') {
      const resDeClase = reservasHistoricas.filter(
        (r) =>
          !isMasterPass0Kc(r) &&
          (String(r.servicio_id || '') === String(s.id) ||
            String(r.servicio || r.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase() ===
              String(s.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase())
      );
      let ingresosClase = 0;
      let reservadosActivos = 0;
      resDeClase.forEach((r) => {
        const st = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase();
        const tot = Math.max(Number(r.costo_total) || 0, Number(r.precio_kc) || 0);
        if (st === 'completada') {
          ingresosClase += tot;
          reservadosActivos += 1;
        } else if (['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'].includes(st)) {
          reservadosActivos += 1;
        }
      });
      const cupoMax = Math.max(1, Number(s.cupo_maximo) || 10);
      const hostSocio = socioByKey.get(normalizeAffiliateKey(String(s.negocio_nombre || '')));
      const attrs = (s?.atributos && typeof s.atributos === 'object' ? s.atributos : {}) as AdminDashRow;
      experienciasActivas.push({
        ...s,
        estado: rawSt === 'oculto' || rawSt === 'inactivo' ? 'inactivo' : 'aprobado',
        es_especialista: isSpecialistEventOrCategory(
          String(s.categoria || ''),
          String(s.negocio_nombre || ''),
          Boolean(hostSocio?.es_especialista)
        ),
        requisitos_traer: s.requisitos_traer || attrs.requisitos_traer || '',
        edad_minima: s.edad_minima || attrs.edad_minima || '',
        ingresosGenerados: ingresosClase,
        reservadosCount: reservadosActivos,
        ocupacionPct: Math.min(100, Math.round((reservadosActivos / cupoMax) * 100)),
      });
    }
  });

  experienciasActivas.sort((a, b) => Number(b.ingresosGenerados || 0) - Number(a.ingresosGenerados || 0));
  return { solicitudes, experienciasActivas };
}

    // === src/services/admin/adminDispersionSpeiPeriodHelpers.ts === //
import type { IB2BPeriodOverride } from '../adminB2BCutEngine';
import { getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds } from '../kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

const MONTHS_ES = ['Ene', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Abr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Ago', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dic'];
const MONTHS_EN = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

const formatIsoToDayMon = (iso: string, isEn: boolean): string => {
  const parts = String(iso || '').split('-').map(Number);
  const monIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(11, (parts[1] || 1) - 1));
  const day = parts[2] || 1;
  return `${String(day).padStart(2, '0')} ${(isEn ? MONTHS_EN : MONTHS_ES)[monIdx]}`;
};

const toIsoUtc = (y: number, m: number, d: number) =>
  `${y}-${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export interface ISpeiPeriodOption {
  key: string;
  title: string;
  rangeLabel: string;
  override: IB2BPeriodOverride;
}

export function buildAdminSpeiPeriodOptions(isEn: boolean, tx: (es: string, en: string) => string): ISpeiPeriodOption[] {
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  const weeks: ISpeiPeriodOption[] = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((w) => {
    const bounds = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds(new Date(nowMs - w * 7 * 86_400_000));
    return {
      key: `week_${w}`,
      title: w === 0 ? tx('Semana Actual (En Curso)', 'Current Week (Live)') : tx(`Semana -${w}`, `Week -${w}`),
      rangeLabel: `${formatIsoToDayMon(bounds.periodoInicio, isEn)} – ${formatIsoToDayMon(bounds.periodoFin, isEn)}`,
      override: {
        startMs: bounds.startMs,
        endMs: bounds.endMs,
        inicioIso: bounds.periodoInicio,
        finIso: bounds.periodoFin,
        strictWindow: true,
      },
    };
  });

  const curBounds = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds(new Date(nowMs));
  const { year: curY, month: curM } = curBounds.parts;
  const curLastD = new Date(Date.UTC(curY, curM, 0)).getUTCDate();
  const m0Inicio = toIsoUtc(curY, curM, 1);
  const m0Fin = toIsoUtc(curY, curM, curLastD);

  const prevMonthRef = new Date(Date.UTC(curY, curM - 2, 15, 19, 0, 0));
  const prevBounds = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds(prevMonthRef);
  const { year: prevY, month: prevM } = prevBounds.parts;
  const prevLastD = new Date(Date.UTC(prevY, prevM, 0)).getUTCDate();
  const m1Inicio = toIsoUtc(prevY, prevM, 1);
  const m1Fin = toIsoUtc(prevY, prevM, prevLastD);

  return [
    ...weeks,
    {
      key: 'month_0',
      title: tx('Mes Actual', 'Current Month'),
      rangeLabel: `${formatIsoToDayMon(m0Inicio, isEn)} – ${formatIsoToDayMon(m0Fin, isEn)}`,
      override: { startMs: curBounds.monthStartMs, endMs: curBounds.monthEndMs, inicioIso: m0Inicio, finIso: m0Fin, strictWindow: true },
    },
    {
      key: 'month_1',
      title: tx('Mes Anterior', 'Previous Month'),
      rangeLabel: `${formatIsoToDayMon(m1Inicio, isEn)} – ${formatIsoToDayMon(m1Fin, isEn)}`,
      override: { startMs: prevBounds.monthStartMs, endMs: prevBounds.monthEndMs, inicioIso: m1Inicio, finIso: m1Fin, strictWindow: true },
    },
  ];
}

import { getLevelNumericWeight } from '../../constants/memberships';
import {
  isAdminOrB2BAccount,
  isMasterPass0Kc,
  isOrphanEscrowReservation,
  isSandboxAccountOrReservation,
} from '../adminB2BCutEngine';

export {
  IPdfInvoiceConfig,
  IPdfTableRow,
  buildKineticInvoicePdfBytes,
  exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado,
} from '../adminPdfTemplates';
export { isAdminOrB2BAccount, isOrphanEscrowReservation };

const SPECIALIST_CATEGORY_KEYWORDS = [
  'especialista',
  'fisioterapia',
  'fisio',
  'nutricion',
  'psicologia',
  'masaje',
  'rehabilitacion',
  'recovery',
  'quiropractica',
  'osteopatia',
  'medicina deportiva',
  'terapia',
];

export function isSpecialistEventOrCategory(
  rawCategoria?: string | null,
  rawNegocio?: string | null,
  socioEsEspecialista?: boolean
): boolean {
  if (socioEsEspecialista) return true;
  const norm = `${rawCategoria || ''} ${rawNegocio || ''}`
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '');
  return SPECIALIST_CATEGORY_KEYWORDS.some((kw) => norm.includes(kw));
}

type VipSubscriberCandidate = {
  nivel?: string | null;
  membresia_pagada_activa?: boolean | null;
  stripe_subscription_id?: string | null;
  kc_membresia?: number | string | null;
  [k: string]: unknown;
} | null | undefined;

export const isPaidActiveVipSubscriber = (a: VipSubscriberCandidate): boolean => {
  if (!a || isAdminOrB2BAccount(a) || isSandboxAccountOrReservation(a, a)) return false;
  if (getLevelNumericWeight(a?.nivel) <= 0) return false;
  if (a?.membresia_pagada_activa === true || Boolean(a?.stripe_subscription_id)) return true;
  return (Number(a?.kc_membresia) || 0) > 0;
};

export function computeAdminTierAndDisciplineMetrics(
  atletasReales: any[],
  reservasValidas: any[],
  isEn: boolean
) {
  const safeAthletes = (atletasReales || []).filter(
    (a) => !isAdminOrB2BAccount(a) && !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(a, a)
  );
  const totalAtletas = Math.max(1, safeAthletes.length);

  const tiersConfig = [
    {
      tierKey: 'platinum_legend',
      label: isEn ? 'Platinum' : 'Platino',
      color: '#00F0FF',
      precioMensual: 3600,
      beneficioResumen: isEn
        ? 'Full 1–5 Star access, Flash Sales -20%, 3 Grace Waivers <72h & 0% app fee.'
        : 'Acceso total 1 a 5 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -20%, 3 Perdones <72h y 0% cuota app.',
      matcher: (w: number) => w >= 4,
    },
    {
      tierKey: 'gold',
      label: isEn ? 'Gold' : 'Oro',
      color: '#FFCC00',
      precioMensual: 2529,
      beneficioResumen: isEn
        ? '1–5 Star access, Flash Sales -15%, 2 Grace Waivers <72h & 1:1 VIP parity.'
        : 'Acceso 1 a 5 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -15%, 2 Perdones <72h y paridad VIP 1:1.',
      matcher: (w: number) => w === 3,
    },
    {
      tierKey: 'silver',
      label: isEn ? 'Silver' : 'Plata',
      color: '#A8B2C1',
      precioMensual: 1549,
      beneficioResumen: isEn
        ? '1–4 Star access, Flash Sales -10%, 1 Grace Waiver <72h & zero-fee top-ups.'
        : 'Acceso 1 a 4 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -10%, 1 Perdón <72h y recargas sin comisión.',
      matcher: (w: number) => w === 2,
    },
    {
      tierKey: 'bronze',
      label: isEn ? 'Bronze' : 'Bronce',
      color: '#CD7F32',
      precioMensual: 699,
      beneficioResumen: isEn
        ? '1–3 Star access, Flash Sales -5% & direct top-ups.'
        : 'Acceso 1 a 3 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -5% y recargas directas sin cuota adicional.',
      matcher: (w: number) => w === 1,
    },
    {
      tierKey: 'base',
      label: isEn ? 'Base Access' : 'Acceso Base',
      color: '#00FF66',
      precioMensual: 0,
      beneficioResumen: isEn
        ? 'Free showcase tier ($0 MXN/mo). Explore catalog and book experiences via wallet.'
        : 'Modo escaparate gratuito ($0 MXN/mes). Permite explorar y reservar mediante billetera.',
      matcher: (w: number) => w === 0,
    },
  ];

  const distribucionNiveles = tiersConfig.map((t) => {
    const matched = safeAthletes.filter((a: any) =>
      t.matcher(getLevelNumericWeight(a.nivel))
    );
    const count = matched.length;
    const paidCount = t.precioMensual > 0 ? matched.filter(isPaidActiveVipSubscriber).length : 0;
    const saldoAcumuladoKc = matched.reduce(
      (acc: number, a: any) => acc + (Number(a.saldo_total_kc) || 0),
      0
    );
    return {
      tierKey: t.tierKey,
      label: t.label,
      color: t.color,
      count,
      paidCount,
      pct: safeAthletes.length > 0 ? Math.round((count / totalAtletas) * 100) : 0,
      precioMensual: t.precioMensual,
      ingresoRecurrenteMxn: paidCount * t.precioMensual,
      saldoAcumuladoKc,
      beneficioResumen: t.beneficioResumen,
      atletas: matched.map((a: any) => ({
        id: a.id,
        nombre: a.nombre || (isEn ? 'Athlete' : 'Atleta'),
        saldo_total_kc: a.saldo_total_kc,
        nivel: a.nivel || 'Acceso Base',
      })),
    };
  });

  const discMap = new Map<
    string,
    { count: number; volumenKc: number; completadas: number; clubesSet: Set<string> }
  >();
  const VALID_BOOKING_STATUSES = ['completada', 'activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada'];
  const eligibleReservas = (reservasValidas || []).filter((r: any) => {
    if (
      isMasterPass0Kc(r) ||
      isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r) ||
      isAdminOrB2BAccount(
        r?.perfiles || { nombre: r?.atleta_nombre, email: r?.atleta_email || r?.email, rol: r?.atleta_rol }
      )
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    return VALID_BOOKING_STATUSES.includes(st) && st !== 'cancelada';
  });
  eligibleReservas.forEach((r: any) => {
    const cat = String(
      r.categoria || r.servicio || r.nombre_servicio || 'Fuerza'
    ).trim();
    const prev = discMap.get(cat) || {
      count: 0,
      volumenKc: 0,
      completadas: 0,
      clubesSet: new Set<string>(),
    };
    const monto = Math.max(
      Number(r.costo_total) || 0,
      Number(r.precio_kc) || 0,
      (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
    );
    prev.count += 1;
    prev.volumenKc += monto;
    if (String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'completada') {
      prev.completadas += 1;
    }
    if (r.negocio_nombre) prev.clubesSet.add(String(r.negocio_nombre));
    discMap.set(cat, prev);
  });

  const totalResCount = Math.max(1, eligibleReservas.length);
  const topDisciplinas = Array.from(discMap.entries())
    .map(([cat, info]) => ({
      cat,
      count: info.count,
      pct: Math.round((info.count / totalResCount) * 100),
      volumenKc: info.volumenKc,
      completadas: info.completadas,
      tasaAsistenciaPct:
        info.count > 0 ? Math.round((info.completadas / info.count) * 100) : 0,
      clubes: Array.from(info.clubesSet),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
    .slice(0, 8);

  return { distribucionNiveles, topDisciplinas };
}

    FILE: src/services/admin/adminOpsHelpers.ts

    // === src/services/admin/adminOpsHelpers.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { fetchAllDeterministicRangePages } from '../../utils/kineticRangePaginator';
import { normalizeAffiliateKey } from '../adminB2BCutEngine';

type OpsDynamicRow = Record<string, unknown>;

export {
  deleteKinetiClubPost,
  deleteKinetiClubStory,
  deleteKinetiClubTeam,
  fetchKinetiClubModerationData,
  fetchPendingUgcReportsCount,
  getInitialKinetiClubModerationSnapshot,
  isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow,
  updateKinetiClubUgcReportStatus,
} from './adminUgcModerationHelpers';

export const DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_deleted_affiliates_v1';
const EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_events_snapshot_v2';

const emitBovedaUpdated = () => {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kinetic:boveda-updated')); } catch {}
  }
};

async function refundSingleWalletSafe(userId: string, amount: number, wallet: 'membresia' | 'topup'): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || amount <= 0) return;
  const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('inyectar_saldo_boveda', { p_user_id: userId, p_cantidad: amount, p_billetera: wallet });
  if (rpcErr) throw new Error(rpcErr.message);
}

/**
 * Reembolsa el 40% Escrow a todos los atletas con reserva activa/pendiente/confirmada,
 * elimina en cascada TODAS las reservas asociadas (por servicio_id y huérfanas con
 * servicio_id IS NULL cuyo servicio o nombre_servicio coincida con el título del evento)
 * y finalmente elimina el evento de public.servicios.
 */
export async function deleteServiceWithCascadeAndRefund(
  serviceId: string,
  fallbackTitle?: string
): Promise<{ refundedAthletes: number; deletedReservations: number }> {
  const cleanId = String(serviceId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) throw new Error('ID de servicio inválido.');

  const { data: servRow } = await supabase
    .from('servicios')
    .select('id, nombre_servicio, negocio_nombre')
    .eq('id', cleanId)
    .maybeSingle();

  const rawTitle = String(servRow?.nombre_servicio || fallbackTitle || '').trim();
  const normTitle = rawTitle.toLowerCase();

  const [byIdRes, orphanNullRes] = await Promise.all([
    fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<OpsDynamicRow>(
      (from, to) =>
        supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select(
            'id, usuario_id, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, estado, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup'
          )
          .eq('servicio_id', cleanId)
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to),
      { pageSize: 500 }
    ),
    normTitle
      ? fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<OpsDynamicRow>(
          (from, to) =>
            supabase
              .from('reservas')
              .select(
                'id, usuario_id, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, estado, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup'
              )
              .is('servicio_id', null)
              .order('id', { ascending: true })
              .range(from, to),
          { pageSize: 500 }
        )
      : Promise.resolve({ rows: [] as OpsDynamicRow[], totalFetched: 0, pagesFetched: 0, hasMore: false, isTruncated: false, error: null }),
  ]);

  const matchedMap = new Map<string, OpsDynamicRow>();
  (byIdRes.rows || []).forEach((r) => {
    if (r?.id) matchedMap.set(String(r.id), r);
  });

  if (normTitle && Array.isArray(orphanNullRes.rows)) {
    orphanNullRes.rows.forEach((r) => {
      if (!r?.id) return;
      const s1 = String(r.servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const s2 = String(r.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const s3 = String(r.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (s1 === normTitle || s2 === normTitle || s3 === normTitle) {
        matchedMap.set(String(r.id), r);
      }
    });
  }

  const allMatched = Array.from(matchedMap.values());
  let refundedAthletes = 0;
  const notifyTasks: Promise<unknown>[] = [];

  for (const r of allMatched) {
    const st = String(r.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const isRefundableStatus = st === 'activa' || st === 'pendiente' || st === 'confirmada';
    if (!isRefundableStatus || !r.usuario_id) continue;

    const escMem = Math.max(0, Number(r.escrow_membresia) || 0);
    const escTop = Math.max(0, Number(r.escrow_topup) || 0);
    const rawMontoEscrow = Number(r.monto_escrow);
    const fallbackTotal =
      !Number.isNaN(rawMontoEscrow) && rawMontoEscrow > 0
        ? Math.round(rawMontoEscrow)
        : 0;

    let refunded = false;
    let totalRefundKc = 0;
    if (escMem > 0 || escTop > 0) {
      if (escMem > 0) await refundSingleWalletSafe(String(r.usuario_id), escMem, 'membresia');
      if (escTop > 0) await refundSingleWalletSafe(String(r.usuario_id), escTop, 'topup');
      refunded = true;
      totalRefundKc = escMem + escTop;
    } else if (fallbackTotal > 0) {
      await refundSingleWalletSafe(String(r.usuario_id), fallbackTotal, 'membresia');
      refunded = true;
      totalRefundKc = fallbackTotal;
    }

    if (refunded) {
      refundedAthletes += 1;
      notifyTasks.push(
        Promise.resolve(
          supabase.from('notificaciones').insert({
            usuario_id: String(r.usuario_id),
            titulo: 'Reembolso 100% Escrow por Cancelación',
            mensaje: `Se ha reembolsado íntegramente ${totalRefundKc} KC a tu Bóveda por la cancelación del evento "${rawTitle || 'Experiencia'}". Tu recibo está disponible en Bóveda.`,
            tipo: 'reembolso',
            leida: false,
            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
        ),
        Promise.resolve(
          supabase.from('admin_audit_logs').insert({
            accion: 'CANCELACION_EVENTO_REEMBOLSO_100_ESCROW',
            usuario_id: String(r.usuario_id),
            servicio_id: cleanId,
            detalles: { evento: rawTitle, reembolso_kc: totalRefundKc, reserva_id: r.id },
            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
        )
      );
    }
  }

  if (notifyTasks.length > 0) {
    await Promise.allSettled(notifyTasks);
  }

  // Borrado total en cascada de public.reservas para erradicar pases huérfanos en Bóveda
  const matchedIds = allMatched.map((r) => String(r.id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (matchedIds.length > 0) {
    await supabase.from('reservas').delete().in('id', matchedIds);
  }
  await supabase.from('reservas').delete().eq('servicio_id', cleanId);

  if (rawTitle) {
    await Promise.allSettled([
      supabase.from('reservas').delete().is('servicio_id', null).ilike('servicio', rawTitle),
      supabase.from('reservas').delete().is('servicio_id', null).ilike('nombre_servicio', rawTitle),
    ]);
  }

  const { error: delServErr } = await supabase.from('servicios').delete().eq('id', cleanId);
  if (delServErr) throw new Error(delServErr.message);

  AsyncStorage.removeItem(EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => {});
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  if (refundedAthletes > 0 || matchedIds.length > 0) {
    emitBovedaUpdated();
  }

  return { refundedAthletes, deletedReservations: matchedIds.length };
}

// === PERSISTENCIA DE AFILIADOS ELIMINADOS (EVITA RESURRECCIÓN DE SEMILLA LOCAL) === //
export async function readDeletedAffiliatesSet(): Promise<Set<string>> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY);
    const arr = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    return new Set(Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.map((x) => String(x || '').trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean) : []);
  } catch {
    return new Set();
  }
}

export async function markAffiliateDeleted(id?: string | null, nombre?: string | null): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const set = await readDeletedAffiliatesSet();
    const cleanId = String(id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(nombre || ''));
    const rawLower = String(nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (cleanId) set.add(`id:${cleanId}`);
    if (normKey) set.add(`key:${normKey}`);
    if (rawLower) set.add(`name:${rawLower}`);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(set)));
  } catch {}
}

export async function unmarkAffiliateDeleted(id?: string | null, nombre?: string | null): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const set = await readDeletedAffiliatesSet();
    const cleanId = String(id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(nombre || ''));
    const rawLower = String(nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    set.delete(`id:${cleanId}`);
    set.delete(`key:${normKey}`);
    set.delete(`name:${rawLower}`);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(set)));
  } catch {}
}

export function isAffiliateMarkedDeleted(deletedSet: Set<string>, id?: string | null, nombre?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!deletedSet || deletedSet.size === 0) return false;
  const cleanId = String(id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(nombre || ''));
  const rawLower = String(nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return (
    (Boolean(cleanId) && deletedSet.has(`id:${cleanId}`)) ||
    (Boolean(normKey) && deletedSet.has(`key:${normKey}`)) ||
    (Boolean(rawLower) && deletedSet.has(`name:${rawLower}`))
  );
}

  id?: string | null;
  estado?: string | null;
  costo_total?: number | string | null;
  precio_kc?: number | string | null;
  monto_escrow?: number | string | null;
  monto_puerta?: number | string | null;
  es_pase_maestro?: boolean | null;
  tipo_pase?: string | null;
  updated_at?: string | null;
  created_at?: string | null;
  negocio_nombre?: string | null;
  servicio?: string | null;
