import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import {
  WALL_COLS_FULL,
  WALL_COLS_LEGACY,
  getMemoryWallSnapshot,
  isOffTopicGamingPost,
  purgePostFromMemoryWallSnapshot,
} from '../kineticlubWallSnapshotStore';
import {
  publishStoryEventToCloud,
  readLocalStoriesMap,
  syncStoriesFromCloud,
  writeLocalStoriesMap,
} from '../kineticStoriesService';

const WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY = '@kinetic_real_wall_posts_v26';
const LEGACY_WALL_POSTS_KEYS = ['@kinetic_social_feed_posts_v25', '@kinetic_community_feed_cache'];
const UGC_REPORT_OVERRIDES_KEY = '@kinetic_ugc_report_status_overrides_v1';

export interface IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot {
  posts: any[];
  stories: any[];
  teams: any[];
  reports: any[];
}

let _moderationSwrCache: IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot | null = null;

const cleanTeamLabel = (raw?: string | null, fallback = 'Equipo Kinetic'): string => {
  const cleaned = String(raw || '')
    .replace(/\bescuadr[oó]n\b/gi, 'Equipo')
    .replace(/\s+(?:en|in)\s+los\s+cabos\.?/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s*[•|-]\s*los\s+cabos\.?/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return cleaned || fallback;
};

const resolvePostMediaThumb = (p: any): string | null => {
  const direct = String(p?.imagen_url || p?.image_url || '').trim();
  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/|data:image\/)/i.test(direct)) return direct;
  const arr = Array.isArray(p?.galeria) ? p.galeria : Array.isArray(p?.mediaUris) ? p.mediaUris : [];
  const first = arr.find((u: any) => typeof u === 'string' && /^(?:https?:\/\/|data:image\/)/i.test(u.trim()));
  return first ? String(first).trim() : null;
};

export function getInitialKinetiClubModerationSnapshot(): IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot {
  if (_moderationSwrCache) return _moderationSwrCache;
  const memPosts = (getMemoryWallSnapshot().posts || [])
    .filter((p: any) => p?.id && !isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(p) && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p))
    .map((p: any) => ({
      ...p,
      id: String(p.id),
      autor_nombre: p.authorName || p.autor_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic',
      contenido: String(p.bodyEs || p.bodyEn || p.contenido || p.caption || '').trim() || 'Publicación multimedia en KinetiClub.',
      disciplina: p.discipline || p.disciplina || 'Comunidad',
      imagen_url: resolvePostMediaThumb(p),
      created_at: p.created_at || p.createdAt || '',
    }));
  return { posts: memPosts, stories: [], teams: [], reports: [] };
}

async function readUgcOverrides(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(UGC_REPORT_OVERRIDES_KEY);
    const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

async function writeUgcOverride(reportId: string, estado: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const current = await readUgcOverrides();
    current[reportId] = estado;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(UGC_REPORT_OVERRIDES_KEY, JSON.stringify(current));
  } catch {}
}

export function isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(row: any): boolean {
  if (!row) return true;
  const codeLower = String(row.codigo_invitacion || row.canal || row.channel || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const name = String(row.nombre || row.name || '').trim();
  const nameLower = name.toLowerCase();
  const disc = String(row.disciplina || row.tipo || '').trim().toLowerCase();

  return (
    codeLower.startsWith('dm-') ||
    codeLower.startsWith('dm__') ||
    codeLower === 'stories-v25' ||
    nameLower.startsWith('dm__') ||
    nameLower.startsWith('dm-') ||
    name.startsWith('{') ||
    disc === 'stories_24h' ||
    disc === 'dm' ||
    disc === 'privado_1a1' ||
    Boolean(row.es_mensaje_directo)
  );
}

const SQUAD_ADMIN_MOD_COLS =
  'id, nombre, creador_id, creador_nombre, disciplina, cupo_maximo, codigo_invitacion, created_at';

async function fetchSafeModerationPosts(limit = 80) {
  try {
    const channelFilter = 'disciplina.is.null,and(disciplina.neq.stories_24h,disciplina.neq.dm,disciplina.neq.privado_1a1)';
    const r1 = await supabase
      .from('comunidad_posts')
      .select(WALL_COLS_FULL)
      .or(channelFilter)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(limit);
    if (!r1.error && Array.isArray(r1.data)) return r1.data;
    const r2 = await supabase
      .from('comunidad_posts')
      .select(WALL_COLS_LEGACY)
      .or(channelFilter)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(limit);
    return !r2.error && Array.isArray(r2.data) ? r2.data : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

async function fetchSafeModerationTeams(limit = 100) {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('equipos_kinetic')
      .select(SQUAD_ADMIN_MOD_COLS)
      .or('codigo_invitacion.is.null,and(codigo_invitacion.neq.STORIES-V25,codigo_invitacion.not.like.dm-%,codigo_invitacion.not.like.dm__%)')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(limit);
    return !error && Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function fetchKinetiClubModerationData(): Promise<{
  posts: any[];
  stories: any[];
  teams: any[];
  reports: any[];
}> {
  const safeUgcReports = async (): Promise<any[]> => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('comunidad_reportes_ugc')
        .select('id, reporter_id, target_user_id, target_post_id, tipo_contenido, categoria_motivo, detalle, estado, created_at')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(80);
      return !error && Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  };

  const [
    comunidadPosts, storiesCloudMap, equiposKineticTbl, equipoMiembrosTbl,
    localWallRaw, ugcReportsRows, ugcOverrides,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    fetchSafeModerationPosts(80),
    syncStoriesFromCloud(true).catch(() => ({} as Record<string, any[]>)),
    fetchSafeModerationTeams(100),
    supabase.from('equipo_miembros').select('equipo_id, estado').limit(400).then((r) => (!r.error && Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : []), () => []),
    AsyncStorage.getItem(WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY).catch(() => null),
    safeUgcReports(),
    readUgcOverrides(),
  ]);

  let localWallPosts: any[] = [];
  if (localWallRaw) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(localWallRaw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        const cleanLocal = parsed.filter((p: any) => p && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p));
        if (cleanLocal.length !== parsed.length) {
          AsyncStorage.setItem(WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY, JSON.stringify(cleanLocal)).catch(() => {});
        }
        localWallPosts = cleanLocal;
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  const postsMap = new Map<string, any>();
  [...comunidadPosts, ...localWallPosts].forEach((p: any) => {
    if (!p?.id || isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(p) || isOffTopicGamingPost(p)) return;
    const text = String(p.contenido || p.texto || p.caption || p.bodyEs || p.bodyEn || p.mensaje || '').trim();
    if (text.startsWith('__KINETIC_') || isOffTopicGamingPost(text)) return;
    postsMap.set(String(p.id), {
      ...p,
      id: String(p.id),
      autor_nombre: p.autor_nombre || p.usuario_nombre || p.authorName || p.autor || 'Atleta Kinetic',
      contenido: text || 'Publicación multimedia en el Muro KinetiClub.',
      disciplina: p.disciplina || p.tag || p.categoria || 'Comunidad',
      imagen_url: resolvePostMediaThumb(p),
      created_at: p.created_at || p.createdAt || p.fecha || '',
    });
  });

  const storiesMap = new Map<string, any>();
  Object.values(storiesCloudMap || {}).forEach((arr: any) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return;
    arr.forEach((s: any) => {
      if (!s?.id) return;
      storiesMap.set(String(s.id), {
        id: String(s.id),
        ownerKey: s.ownerKey || '',
        autor_nombre: s.ownerName || 'Atleta Kinetic',
        contenido: s.caption || s.tag || 'Historia efímera activa (24h)',
        disciplina: s.tag || 'STORY 24H',
        imageUrl: s.imageUrl || '',
        created_at: s.createdAtMs ? new Date(Number(s.createdAtMs)).toISOString() : '',
      });
    });
  });

  const membersCountByTeam = new Map<string, number>();
  (equipoMiembrosTbl || []).forEach((m: any) => {
    const eid = String(m?.equipo_id || '').trim();
    const mst = String(m?.estado || 'aceptado').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!eid || mst === 'rechazado' || mst === 'eliminado') return;
    membersCountByTeam.set(eid, (membersCountByTeam.get(eid) || 0) + 1);
  });

  const teamsMap = new Map<string, any>();
  (equiposKineticTbl || []).forEach((t: any) => {
    if (!t?.id || isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(t)) return;
    const tid = String(t.id);
    const cleanedName = cleanTeamLabel(t.nombre, 'Equipo Kinetic');
    const cleanedDesc = cleanTeamLabel(
      t.descripcion,
      t.codigo_invitacion ? `Código de acceso: ${t.codigo_invitacion}` : 'Equipo deportivo activo en KinetiClub.'
    );
    teamsMap.set(tid, {
      ...t,
      id: tid,
      nombre: cleanedName,
      disciplina: t.disciplina || t.categoria || 'Fuerza',
      creador_nombre: t.creador_nombre || t.lider_nombre || 'Capitán Kinetic',
      miembros_count: Math.max(1, membersCountByTeam.get(tid) || Number(t.miembros_count) || 1),
      max_miembros: Math.max(2, Number(t.cupo_maximo || t.max_miembros) || 20),
      avatar_url: t.avatar_url || t.icono_url || null,
      descripcion: cleanedDesc,
    });
  });

  const rawReports = Array.isArray(ugcReportsRows) ? ugcReportsRows : [];
  const profileIds = Array.from(
    new Set(
      rawReports
        .flatMap((r: any) => [r?.reporter_id, r?.target_user_id])
        .map((id) => String(id || '').trim())
        .filter((id) => id.length >= 16)
    )
  );
  const namesMap: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (profileIds.length > 0) {
    try {
      const { data: pubRows } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles_publicos')
        .select('id, nombre')
        .in('id', profileIds);
      (pubRows || []).forEach((p: any) => {
        if (p?.id && p?.nombre) {
          namesMap[String(p.id)] = String(p.nombre).trim();
        }
      });
    } catch {}
    const missingIds = profileIds.filter((id) => !namesMap[id]);
    if (missingIds.length > 0) {
      try {
        const { data: perfRows } = await supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .select('id, nombre')
          .in('id', missingIds);
        (perfRows || []).forEach((p: any) => {
          if (p?.id && p?.nombre) {
            namesMap[String(p.id)] = String(p.nombre).trim();
          }
        });
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  const reports = rawReports.map((r) => {
    const rid = String(r?.id || '').trim();
    const dbEstado = String(r?.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim();
    const override = rid && dbEstado === 'pendiente' ? ugcOverrides[rid] : undefined;
    return {
      ...r,
      estado: override || dbEstado || 'pendiente',
      reporter_nombre: namesMap[String(r?.reporter_id || '')] || r?.reporter_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic',
      target_nombre: namesMap[String(r?.target_user_id || '')] || r?.target_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic',
    };
  });

  const snapshot: IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot = {
    posts: Array.from(postsMap.values()),
    stories: Array.from(storiesMap.values()),
    teams: Array.from(teamsMap.values()),
    reports,
  };
  _moderationSwrCache = snapshot;
  return snapshot;
}

export async function fetchPendingUgcReportsCount(): Promise<number> {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('comunidad_reportes_ugc')
      .select('id, estado')
      .limit(120);
    if (error || !Array.isArray(data)) return 0;
    return data.filter(
      (row) => String(row?.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim() === 'pendiente'
    ).length;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}

export async function updateKinetiClubUgcReportStatus(
  reportId: string,
  estado: 'revisado' | 'accion_tomada' | 'descartado'
): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(reportId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  let persisted = false;
  try {
    const { data: sess } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!sess?.session?.user?.id) return false;
    const { error: adminRpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('admin_resolver_reporte_ugc', {
      p_reporte_id: cleanId,
      p_nuevo_estado: estado,
    });
    if (!adminRpcErr) {
      persisted = true;
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('comunidad_reportes_ugc')
        .update({ estado })
        .eq('id', cleanId);
      if (!error) {
        persisted = true;
      } else {
        const modRes = await supabase.rpc('moderar_reporte_ugc', { p_reporte_id: cleanId, p_estado: estado });
        if (!modRes.error) {
          persisted = true;
        } else {
          const resRpc = await supabase.rpc('resolver_reporte_ugc', { p_report_id: cleanId, p_estado: estado });
          if (!resRpc.error) persisted = true;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
  if (!persisted) return false;
  await writeUgcOverride(cleanId, estado);
  if (_moderationSwrCache) {
    _moderationSwrCache = {
      ..._moderationSwrCache,
      reports: _moderationSwrCache.reports.map((r) => (String(r?.id) === cleanId ? { ...r, estado } : r)),
    };
  }
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return true;
}

export async function deleteKinetiClubPost(postId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(postId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  let deleted = false;
  try {
    const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('eliminar_post_comunidad_admin', { p_post_id: cleanId });
    if (!rpcErr) deleted = true;
  } catch {}
  if (!deleted) {
    await supabase.from('comunidad_posts').delete().eq('id', cleanId).then(() => {}, () => {});
  }
  purgePostFromMemoryWallSnapshot(cleanId);
  if (_moderationSwrCache) {
    _moderationSwrCache = {
      ..._moderationSwrCache,
      posts: _moderationSwrCache.posts.filter((p) => String(p?.id) !== cleanId && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p)),
    };
  }
  for (const key of [WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY, ...LEGACY_WALL_POSTS_KEYS]) {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(key);
      if (!raw) continue;
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(
          key,
          JSON.stringify(parsed.filter((p: any) => String(p?.id) !== cleanId && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p)))
        );
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return true;
}

export async function deleteKinetiClubStory(
  storyId: string,
  ownerKey?: string,
  ownerName?: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(storyId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  try {
    const map = await readLocalStoriesMap();
    const nextMap: Record<string, any[]> = {};
    Object.keys(map || {}).forEach((k) => {
      const remaining = (map[k] || []).filter((s) => String(s?.id) !== cleanId);
      if (remaining.length > 0) nextMap[k] = remaining;
    });
    await writeLocalStoriesMap(nextMap);
  } catch {}
  await Promise.allSettled([
    publishStoryEventToCloud({
      type: 'DELETE_STORY',
      storyId: cleanId,
      ownerKey: ownerKey || ownerName || 'kinetic',
      ownerName: ownerName || 'Kinetic Oficial',
    }),
    supabase.from('kinetic_stories').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('historias_club').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('historias_24h').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
  ]);
  return true;
}

export async function deleteKinetiClubTeam(teamId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(teamId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  await Promise.allSettled([
    supabase.from('equipo_mensajes').delete().eq('equipo_id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('equipo_miembros').delete().eq('equipo_id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('equipos_kinetic').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('equipos').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('club_squads').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
  ]);
  return true;
}

    FILE: src/services/admin/adminWaitlistService.ts

    // === src/services/admin/adminWaitlistService.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { fetchAllDeterministicRangePages } from '../../utils/kineticRangePaginator';

type WaitlistDbRow = Record<string, unknown>;

const WAITLIST_STATUS_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_waitlist_status_map_v1';

export type WaitlistReviewState = 'pendiente' | 'aceptado' | 'descartado';

export interface IWaitlistPreRegistro {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;
  email: string;
  telefono: string;
  tipo_perfil: string;
  esSocioB2B: boolean;
  esEspecialistaB2B: boolean;
  ciudad_zona: string;
  disciplina_interes: string;
  origen: string;
  estado: 'pendiente' | 'aceptado' | 'descartado';
  estadoRevision: WaitlistReviewState;
  created_at: string | null;
  afiliado_id?: string | null;
  cambios_pendientes_b2b?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

function parsePendingB2BPayload(raw: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (!raw) return null;
  if (typeof raw === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.trim().startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

const B2B_RE = /\b(socio|b2b|club|estudio|especialista|consultorio|partner|clinic|studio|clinica|clínica)\b/i;
const SPEC_RE = /\b(especialista|consultorio|fisioterap|nutri|medico|médico|specialist|clinic|clínica)\b/i;

async function readLocalStatusMap(): Promise<Record<string, WaitlistReviewState>> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(WAITLIST_STATUS_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

async function writeLocalStatus(id: string, status: WaitlistReviewState): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const map = await readLocalStatusMap();
    map[String(id)] = status;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(WAITLIST_STATUS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
  } catch {
    // Silencioso en caso de cuota de almacenamiento llena
  }
}

function resolveRowStatus(row: WaitlistDbRow, localMap: Record<string, WaitlistReviewState>): WaitlistReviewState {
  const rawEstado = String(row?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (rawEstado === 'aceptado' || rawEstado === 'aprobado') return 'aceptado';
  if (rawEstado === 'descartado' || rawEstado === 'rechazado') return 'descartado';
  if (rawEstado === 'pendiente') {
    const idStr = String(row?.id || '');
    if (idStr && localMap[idStr] === 'aceptado') return 'aceptado';
    if (idStr && localMap[idStr] === 'descartado') return 'descartado';
    return 'pendiente';
  }
  const idStr = String(row?.id || '');
  if (idStr && localMap[idStr]) return localMap[idStr];
  const rawOrigen = String(row?.origen || '').toLowerCase();
  if (rawOrigen.includes('descartado')) return 'descartado';
  if (rawOrigen.includes('aceptado')) return 'aceptado';
  return 'pendiente';
}

export async function fetchWaitlistPreRegistros(): Promise<IWaitlistPreRegistro[]> {
  try {
    const [res, localMap] = await Promise.all([
      fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<WaitlistDbRow>(
        (from, to) =>
          supabase
            .from('fundadores_waitlist')
            .select('*')
            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
            .order('id', { ascending: true })
            .range(from, to),
        { pageSize: 500 }
      ),
      readLocalStatusMap(),
    ]);

    const rows = Array.isArray(res?.rows) ? res.rows : [];
    return rows
      .map((row: WaitlistDbRow, idx: number): IWaitlistPreRegistro => {
        const id = String(row?.id || `waitlist_${idx}_${row?.email || ''}`);
        const tipoPerfil = String(
          row?.tipo_perfil || row?.perfil_interes || 'Atleta Acceso Prioritario'
        ).trim();
        const rawOrigen = String(row?.origen || 'kineticapp.com.mx')
          .replace(/\s*•\s*(aceptado|descartado)/gi, '')
          .trim();
        const resolvedStatus = resolveRowStatus(row, localMap);
        return {
          id,
          nombre: String(row?.nombre || 'Registro Web').trim(),
          email: String(row?.email || '').trim(),
          telefono: String(row?.telefono || row?.whatsapp || '').trim(),
          tipo_perfil: tipoPerfil,
          esSocioB2B: B2B_RE.test(tipoPerfil),
          esEspecialistaB2B: SPEC_RE.test(tipoPerfil),
          ciudad_zona: String(row?.ciudad_zona || row?.zona || '').trim(),
          disciplina_interes: String(
            row?.disciplina_interes || row?.disciplina_favorita || ''
          ).trim(),
          origen: rawOrigen || 'kineticapp.com.mx',
          estado: resolvedStatus,
          estadoRevision: resolvedStatus,
          created_at: row?.created_at ? String(row.created_at) : null,
          afiliado_id: row?.afiliado_id ? String(row.afiliado_id).trim() : null,
          cambios_pendientes_b2b: parsePendingB2BPayload(row?.cambios_pendientes_b2b),
        };
      })
      .filter((item) => item.estadoRevision !== 'descartado');
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function resolverEstadoPreRegistro(
  registro: IWaitlistPreRegistro,
  accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar',
  onRegisterProspectB2B?: (payload: {
    nombre: string;
    disciplina: string;
    es_especialista: boolean;
    ubicacion: string;
    whatsapp: string;
    activo: boolean;
  }) => Promise<unknown>
): Promise<boolean> {
  const id = String(registro?.id || '').trim();
  if (!id) return false;
  const nextStatus: WaitlistReviewState = accion === 'aceptar' ? 'aceptado' : 'descartado';

  const cleanBaseOrigen = String(registro.origen || 'kineticapp.com.mx')
    .replace(/\s*•\s*(aceptado|descartado)/gi, '')
    .trim();

  if (accion === 'descartar') {
    const { error: delErr } = await supabase
      .from('fundadores_waitlist')
      .delete()
      .eq('id', id);
    if (delErr) {
      await supabase
        .from('fundadores_waitlist')
        .update({ estado: 'descartado' })
        .eq('id', id);
    }
    await writeLocalStatus(id, 'descartado');
  } else {
    const { data: updData, error: updErr } = await supabase
      .from('fundadores_waitlist')
      .update({ estado: 'aceptado' })
      .eq('id', id)
      .select('id');
    if (updErr || !updData || updData.length === 0) {
      await supabase
        .from('fundadores_waitlist')
        .update({ origen: `${cleanBaseOrigen} • aceptado` })
        .eq('id', id);
    }
    await writeLocalStatus(id, 'aceptado');

    if (registro.esSocioB2B && typeof onRegisterProspectB2B === 'function') {
      try {
        await onRegisterProspectB2B({
          nombre: registro.nombre,
          disciplina: registro.disciplina_interes || 'Fuerza',
          es_especialista: registro.esEspecialistaB2B,
          ubicacion: registro.ciudad_zona || 'Ubicación por confirmar',
          whatsapp: registro.telefono,
          activo: false,
        });
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  return true;
}

    FILE: src/services/adminB2BCutEngine.ts

    // === src/services/adminB2BCutEngine.ts === //
import {
  ICorteSemanalAfiliado,
  IDesgloseReservaCorte,
  IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO,
} from '../types';
import {
  DynamicAuditRecord,
  isAdminOrB2BAccount,
  isMasterPass0Kc,
  isOrphanEscrowReservation,
  isSandboxAccountOrReservation,
  MASTER_PASS_COUNT_STATUSES,
  normalizeAffiliateKey,
  resolveSatRetentionRates,
  validateOfficialSatRfc,
} from './adminSatAndAccountGuards';
import { getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds, parseMazatlanTimestampMs } from './kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

export {
  isAdminOrB2BAccount,
  isMasterPass0Kc,
  isOrphanEscrowReservation,
  isSandboxAccountOrReservation,
  normalizeAffiliateKey,
  resolveSatRetentionRates,
  validateOfficialSatRfc,
};

export interface IB2BPeriodOverride {
  startMs: number;
  endMs: number;
  inicioIso: string;
  finIso: string;
  strictWindow?: boolean;
}

const parseSafeTimestampMs = (rawDate: unknown): number => parseMazatlanTimestampMs(rawDate);

export interface ICorteSemanalAfiliadoConPases extends ICorteSemanalAfiliado {
  pases_maestros_dia: number;
  pases_maestros_semana: number;
  pases_maestros_mes: number;
}

export interface IReporteMaestroConPases extends IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO {
  periodoInicio: string;
  periodoFin: string;
  total_pases_maestros_dia: number;
  total_pases_maestros_semana: number;
  total_pases_maestros_mes: number;
  cortes_por_afiliado: ICorteSemanalAfiliadoConPases[];
}

export const calcularCorteSemanalB2B = (
  afiliados: DynamicAuditRecord[],
  reservas: DynamicAuditRecord[],
  periodOverride?: IB2BPeriodOverride,
  validServiceIds?: Set<string>,
  validServiceNames?: Set<string>
): IReporteMaestroConPases => {
  const nonOrphanReservas = (reservas || []).filter(
    (r) => !isOrphanEscrowReservation(r, validServiceIds, validServiceNames)
  );
  const cleanReservas = nonOrphanReservas.filter(
    (r) =>
      !isMasterPass0Kc(r) &&
      !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r) &&
      !isAdminOrB2BAccount(
        (r?.perfiles as DynamicAuditRecord | undefined) || {
          nombre: r?.atleta_nombre,
          email: r?.atleta_email || r?.email,
          rol: r?.atleta_rol,
        }
      )
  );
  const masterPassReservas = nonOrphanReservas.filter((r) => {
    if (!isMasterPass0Kc(r) || isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r)) return false;
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    return !st || MASTER_PASS_COUNT_STATUSES.has(st);
  });

  const mz = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds(new Date());
  const dayStartMs = mz.startOfTodayMs;
  const dayEndMs = mz.endOfTodayMs;
  const monthStartMs = mz.monthStartMs;
  const monthEndMs = mz.monthEndMs;
  const startMs = periodOverride ? periodOverride.startMs : mz.startMs;
  const endMs = periodOverride ? periodOverride.endMs : mz.endMs;
  const periodoInicio = periodOverride ? periodOverride.inicioIso : mz.periodoInicio;
  const periodoFin = periodOverride ? periodOverride.finIso : mz.periodoFin;
  const strictWindow = Boolean(periodOverride?.strictWindow);

  const hasWindowTransactions = strictWindow
    ? true
    : cleanReservas.some((r) => {
        const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (!['completada', 'no_show', 'penalizada'].includes(st)) return false;
        const ts = parseSafeTimestampMs(r?.updated_at || r?.fecha_clase || r?.created_at);
        return !Number.isNaN(ts) && ts >= startMs && ts <= endMs;
      });

  const countMasterPassWindows = (list: DynamicAuditRecord[]) => {
    let dia = 0;
    let semana = 0;
    let mes = 0;
    list.forEach((r) => {
      const ts = parseSafeTimestampMs(r.updated_at || r.fecha_clase || r.created_at);
      const effectiveTs = Number.isNaN(ts) ? Date.now() : ts;
      if (effectiveTs >= dayStartMs && effectiveTs <= dayEndMs) dia += 1;
      if (effectiveTs >= startMs && effectiveTs <= endMs) semana += 1;
      if (effectiveTs >= monthStartMs && effectiveTs <= monthEndMs) mes += 1;
    });
    return { dia, semana, mes };
  };

  const effectiveAfiliados: DynamicAuditRecord[] = [...(afiliados || [])];
  const seenKeys = new Set(
    effectiveAfiliados.map((af) => normalizeAffiliateKey(String(af?.nombre || ''))).filter(Boolean)
  );
  cleanReservas.forEach((r) => {
    const rawNeg = String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim();
    const k = normalizeAffiliateKey(rawNeg);
    if (!k || seenKeys.has(k)) return;
    seenKeys.add(k);
    effectiveAfiliados.push({
      id: k === 'kinetic oficial' ? 'kinetic_oficial' : `synth_${k.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}`,
      nombre: k === 'kinetic oficial' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : rawNeg,
      disciplina: 'Fitness',
      categoria: 'Club',
      estado: 'activo',
      regimen_fiscal: 'sin_rfc',
    });
  });

  const cortesPorAfiliado: ICorteSemanalAfiliadoConPases[] = effectiveAfiliados.map((af) => {
    const nombreAf = String(af.nombre || '').trim();
    const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(nombreAf);
    const reservasAf = cleanReservas.filter(
      (r) => normalizeAffiliateKey(String(r.negocio_nombre || '')) === normKey
    );
    const masterAf = masterPassReservas.filter(
      (r) => normalizeAffiliateKey(String(r.negocio_nombre || '')) === normKey
    );
    const masterCounts = countMasterPassWindows(masterAf);
    let volumenBruto = 0;
    let brutoCheckinsKc = 0;
    let brutoNoshowKc = 0;
    let custodiaActiva = 0;
    let countReservasActivas = 0;
    let sesionesCompletadas = 0;
    let compensacionesNoshow = 0;
    const desgloseReservas: IDesgloseReservaCorte[] = [];

    reservasAf.forEach((r) => {
      const estado = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const precioTotal = Math.max(
        Number(r.costo_total) || 0,
        Number(r.precio_kc) || 0,
        (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
      );
      const escrow = Number(r.monto_escrow) || Math.round(precioTotal * 0.4);
      const puerta = Number(r.monto_puerta) || Math.max(0, precioTotal - escrow);
      const rawDate = r.updated_at || r.fecha_clase || r.created_at;
      const ts = parseSafeTimestampMs(rawDate);
      const isInCurrentWeek = strictWindow
        ? !Number.isNaN(ts) && ts >= startMs && ts <= endMs
        : !hasWindowTransactions || Number.isNaN(ts) || (ts >= startMs && ts <= endMs);

      if (estado === 'completada' && isInCurrentWeek) {
        volumenBruto += precioTotal;
        brutoCheckinsKc += precioTotal;
        sesionesCompletadas += 1;
        desgloseReservas.push({
          id: String(r.id),
          folio: `#KNT-${String(r.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`,
          servicio: String(r.servicio || r.nombre_servicio || r.titulo_clase || 'Sesión Kinetic'),
          fecha: String(rawDate || periodoFin),
          estado: 'completada',
          precio_total_kc: precioTotal,
          monto_escrow_kc: escrow,
          monto_puerta_kc: puerta,
          ingreso_computable_mxn: precioTotal,
        });
      } else if ((estado === 'no_show' || estado === 'penalizada') && isInCurrentWeek) {
        volumenBruto += escrow;
        brutoNoshowKc += escrow;
        compensacionesNoshow += 1;
        desgloseReservas.push({
          id: String(r.id),
          folio: `#KNT-${String(r.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`,
          servicio: String(r.servicio || r.nombre_servicio || r.titulo_clase || 'Sesión (Garantía 40% No-Show)'),
          fecha: String(rawDate || periodoFin),
          estado: 'no_show',
          precio_total_kc: precioTotal,
          monto_escrow_kc: escrow,
          monto_puerta_kc: 0,
          ingreso_computable_mxn: escrow,
        });
      } else if (['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'].includes(estado)) {
        custodiaActiva += escrow;
        countReservasActivas += 1;
      }
    });

    volumenBruto = Number(volumenBruto.toFixed(2));
    brutoCheckinsKc = Number(brutoCheckinsKc.toFixed(2));
    brutoNoshowKc = Number(brutoNoshowKc.toFixed(2));
    const comisionTotal20 = Number((volumenBruto * 0.2).toFixed(2));
    const comisionBase = Number((comisionTotal20 / 1.16).toFixed(2));
    const comisionIva = Number((comisionTotal20 - comisionBase).toFixed(2));
    const subtotalSocio80 = Number((volumenBruto * 0.8).toFixed(2));

    const sat = resolveSatRetentionRates(af.rfc, af.regimen_fiscal, af.csf_validada);
    const retencionIsr = Number((subtotalSocio80 * (sat.tasaIsr / 100)).toFixed(2));
    const retencionIva = Number((subtotalSocio80 * (sat.tasaIva / 100)).toFixed(2));
    const totalRetenciones = Number((retencionIsr + retencionIva).toFixed(2));
    const netoSpei = Number(Math.max(0, subtotalSocio80 - totalRetenciones).toFixed(2));

    return {
      afiliado_id: String(af.id || ''),
      afiliado_nombre: nombreAf,
      categoria_operador: af.es_especialista ? 'Especialista' : 'Club',
      disciplina: String(af.disciplina || af.categoria || 'Fitness'),
      clabe_bancaria: String(af.clabe_bancaria || ''),
      rfc: sat.cleanRfc,
      regimen_fiscal: sat.regimen,
      csf_validada: sat.csfValidada,
      periodo_inicio: periodoInicio,
      periodo_fin: periodoFin,
      sesiones_completadas: sesionesCompletadas,
      compensaciones_noshow: compensacionesNoshow,
      checkins_completados_count: sesionesCompletadas,
      noshow_penalizados_count: compensacionesNoshow,
      bruto_checkins_kc: brutoCheckinsKc,
      bruto_noshow_kc: brutoNoshowKc,
      ingreso_bruto_devengado_kc: volumenBruto,
      volumen_bruto_mxn: volumenBruto,
      comision_kinetic_total_mxn: comisionTotal20,
      comision_kinetic_base_mxn: comisionBase,
      comision_kinetic_iva_mxn: comisionIva,
      subtotal_socio_80_mxn: subtotalSocio80,
      tasa_retencion_isr_pct: sat.tasaIsr,
      tasa_retencion_iva_pct: sat.tasaIva,
      retencion_isr_mxn: retencionIsr,
      retencion_iva_mxn: retencionIva,
      total_retenciones_sat_mxn: totalRetenciones,
      neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: netoSpei,
      en_custodia_escrow_kc: custodiaActiva,
      reservas_activas_escrow_count: countReservasActivas,
      pases_maestros_dia: masterCounts.dia,
      pases_maestros_semana: masterCounts.semana,
      pases_maestros_mes: masterCounts.mes,
      desglose_reservas: desgloseReservas,
    };
  });

  cortesPorAfiliado.sort(
    (a, b) =>
      b.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn - a.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn ||
      a.afiliado_nombre.localeCompare(b.afiliado_nombre, 'es')
  );

  const sumField = (fn: (c: ICorteSemanalAfiliado) => number) =>
    Number(cortesPorAfiliado.reduce((acc, c) => acc + fn(c), 0).toFixed(2));
  const totalMasterCounts = countMasterPassWindows(masterPassReservas);

  return {
    periodo_inicio: periodoInicio,
    periodo_fin: periodoFin,
    periodoInicio,
    periodoFin,
    zona_horaria: 'America/Mazatlan',
    generado_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    total_volumen_bruto_mxn: sumField((c) => c.volumen_bruto_mxn),
    total_comision_kinetic_20_mxn: sumField((c) => c.comision_kinetic_total_mxn),
    total_comision_kinetic_base_mxn: sumField((c) => c.comision_kinetic_base_mxn),
    total_comision_kinetic_iva_mxn: sumField((c) => c.comision_kinetic_iva_mxn),
    total_subtotal_socios_80_mxn: sumField((c) => c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn),
    total_retencion_isr_sat_mxn: sumField((c) => c.retencion_isr_mxn),
    total_retencion_iva_sat_mxn: sumField((c) => c.retencion_iva_mxn),
    total_retenciones_sat_mxn: sumField((c) => c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn),
    total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: sumField((c) => c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn),
    total_pases_maestros_dia: totalMasterCounts.dia,
    total_pases_maestros_semana: totalMasterCounts.semana,
    total_pases_maestros_mes: totalMasterCounts.mes,
    cortes_por_afiliado: cortesPorAfiliado,
  };
};

    FILE: src/services/adminDashboardFetcher.ts

    // === src/services/adminDashboardFetcher.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY } from '../constants/exploreData';
import {
  isSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error,
  setSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  supabase,
} from '../lib/supabase';
import { fetchAllDeterministicRangePages } from '../utils/kineticRangePaginator';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import {
  type AdminDashRow,
  type IAdminSocioRecord,
  buildEnrichedAdminExperiences,
  buildEnrichedAdminSociosList,
} from './admin/adminDashboardEnrichmentHelpers';
import { computeAdminTierAndDisciplineMetrics } from './admin/adminMetricsCalculator';
import { fetchPendingUgcReportsCount, readDeletedAffiliatesSet } from './admin/adminOpsHelpers';
import { registerSessionCleanupHook } from './kineticSessionPersistence';
import { type IWaitlistPreRegistro, fetchWaitlistPreRegistros } from './admin/adminWaitlistService';
import {
  calcularCorteSemanalB2B,
  isAdminOrB2BAccount,
  isMasterPass0Kc,
  isOrphanEscrowReservation,
  isSandboxAccountOrReservation,
} from './adminB2BCutEngine';
import { getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds } from './kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

const ADMIN_PERFILES_COLS_BASE =
  'id, nombre, email, rol, tipo_cuenta, nivel, kc_membresia, kc_topup, racha_dias, racha, codigo_referido, codigo_amigo_squad, avatar_url, created_at';
const ADMIN_PERFILES_COLS = `${ADMIN_PERFILES_COLS_BASE}, es_cuenta_sandbox`;
const ADMIN_AFILIADOS_COLS =
  'id, propietario_id, nombre, disciplina, categoria, ubicacion, latitud, longitud, email_contacto, telefono, avatar_url, portada_url, galeria, descripcion, whatsapp, clabe_bancaria, instagram, horarios, amenidades, estado, verificado, es_especialista, rfc, regimen_fiscal, csf_validada, strikes_operativos, waiver_firmado, estrellas_exclusividad, created_at';
const ADMIN_SERVICIOS_COLS =
  'id, negocio_nombre, nombre_servicio, categoria, estado, precio_kc, cupo_maximo, fecha, hora_inicio, duracion_minutos, instructor_nombre, ubicacion, frecuencia, nivel_requerido, atributos, descripcion, imagen_url, portada_url, created_at';
const ADMIN_RESERVAS_COLS_BASE =
  'id, usuario_id, negocio_id, negocio_nombre, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, categoria, estado, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup, fecha_hora, fecha_clase, perdon_vip_aplicado, penalizacion_aplicada, created_at, updated_at';
const ADMIN_RESERVAS_COLS = `${ADMIN_RESERVAS_COLS_BASE}, es_cuenta_sandbox`;

export interface IAdminDashboardSnapshot {
  kpis: {
    masaMonetariaTotal: number;
    sumaMembresia: number;
    sumaTopup: number;
    saldoDisponibleUsuarios: number;
    escrowTotal: number;
    reservasActivasCount: number;
    atletasCount: number;
    clubesTotales: number;
    clubesActivos: number;
    clubesProspectos: number;
    liquidezTotalCapturada: number;
    comisionKinetic20: number;
    netoSocios80: number;
    mrrVipMxn: number;
    suscriptoresVipActivos: number;
    ticketPromedio: number;
    checkInsTotales: number;
    tasaConversionQR: number;
  };
  solicitudes: AdminDashRow[];
  preRegistrosWeb: IWaitlistPreRegistro[];
  ugcPendingCount: number;
  experienciasActivas: AdminDashRow[];
  sociosReales: IAdminSocioRecord[];
  atletasList: AdminDashRow[];
  reservasEscrowActivas: AdminDashRow[];
  reservasHistoricas: AdminDashRow[];
  auditLogs: AdminDashRow[];
  distribucionNiveles: ReturnType<typeof computeAdminTierAndDisciplineMetrics>['distribucionNiveles'];
  topDisciplinas: ReturnType<typeof computeAdminTierAndDisciplineMetrics>['topDisciplinas'];
  reporteCorteSemanal: ReturnType<typeof calcularCorteSemanalB2B>;
  paginationMeta: { hasMore: boolean; isTruncated: boolean };
}

const _adminDashboardCacheByUser = new Map<string, IAdminDashboardSnapshot>();

export function invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache(): void {
  _adminDashboardCacheByUser.clear();
}
registerSessionCleanupHook(invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache);

const isMissingSandboxColErr = (err: unknown) =>
  /es_cuenta_sandbox/i.test(String((err as { message?: string } | null)?.message || ''));

const asQuotaError = (err: unknown) =>
  (err || undefined) as Parameters<typeof isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error>[0];

export async function fetchAdminDashboardData(forceFresh = false): Promise<IAdminDashboardSnapshot> {
  const sessRes = typeof supabase?.auth?.getSession === 'function'
    ? await supabase.auth.getSession().catch(() => null)
    : null;
  const cacheOwnerUid = String(sessRes?.data?.session?.user?.id || '').trim();
  const backupSnapshot = cacheOwnerUid ? _adminDashboardCacheByUser.get(cacheOwnerUid) || null : null;
  if (forceFresh && cacheOwnerUid) _adminDashboardCacheByUser.delete(cacheOwnerUid);
  if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded() && backupSnapshot && !forceFresh) return backupSnapshot;
  const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';

  const fetchPagedPerfiles = async (cols: string) =>
    fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<AdminDashRow>(
      async (from, to) => {
        const res = await supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .select(cols)
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to);
        return { data: (res.data as unknown as AdminDashRow[] | null) || null, error: res.error };
      },
      { pageSize: 500 }
    );
  const fetchPagedReservas = async (cols: string) =>
    fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<AdminDashRow>(
      async (from, to) => {
        const res = await supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select(cols)
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
          .order('id', { ascending: true })
          .range(from, to);
        return { data: (res.data as unknown as AdminDashRow[] | null) || null, error: res.error };
      },
      { pageSize: 500 }
    );

  let [perfRes, afilRes, servRes, resReq, auditRes, preRegistrosWeb, deletedAffSet, runtimeAffRaw, ugcPendingCount, sqlKpiRes] =
    await Promise.all([
      fetchPagedPerfiles(ADMIN_PERFILES_COLS),
      fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<AdminDashRow>(
        (from, to) =>
          supabase
            .from('afiliados')
            .select(ADMIN_AFILIADOS_COLS)
            .order('nombre', { ascending: true })
            .order('id', { ascending: true })
            .range(from, to),
        { pageSize: 500 }
      ),
      fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<AdminDashRow>(
        (from, to) =>
          supabase
            .from('servicios')
            .select(ADMIN_SERVICIOS_COLS)
            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
            .order('id', { ascending: true })
            .range(from, to),
        { pageSize: 500 }
      ),
      fetchPagedReservas(ADMIN_RESERVAS_COLS),
      fetchAllDeterministicRangePages<AdminDashRow>(
        (from, to) =>
          supabase
            .from('admin_audit_logs')
            .select('id, admin_id, target_user_id, accion, cantidad_kc, created_at')
            .not('accion', 'ilike', '__KINETIC_%')
            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
            .order('id', { ascending: true })
            .range(from, to),
        { pageSize: 500 }
      ),
      fetchWaitlistPreRegistros(),
      readDeletedAffiliatesSet(),
      AsyncStorage.getItem(AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY).catch(() => null),
      fetchPendingUgcReportsCount(),
      Promise.resolve(supabase.rpc('admin_obtener_kpis_globales_v282')).catch(() => ({
        data: null,
        error: new Error('rpc_unavailable'),
      })),
    ]);

  if (perfRes.error && isMissingSandboxColErr(perfRes.error)) {
    perfRes = await fetchPagedPerfiles(ADMIN_PERFILES_COLS_BASE);
  }
  if (resReq.error && isMissingSandboxColErr(resReq.error)) {
    resReq = await fetchPagedReservas(ADMIN_RESERVAS_COLS_BASE);
  }

  if (
    isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(asQuotaError(perfRes.error)) ||
    isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(asQuotaError(afilRes.error)) ||
    isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(asQuotaError(resReq.error))
  ) {
    setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
    if (backupSnapshot && cacheOwnerUid) {
      _adminDashboardCacheByUser.set(cacheOwnerUid, backupSnapshot);
      return backupSnapshot;
    }
  }
  if (perfRes.error && afilRes.error && resReq.error && backupSnapshot && cacheOwnerUid) {
    _adminDashboardCacheByUser.set(cacheOwnerUid, backupSnapshot);
    return backupSnapshot;
  }

  const perfilesRaw = perfRes.rows || [];
  const afiliadosDb = afilRes.rows || [];
  const serviciosRaw = servRes.rows || [];
  const rawReservasDb = resReq.rows || [];
  const auditRaw = auditRes.rows || [];

  const sandboxUserIds = new Set<string>();
  perfilesRaw.forEach((p) => {
    if (p?.id && p?.es_cuenta_sandbox === true) sandboxUserIds.add(String(p.id));
  });
  const enrichedPerfilesRaw = perfilesRaw;

  const profileById = new Map<string, AdminDashRow>();
  const athleteNameById = new Map<string, string>();
  const athleteCodeById = new Map<string, string>();
  enrichedPerfilesRaw.forEach((p) => {
    if (p?.id) {
      const idStr = String(p.id);
      profileById.set(idStr, p);
      const emailStr = typeof p.email === 'string' ? p.email : '';
      athleteNameById.set(
        idStr,
        String(p.nombre || emailStr.split('@')[0] || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Athlete' : 'Atleta Kinetic'))
      );
      athleteCodeById.set(idStr, String(p.codigo_referido || p.codigo_amigo_squad || '').trim());
    }
  });

  const serviceById = new Map<string, AdminDashRow>();
  const serviceByName = new Map<string, AdminDashRow>();
  const validServiceIds = new Set<string>();
  const validServiceNames = new Set<string>();
  serviciosRaw.forEach((s) => {
    const sid = String(s?.id || '').trim();
    const sname = String(s?.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (sid) { validServiceIds.add(sid); serviceById.set(sid, s); }
    if (sname) { validServiceNames.add(sname); if (!serviceByName.has(sname)) serviceByName.set(sname, s); }
  });

  const reservasRaw = rawReservasDb
    .map((r) => {
      const uid = String(r?.usuario_id || '');
      const prof = profileById.get(uid) || null;
      const isSbx = Boolean(r?.es_cuenta_sandbox || prof?.es_cuenta_sandbox || (uid && sandboxUserIds.has(uid)));
      return isSbx ? { ...r, es_cuenta_sandbox: true, perfiles: r?.perfiles || prof } : r;
    })
    .filter(
      (r) =>
        !isOrphanEscrowReservation(r, validServiceIds, validServiceNames) &&
        !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r, profileById.get(String(r?.usuario_id || '')) || null)
    );

  const lastCheckinByUser = new Map<string, string>();
  reservasRaw.forEach((r) => {
    if (String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'completada' && r.usuario_id) {
      const uid = String(r.usuario_id);
      const ts = String(r.updated_at || r.created_at || '');
      if (!lastCheckinByUser.has(uid) && ts) lastCheckinByUser.set(uid, ts);
    }
  });

  const atletasReales = enrichedPerfilesRaw
    .filter((p) => !isAdminOrB2BAccount(p) && !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(p, p))
    .map((p) => {
      const kcMem = Math.max(0, Number(p.kc_membresia) || 0);
      const kcTop = Math.max(0, Number(p.kc_topup) || 0);
      return {
        ...p,
        kc_membresia: kcMem,
        kc_topup: kcTop,
        saldo_total_kc: kcMem + kcTop,
        racha_dias: Math.max(0, Number(p.racha_dias ?? p.racha ?? 0)),
        ultimo_checkin: lastCheckinByUser.get(String(p.id)) || null,
      };
    });

  let sumaMembresiaLocal = 0;
  let sumaTopupLocal = 0;
  atletasReales.forEach((a) => {
    sumaMembresiaLocal += a.kc_membresia;
    sumaTopupLocal += a.kc_topup;
  });

  const auditLogs = auditRaw
    .filter((row) => !String(row?.accion || '').trim().toUpperCase().startsWith('__KINETIC_'))
    .map((row) => {
      const adminIdStr = row?.admin_id ? String(row.admin_id) : '';
      const targetIdStr = row?.target_user_id ? String(row.target_user_id) : '';
      return {
        ...row,
        cantidad_kc: Number(row?.cantidad_kc) || 0,
        admin_nombre: (adminIdStr && athleteNameById.get(adminIdStr)) || 'Kinetic Admin',
        atleta_nombre: (targetIdStr && athleteNameById.get(targetIdStr)) || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Athlete' : 'Atleta Kinetic'),
        atleta_codigo: (targetIdStr && athleteCodeById.get(targetIdStr)) || '',
      };
    });

  let escrowLocal = 0;
  let liquidezLocal = 0;
  let checkInsLocal = 0;
  const reservasEscrowActivas: AdminDashRow[] = [];
  const reservasHistoricas: AdminDashRow[] = [];

  reservasRaw.forEach((r) => {
    const st = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase();
    const precioTotal = Math.max(
      Number(r.costo_total) || 0,
      Number(r.precio_kc) || 0,
      (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
    );
    const escrow = Number(r.monto_escrow) || Math.round(precioTotal * 0.4);
    const puerta = Number(r.monto_puerta) || Math.max(0, precioTotal - escrow);
    const srv =
      (r.servicio_id ? serviceById.get(String(r.servicio_id).trim()) : null) ||
      serviceByName.get(String(r.servicio || r.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase()) ||
      null;
    const enrichedReserva: AdminDashRow = {
      ...r,
      categoria: r.categoria || srv?.categoria || null,
      hora_inicio: r.hora_inicio || srv?.hora_inicio || null,
      perfiles: r.perfiles || profileById.get(String(r.usuario_id || '')) || null,
      atleta_nombre: r.atleta_nombre || athleteNameById.get(String(r.usuario_id)) || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Athlete' : 'Atleta Kinetic'),
      folio_corto: `#KNT-${String(r.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`,
      monto_escrow: escrow,
      monto_puerta: puerta,
      precio_total_kc: precioTotal,
    };
    const isInternalOrSbx =
      isSandboxAccountOrReservation(enrichedReserva) ||
      isAdminOrB2BAccount(
        (enrichedReserva.perfiles as AdminDashRow | undefined) || {
          nombre: r.atleta_nombre,
          email: r.atleta_email || r.email,
          rol: r.atleta_rol,
        }
      );
    if (isInternalOrSbx) return;
    reservasHistoricas.push(enrichedReserva);
    if (isMasterPass0Kc(r)) return;

    if (['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'].includes(st)) {
      escrowLocal += escrow;
      reservasEscrowActivas.push(enrichedReserva);
    } else if (st === 'completada') {
      liquidezLocal += precioTotal;
      checkInsLocal += 1;
    } else if (st === 'no_show' || st === 'penalizada') {
      liquidezLocal += escrow;
    }
  });

  const { sociosReales, clubesActivos: clubesActivosLocal, socioByKey } = buildEnrichedAdminSociosList({
    afiliadosDb,
    serviciosRaw,
    reservasHistoricas,
    deletedAffSet,
    runtimeAffRaw,
  });
  const { solicitudes, experienciasActivas } = buildEnrichedAdminExperiences({
    serviciosRaw,
    reservasHistoricas,
    socioByKey,
  });
  const { distribucionNiveles, topDisciplinas } = computeAdminTierAndDisciplineMetrics(
    atletasReales,
    reservasHistoricas.filter((r) => !isMasterPass0Kc(r)),
    isEn
  );

  const sqlKpi =
    !sqlKpiRes?.error && sqlKpiRes?.data && typeof sqlKpiRes.data === 'object'
      ? (sqlKpiRes.data as AdminDashRow)
      : null;

  const sumaMembresia = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.suma_membresia_kc ?? sumaMembresiaLocal) : sumaMembresiaLocal;
  const sumaTopup = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.suma_topup_kc ?? sumaTopupLocal) : sumaTopupLocal;
  const escrowTotal = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.escrow_activo_kc ?? escrowLocal) : escrowLocal;
  const atletasCount = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.atletas_reales_count ?? atletasReales.length) : atletasReales.length;
  const reservasActivasCount = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.reservas_activas_count ?? reservasEscrowActivas.length) : reservasEscrowActivas.length;
  const liquidezTotalCapturada = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.liquidez_capturada_kc ?? liquidezLocal) : liquidezLocal;
  const comisionKinetic20 = Number(Number(sqlKpi?.comision_kinetic_20_kc ?? liquidezTotalCapturada * 0.2).toFixed(2));
  const netoSocios80 = Number(Number(sqlKpi?.neto_socios_80_kc ?? (liquidezTotalCapturada - comisionKinetic20)).toFixed(2));
  const checkInsTotales = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.checkins_totales ?? checkInsLocal) : checkInsLocal;
  const mrrVipMxn = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.mrr_vip_mxn ?? 0) : 0;
  const suscriptoresVipActivos = sqlKpi ? Number(sqlKpi.suscriptores_vip_activos ?? 0) : 0;
  const clubesTotales = sqlKpi ? Math.max(sociosReales.length, Number(sqlKpi.afiliados_totales_count) || 0) : sociosReales.length;
  const clubesActivos = sqlKpi ? Math.max(clubesActivosLocal, Number(sqlKpi.afiliados_activos_count) || 0) : clubesActivosLocal;
  const clubesProspectos = Math.max(0, clubesTotales - clubesActivos);
  const ticketPromedio = checkInsTotales > 0 ? Math.round(liquidezTotalCapturada / checkInsTotales) : 0;
  const totalOperaciones = checkInsTotales + reservasActivasCount;
  const tasaConversionQR = totalOperaciones > 0 ? Math.round((checkInsTotales / totalOperaciones) * 100) : 0;

  const mz = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds();
  const liveWeekOverride = { startMs: mz.startMs, endMs: mz.endMs, inicioIso: mz.periodoInicio, finIso: mz.periodoFin, strictWindow: true };
  const paginationMeta = {
    hasMore: Boolean(perfRes.hasMore || afilRes.hasMore || servRes.hasMore || resReq.hasMore),
    isTruncated: Boolean(perfRes.isTruncated || afilRes.isTruncated || servRes.isTruncated || resReq.isTruncated),
  };

  const dashboardResult: IAdminDashboardSnapshot = {
    kpis: {
      masaMonetariaTotal: sumaMembresia + sumaTopup + escrowTotal,
      sumaMembresia,
      sumaTopup,
      saldoDisponibleUsuarios: sumaMembresia + sumaTopup,
      escrowTotal,
      reservasActivasCount,
      atletasCount,
      clubesTotales,
      clubesActivos,
      clubesProspectos,
      liquidezTotalCapturada,
      comisionKinetic20,
      netoSocios80,
      mrrVipMxn,
      suscriptoresVipActivos,
      ticketPromedio,
      checkInsTotales,
      tasaConversionQR,
    },
    solicitudes,
    preRegistrosWeb,
    ugcPendingCount: sqlKpi
      ? Math.max(Number(ugcPendingCount) || 0, Number(sqlKpi.ugc_pendientes_count) || 0)
      : Number(ugcPendingCount) || 0,
    experienciasActivas,
    sociosReales,
    atletasList: atletasReales,
    reservasEscrowActivas,
    reservasHistoricas,
    auditLogs,
    distribucionNiveles,
    topDisciplinas,
    reporteCorteSemanal: calcularCorteSemanalB2B(sociosReales, reservasHistoricas, liveWeekOverride, validServiceIds, validServiceNames),
    paginationMeta,
  };
  if (cacheOwnerUid) _adminDashboardCacheByUser.set(cacheOwnerUid, dashboardResult);
  return dashboardResult;
}

    FILE: src/services/adminPdfTemplates.ts

import { formatSatRegimenLabel } from '../styles/billingCfdi.styles';
import { ICorteSemanalAfiliado, IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../types';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { maskClabeAccount } from './kineticSecurityMfaService';
import {
  IPdfInvoiceConfig,
  IPdfTableRow,
  buildKineticInvoicePdfBytes,
  escapeHtmlSafe,
  formatMxn,
  safeOpenOrShareInvoicePdf,
  uploadAndOpenInvoicePdf,
} from './kineticPdfEngine';

export {
  IPdfInvoiceConfig,
  IPdfTableRow,
  buildKineticInvoicePdfBytes,

export const sanitizePdfHtmlForBlob = (rawHtml: string): string => {
  const cspMeta = `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src data: https:;">`;
  const noScripts = String(rawHtml || '')
    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, '')
    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>/gi, '');
  if (/<head\b[^>]*>/i.test(noScripts)) {
    return noScripts.replace(/<head\b[^>]*>/i, (m) => `${m}${cspMeta}`);
  }
  return `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head>${cspMeta}</head><body>${noScripts}</body></html>`;
};

const formatSignedDeductionMxn = (val: number) => {
  const n = Number(val) || 0;
  return Math.abs(n) < 0.005 ? formatMxn(0) : `-${formatMxn(Math.abs(n))}`;
};

const formatPdfPeriodDate = (rawIso: string | undefined, isEn: boolean): string => {
  if (!rawIso) return '';
  const clean = String(rawIso).trim();
  const parsed = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(clean)
    ? new Date(`${clean}T12:00:00`)
    : new Date(clean);
  if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return clean;
  return parsed.toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
  });
};

const localizeCategoryOperator = (cat: string | undefined, isEn: boolean): string => {
  const c = String(cat || 'Club').trim();
  if (!isEn) return c;
  return c.toLowerCase().includes('especialista') ? 'Specialist' : 'Club';
};

export interface IPasesMaestrosResumenPdf {
  dia?: number;
  semana?: number;
  mes?: number;
}

type CorteSocioPdfExt = ICorteSemanalAfiliado & {
  pases_maestros_dia?: number;
  pases_maestros_semana?: number;
  pases_maestros_mes?: number;
  esClubActivo?: boolean;
};

type ReporteMaestroPdfExt = IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO & {
  total_pases_maestros_dia?: number;
  total_pases_maestros_semana?: number;
  total_pases_maestros_mes?: number;
  cortes_por_afiliado: CorteSocioPdfExt[];
};

export const exportarPDFReporteMaestroCEO = async (
  reporte: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO,
  pasesMaestrosResumen?: IPasesMaestrosResumenPdf
) => {
  const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
  if (!reporte) {
    throw new Error(
      isEn
        ? 'No weekly settlement data available to export.'
        : 'No hay datos de corte semanal para exportar.'
    );
  }
  const repExt = reporte as ReporteMaestroPdfExt;
  const folio = `CEO-${String(reporte.periodo_fin || '').replace(/-/g, '')}`;
  const startLabel = formatPdfPeriodDate(reporte.periodo_inicio, isEn).toUpperCase();
  const endLabel = formatPdfPeriodDate(reporte.periodo_fin, isEn).toUpperCase();

  const allCortes: CorteSocioPdfExt[] = repExt.cortes_por_afiliado || [];
  const pasesDia = Number(pasesMaestrosResumen?.dia ?? repExt.total_pases_maestros_dia ?? 0) || 0;
  const pasesSemana = Number(
    pasesMaestrosResumen?.semana ??
      repExt.total_pases_maestros_semana ??
      allCortes.reduce((acc, c) => acc + (Number(c.pases_maestros_semana) || 0), 0)
  ) || 0;
  const pasesMes = Number(
    pasesMaestrosResumen?.mes ??
      repExt.total_pases_maestros_mes ??
      allCortes.reduce((acc, c) => acc + (Number(c.pases_maestros_mes) || 0), 0)
  ) || 0;

  const cortesActivos = allCortes.filter(
    (c) =>
      (Number(c.volumen_bruto_mxn) || 0) > 0 ||
      (Number(c.en_custodia_escrow_kc) || 0) > 0 ||
      (Number(c.sesiones_completadas) || 0) > 0 ||
      (Number(c.pases_maestros_semana) || 0) > 0 ||
      (Number(c.pases_maestros_mes) || 0) > 0 ||
      Boolean(c.esClubActivo)
  );
  const omitidosCount = Math.max(0, allCortes.length - cortesActivos.length);

  const tableRows: IPdfTableRow[] = cortesActivos.map((c, idx) => {
    const regimenShort = formatSatRegimenLabel(c.regimen_fiscal, isEn);
    const opCat = localizeCategoryOperator(c.categoria_operador, isEn);
    const pmSem = Number(c.pases_maestros_semana) || 0;
    const opStr = pmSem > 0
      ? `${c.sesiones_completadas}QR/${pmSem}PM`
      : `${c.sesiones_completadas} QR | ${c.en_custodia_escrow_kc} KC Esc`;
    return {
      idx: String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
      afiliado: String(c.afiliado_nombre || ''),
      disciplina: `${opCat} - ${String(c.disciplina || '')}`,
      rfcStatus: c.rfc ? `${c.rfc} (${regimenShort})` : regimenShort,
      clabe: c.clabe_bancaria ? maskClabeAccount(c.clabe_bancaria) : (isEn ? 'Pending registration' : 'Pendiente registro'),
      operacion: opStr,
      bruto: formatMxn(c.volumen_bruto_mxn),
      kinetic20: formatMxn(c.comision_kinetic_total_mxn),
      retSat: formatSignedDeductionMxn(c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn),
      netoSpei: formatMxn(c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn),
      hasPositivePayout: c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn > 0,
    };
  });

  return safeOpenOrShareInvoicePdf(
    isEn ? 'Kinetic_CEO_Weekly_Master_Report' : 'Reporte_Maestro_CEO_Kinetic',
    {
      lang: isEn ? 'en' : 'es',
      pdfKind: 'ceo',
      periodDateIso: reporte.periodo_fin,
      docTitle: isEn
        ? 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - CEO WEEKLY MASTER REPORT (SPEI / TAX)'
        : 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - REPORTE MAESTRO SEMANAL CEO (SPEI / SAT)',
      docSubtitle: isEn
        ? 'Consolidated B2B Settlement Statement, 20% Platform Fee & SAT Tax Withholdings'
        : 'Estado de Cuenta Consolidado de Dispersiones B2B, Comisión Tecnológica 20% y Retenciones Fiscales SAT',
      folio,
      periodoLabel: isEn
        ? `ACCOUNTING PERIOD: ${startLabel} TO ${endLabel} (SUNDAY 23:59:59 CUTOFF)`
        : `PERIODO CONTABLE: ${startLabel} AL ${endLabel} (CORTE DOMINGO 23:59:59 HRS)`,
      emisorInfo: isEn
        ? 'Corporate Issuer: KineticApp S.A.S. (Simplified Joint-Stock Company - Mexico)'
        : 'Entidad Emisora: KineticApp S.A.S. (Sociedad por Acciones Simplificada - México)',
      tableSectionTitle: isEn
        ? 'ACCOUNTING & BANKING SPEI PAYOUT BREAKDOWN BY AFFILIATE (PARITY 1 KC = $1.00 MXN)'
        : 'DESGLOSE CONTABLE Y BANCARIO DE DISPERSIONES SPEI POR AFILIADO (PARIDAD 1 KC = $1.00 MXN)',
      tableHeaders: isEn
        ? ['#', 'AFFILIATE / DISCIPLINE', 'TAX STATUS / CLABE', 'ACTIVITY', 'GROSS 100%', 'KINETIC 20%', 'SAT TAX', 'NET SPEI']
        : ['#', 'AFILIADO / DISCIPLINA', 'ESTATUS FISCAL / CLABE', 'OPERACIÓN', 'BRUTO 100%', 'KINETIC 20%', 'RET. SAT', 'NETO SPEI'],
      kpis: [
        {
          label: isEn ? 'GROSS VOLUME (100%)' : 'VOLUMEN BRUTO (100%)',
          value: formatMxn(reporte.total_volumen_bruto_mxn),
          sub: isEn ? 'Settled QR check-ins' : 'Check-ins QR liquidados',
          accentRgb: [0.06, 0.45, 0.68],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'KINETIC FEE (20%)' : 'COMISIÓN KINETIC (20%)',
          value: formatMxn(reporte.total_comision_kinetic_20_mxn),
          sub: isEn
            ? `Base ${formatMxn(reporte.total_comision_kinetic_base_mxn)} + VAT`
            : `Base ${formatMxn(reporte.total_comision_kinetic_base_mxn)} + IVA`,
          accentRgb: [0.55, 0.22, 0.82],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'SAT TAX WITHHOLDINGS' : 'RETENCIONES SAT',
          value: formatSignedDeductionMxn(reporte.total_retenciones_sat_mxn),
          sub: isEn
            ? `ISR ${formatMxn(reporte.total_retencion_isr_sat_mxn)} | VAT ${formatMxn(reporte.total_retencion_iva_sat_mxn)}`
            : `ISR ${formatMxn(reporte.total_retencion_isr_sat_mxn)} | IVA ${formatMxn(reporte.total_retencion_iva_sat_mxn)}`,
          accentRgb: [0.82, 0.2, 0.2],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'NET SPEI PAYOUT' : 'NETO SPEI A DISPERSAR',
          value: formatMxn(reporte.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn),
          sub: isEn ? 'B2B partner net liquidity' : 'Liquidez neta socios B2B',
          accentRgb: [0.0, 0.62, 0.28],
        },
      ],
      tableRows,
      detailBlocks: [
        {
          sectionTitle: isEn
            ? '0 KC MASTER PASSES AUDIT (EXEMPT FROM SPEI PAYOUT $0.00)'
            : 'AUDITORÍA PASES MAESTROS 0 KC (EXENTO DE DISPERSIÓN SPEI $0.00)',
          lines: [
            {
              label: isEn
                ? '0 KC Master Passes (Day / Week / Month):'
                : 'Pases Maestros 0 KC (Día / Semana / Mes):',
              value: isEn
                ? `${pasesDia} Day / ${pasesSemana} Week / ${pasesMes} Month (SPEI $0.00)`
                : `${pasesDia} Día / ${pasesSemana} Sem / ${pasesMes} Mes (Exento SPEI $0.00)`,
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      footerLegal: isEn
        ? [
            'OFFICIAL SETTLEMENT STATEMENT ISSUED BY KINETICAPP S.A.S. UNDER 1 KC = $1.00 MXN PARITY (VAT INCLUDED).',
            'Commercial Partners have 48 calendar hours from the issuance of this statement to request accounting clarifications.',
            'Tax withholdings calculated pursuant to Articles 113-A of the Mexican Income Tax Law (ISR) and 18-J of the VAT Law.',
            ...(omitidosCount > 0 ? [`* Omitted ${omitidosCount} prospective partner accounts with $0.00 activity.`] : []),
          ]
        : [
            'CARÁTULA OFICIAL DE LIQUIDACIÓN EMITIDA POR KINETICAPP S.A.S. BAJO PARIDAD 1 KC = $1.00 MXN (IVA INCLUIDO).',
            'El Socio Comercial cuenta con 48 horas naturales a partir de la emisión de este estado de cuenta para solicitar aclaraciones.',
            'Retenciones calculadas conforme a los Artículos 113-A de la Ley del ISR y 18-J de la Ley del IVA vigentes.',
            ...(omitidosCount > 0 ? [`* Se omitieron ${omitidosCount} cuentas prospecto con $0.00 de actividad.`] : []),
          ],
    }
  );
};

export const exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado = async (

) => {

  if (!corteSocio) {
    throw new Error(
      isEn

        : 'No se encontró el corte del afiliado seleccionado.'
    );
  }

  const pasesDia = Number(pasesMaestrosResumen?.dia ?? corteExt.pases_maestros_dia ?? 0) || 0;
  const pasesSemana = Number(pasesMaestrosResumen?.semana ?? corteExt.pases_maestros_semana ?? 0) || 0;
  const pasesMes = Number(pasesMaestrosResumen?.mes ?? corteExt.pases_maestros_mes ?? 0) || 0;
  const folio = `B2B-${String(corteSocio.afiliado_id || 'SOCIO')
    .slice(0, 6)
    .toUpperCase()}-${String(periodoFin || '').replace(/-/g, '')}`;

  const endLabel = formatPdfPeriodDate(periodoFin, isEn).toUpperCase();
  const opCat = localizeCategoryOperator(corteSocio.categoria_operador, isEn);

    `${isEn ? 'Account_Statement' : 'Estado_Cuenta'}_${corteSocio.afiliado_nombre}`,

        ? 'Issuer: KINETICAPP S.A.S. | Technology Intermediary & 40/60 Escrow Payment Processor'
        : 'Emisor: KINETICAPP S.A.S. | Intermediario Tecnológico y Procesador de Pagos en Custodia (Escrow 40/60)',
      kpis: [
        {
          label: isEn ? 'GROSS ACCRUED (100%)' : 'BRUTO DEVENGADO (100%)',
          value: formatMxn(corteSocio.volumen_bruto_mxn),
          sub: isEn ? `${corteSocio.sesiones_completadas} QR Check-ins` : `${corteSocio.sesiones_completadas} Check-ins QR`,
          accentRgb: [0.06, 0.45, 0.68],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'KINETIC FEE (20%)' : 'PEAJE KINETIC (20%)',
          value: formatMxn(corteSocio.comision_kinetic_total_mxn),
          sub: isEn ? 'Platform usage & VAT' : 'Uso de plataforma e IVA',
          accentRgb: [0.55, 0.22, 0.82],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'SAT TAX WITHHOLDINGS' : 'RETENCIONES SAT',
          value: formatSignedDeductionMxn(corteSocio.total_retenciones_sat_mxn),
          sub: isEn
            ? `ISR ${corteSocio.tasa_retencion_isr_pct}% | VAT ${corteSocio.tasa_retencion_iva_pct}%`
            : `ISR ${corteSocio.tasa_retencion_isr_pct}% | IVA ${corteSocio.tasa_retencion_iva_pct}%`,
          accentRgb: [0.82, 0.2, 0.2],

            { label: isEn ? 'Category & Discipline:' : 'Categoría y Disciplina:', value: `${opCat} - ${String(corteSocio.disciplina || '')}` },
            {
              label: isEn ? 'Registered SAT Tax ID (RFC):' : 'RFC Registrado ante SAT:',

            },

              value: corteSocio.clabe_bancaria ? maskClabeAccount(corteSocio.clabe_bancaria) : (isEn ? 'Pending registration' : 'Pendiente de registro'),
            },
          ],
        },
        {
          sectionTitle: isEn ? '2. WEEKLY SETTLEMENT FINANCIAL BREAKDOWN' : '2. DESGLOSE FINANCIERO DE LA LIQUIDACIÓN SEMANAL',
          lines: [

              value: formatMxn(corteSocio.volumen_bruto_mxn),

          ],

    FILE: src/services/adminSatAndAccountGuards.ts

    // === src/services/adminSatAndAccountGuards.ts === //
import { RegimenFiscalSAT } from '../types';
import {
  isKineticB2BRoleText,
  isKineticMasterAdminRole,
  isMasterAdminEmailOnly,
  normalizeRoleToken,
} from '../utils/kineticRoleLeafGuards';

export type DynamicAuditRecord = Record<string, unknown>;

const OFFICIAL_ACCOUNT_NAMES = new Set([
  'kinetic oficial', 'kinetic sports club', 'kinetic especialista',
  'kinetic admin', 'kineticapp s.a.s.', 'kineticapp sas',
]);

const SANDBOX_REVIEWER_EMAILS = new Set([
  'apple.tester@kineticapp.com.mx',
  'google.tester@kineticapp.com.mx',
  'reviewer@kineticapp.com.mx',
  'sandbox@kineticapp.com.mx',
]);

const GENERIC_SAT_RFCS = new Set(['XAXX010101000', 'XEXX010101000']);
const SAT_PM_RFC_RE = /^([A-Z&Ñ]{3})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})([A-Z0-9]{3})$/;
const SAT_PF_RFC_RE = /^([A-Z&Ñ]{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})([A-Z0-9]{3})$/;
const MASTER_PASS_COUNT_STATUSES = new Set(['completada', 'activa', 'confirmada', 'pendiente']);

export { MASTER_PASS_COUNT_STATUSES };

export const isAdminOrB2BAccount = (p: DynamicAuditRecord | null | undefined): boolean => {
  if (!p) return false;
  const email = normalizeRoleToken(p.email);
  const rol = normalizeRoleToken(p.rol);
  const tipoCuenta = normalizeRoleToken(p.tipo_cuenta);
  const nombre = String(p.nombre || '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();

  if (
    isMasterAdminEmailOnly(email) ||
    email.startsWith('administrador@') ||
    email.startsWith('club@') ||
    email.startsWith('especialista@')
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  return (
    isKineticMasterAdminRole(rol, email) ||
    isKineticMasterAdminRole(tipoCuenta, email) ||
    rol === 'ceo' ||
    tipoCuenta === 'ceo' ||
    isKineticB2BRoleText(rol) ||
    isKineticB2BRoleText(tipoCuenta) ||
    OFFICIAL_ACCOUNT_NAMES.has(nombre)
  );
};

export const isMasterPass0Kc = (r: DynamicAuditRecord | null | undefined): boolean => {
  if (!r) return false;
  if (r.es_pase_maestro === true || r.is_master_pass === true || r.pase_maestro === true) return true;
  const tipo = String(r.tipo_pase || r.tipo_reserva || r.origen || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (tipo.includes('pase_maestro') || tipo.includes('master_pass')) return true;
  const precioTotal = Math.max(
    Number(r.costo_total) || 0,
    Number(r.precio_kc) || 0,
    Number(r.precio_total_kc) || 0,
    (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
  );
  return precioTotal <= 0;
};

export const isSandboxAccountOrReservation = (
  r: DynamicAuditRecord | null | undefined,
  perfilOverride?: DynamicAuditRecord | null
): boolean => {
  if (!r && !perfilOverride) return false;
  const perfil =
    perfilOverride ||
    (r?.perfiles as DynamicAuditRecord | undefined) ||
    (r?.perfil as DynamicAuditRecord | undefined) ||
    {};
  if (
    r?.es_cuenta_sandbox === true || r?.is_sandbox === true || r?.sandbox === true ||
    r?.es_demo === true || r?.is_demo === true || r?.is_test === true ||
    perfil?.es_cuenta_sandbox === true || perfil?.is_sandbox === true || perfil?.sandbox === true ||
    perfil?.es_demo === true || perfil?.is_demo === true || perfil?.is_test === true
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  const roleStr = String(r?.atleta_rol || perfil?.rol || perfil?.tipo_cuenta || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (roleStr === 'sandbox' || roleStr === 'demo' || roleStr === 'test' || roleStr === 'qa' || roleStr === 'tester') {
    return true;
  }
  const emailCandidates = [r?.email, r?.atleta_email, r?.usuario_email, perfil?.email]
    .map((v) => String(v || '').toLowerCase().trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (
    emailCandidates.some(
      (em) =>
        SANDBOX_REVIEWER_EMAILS.has(em) ||
        em.endsWith('@sandbox.kineticapp.com.mx') ||
        em.startsWith('apple.tester@') ||
        em.startsWith('google.tester@') ||
        em.startsWith('sandbox.') ||
        /^(sandbox|demo|test|qa|tester)([._+@]|-|$)/.test(em) ||
        /[.+_-](sandbox|demo|qa|test|tester)@/.test(em) ||
        /\+(sandbox|test|demo|qa)@/.test(em)
    )
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  const originStr = String(r?.tipo_pase || r?.tipo_reserva || r?.origen || r?.canal_origen || '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim();
  if (originStr.includes('sandbox') || originStr.includes('app_review') || originStr.includes('appreview')) {
    return true;
  }
  const paymentRef = String(r?.stripe_payment_intent_id || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  return paymentRef.startsWith('sandbox_') || paymentRef.startsWith('test_') || paymentRef.startsWith('demo_');
};

export const isOrphanEscrowReservation = (
  r: DynamicAuditRecord | null | undefined,
  validServiceIds?: Set<string>,
  validServiceNames?: Set<string>
): boolean => {
  if (!r) return true;
  const st = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (st === 'cancelada' || st === 'rechazada' || st === 'eliminada') return true;
  const isActiveCustody = st === 'activa' || st === 'pendiente' || st === 'confirmada';
  if (!isActiveCustody) return false;
  if (!validServiceIds || validServiceIds.size === 0) return false;
  const sid = String(r.servicio_id || '').trim();
  if (sid) return !validServiceIds.has(sid);
  if (validServiceNames && validServiceNames.size > 0) {
    const candidateNames = [r.servicio, r.nombre_servicio, r.titulo_clase]
      .map((x) => String(x || '').trim().toLowerCase())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (candidateNames.length === 0) return true;
    return !candidateNames.some((n) => validServiceNames.has(n));
  }
  return false;
};

export const normalizeAffiliateKey = (rawName?: string): string => {
  const clean = String(rawName || '').trim().toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  if (!clean) return '';
  if (clean.includes('baja fitness') && clean.includes('medano')) return 'baja fitness medano';
  if (clean === 'palestra club') return 'palesttra club';
  if (clean.includes('40-0') || clean.includes('40 0')) return '40-0 padel club';
  if (clean.includes('physio cabo') || clean.includes('physiocabo')) return 'physiocabo sports rehab';
  if (clean.includes('fisio reba') || clean.includes('fisioreba')) return 'fisioreba rehabilitacion deportiva';
  if (clean.includes('fisio balance') || clean.includes('fisiobalance')) return 'fisiobalance clinica deportiva';
  for (const [needle, canonical] of AFFILIATE_ALIASES) {
    if (clean.includes(needle)) return canonical;
  }
  return clean;
};

const isValidSatRfcDate = (yy: number, mm: number, dd: number): boolean => {
  if (mm < 1 || mm > 12 || dd < 1) return false;
  if (mm === 4 || mm === 6 || mm === 9 || mm === 11) return dd <= 30;
  if (mm === 2) return dd <= (yy % 4 === 0 ? 29 : 28);
  return dd <= 31;
};

export interface ISatRfcValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  kind: 'persona_moral' | 'persona_fisica' | 'invalid';
  cleanRfc: string;
}

export const validateOfficialSatRfc = (rawRfc: unknown): ISatRfcValidationResult => {
  const cleanRfc = String(rawRfc ?? '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!cleanRfc || GENERIC_SAT_RFCS.has(cleanRfc)) {
    return { valid: false, kind: 'invalid', cleanRfc };
  }
  const pmMatch = cleanRfc.match(SAT_PM_RFC_RE);
  if (pmMatch && isValidSatRfcDate(Number(pmMatch[2]), Number(pmMatch[3]), Number(pmMatch[4]))) {
    return { valid: true, kind: 'persona_moral', cleanRfc };
  }
  const pfMatch = cleanRfc.match(SAT_PF_RFC_RE);
  if (pfMatch && isValidSatRfcDate(Number(pfMatch[2]), Number(pfMatch[3]), Number(pfMatch[4]))) {
    return { valid: true, kind: 'persona_fisica', cleanRfc };
  }
  return { valid: false, kind: 'invalid', cleanRfc };
};

export interface ISatRetentionRatesResult {
  regimen: RegimenFiscalSAT;
  tasaIsr: number;
  tasaIva: number;
  totalPct: number;
  csfValidada: boolean;
  rfcValido: boolean;
  cleanRfc: string;
}

export const resolveSatRetentionRates = (
  rawRfc: unknown,
  _rawRegimen?: unknown,
  csfValidada?: unknown
): ISatRetentionRatesResult => {
  const rfcCheck = validateOfficialSatRfc(rawRfc);
  if (!rfcCheck.valid) {
    return {
      regimen: 'sin_rfc',
      tasaIsr: 20,
      tasaIva: 16,
      totalPct: 36,
      csfValidada: false,
      rfcValido: false,
      cleanRfc: rfcCheck.cleanRfc,
    };
  }
  if (rfcCheck.kind === 'persona_moral') {
    return {
      regimen: 'persona_moral',
      tasaIsr: 0,
      tasaIva: 0,
      totalPct: 0,
      csfValidada: Boolean(csfValidada || true),
      rfcValido: true,
      cleanRfc: rfcCheck.cleanRfc,
    };
  }
  return {
    regimen: 'persona_fisica_con_rfc',
    tasaIsr: 2.1,
    tasaIva: 8,
    totalPct: 10.1,
    csfValidada: Boolean(csfValidada || true),
    rfcValido: true,
    cleanRfc: rfcCheck.cleanRfc,
  };
};

const _inFlightTreasuryUsers = new Set<string>();

export async function eliminarPerfilAtleta(
  atletaId: string
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; releasedSpots: number }> {
  const cleanId = String(atletaId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId || !UUID_ANY_RE.test(cleanId)) {
    throw new Error('Identificador de atleta inválido.');
  }
  const { data: perfRow } = await supabase
    .from('perfiles')
    .select('id, email, rol, tipo_cuenta')
    .eq('id', cleanId)
    .maybeSingle();
  const roleLow = String(perfRow?.rol || perfRow?.tipo_cuenta || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (
    (perfRow && isMasterAdminAccount(perfRow.email)) ||
    roleLow === 'admin' ||
    roleLow === 'fundador' ||
    roleLow === 'ceo'
  ) {
    throw new Error('No es posible eliminar una cuenta administradora maestra.');
  }

  const { data: rpcData, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('admin_eliminar_perfil_atleta_seguro', {
    p_atleta_id: cleanId,
  });
  if (rpcErr) {
    throw new Error(rpcErr.message);
  }

  const releasedSpots = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.round(Number((rpcData as any)?.released_spots ?? (rpcData as any)?.releasedSpots ?? 0) || 0)
  );
  invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return { ok: true, releasedSpots };
}

export async function conciliarCorteSemanalSql(
  fechaInicioIso: string,
  fechaFinIso: string
): Promise<any[]> {
  const cleanIni = String(fechaInicioIso || '').trim();
  const cleanFin = String(fechaFinIso || '').trim();
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(cleanIni) || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(cleanFin)) {
    throw new Error('Rango de fechas ISO inválido para conciliación SPEI SQL.');
  }
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_corte_semanal_b2b_sql', {
    p_fecha_inicio: cleanIni,
    p_fecha_fin: cleanFin,
  });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}
