  async toggleEstadoAfiliado(
    afiliado: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined,
    nuevoEstado: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'oculto' | 'prospecto'
  ) {
    const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
    if (!afiliado || !afiliado.nombre) {
      throw new Error(isEn ? 'Select a valid operator to update its status.' : 'Selecciona un operador válido para actualizar su estado.');
    }
    const isActivating = String(nuevoEstado).toLowerCase() === 'activo';
    const targetEstado: 'activo' | 'prospecto' = isActivating ? 'activo' : 'prospecto';
    const cleanId = String(afiliado.id || '').trim();
    const cleanNombre = String(afiliado.nombre || '').trim();
    const cleanWhatsapp = String(afiliado.whatsapp || '').trim();
    const cleanIg = extractInstagramFromAffiliate(afiliado);
    const cleanHorarios = stripInstagramMetaFromHorarios(typeof afiliado.horarios === 'string' ? afiliado.horarios : null) || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00';
    const amenidades = buildAmenidadesWithIg(afiliado.amenidades, cleanIg);
    const latNum = Number(afiliado.latitud ?? afiliado.latitude);
    const lonNum = Number(afiliado.longitud ?? afiliado.longitude);

    const { data: rpcRow, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('admin_upsert_afiliado_b2b', {
      p_id: cleanId && UUID_ANY_RE.test(cleanId) ? cleanId : null,
      p_nombre: cleanNombre,
      p_estado: targetEstado,
      p_disciplina: String(afiliado.disciplina || 'Fuerza').trim() || 'Fuerza',
      p_categoria: String(afiliado.categoria || (afiliado.es_especialista ? 'Especialista' : 'Club')).trim(),
      p_es_especialista: Boolean(afiliado.es_especialista),
      p_ubicacion: String(afiliado.ubicacion || 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.').trim(),
      p_latitud: Number.isFinite(latNum) && Math.abs(latNum) > 0.1 ? latNum : null,
      p_longitud: Number.isFinite(lonNum) && Math.abs(lonNum) > 0.1 ? lonNum : null,
      p_portada_url: typeof afiliado.portada_url === 'string' && afiliado.portada_url ? afiliado.portada_url : null,
      p_descripcion: typeof afiliado.descripcion === 'string' && afiliado.descripcion ? afiliado.descripcion : null,
      p_horarios: cleanHorarios,
      p_instagram: cleanIg || null,
      p_whatsapp: cleanWhatsapp || null,
    });
    if (rpcErr) throw new Error(rpcErr.message);

    const rpcObj = rpcRow && typeof rpcRow === 'object' ? (rpcRow as { id?: unknown; nombre?: unknown }) : null;
    const persistedId = String(rpcObj?.id || cleanId).trim() || cleanId;
    upsertRuntimeAffiliateState({
      ...afiliado,
      id: persistedId,
      nombre: String(rpcObj?.nombre || cleanNombre),
      whatsapp: cleanWhatsapp,
      instagram: cleanIg,
      horarios: cleanHorarios,
      amenidades,
      estado: targetEstado,
      verificado: isActivating,
      esClubActivo: isActivating,
    });

    exploreService.clearRadarCache();
    invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return {
      id: persistedId,
      nombre: String(rpcObj?.nombre || cleanNombre),
      whatsapp: cleanWhatsapp,
      instagram: cleanIg,
      horarios: cleanHorarios,
      estado: targetEstado,
      verificado: isActivating,
      esClubActivo: isActivating,
    };
  },

  async updateServiceStatus(id: string, nuevoEstado: 'aprobado' | 'rechazado' | string) {
    const norm = String(nuevoEstado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (norm === 'rechazado' || norm === 'eliminado' || norm === 'cancelado') {
      await deleteServiceWithCascadeAndRefund(id);
      invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
      return true;
    }
    const dbEstado = norm === 'activo' ? 'aprobado' : norm === 'oculto' ? 'inactivo' : nuevoEstado;
    const { error } = await supabase.from('servicios').update({ estado: dbEstado }).eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return true;
  },

  async cambiarEstadoServicio(id: string, nuevoEstado: string) {
    return adminService.updateServiceStatus(id, nuevoEstado);
  },

  async saveEvent(nuevoEvento: any, isEditing: boolean, _userId: string, negocioNombre: string) {
    const rawPrice = Math.max(0, Number(nuevoEvento.precio ?? nuevoEvento.precio_kc) || 0);
    const cleanPrice = rawPrice === 0 ? 0 : Math.round(rawPrice / 10) * 10;
    const cleanDescBase = String(nuevoEvento.descripcion || '').replace(/\[Frecuencia Programada:.*?\]/gi, '').trim();
    const freqLabel = nuevoEvento.frecuencia || 'Única Vez';
    const reqTraer = String(nuevoEvento.requisitos_traer || 'Agua / Hidratación personal').trim();
    const edadMin = String(nuevoEvento.edad_minima || '18+ Adultos').trim();
    const targetEstado = nuevoEvento.estado === 'inactivo' || nuevoEvento.activo === false ? 'inactivo' : 'aprobado';
    const basePayload: Record<string, any> = {
      nombre_servicio: String(nuevoEvento.titulo || nuevoEvento.nombre_servicio || '').trim(),
      categoria: nuevoEvento.disciplina || nuevoEvento.categoria || 'Fuerza',
      fecha: nuevoEvento.fecha,
      hora_inicio: nuevoEvento.hora_inicio || nuevoEvento.hora || '08:00',
      duracion_minutos: Number(nuevoEvento.duracion_minutos) || 60,
      cupo_maximo: Math.min(50, Math.max(1, Number(nuevoEvento.cupo ?? nuevoEvento.cupo_maximo) || 10)),
      precio_kc: cleanPrice,
      ubicacion: String(nuevoEvento.ubicacion || '').trim() || 'Ubicación Verificada',
      descripcion: `${cleanDescBase} [Frecuencia Programada: ${freqLabel}]`.trim(),
      instructor_nombre: String(
        nuevoEvento.instructor_nombre ||
          (getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en' ? 'Official Kinetic Coach' : 'Coach Oficial Kinetic')
      ).trim(),
      frecuencia: freqLabel,
      estado: targetEstado,
      atributos: { ...(nuevoEvento.atributos || {}), requisitos_traer: reqTraer, edad_minima: edadMin },
    };
    const geoPatch = await resolveServiceWriteOnceGeoPatch({
      serviceId: isEditing ? String(nuevoEvento.id || '') : '',
      ubicacion: String(basePayload.ubicacion || ''),
      existingPlaceId: typeof nuevoEvento.place_id === 'string' ? nuevoEvento.place_id : null,
      existingGeoVerifiedAt: typeof nuevoEvento.geo_verified_at === 'string' ? nuevoEvento.geo_verified_at : null,
      prevUbicacion: typeof nuevoEvento.prev_ubicacion === 'string' ? nuevoEvento.prev_ubicacion : null,
    });
    const fullPayload = { ...basePayload, ...geoPatch, requisitos_traer: reqTraer, edad_minima: edadMin };
    const safeGeoBase = (errMsg: string) => (/place_id|formatted_address|geo_verified_at/i.test(errMsg) ? basePayload : { ...basePayload, ...geoPatch });

    if (isEditing && nuevoEvento.id) {
      let { error } = await supabase.from('servicios').update(fullPayload).eq('id', nuevoEvento.id);
      if (error) ({ error } = await supabase.from('servicios').update(safeGeoBase(String(error.message || ''))).eq('id', nuevoEvento.id));
      if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
      invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
      emitKineticRadarUpdated();
      return true;
    }
    const hostName = nuevoEvento.negocio_nombre || negocioNombre || 'Kinetic Oficial';
    let { error } = await supabase.from('servicios').insert([{ ...fullPayload, negocio_nombre: hostName }]);
    if (error) ({ error } = await supabase.from('servicios').insert([{ ...safeGeoBase(String(error.message || '')), negocio_nombre: hostName }]));
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return true;
  },

  async deleteEvent(id: string) {
    const res = await deleteServiceWithCascadeAndRefund(id);
    invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
    return res;
  },

  async inyectarFondosAtleta(
    atletaId: string,
    montoDelta: number,
    billetera: 'membresia' | 'topup' = 'topup',
    nuevoNivel?: string | null,
    modoOperacion: 'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset' = 'inyectar',
    adminId?: string
  ) {
    const cleanTargetId = String(atletaId || '').trim();
    if (!UUID_ANY_RE.test(cleanTargetId)) {
      throw new Error('Identificador de atleta inválido para operación de tesorería.');
    }
    if (_inFlightTreasuryUsers.has(cleanTargetId)) {
      throw new Error(getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en' ? 'Treasury update already in progress.' : 'Actualización de tesorería ya en progreso.');
    }
    _inFlightTreasuryUsers.add(cleanTargetId);
    try {
      const { data: currentPerf, error: readErr } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles')
        .select('kc_membresia, kc_topup, nivel')
        .eq('id', cleanTargetId)
        .single();
      if (readErr) throw new Error(readErr.message);

      const rawMagnitude =
        modoOperacion === 'retirar' && typeof montoDelta === 'number' && Number.isFinite(montoDelta) && montoDelta < 0
          ? Math.abs(montoDelta)
          : montoDelta;

      const safeMut = computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation({
        currentMem: currentPerf?.kc_membresia,
        currentTop: currentPerf?.kc_topup,
        rawDelta: rawMagnitude,
        wallet: billetera,
        mode: modoOperacion,
        nuevoNivel,
      });
      if (!safeMut.ok) {
        const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
        throw new Error(
          isEn
            ? 'Invalid treasury amount or operation (must be 0..100,000 KC).'
            : 'Monto u operación de tesorería inválida (rango permitido 0..100,000 KC).'
        );
      }

      const { data: rpcRes, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('inyectar_saldo_boveda', {
        p_user_id: cleanTargetId,
        p_cantidad: safeMut.effectiveDelta,
        p_billetera: billetera,
        p_nuevo_nivel: nuevoNivel || null,
      });
      if (rpcErr) throw new Error(rpcErr.message);

      try {
        const resolvedAdminId =
          adminId || (await supabase.auth.getSession())?.data?.session?.user?.id || null;
        const detalles = `Wallet:${billetera} | Delta:${safeMut.effectiveDelta} KC | Proyectado:${safeMut.projectedMem}+${safeMut.projectedTop}=${safeMut.projectedTotal} KC${nuevoNivel ? ` | Nivel:${nuevoNivel}` : ''}`.slice(0, 180);
        const baseAuditRow: Record<string, any> = {
          ...(resolvedAdminId ? { admin_id: resolvedAdminId } : {}),
          target_user_id: cleanTargetId,
          accion: safeMut.auditAction,
          cantidad_kc: safeMut.effectiveDelta,
        };
        const { error: audErr } = await supabase
          .from('admin_audit_logs')
          .insert([{ ...baseAuditRow, detalles }]);
        if (audErr) {
          await supabase.from('admin_audit_logs').insert([{ ...baseAuditRow, motivo: detalles }]);
        }
      } catch {}

      invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
        try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kinetic:boveda-updated')); } catch {}
      }
      return {
        kc_membresia: rpcRes?.kc_membresia ?? safeMut.projectedMem,
        kc_topup: rpcRes?.kc_topup ?? safeMut.projectedTop,
        nivel: rpcRes?.nivel || nuevoNivel || currentPerf?.nivel || 'Acceso Base',
      };
    } finally {
      _inFlightTreasuryUsers.delete(cleanTargetId);
    }
  },

  async actualizarDatosFiscalesAfiliado(
    afiliadoId: string, rfc: string, regimenFiscal: RegimenFiscalSAT, clabeBancaria: string, csfValidada: boolean
  ) {
    const cleanRfc = String(rfc || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const cleanClabe = String(clabeBancaria || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 18);
    if (cleanClabe.length > 0 && cleanClabe.length !== 18) {
      throw new Error('La CLABE interbancaria SPEI debe tener exactamente 18 dígitos.');
    }
    upsertRuntimeAffiliateState({ id: afiliadoId, rfc: cleanRfc, regimen_fiscal: regimenFiscal, clabe_bancaria: cleanClabe, csf_validada: csfValidada });
    if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(String(afiliadoId || ''))) {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('afiliados').update({ rfc: cleanRfc, regimen_fiscal: regimenFiscal, clabe_bancaria: cleanClabe, csf_validada: csfValidada }).eq('id', afiliadoId);
      if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    }
    invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
    return true;
  },

  async actualizarStrikesAfiliado(afiliado: any, nextStrikes: number, motivo?: string) {
    const cleanStrikes = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Math.round(Number(nextStrikes) || 0)));
    const isSuspended = cleanStrikes >= 3;
    const cleanId = String(afiliado?.id || '').trim();
    const cleanNombre = String(afiliado?.nombre || '').trim();
    upsertRuntimeAffiliateState({
      id: cleanId, nombre: cleanNombre, strikes_operativos: cleanStrikes,
      ...(isSuspended ? { estado: 'inactivo', esClubActivo: false, verificado: false } : {}),
    });
    try {
      const updPayload: Record<string, any> = { strikes_operativos: cleanStrikes };
      if (isSuspended) updPayload.estado = 'inactivo';
      if (cleanId && UUID_ANY_RE.test(cleanId)) {
        await supabase.from('afiliados').update(updPayload).eq('id', cleanId);
      } else if (cleanNombre && !/[%_]/.test(cleanNombre)) {
        await supabase.from('afiliados').update(updPayload).ilike('nombre', escapeLikePattern(cleanNombre));
      }
      await supabase.from('admin_audit_logs').insert([{
        accion: cleanStrikes === 0 ? 'RESET_STRIKES_B2B' : `STRIKE_CALIDAD_B2B_${cleanStrikes}`,
        atleta_nombre: cleanNombre,
        motivo: motivo || (isSuspended ? 'Suspensión preventiva 3/3 Strikes' : `Strikes actualizados a ${cleanStrikes}/3`),
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      }]);
    } catch {}
    invalidateAdminDashboardFetcherCache();
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return { strikes: cleanStrikes, suspended: isSuspended };
  },

  async copiarAlPortapapeles(texto: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const clean = String(texto || '').trim();
    if (!clean) return false;
    try {
      if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator?.clipboard?.writeText) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(clean);
        return true;
      }
      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
      const RN = require('react-native');
      if (RN?.Clipboard?.setString) RN.Clipboard.setString(clean);
      return true;
    } catch { return true; }
  },

  eliminarPerfilAtleta,
  fetchKinetiClubModerationData,
  deleteKinetiClubPost,
  deleteKinetiClubStory,
  deleteKinetiClubTeam,
  exportarPDFReporteMaestroCEO,
  exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado,
  exportarPDFReciboReservaAtleta,
};

    FILE: src/services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService.ts

    // === src/services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService.ts === //
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { ICorteSemanalAfiliado, IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../types';
import { calcularCorteSemanalB2B, IB2BPeriodOverride } from './adminB2BCutEngine';
import { exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado } from './adminPdfTemplates';
import { clampStudioGridToMaxSpots, IStudioLayoutConfig, loadStudioLayoutLocal, MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, resolveDefaultStudioLayout, saveStudioLayoutLocal } from './spotCollisionService';
import { isNetworkOfflineError } from './terminalSwrCacheHelper';

export type TerminalQrFormat = 'kinetic_totp' | 'json' | 'manual' | 'invalid';
export interface ITerminalQrPacketResult { ok: boolean; qrIdentifier: string; totpStep: number | null; qrNonce: string | null; isExpired: boolean; format: TerminalQrFormat; }
export interface IPartnerPeriodOption { key: 'week_0' | 'week_1' | 'week_2' | 'month_0' | 'month_1'; label: string; rangeLabel: string; override: IB2BPeriodOverride; }
export interface ILiveStudioSpotItem { spotNumber: number; row: number; col: number; status: 'checked_in' | 'reserved' | 'free'; checkedIn: boolean; reservaId: string | null; athleteShortName: string | null; vipTier: string | null; folio: string | null; }
export interface ILiveStudioRosterResult { layout: IStudioLayoutConfig; spots: ILiveStudioSpotItem[]; totalSpots: number; occupiedCount: number; checkedInCount: number; waitingCount: number; freeCount: number; }

const PROTO_POISON = new Set(['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype']);
const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const SAFE_PASS_ID_RE = /^([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}|KNT-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,28}|opt[-_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{4,32}|[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,64})$/i;
const SAFE_MANUAL_RE = /^([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}|KNT-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,24}|[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,24})$/i;
const SAFE_NONCE_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,64}$/;
const INVALID_PACKET: ITerminalQrPacketResult = Object.freeze({ ok: false, qrIdentifier: '', totpStep: null, qrNonce: null, isExpired: false, format: 'invalid' });
const isPoisoned = (val: string): boolean => PROTO_POISON.has(val.trim().toLowerCase());
const hasPoisonOwnKeys = (obj: Record<string, unknown>): boolean =>
  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, '__proto__') || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'constructor') || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'prototype');

const isValidTotpWindow60s = (bucket: number, syncClockMs: number): boolean => {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(bucket) || bucket <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(syncClockMs)) return false;
  const cur = Math.floor(syncClockMs / 60_000);
  const offsetMs = ((syncClockMs % 60_000) + 60_000) % 60_000;
  return bucket === cur || (bucket === cur - 1 && offsetMs <= 10_000) || (bucket === cur + 1 && offsetMs >= 57_000);
};

export function parseTerminalQrPacket(raw: unknown, nowSyncedMs: number): ITerminalQrPacketResult {
  try {
    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
    const trimmed = raw.trim();
    if (!trimmed || trimmed.length > 2048 || isPoisoned(trimmed)) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
    const syncClock = Number.isFinite(nowSyncedMs) ? nowSyncedMs : Date.now();

    if (trimmed.includes('|')) {
      const parts = trimmed.split('|');
      if ((parts.length !== 3 && parts.length !== 4) || parts[0] !== 'KINETIC_PASS') return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const qrId = parts[1].replace(/^#/, '').trim();
      if (!qrId || isPoisoned(qrId) || !SAFE_PASS_ID_RE.test(qrId)) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const stepStr = parts[2].trim();
      const stepNum = /^\d+$/.test(stepStr) ? Number(stepStr) : NaN;
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(stepNum) || stepNum <= 0) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const rawNonce = parts.length === 4 ? parts[3].trim() : '';
      if (rawNonce && (isPoisoned(rawNonce) || !SAFE_NONCE_RE.test(rawNonce))) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const valid = isValidTotpWindow60s(stepNum, syncClock);
      return { ok: valid, qrIdentifier: qrId, totpStep: stepNum, qrNonce: rawNonce || null, isExpired: !valid, format: 'kinetic_totp' };
    }

    if (trimmed.startsWith('{')) {
      if (!trimmed.endsWith('}')) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed);
      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed) || hasPoisonOwnKeys(parsed)) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const qrId = String(parsed.qr_token ?? parsed.id ?? parsed.folio ?? '').replace(/^#/, '').trim();
      if (!qrId || isPoisoned(qrId) || !SAFE_PASS_ID_RE.test(qrId)) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const candNonce = parsed.nonce != null ? String(parsed.nonce).trim() : '';
      if (candNonce && (isPoisoned(candNonce) || !SAFE_NONCE_RE.test(candNonce))) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
      const nonce = candNonce || null;
      if (parsed.totp != null) {
        const s = String(parsed.totp).trim();
        const stepNum = /^\d+$/.test(s) ? Number(s) : NaN;
        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(stepNum) || stepNum <= 0) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
        const valid = isValidTotpWindow60s(stepNum, syncClock);
        return { ok: valid, qrIdentifier: qrId, totpStep: stepNum, qrNonce: nonce, isExpired: !valid, format: 'json' };
      }
      if (parsed.exp != null) {
        const expMs = Number(parsed.exp);
        if (!Number.isFinite(expMs) || expMs > syncClock + 90_000) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
        const expired = syncClock > expMs + 10_000;
        return { ok: !expired, qrIdentifier: qrId, totpStep: null, qrNonce: nonce, isExpired: expired, format: 'json' };
      }
      return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
    }
    const manual = trimmed.replace(/^#/, '').trim();
    if (!manual || isPoisoned(manual) || !SAFE_MANUAL_RE.test(manual)) return { ...INVALID_PACKET };
    return { ok: true, qrIdentifier: manual, totpStep: null, qrNonce: null, isExpired: false, format: 'manual' };
  } catch { return { ...INVALID_PACKET }; }
}

export function classifyOfflineFlushOutcome(err: unknown): 'synced_or_terminal' | 'retry_later' {
  const code = String((err as any)?.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const msg = String((err as any)?.message || err || '').trim();
  if (['42501', 'PGRST116', '23505', '22P02'].includes(code) ||
      /QR_YA_UTILIZADO|QR_EXPIRADO|Reserva no encontrada|Recepcion no autorizada|SALDO_PUERTA_INSUFICIENTE|Token QR invalido|42501|PGRST116/i.test(msg)) {
    return 'synced_or_terminal';
  }
  return isNetworkOfflineError(err) ? 'retry_later' : 'synced_or_terminal';
}

const fmtIsoLocal = (d: Date): string => `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export function buildPartnerPeriodOptions(nowMs = Date.now(), isEn = false): IPartnerPeriodOption[] {
  const safeNow = new Date(Number.isFinite(nowMs) ? nowMs : Date.now());
  const dow = safeNow.getDay();
  const mon0 = new Date(safeNow.getFullYear(), safeNow.getMonth(), safeNow.getDate() + (dow === 0 ? -6 : 1 - dow), 0, 0, 0, 0);
  const makeWeek = (w: number) => {
    const s = new Date(mon0.getFullYear(), mon0.getMonth(), mon0.getDate() - w * 7, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    const e = new Date(s.getFullYear(), s.getMonth(), s.getDate() + 6, 23, 59, 59, 999);
    return { startMs: s.getTime(), endMs: e.getTime(), inicioIso: fmtIsoLocal(s), finIso: fmtIsoLocal(e), strictWindow: true };
  };
  const makeMonth = (m: number) => {
    const s = new Date(safeNow.getFullYear(), safeNow.getMonth() - m, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    const e = new Date(s.getFullYear(), s.getMonth() + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999);
    return { startMs: s.getTime(), endMs: e.getTime(), inicioIso: fmtIsoLocal(s), finIso: fmtIsoLocal(e), strictWindow: true };
  };
  const w0 = makeWeek(0), w1 = makeWeek(1), w2 = makeWeek(2), m0 = makeMonth(0), m1 = makeMonth(1);
  return [
    { key: 'week_0', label: isEn ? 'Current Week' : 'Semana Actual', rangeLabel: `${w0.inicioIso} → ${w0.finIso}`, override: w0 },
    { key: 'week_1', label: isEn ? 'Last Week' : 'Semana Anterior', rangeLabel: `${w1.inicioIso} → ${w1.finIso}`, override: w1 },
    { key: 'week_2', label: isEn ? '2 Weeks Ago' : 'Hace 2 Semanas', rangeLabel: `${w2.inicioIso} → ${w2.finIso}`, override: w2 },
    { key: 'month_0', label: isEn ? 'Current Month' : 'Mes Actual', rangeLabel: `${m0.inicioIso} → ${m0.finIso}`, override: m0 },
    { key: 'month_1', label: isEn ? 'Last Month' : 'Mes Anterior', rangeLabel: `${m1.inicioIso} → ${m1.finIso}`, override: m1 },
  ];
}

export function computePartnerSpeiStatement(
  businessData: any, historyReservas: any[], activeReservas: any[], periodOverride?: IB2BPeriodOverride
): { report: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO; corteSocio: ICorteSemanalAfiliado } {
  const bizName = String(businessData?.nombre || 'Sede Kinetic').trim() || 'Sede Kinetic';
  const rawRfc = String(businessData?.rfc || businessData?.rfc_fiscal || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const rawReg = String(businessData?.regimen_fiscal || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const isOfficialKinetic = Boolean(businessData?.isOfficialKinetic) || bizName.toLowerCase() === 'kinetic oficial';
  const isGenericOrMissingRfc = !rawRfc || rawRfc === 'XAXX010101000' || rawRfc === 'XEXX010101000';
  const explicitSinRfc = rawReg.includes('sin_rfc') || rawReg.includes('sin rfc');
  const explicitMoral = (rawReg === 'persona_moral' || rawReg.includes('persona moral') || /\bmoral\b/.test(rawReg)) && !explicitSinRfc;
  const normRegimen = isOfficialKinetic || explicitMoral
    ? 'persona_moral'
    : !isGenericOrMissingRfc && !explicitSinRfc
      ? (/^[A-ZÑ&]{3}\d{6}[A-Z0-9]{3}$/.test(rawRfc) || rawRfc.length === 12
        ? 'persona_moral'
        : (/^[A-ZÑ&]{4}\d{6}[A-Z0-9]{3}$/.test(rawRfc) || rawRfc.length === 13 || rawReg.includes('fisica') || rawReg.includes('física')
          ? 'persona_fisica_con_rfc'
          : 'sin_rfc'))
      : 'sin_rfc';
  const afiliado = {
    id: String(businessData?.id || 'socio-b2b'), nombre: bizName,
    disciplina: String(businessData?.disciplina || businessData?.categoria || 'Fuerza'), categoria: String(businessData?.categoria || 'Club'),
    es_especialista: Boolean(businessData?.es_especialista || String(businessData?.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes('especialista')),
    clabe_bancaria: String(businessData?.clabe_bancaria || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 18),
    rfc: rawRfc, regimen_fiscal: normRegimen, csf_validada: Boolean(businessData?.csf_validada),
  };
  const dedupMap = new Map<string, any>();
  const clampKc = (v: unknown) => Math.min(1_000_000, Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(v) || 0)));
  [...(Array.isArray(activeReservas) ? activeReservas : []), ...(Array.isArray(historyReservas) ? historyReservas : [])].forEach((r, idx) => {
    if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') return;
    const rid = String(r.id || `row_${idx}`).trim();
    if (!rid || isPoisoned(rid)) return;
    const costo = Math.max(clampKc(r.costo_total), clampKc(r.precio_kc), clampKc(r.monto_escrow) + clampKc(r.monto_puerta));
    const escrow = r.monto_escrow != null ? Math.min(costo, clampKc(r.monto_escrow)) : Math.round(costo * 0.4);
    dedupMap.set(rid, {
      ...r, id: rid, negocio_nombre: String(r.negocio_nombre || bizName).trim() || bizName,
      costo_total: costo, precio_kc: costo, monto_escrow: escrow, monto_puerta: Math.max(0, costo - escrow),
    });
  });
  const report = calcularCorteSemanalB2B([afiliado], Array.from(dedupMap.values()), periodOverride);
  return { report, corteSocio: report.cortes_por_afiliado[0] };
}

const sanitizeShortAthleteName = (raw: unknown): string => {
  const s = String(raw || '').replace(/\S+@\S+/g, '').replace(/\+?[\d().\s-]{6,}/g, '').replace(/[<>"'`;]/g, '').trim();
  if (!s || isPoisoned(s)) return 'Atleta K.';
  const parts = s.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  return parts.length === 1 ? parts[0].slice(0, 18) : `${parts[0].slice(0, 14)} ${parts[1][0].toUpperCase()}.`;
};

const sanitizeVipTier = (raw: unknown): string => {
  const s = String(raw || '').replace(/\S+@\S+/g, '').replace(/\+?[\d().\s-]{6,}/g, '').replace(/[<>"'`;]/g, '').trim();
  return !s || isPoisoned(s) ? 'Acceso Base' : s.slice(0, 22);
};

export function mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster(params: {
  layoutRaw?: any; discipline?: string; eventTitle?: string; maxCupo?: number;
  cloudSpots?: any[]; localActivePasses?: any[]; localCompletedPasses?: any[];
}): ILiveStudioRosterResult {
  const baseLayout = resolveDefaultStudioLayout(params?.discipline, params?.maxCupo, params?.layoutRaw, params?.eventTitle);
  const layout = clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(baseLayout.rows, baseLayout.cols, baseLayout.disabledCells, baseLayout.equipmentType);
  const disabledSet = new Set(layout.disabledCells);
  const coordsBySpot = new Map<number, { row: number; col: number }>();
  let seq = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < layout.rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < layout.cols; c++) {
      if (!disabledSet.has(`${r}-${c}`) && seq < layout.totalSpots) coordsBySpot.set(++seq, { row: r, col: c });
    }
  }
  const totalSpots = Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, seq));
  const byReserva = new Map<string, { reservaId: string; checkedIn: boolean; reqSpot: number | null; name: string; tier: string; folio: string }>();
  const ingest = (rows: any[] | undefined, forceCheckedIn: boolean) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return;
    rows.forEach((row, idx) => {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return;
      const st = String(row.estado || row.status || 'activa').toLowerCase().trim();
      if (['cancelada', 'rechazada', 'eliminada', 'reembolsada', 'free', 'libre'].includes(st)) return;
      const rid = String(row.reserva_id ?? row.id ?? (row.spot_number ? `cloud_spot_${row.spot_number}_${idx}` : '')).trim();
      if (!rid || isPoisoned(rid)) return;
      const rawSp = Number(row.spot_number ?? row.estacion ?? row.spot ?? (String(row.notas || '').match(/(?:#|spot\s*|estaci[oó]n\s*)(\d+)\b/i)?.[1] ?? NaN));
      const validSp = Number.isInteger(rawSp) && rawSp >= 1 && rawSp <= totalSpots ? rawSp : null;
      const isChecked = forceCheckedIn || Boolean(row.checked_in || row.checkedIn || row.qr_usado_at || row.scanned_at_utc || ['completada', 'usada', 'checked_in'].includes(st));
      const prev = byReserva.get(rid);
      byReserva.set(rid, {
        reservaId: rid, checkedIn: Boolean(prev?.checkedIn || isChecked), reqSpot: prev?.reqSpot ?? validSp,
        name: prev?.name || sanitizeShortAthleteName(row.athlete_short_name ?? row.atleta_inicial ?? row.perfiles?.nombre ?? row.nombre_atleta ?? row.atleta_nombre),
        tier: prev?.tier || sanitizeVipTier(row.vip_tier ?? row.nivel_vip ?? row.perfiles?.nivel ?? row.nivel),
        folio: prev?.folio || String(row.folio || rid.replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 8)).toUpperCase(),
      });
    });
  };
  ingest(params?.cloudSpots, false); ingest(params?.localActivePasses, false); ingest(params?.localCompletedPasses, true);

  const assigned = new Map<number, typeof byReserva extends Map<string, infer V> ? V : never>();
  const overflow: (typeof byReserva extends Map<string, infer V> ? V : never)[] = [];
  for (const item of Array.from(byReserva.values()).sort((a, b) => Number(b.checkedIn) - Number(a.checkedIn))) {
    if (item.reqSpot != null && !assigned.has(item.reqSpot)) assigned.set(item.reqSpot, item);
    else overflow.push(item);
  }
  let nextFree = 1;
  for (const item of overflow) {
    while (nextFree <= totalSpots && assigned.has(nextFree)) nextFree += 1;
    if (nextFree > totalSpots) break;
    assigned.set(nextFree, item);
  }
  let occupiedCount = 0, checkedInCount = 0;
  const spots: ILiveStudioSpotItem[] = [];
  for (let s = 1; s <= totalSpots; s++) {
    const coord = coordsBySpot.get(s) || { row: 0, col: s - 1 };
    const hit = assigned.get(s);
    if (hit) {
      occupiedCount += 1; if (hit.checkedIn) checkedInCount += 1;
      spots.push({ spotNumber: s, row: coord.row, col: coord.col, status: hit.checkedIn ? 'checked_in' : 'reserved', checkedIn: hit.checkedIn, reservaId: hit.reservaId, athleteShortName: hit.name, vipTier: hit.tier, folio: hit.folio });
    } else {
      spots.push({ spotNumber: s, row: coord.row, col: coord.col, status: 'free', checkedIn: false, reservaId: null, athleteShortName: null, vipTier: null, folio: null });
    }
  }
  return { layout, spots, totalSpots, occupiedCount, checkedInCount, waitingCount: occupiedCount - checkedInCount, freeCount: totalSpots - occupiedCount };
}

export async function exportPartnerSpeiStatementPdf(
  businessData: any,
  historyReservas: any[],
  activeReservas: any[],
  periodOverride?: IB2BPeriodOverride,
  masterPassSummary?: any
) {
  const { report, corteSocio } = computePartnerSpeiStatement(businessData, historyReservas, activeReservas, periodOverride);
  return exportarPDFEstadoCuentaAfiliado(corteSocio, report.periodo_inicio, report.periodo_fin, masterPassSummary);
}

export async function fetchLiveStudioRoomRosterB2B(service: any, fechaClase?: string | null, localActive: any[] = [], localCompleted: any[] = []): Promise<ILiveStudioRosterResult> {
  const sid = String(service?.id || '').trim();
  const sName = String(service?.nombre_servicio || service?.titulo || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const targetDate = String(fechaClase || service?.fecha || '').slice(0, 10);
  const matchSrv = (r: any) => {
    const rSid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
    if (sid && rSid) return rSid === sid;
    const rName = String(r?.servicio || r?.nombre_servicio || r?.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (sName && rName && rName !== sName) return false;
    const rDate = String(r?.fecha_clase || r?.fecha_hora || '').slice(0, 10);
    return !targetDate || !rDate || rDate === targetDate;
  };
  const savedLocal = await loadStudioLayoutLocal(sid || sName);
  let layoutRaw = service?.studio_layout_json || service?.atributos?.studio_layout || savedLocal;
  let cloudSpots: any[] = [];
  if (UUID_RE.test(sid)) {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_mapa_spots_servicio_b2b', { p_servicio_id: sid, p_fecha_clase: targetDate || null });
      if (!error && data) {
        layoutRaw = (data as any).studio_layout_json || (data as any).studio_layout || layoutRaw;
        cloudSpots = Array.isArray((data as any).spots) ? (data as any).spots : Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const rawDisc = String(service?.disciplina || '').trim();
  const resolvedDisc = rawDisc && rawDisc.toLowerCase() !== 'club' ? rawDisc : String(service?.categoria || 'Fuerza');
  return mergeLiveStudioSpotsRoster({
    layoutRaw, discipline: resolvedDisc, eventTitle: service?.nombre_servicio || service?.titulo,
    maxCupo: Number(service?.cupo_maximo ?? service?.cupo_total ?? 12), cloudSpots,
    localActivePasses: (localActive || []).filter(matchSrv), localCompletedPasses: (localCompleted || []).filter(matchSrv),
  });
}

export async function syncStudioLayoutToCloud(serviceId?: string | null, layout?: any): Promise<void> {
  const safeLayout = resolveDefaultStudioLayout(undefined, layout?.totalSpots, layout);
  const cleanId = String(serviceId || '').trim();
  if (cleanId) await saveStudioLayoutLocal(cleanId, safeLayout);
  if (!UUID_RE.test(cleanId)) return;
  try {
    const { error } = await supabase.from('servicios').update({ studio_layout_json: safeLayout, cupo_maximo: safeLayout.totalSpots }).eq('id', cleanId);
    if (error && /studio_layout_json/i.test(String(error.message || ''))) await supabase.from('servicios').update({ cupo_maximo: safeLayout.totalSpots }).eq('id', cleanId);
  } catch {}
}

export interface IQrSettlementGuardInput {
  qrToken?: unknown;
  totpStep?: unknown;
  nowSyncedMs?: number;
  isMasterAdmin?: boolean;
}

export interface IQrSettlementGuardResult {
  ok: boolean;
  reason: 'ok' | 'missing_qr_token' | 'poisoned_input' | 'missing_totp_step' | 'totp_out_of_window';
}

export function validateQrCheckinSettlementGuard(input: IQrSettlementGuardInput): IQrSettlementGuardResult {
  const tokenStr = typeof input?.qrToken === 'string' ? input.qrToken.trim() : '';
  if (!tokenStr) return { ok: false, reason: 'missing_qr_token' };
  if (isPoisoned(tokenStr) || tokenStr.length > 160) return { ok: false, reason: 'poisoned_input' };
  if (input?.isMasterAdmin) return { ok: true, reason: 'ok' };
  const rawStep = input?.totpStep;
  const stepNum =
    typeof rawStep === 'number'
      ? rawStep
      : typeof rawStep === 'string' && /^\d+$/.test(rawStep.trim())
      ? Number(rawStep.trim())
      : NaN;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(stepNum) || stepNum <= 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'missing_totp_step' };
  const syncClock = Number.isFinite(input?.nowSyncedMs) ? Number(input.nowSyncedMs) : Date.now();
  if (!isValidTotpWindow60s(stepNum, syncClock)) return { ok: false, reason: 'totp_out_of_window' };
  return { ok: true, reason: 'ok' };
}

const SHA256_K = [
  0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
  0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
  0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
  0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
  0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
  0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
  0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
  0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
];

export function sha256HexSync(raw: string): string {
  const utf8 = unescape(encodeURIComponent(String(raw ?? '')));
  const bitLen = utf8.length * 8;
  const words: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < utf8.length; i += 1) words[i >> 2] |= (utf8.charCodeAt(i) & 0xff) << (24 - (i % 4) * 8);
  words[utf8.length >> 2] |= 0x80 << (24 - (utf8.length % 4) * 8);
  words[(((utf8.length + 8) >> 6) << 4) + 15] = bitLen;
  const H = [0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19];
  const rotr = (n: number, x: number) => (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n));
  const w = new Array<number>(64);
  for (let j = 0; j < words.length; j += 16) {
    let [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h] = H;
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i += 1) {
      w[i] = i < 16 ? (words[j + i] | 0) : ((rotr(17, w[i - 2]) ^ rotr(19, w[i - 2]) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10)) + w[i - 7] + (rotr(7, w[i - 15]) ^ rotr(18, w[i - 15]) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)) + w[i - 16]) | 0;
      const t1 = (h + (rotr(6, e) ^ rotr(11, e) ^ rotr(25, e)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + SHA256_K[i] + w[i]) | 0;
      const t2 = ((rotr(2, a) ^ rotr(13, a) ^ rotr(22, a)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c))) | 0;
      [h, g, f, e, d, c, b, a] = [g, f, e, (d + t1) | 0, c, b, a, (t1 + t2) | 0];
    }
    [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h].forEach((v, idx) => { H[idx] = (H[idx] + v) | 0; });
  }
  return H.map((x) => (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')).join('');
}

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. PROTECCIÓN ESTRICTA DE public.afiliados (CONSERVANDO CLUBES REALES)
-- ============================================================================
DELETE FROM public.afiliados
WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(nombre, ''))) IN (
        'kinetic oficial',
        'sede kinetic',
        'sede oficial kinetic'
      )
   OR LOWER(COALESCE(nombre, '')) LIKE '%atleta 5975%'
   OR LOWER(COALESCE(nombre, '')) LIKE '%atleta kinetic%';

UPDATE public.afiliados
SET propietario_id = NULL,
    email_contacto = CASE
      WHEN LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(email_contacto, ''))) IN (
        'club@kinetic.com',
        'especialista@kinetic.com',
        'kinetic@kinetic.com',
        'atleta@kinetic.com'
      ) THEN NULL
      ELSE email_contacto
    END;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. VACIADO TOTAL DE DATOS DE PRUEBA (EVENTOS, RESERVAS, CHATS, HISTORIAS, WAITLIST)
-- ============================================================================
DELETE FROM public.reservas;
DELETE FROM public.servicios;
DELETE FROM public.equipo_mensajes;
DELETE FROM public.equipo_miembros;
DELETE FROM public.equipos_kinetic;
DELETE FROM public.admin_audit_logs;

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_tbl TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH v_tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY[
    'comunidad_comentarios',
    'comunidad_posts',
    'historias_24h',
    'kinetic_stories',
    'mensajes_directos',
    'chats_privados',
    'negocios'
  ]
  LOOP
    IF EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM information_schema.tables
      WHERE table_schema = 'public'
        AND table_name = v_tbl
    ) THEN
      EXECUTE format('DELETE FROM public.%I', v_tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;

ALTER TABLE public.fundadores_waitlist
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS estado TEXT DEFAULT 'pendiente';

DELETE FROM public.fundadores_waitlist;

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. CUENTA ÚNICA DE ADMINISTRADOR ('administrador@kinetic.com.mx' -> 'Kinetic Oficial')
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.perfiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS email TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_membresia NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_topup NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS saldo_kc NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS xp_total INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS racha_dias INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recompensas_reclamadas TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}';

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_admin_id UUID;
  v_has_tipo_cuenta BOOLEAN;
  r_fk RECORD;
BEGIN
  -- Localizar la cuenta base kinetic@kinetic.com o administrador@kinetic.com.mx conservando su encrypted_password
  SELECT u.id
    INTO v_admin_id
  FROM auth.users u
  WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
    'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
    'kinetic@kinetic.com',
    'administrador@kinetic.com'
  )
  ORDER BY
    (LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx') DESC,
    (LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = 'kinetic@kinetic.com') DESC,
    u.created_at ASC
  LIMIT 1;

  IF v_admin_id IS NULL THEN
    SELECT p.id
      INTO v_admin_id
    FROM public.perfiles p
    JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = p.id
    WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
    ORDER BY p.created_at ASC NULLS LAST
    LIMIT 1;
  END IF;

  IF v_admin_id IS NULL THEN
    v_admin_id := gen_random_uuid();
    INSERT INTO auth.users (
      id,
      instance_id,
      email,
      encrypted_password,
      email_confirmed_at,
      created_at,
      updated_at,
      raw_app_meta_data,
      raw_user_meta_data,
      is_super_admin,
      role,
      aud
    ) VALUES (
      v_admin_id,
      '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
      'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      crypt('KineticAdmin2026!', gen_salt('bf')),
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      '{"provider":"email","providers":["email"]}'::jsonb,
      '{"nombre":"Kinetic Oficial","rol":"admin","tipo_cuenta":"admin"}'::jsonb,
      FALSE,
      'authenticated',
      'authenticated'
    );
  END IF;

  -- Limpiar referencias residuales en tablas satélite públicas que apunten a perfiles/users de prueba
  FOR r_fk IN
    SELECT DISTINCT
      kcu.table_name,
      kcu.column_name
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
    JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
      ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
     AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
    JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
      ON ccu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name
    WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
      AND tc.table_schema = 'public'
      AND tc.table_name NOT IN ('perfiles', 'afiliados')
      AND (
        (ccu.table_schema = 'auth' AND ccu.table_name = 'users')
        OR (ccu.table_schema = 'public' AND ccu.table_name = 'perfiles')
      )
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format(
      'DELETE FROM public.%I WHERE %I IS NOT NULL AND %I <> $1',
      r_fk.table_name,
      r_fk.column_name,
      r_fk.column_name
    ) USING v_admin_id;
  END LOOP;

  -- Eliminar TODAS las cuentas de prueba en public.perfiles, auth.identities y auth.users
  DELETE FROM public.perfiles WHERE id <> v_admin_id;
  DELETE FROM auth.identities WHERE user_id <> v_admin_id;
  DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id <> v_admin_id;

  -- Transformar la cuenta Admin a 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx' conservando su encrypted_password actual
  UPDATE auth.users
  SET email = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      email_confirmed_at = NOW(),
      updated_at = NOW(),
      raw_app_meta_data = COALESCE(raw_app_meta_data, '{"provider":"email","providers":["email"]}'::jsonb),
      raw_user_meta_data = '{"nombre":"Kinetic Oficial","rol":"admin","tipo_cuenta":"admin"}'::jsonb
  WHERE id = v_admin_id;

  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM auth.identities WHERE user_id = v_admin_id AND provider = 'email'
  ) THEN
    UPDATE auth.identities
    SET identity_data = COALESCE(identity_data, '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
          'sub', v_admin_id::text,
          'email', 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'email_verified', true
        ),
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE user_id = v_admin_id
      AND provider = 'email';
  ELSE
    INSERT INTO auth.identities (
      id,
      user_id,
      identity_data,
      provider,
      provider_id,
      last_sign_in_at,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    ) VALUES (
      v_admin_id,
      v_admin_id,
      jsonb_build_object(
        'sub', v_admin_id::text,
        'email', 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
        'email_verified', true
      ),
      'email',
      v_admin_id::text,
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      NOW()
    );
  END IF;

  -- Asegurar registro único en public.perfiles con saldos y contadores en 0
  INSERT INTO public.perfiles (
    id,
    email,
    nombre,
    rol,
    nivel,
    kc_membresia,
    kc_topup,
    saldo_kc,
    xp_total,
    racha_dias,
    recompensas_reclamadas
  ) VALUES (
    v_admin_id,
    'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
    'Kinetic Oficial',
    'admin',
    'Black',
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0,
    '{}'
  )
  ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
  SET email = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      nombre = 'Kinetic Oficial',
      rol = 'admin',
      nivel = 'Black',
      kc_membresia = 0,
      kc_topup = 0,
      saldo_kc = 0,
      xp_total = 0,
      racha_dias = 0,
      recompensas_reclamadas = '{}';

  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_schema = 'public'
      AND table_name = 'perfiles'
      AND column_name = 'tipo_cuenta'
  ) INTO v_has_tipo_cuenta;

  IF v_has_tipo_cuenta THEN
    EXECUTE 'UPDATE public.perfiles SET tipo_cuenta = ''admin'' WHERE id = $1' USING v_admin_id;
  END IF;
END $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com'
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  );
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin() TO authenticated, anon;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_is_admin BOOLEAN;
  v_mem NUMERIC;
  v_top NUMERIC;
  v_nivel TEXT;
BEGIN
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com'
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  ) INTO v_is_admin;

  IF NOT COALESCE(v_is_admin, FALSE) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado: requiere privilegios de Admin Maestro Kinetic.';
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base')
    INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = p_user_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN
    v_mem := GREATEST(0, v_mem + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  ELSE
    v_top := GREATEST(0, v_top + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  END IF;

  IF p_nuevo_nivel IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel)) > 0 THEN
    v_nivel := TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel);
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET kc_membresia = v_mem,
      kc_topup = v_top,
      saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top,
      nivel = v_nivel
  WHERE id = p_user_id;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'kc_membresia', v_mem,
    'kc_topup', v_top,
    'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top,
    'nivel', v_nivel
  );
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_fondos_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(p_user_id, p_cantidad, p_billetera, p_nuevo_nivel);
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_fondos_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;

-- Políticas RLS de Admin Maestro sobre tablas operativas y fundadores_waitlist
ALTER TABLE public.fundadores_waitlist ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Insercion publica fundadores_waitlist" ON public.fundadores_waitlist;
CREATE POLICY "Insercion publica fundadores_waitlist"
ON public.fundadores_waitlist
FOR INSERT
TO anon, authenticated
WITH CHECK (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona fundadores_waitlist" ON public.fundadores_waitlist;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona fundadores_waitlist"
ON public.fundadores_waitlist
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona afiliados" ON public.afiliados;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona afiliados"
ON public.afiliados
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona servicios" ON public.servicios;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona servicios"
ON public.servicios
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona perfiles" ON public.perfiles;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona perfiles"
ON public.perfiles
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';
