-- 5. VERIFICACIÓN FINAL DE CONTEOS OFICIALES
-- ============================================================================
SELECT
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM auth.users) AS total_auth_users,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.perfiles) AS total_perfiles,
  (SELECT email || ' — ' || nombre FROM public.perfiles LIMIT 1) AS admin_unico,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.servicios) AS total_servicios,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.fundadores_waitlist) AS total_fundadores_waitlist,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.afiliados) AS total_afiliados_reales_conservados;

    FILE: supabase/migrations/20261121210000_migration_v282_ola3_b2b_roles_and_admin_scale.sql

    -- === supabase/migrations/20261121210000_migration_v282_ola3_b2b_roles_and_admin_scale.sql === --
-- KINETIC OS v2.8.2 — OLA 3 (P1-09, P2-01, P1-10):
-- 1) Homologacion canonica de roles B2B en SQL (is_kinetic_b2b_role_text / is_kinetic_b2b_account)
-- 2) Resolucion Auth O(1) por email restringida a service_role (admin_resolve_auth_user_by_email)
-- 3) Escala real Admin Hub sin truncamiento + indices B-Tree compuestos (admin_obtener_kpis_globales_v282)
BEGIN;

-- 1) Master Admin canonico con search_path endurecido
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND u.deleted_at IS NULL
      AND (
        (
          u.email_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
            'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
            'administrador@kinetic.com',
            'kinetic@kinetic.com',
            'admin@kinetic.com'
          )
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  );
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin() FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin() TO authenticated, anon, service_role;

-- 2) Predicado puro e inmutable de roles B2B canonicos + aliases operativos
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p_role TEXT)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_role, ''))) IN (
    'socio',
    'afiliado',
    'club',
    'especialista',
    'operador_b2b',
    'negocio',
    'estadio',
    'b2b',
    'partner_b2b',
    'partner b2b',
    'partner',
    'operador',
    'coach_club'
  );
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(TEXT) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 3) Predicado canonico de cuenta B2B (excluye siempre Master Admin y lee solo claims no editables)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(p_uid UUID DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_jwt_role TEXT := COALESCE(
    NULLIF(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.role', true), ''),
    NULLIF((SELECT auth.jwt() ->> 'role'), ''),
    NULLIF(current_setting('role', true), ''),
    ''
  );
  v_is_service_role BOOLEAN := (
    v_jwt_role = 'service_role'
    OR current_setting('role', true) = 'service_role'
    OR session_user IN ('postgres', 'supabase_admin')
  );
  v_uid UUID;
BEGIN
  IF p_uid IS NOT NULL
     AND v_caller IS NOT NULL
     AND p_uid IS DISTINCT FROM v_caller
     AND pg_trigger_depth() = 0
     AND NOT v_is_service_role
     AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'FORBIDDEN_CROSS_USER_B2B_CHECK' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  v_uid := COALESCE(p_uid, v_caller);
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RETURN FALSE;
  END IF;

  IF (v_uid = v_caller AND COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false))
     OR EXISTS (
       SELECT 1
       FROM auth.users u
       LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
       WHERE u.id = v_uid
         AND u.deleted_at IS NULL
         AND (
           (
             u.email_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL
             AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
               'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
               'administrador@kinetic.com',
               'kinetic@kinetic.com',
               'admin@kinetic.com'
             )
           )
           OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
         )
     ) THEN
    RETURN FALSE;
  END IF;

  RETURN (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM public.perfiles p
      LEFT JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = p.id
      WHERE p.id = v_uid
        AND (
          public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p.rol)
          OR public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p.tipo_cuenta)
          OR public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(u.raw_app_meta_data->>'rol')
          OR public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(u.raw_app_meta_data->>'tipo_cuenta')
          OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, COALESCE(p.email, '')))) LIKE 'club@%'
          OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, COALESCE(p.email, '')))) LIKE 'especialista@%'
        )
    )
    OR EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM auth.users u
      WHERE u.id = v_uid
        AND u.deleted_at IS NULL
        AND (
          public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(u.raw_app_meta_data->>'rol')
          OR public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(u.raw_app_meta_data->>'tipo_cuenta')
          OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) LIKE 'club@%'
          OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) LIKE 'especialista@%'
        )
    )
    OR EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM public.afiliados a
      WHERE a.propietario_id = v_uid
        AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.estado, 'activo'))) NOT IN ('eliminado', 'rechazado', 'inactivo', 'suspendido')
    )
  );
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(UUID) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(UUID) TO authenticated, service_role;

-- 4) Alias retrocompatible apuntando al predicado canonico
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_strict_b2b_partner(p_uid UUID DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(p_uid);
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_strict_b2b_partner(UUID) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_strict_b2b_partner(UUID) TO authenticated, service_role;

-- 5) Homologacion de candados SQL de reservas, Split-Escrow, QR y monedero con public.is_kinetic_b2b_account
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.obtener_semilla_qr_reserva_propia(p_reserva_id UUID)
RETURNS TEXT
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid UUID := (SELECT auth.uid());
  v_tok TEXT;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL OR p_reserva_id IS NULL OR COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(v_uid)), FALSE) THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  SELECT r.qr_token INTO v_tok
  FROM public.reservas r
  WHERE r.id = p_reserva_id AND r.usuario_id = v_uid
  LIMIT 1;
  RETURN v_tok;
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.obtener_semilla_qr_reserva_propia(UUID) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.obtener_semilla_qr_reserva_propia(UUID) TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.obtener_mis_qr_tokens_activos()
RETURNS TABLE (id UUID, qr_token TEXT)
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT r.id, r.qr_token
  FROM public.reservas r
  WHERE (SELECT auth.uid()) IS NOT NULL
    AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account((SELECT auth.uid()))), FALSE)
    AND r.usuario_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
    AND r.qr_token IS NOT NULL
    AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada')
  ORDER BY r.created_at DESC
  LIMIT 60;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.obtener_mis_qr_tokens_activos() FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.obtener_mis_qr_tokens_activos() TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.procesar_reserva_escrow(
  p_usuario_id UUID, p_negocio_nombre TEXT, p_servicio_nombre TEXT, p_precio_kc NUMERIC,
  p_servicio_id UUID DEFAULT NULL, p_fecha_clase TEXT DEFAULT NULL, p_spot_number INTEGER DEFAULT NULL,
  p_split_count INTEGER DEFAULT 1, p_es_pase_maestro BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid()); v_is_admin BOOLEAN := COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), FALSE);
  v_precio_base NUMERIC := 0; v_total_kc INTEGER := 0; v_escrow INTEGER := 0; v_puerta INTEGER := 0;
  v_srv_negocio TEXT := ''; v_srv_titulo TEXT := ''; v_srv_estado TEXT := ''; v_srv_activo BOOLEAN := TRUE;
  v_srv_fecha DATE; v_srv_hora TIME; v_srv_freq TEXT := ''; v_srv_desc TEXT := ''; v_srv_attrs JSONB := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_cupo_max INTEGER := 30; v_ocupados INTEGER := 0; v_mem NUMERIC := 0; v_top NUMERIC := 0;
  v_use_mem NUMERIC := 0; v_use_top NUMERIC := 0; v_nivel TEXT := ''; v_tier_pct INTEGER := 0;
  v_club_flash_pct INTEGER := 0; v_pct INTEGER := 0; v_is_flash BOOLEAN := FALSE;
  v_srv_flash_col BOOLEAN := FALSE; v_srv_flash_pct_col INTEGER := 0; v_srv_afiliado_id UUID;
  v_afiliado_estado TEXT; v_ev_ts TIMESTAMPTZ; v_f_eff TEXT; v_h_parts TEXT[]; v_h_eff TEXT; v_h_full TEXT;
  v_reserva_id UUID; v_qr_token TEXT; v_sched JSONB; v_is_official BOOLEAN := FALSE;
  v_split INTEGER := LEAST(10, GREATEST(1, COALESCE(p_split_count, 1))); v_fecha TEXT := NULLIF(LEFT(TRIM(COALESCE(p_fecha_clase, '')), 10), '');
BEGIN
  IF v_caller IS NULL OR (p_usuario_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_caller AND NOT v_is_admin) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado para reservar a nombre de otro atleta.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  IF (NOT v_is_admin AND COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(v_caller)), FALSE)) OR COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(p_usuario_id)), FALSE) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'B2B_ACCOUNT_ATHLETE_BOOKING_FORBIDDEN: Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden realizar reservas de atleta.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  IF p_servicio_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT COALESCE(s.precio_kc, 0), COALESCE(s.cupo_maximo, 30), TRIM(COALESCE(s.negocio_nombre, '')), TRIM(COALESCE(s.nombre_servicio, '')),
      LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(s.estado, 'aprobado'))), COALESCE(s.activo, TRUE), COALESCE(s.fecha, NULLIF(LEFT(TRIM(COALESCE(to_jsonb(s)->>'fecha_clase', '')), 10), '')::date),
      s.hora_inicio, COALESCE(s.frecuencia, ''), COALESCE(s.descripcion, ''), COALESCE(s.atributos, '{}'::jsonb),
      COALESCE(s.es_venta_flash, FALSE), COALESCE(s.descuento_flash_pct, 0), s.afiliado_id
    INTO v_precio_base, v_cupo_max, v_srv_negocio, v_srv_titulo, v_srv_estado, v_srv_activo, v_srv_fecha,
      v_srv_hora, v_srv_freq, v_srv_desc, v_srv_attrs, v_srv_flash_col, v_srv_flash_pct_col, v_srv_afiliado_id
    FROM public.servicios s WHERE s.id = p_servicio_id FOR UPDATE;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SERVICIO_INEXISTENTE: La experiencia seleccionada ya no existe.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
    IF NOT v_srv_activo OR v_srv_estado NOT IN ('activo', 'aprobado', '') OR COALESCE((v_srv_attrs->>'eliminado')::boolean, FALSE) OR COALESCE((v_srv_attrs->>'cancelado')::boolean, FALSE) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'SERVICIO_RETIRADO: La experiencia seleccionada fue pausada o retirada.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002';
    END IF;
    v_is_official := LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(v_srv_negocio, ''))) IN ('kinetic oficial', 'kinetic sports club', '');
    IF v_srv_afiliado_id IS NULL AND NULLIF(TRIM(COALESCE(v_srv_attrs->>'afiliado_id', '')), '') ~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN
      v_srv_afiliado_id := (v_srv_attrs->>'afiliado_id')::uuid;
    END IF;
    IF NOT v_is_official AND v_srv_afiliado_id IS NOT NULL THEN
      SELECT LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.estado, ''))) INTO v_afiliado_estado FROM public.afiliados a WHERE a.id = v_srv_afiliado_id;
      IF FOUND AND v_afiliado_estado IN ('inactivo', 'prospecto', 'oculto', 'pendiente', 'suspendido', 'eliminado', 'rechazado') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'SERVICIO_RETIRADO: Esta sede se encuentra temporalmente inactiva.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002';
      END IF;
    END IF;
    v_sched := public.es_fecha_valida_servicio_mazatlan(v_fecha, v_srv_fecha, v_srv_hora, v_srv_freq, v_srv_desc, v_srv_attrs);
    IF NOT v_is_admin AND NOT COALESCE((v_sched->>'valid')::boolean, FALSE) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION '%', COALESCE(NULLIF(v_sched->>'reason', ''), 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE: Fecha u horario fuera de vigencia.') USING ERRCODE = '22023';
    END IF;
    v_fecha := COALESCE(NULLIF(v_sched->>'resolved_date', ''), v_fecha, to_char(NOW() AT TIME ZONE 'America/Mazatlan', 'YYYY-MM-DD'));
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_ocupados FROM public.reservas r WHERE r.servicio_id = p_servicio_id AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada')
      AND COALESCE(NULLIF(LEFT(TRIM(COALESCE(r.fecha_clase, '')), 10), ''), to_char(COALESCE(r.created_at, NOW()) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Mazatlan', 'YYYY-MM-DD')) = v_fecha;
    IF v_ocupados >= v_cupo_max THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'CUPO_AGOTADO: No quedan lugares disponibles en esta sesion.'; END IF;
    IF p_spot_number IS NOT NULL THEN
      IF p_spot_number < 1 OR p_spot_number > v_cupo_max THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Estacion #% fuera del rango permitido (%).', p_spot_number, v_cupo_max USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.reservas r WHERE r.servicio_id = p_servicio_id AND r.spot_number = p_spot_number AND LOWER(COALESCE(r.estado, 'activa')) IN ('activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada')
        AND COALESCE(NULLIF(LEFT(TRIM(COALESCE(r.fecha_clase, '')), 10), ''), to_char(COALESCE(r.created_at, NOW()) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Mazatlan', 'YYYY-MM-DD')) = v_fecha
      ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SPOT_OCUPADO: La estacion #% ya fue reservada.', p_spot_number USING ERRCODE = '23505'; END IF;
    END IF;
  ELSE
    IF NOT v_is_admin THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Reserva sin servicio de catalogo prohibida para clientes.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
    v_precio_base := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(p_precio_kc, 0)); v_fecha := COALESCE(v_fecha, to_char(NOW() AT TIME ZONE 'America/Mazatlan', 'YYYY-MM-DD'));
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(nivel, ''))) INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = p_usuario_id FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Perfil de atleta no encontrado.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
  v_f_eff := COALESCE(v_fecha, to_char(NOW() AT TIME ZONE 'America/Mazatlan', 'YYYY-MM-DD'));
  v_h_parts := REGEXP_MATCH(TRIM(COALESCE(v_srv_hora::text, '')), '^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?');
  v_h_eff := CASE WHEN v_h_parts IS NOT NULL THEN LPAD(v_h_parts[1], 2, '0') || ':' || v_h_parts[2] ELSE '08:00' END;
  v_h_full := CASE WHEN v_h_parts IS NOT NULL THEN v_h_eff || ':' || COALESCE(v_h_parts[3], '00') ELSE '08:00:00' END;

  IF COALESCE(p_es_pase_maestro, FALSE) THEN
    IF NOT v_is_admin THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado para emitir Pase Maestro Admin 0 KC.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
    v_total_kc := 0; v_escrow := 0; v_puerta := 0;
  ELSE
    IF v_precio_base < 0 OR (v_precio_base > 0 AND MOD(v_precio_base, 10) <> 0) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Precio base invalido (debe ser multiplo de 10 KC).'; END IF;
    IF v_f_eff ~ '^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$' AND v_h_full ~ '^\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}$' THEN
      v_ev_ts := ((LEFT(TRIM(v_f_eff), 10) || ' ' || v_h_full)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Mazatlan');
      IF v_ev_ts > NOW() AND v_ev_ts <= (NOW() + INTERVAL '180 minutes') THEN v_is_flash := TRUE; END IF;
    END IF;
    IF v_srv_flash_col OR COALESCE((v_srv_attrs->>'es_venta_flash')::boolean, FALSE) THEN v_is_flash := TRUE; END IF;
    IF v_is_flash AND v_precio_base > 0 THEN
      v_tier_pct := CASE WHEN v_nivel ~ '(platinum|platino|leyenda|black)' THEN 20 WHEN v_nivel ~ '(gold|oro)' THEN 15 WHEN v_nivel ~ '(silver|plata)' THEN 10 WHEN v_nivel ~ '(bronze|bronce)' THEN 5
        WHEN v_nivel = '' OR v_nivel ~ '(acceso\s*base|base\s*access|free|ninguno|top[\s_-]*up)' THEN
          CASE WHEN v_mem >= 3600 THEN 20 WHEN v_mem >= 2500 THEN 15 WHEN v_mem >= 1500 THEN 10 WHEN v_mem >= 600 THEN 5 ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END;
      v_club_flash_pct := LEAST(40, GREATEST(0, GREATEST(v_srv_flash_pct_col, COALESCE(NULLIF(REGEXP_REPLACE(COALESCE(v_srv_attrs->>'descuento_flash_pct', ''), '[^0-9]', '', 'g'), '')::int, 0))));
      v_pct := CASE WHEN v_tier_pct > 0 THEN LEAST(40, GREATEST(v_tier_pct, v_club_flash_pct)) ELSE 0 END;
    END IF;
    IF v_precio_base = 0 THEN v_total_kc := 0; v_escrow := 0; v_puerta := 0;
    ELSE
      v_total_kc := GREATEST(10, (ROUND(((v_precio_base * (100 - v_pct)) / 100.0) / 10.0)::integer * 10));
      v_escrow := CASE WHEN v_total_kc >= 20 THEN GREATEST(10, LEAST(v_total_kc - 10, (ROUND((v_total_kc * 0.40) / 10.0)::integer * 10))) ELSE 10 END;
      v_puerta := v_total_kc - v_escrow;
    END IF;
  END IF;

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true); PERFORM set_config('kinetic.rpc_authorized', 'true', true);
  IF v_escrow > 0 THEN
    IF (v_mem + v_top) < v_escrow THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SALDO_INSUFICIENTE: Tu boveda no cubre el 40%% de custodia (% KC).', v_escrow; END IF;
    v_use_mem := LEAST(v_mem, v_escrow); v_use_top := v_escrow - v_use_mem;
    UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = v_mem - v_use_mem, kc_topup = v_top - v_use_top, saldo_kc = (v_mem - v_use_mem) + (v_top - v_use_top) WHERE id = p_usuario_id;
  END IF;
  v_qr_token := md5(gen_random_uuid()::text || clock_timestamp()::text || random()::text);
  INSERT INTO public.reservas (usuario_id, servicio_id, negocio_nombre, servicio, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup,
    estado, qr_token, fecha_clase, hora_inicio, spot_number, split_count, es_pase_maestro, es_venta_flash, descuento_flash_pct)
  VALUES (p_usuario_id, p_servicio_id, COALESCE(NULLIF(v_srv_negocio, ''), TRIM(COALESCE(p_negocio_nombre, ''))),
    COALESCE(NULLIF(v_srv_titulo, ''), TRIM(COALESCE(p_servicio_nombre, ''))), v_total_kc, v_total_kc, v_escrow, v_puerta, v_use_mem, v_use_top,
    'confirmada', v_qr_token, v_fecha, v_h_eff, p_spot_number, v_split, COALESCE(p_es_pase_maestro, FALSE), v_is_flash, v_pct)
  RETURNING id INTO v_reserva_id;
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('id', v_reserva_id, 'reserva_id', v_reserva_id, 'qr_token', v_qr_token, 'precio_kc', v_total_kc, 'es_venta_flash', v_is_flash,
    'descuento_flash_pct', v_pct, 'monto_escrow', v_escrow, 'monto_puerta', v_puerta, 'escrow_membresia', v_use_mem, 'escrow_topup', v_use_top,
    'fecha_clase', v_fecha, 'hora_inicio', v_h_eff, 'spot_number', p_spot_number, 'split_count', v_split, 'es_pase_maestro', COALESCE(p_es_pase_maestro, FALSE));
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.procesar_reserva_escrow(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, NUMERIC, UUID, TEXT, INTEGER, INTEGER, BOOLEAN) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.procesar_reserva_escrow(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, NUMERIC, UUID, TEXT, INTEGER, INTEGER, BOOLEAN) TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_guard_split_escrow_insert_v282()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE v_res RECORD; v_eff_split INT; v_existing_cnt INT := 0; v_dup_guest BOOLEAN := FALSE; v_cap_share NUMERIC; v_existing_sum NUMERIC := 0;
BEGIN
  SELECT id, usuario_id, monto_escrow, split_count, estado INTO v_res FROM public.reservas WHERE id = NEW.reserva_id FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(v_res.estado, ''))) NOT IN ('activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESERVA_INACTIVA: No se pueden emitir invitaciones sobre esta reserva.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
  IF NEW.anfitrion_id IS NULL OR NEW.anfitrion_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_res.usuario_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Solo el titular de la reserva puede emitir invitaciones Split-Escrow.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  IF NEW.invitado_id IS NULL OR NEW.invitado_id IN (NEW.anfitrion_id, v_res.usuario_id) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invitado Split-Escrow invalido (no puede ser el mismo anfitrion).' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
  IF COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(NEW.invitado_id)), FALSE) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'B2B_ACCOUNT_ATHLETE_BOOKING_FORBIDDEN: Cuentas B2B no pueden recibir Split-Escrow.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  IF COALESCE(NEW.monto_kc_parte, 0) < 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Monto Split-Escrow invalido.' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
  SELECT COUNT(*), COALESCE(SUM(monto_kc_parte), 0), COALESCE(BOOL_OR(invitado_id = NEW.invitado_id), FALSE) INTO v_existing_cnt, v_existing_sum, v_dup_guest FROM public.split_escrow_invitaciones WHERE reserva_id = NEW.reserva_id AND LOWER(COALESCE(estado, 'pendiente')) NOT IN ('rechazada', 'cancelada');
  IF v_dup_guest THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Este aliado ya tiene una invitacion activa en esta reserva.' USING ERRCODE = '23505'; END IF;
  IF v_existing_cnt >= 3 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Limite maximo de 3 invitados Split-Escrow alcanzado.' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
  v_eff_split := LEAST(4, GREATEST(2, COALESCE(v_res.split_count, 1), v_existing_cnt + 2));
  v_cap_share := FLOOR((GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_res.monto_escrow, 0)) / v_eff_split::numeric) / 10.0) * 10;
  NEW.monto_kc_parte := LEAST(v_cap_share, FLOOR(COALESCE(NEW.monto_kc_parte, 0) / 10.0) * 10);
  IF NEW.monto_kc_parte > 0 AND (v_existing_sum + NEW.monto_kc_parte) > GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_res.monto_escrow, 0) - GREATEST(10, v_cap_share)) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SPLIT_OVERFLOW: Las invitaciones exceden la proporcion Split-Escrow permitida.' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
  NEW.estado := 'pendiente'; NEW.monto_liquidado_kc := 0; NEW.liquidado_membresia_kc := 0; NEW.liquidado_topup_kc := 0; NEW.reembolsada := FALSE; NEW.liquidada_at := NULL; NEW.reembolsada_at := NULL;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.responder_split_escrow_invitacion(p_invitacion_id UUID, p_aceptar BOOLEAN)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller UUID := (SELECT auth.uid()); v_res_id UUID; v_inv_guest UUID; v_res_ok BOOLEAN := FALSE; v_inv RECORD; v_res RECORD;
  v_guest RECORD; v_host RECORD; v_parte NUMERIC := 0; v_use_mem NUMERIC := 0; v_use_top NUMERIC := 0; v_settled_sum NUMERIC := 0; v_host_mem_rem NUMERIC := 0; v_host_add_mem NUMERIC := 0; v_host_add_top NUMERIC := 0;
BEGIN
  IF v_caller IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sesion requerida para responder Split-Escrow.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  IF COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(v_caller)), FALSE) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'B2B_ACCOUNT_ATHLETE_BOOKING_FORBIDDEN: Cuentas B2B no pueden aceptar Split-Escrow.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  SELECT reserva_id, invitado_id INTO v_res_id, v_inv_guest FROM public.split_escrow_invitaciones WHERE id = p_invitacion_id;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invitacion Split-Escrow no encontrada.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
  IF v_inv_guest IS DISTINCT FROM v_caller THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Solo el atleta invitado puede responder esta invitacion.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_res FROM public.reservas WHERE id = v_res_id FOR UPDATE; v_res_ok := FOUND;
  SELECT * INTO v_inv FROM public.split_escrow_invitaciones WHERE id = p_invitacion_id FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invitacion Split-Escrow no encontrada.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
  IF LOWER(COALESCE(v_inv.estado, 'pendiente')) <> 'pendiente' THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', TRUE, 'idempotent', TRUE, 'id', p_invitacion_id, 'estado', LOWER(COALESCE(v_inv.estado, 'pendiente')), 'monto_liquidado_kc', COALESCE(v_inv.monto_liquidado_kc, 0)); END IF;
  IF NOT v_res_ok OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(v_res.estado, ''))) NOT IN ('activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'RESERVA_INACTIVA: La reserva principal ya no esta activa.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
  IF v_inv.anfitrion_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_res.usuario_id OR v_inv.invitado_id IN (v_inv.anfitrion_id, v_res.usuario_id) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SPLIT_INVALID_PARTICIPANTS: Invitacion Split-Escrow invalida para esta reserva.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  IF NOT COALESCE(p_aceptar, FALSE) THEN
    UPDATE public.split_escrow_invitaciones SET estado = 'rechazada', updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = p_invitacion_id;
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', TRUE, 'idempotent', FALSE, 'id', p_invitacion_id, 'estado', 'rechazada', 'monto_liquidado_kc', 0);
  END IF;
  v_parte := TRUNC(GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_inv.monto_kc_parte, 0)));
  IF v_parte > 0 AND MOD(v_parte, 10) <> 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Monto Split-Escrow debe ser multiplo exacto de 10 KC.' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
  IF v_parte > 0 THEN
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(monto_liquidado_kc), 0) INTO v_settled_sum FROM public.split_escrow_invitaciones WHERE reserva_id = v_inv.reserva_id AND LOWER(COALESCE(estado, '')) = 'aceptada' AND id <> p_invitacion_id;
    IF (v_settled_sum + v_parte) > COALESCE(v_res.monto_escrow, 0) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SPLIT_OVERFLOW: Excede el monto Escrow original.' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
    PERFORM 1 FROM public.perfiles WHERE id IN (v_inv.invitado_id, v_inv.anfitrion_id) ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE;
    SELECT id, COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) AS kc_membresia, COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) AS kc_topup INTO v_guest FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = v_inv.invitado_id;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Perfil de invitado no encontrado.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
    SELECT id, COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) AS kc_membresia, COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) AS kc_topup INTO v_host FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = v_inv.anfitrion_id;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Perfil de anfitrion no encontrado.' USING ERRCODE = 'P0002'; END IF;
    IF (v_guest.kc_membresia + v_guest.kc_topup) < v_parte THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SALDO_INSUFICIENTE: Tu boveda no cubre tu parte del Split-Escrow (% KC).', v_parte; END IF;
    v_use_mem := LEAST(v_guest.kc_membresia, v_parte); v_use_top := v_parte - v_use_mem;
    v_host_mem_rem := GREATEST(0, COALESCE(v_res.escrow_membresia, 0) - v_settled_sum); v_host_add_mem := LEAST(v_parte, v_host_mem_rem); v_host_add_top := v_parte - v_host_add_mem;
    PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true); PERFORM set_config('kinetic.rpc_authorized', 'true', true);
    UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) - v_use_mem, kc_topup = COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) - v_use_top, saldo_kc = GREATEST(0, (COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) - v_use_mem) + (COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) - v_use_top)) WHERE id = v_inv.invitado_id;
    UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) + v_host_add_mem, kc_topup = COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) + v_host_add_top, saldo_kc = (COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0) + v_host_add_mem) + (COALESCE(kc_topup, 0) + v_host_add_top) WHERE id = v_inv.anfitrion_id;
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.split_escrow_invitaciones SET estado = 'aceptada', monto_liquidado_kc = v_parte, liquidado_membresia_kc = v_use_mem, liquidado_topup_kc = v_use_top, liquidada_at = NOW(), updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = p_invitacion_id;
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', TRUE, 'idempotent', FALSE, 'id', p_invitacion_id, 'estado', 'aceptada', 'monto_liquidado_kc', v_parte);
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.responder_split_escrow_invitacion(UUID, BOOLEAN) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.responder_split_escrow_invitacion(UUID, BOOLEAN) TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.procesar_compra_boveda_sandbox(p_plan_id TEXT, p_tipo TEXT DEFAULT 'topup')
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid UUID := (SELECT auth.uid()); v_sandbox_ok BOOLEAN := FALSE; v_plan TEXT := LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_plan_id, '')));
  v_tipo TEXT := LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_tipo, 'topup'))); v_kc NUMERIC := 0; v_mxn NUMERIC := 0; v_tier TEXT := NULL;
  v_mem NUMERIC := 0; v_top NUMERIC := 0; v_curr_nivel TEXT := 'Acceso Base'; v_curr_weight INT := 0; v_next_weight INT := 0; v_curr_base_kc NUMERIC := 0; v_curr_base_mxn NUMERIC := 0;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sesion requerida para operar Boveda.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  IF COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(v_uid)), FALSE) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'B2B_ACCOUNT_WALLET_PURCHASE_FORBIDDEN: Las cuentas B2B no pueden comprar membresias ni Kineticoins.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  SELECT COALESCE(enabled, FALSE) INTO v_sandbox_ok FROM public.kinetic_system_flags WHERE flag_key = 'allow_pre_stripe_sandbox';
  IF NOT v_sandbox_ok AND NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), FALSE) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SANDBOX_DESHABILITADO: Modo pre-Stripe desactivado en servidor.' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END IF;
  CASE
    WHEN v_plan IN ('free', 'acceso_base', 'acceso base', 'base') THEN v_kc := 0; v_mxn := 0; v_tier := 'Acceso Base'; v_next_weight := 0;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('bronze', 'memb_bronze', 'membresia_bronze', 'plan_bronze') THEN v_kc := 600; v_mxn := 699; v_tier := 'Bronze'; v_next_weight := 1;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('silver', 'memb_silver', 'membresia_silver', 'plan_silver') THEN v_kc := 1500; v_mxn := 1549; v_tier := 'Silver'; v_next_weight := 2;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('gold', 'memb_gold', 'membresia_gold', 'plan_gold') THEN v_kc := 2500; v_mxn := 2529; v_tier := 'Gold'; v_next_weight := 3;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('platinum', 'memb_platinum', 'membresia_platinum', 'plan_platinum') THEN v_kc := 3600; v_mxn := 3600; v_tier := 'Platinum'; v_next_weight := 4;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('topup_50', '50', 'pkg_50') THEN v_kc := 50; v_mxn := 50; WHEN v_plan IN ('topup_100', '100', 'pkg_100') THEN v_kc := 100; v_mxn := 100;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('topup_250', '250', 'pkg_250') THEN v_kc := 250; v_mxn := 250; WHEN v_plan IN ('topup_500', '500', 'pkg_500') THEN v_kc := 500; v_mxn := 500;
    WHEN v_plan IN ('topup_1000', '1000', 'pkg_1000') THEN v_kc := 1000; v_mxn := 1000; ELSE RAISE EXCEPTION 'Plan o paquete sandbox desconocido: %', p_plan_id;
  END CASE;
  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true); PERFORM set_config('kinetic.rpc_authorized', 'true', true);
  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base') INTO v_mem, v_top, v_curr_nivel FROM public.perfiles WHERE id = v_uid FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Perfil de atleta no encontrado.'; END IF;
  v_curr_weight := CASE WHEN LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%plat%' OR LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%leyenda%' OR LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%black%' THEN 4 WHEN LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%gold%' OR LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%oro%' THEN 3
    WHEN LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%silver%' OR LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%plata%' THEN 2 WHEN LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%bronze%' OR LOWER(v_curr_nivel) LIKE '%bronce%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END;
  v_curr_base_kc := CASE v_curr_weight WHEN 1 THEN 600 WHEN 2 THEN 1500 WHEN 3 THEN 2500 WHEN 4 THEN 3600 ELSE 0 END;
  v_curr_base_mxn := CASE v_curr_weight WHEN 1 THEN 699 WHEN 2 THEN 1549 WHEN 3 THEN 2529 WHEN 4 THEN 3600 ELSE 0 END;
  IF v_tier IS NOT NULL OR v_tipo IN ('membresia', 'membership') THEN
    IF v_next_weight <= v_curr_weight THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'MEMBERSHIP_DOWNGRADE_OR_SAME_TIER_FORBIDDEN' USING ERRCODE = '22023'; END IF;
    IF v_curr_weight >= 1 THEN v_kc := GREATEST(0, v_kc - v_curr_base_kc); v_mxn := GREATEST(0, v_mxn - v_curr_base_mxn); END IF;
    v_mem := v_mem + v_kc;
    UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_membresia = v_mem, saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top, nivel = COALESCE(v_tier, v_curr_nivel), fecha_activacion_membresia = NOW() WHERE id = v_uid;
  ELSE
    v_mxn := CASE WHEN v_curr_weight = 0 THEN ROUND(v_kc * 1.20, 2) ELSE v_kc END;
    v_top := v_top + v_kc; UPDATE public.perfiles SET kc_topup = v_top, saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top WHERE id = v_uid;
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.transacciones_pagos (usuario_id, tipo_operacion, paquete_id, monto_mxn, kc_inyectados, stripe_payment_intent_id, estado)
  VALUES (v_uid, CASE WHEN v_tier IS NOT NULL THEN 'membresia' ELSE 'topup' END, v_plan, v_mxn, v_kc, 'sandbox_' || replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', ''), 'sandbox_completado');
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', TRUE, 'plan_id', v_plan, 'monto_mxn', v_mxn, 'kc_inyectados', v_kc, 'kc_membresia', v_mem, 'kc_topup', v_top, 'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top, 'nivel', COALESCE(v_tier, v_curr_nivel));
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.procesar_compra_boveda_sandbox(TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.procesar_compra_boveda_sandbox(TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(
  p_user_id uuid,
  p_cantidad numeric,
  p_billetera text DEFAULT 'topup'::text,
  p_nuevo_nivel text DEFAULT NULL::text
)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_mem NUMERIC;
  v_top NUMERIC;
  v_nivel TEXT;
BEGIN
  IF NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado: requiere privilegios de Admin Maestro Kinetic.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_b2b_account(p_user_id)), false) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'B2B_ACCOUNT_WALLET_PURCHASE_FORBIDDEN: Cuenta B2B no elegible para saldo de atleta.' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  PERFORM set_config('kinetic.vault_rpc_authorized', 'true', true);

  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base')
  INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = p_user_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Perfil de atleta no encontrado.';
  END IF;

  IF LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN
    v_mem := GREATEST(0, v_mem + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  ELSE
    v_top := GREATEST(0, v_top + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  END IF;

  IF p_nuevo_nivel IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel)) > 0 THEN
    v_nivel := TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel);
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET kc_membresia = v_mem,
      kc_topup = v_top,
      saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top,
      nivel = v_nivel
  WHERE id = p_user_id;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'kc_membresia', v_mem,
    'kc_topup', v_top,
    'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top,
    'nivel', v_nivel
  );
END;
$function$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(uuid, numeric, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(uuid, numeric, text, text) TO authenticated;

-- 6) Resolucion O(1) de usuario Auth por email normalizado restringida estrictamente a service_role
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_resolve_auth_user_by_email(p_email TEXT)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_jwt_role TEXT := COALESCE(
    NULLIF(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.role', true), ''),
    NULLIF((SELECT auth.jwt() ->> 'role'), ''),
    NULLIF(current_setting('role', true), ''),
    ''
  );
  v_email TEXT := LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_email, '')));
  v_row RECORD;
BEGIN
  IF NOT (
    v_jwt_role = 'service_role'
    OR current_setting('role', true) = 'service_role'
    OR session_user IN ('postgres', 'supabase_admin')
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'FORBIDDEN_SERVICE_ROLE_ONLY' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;

  IF v_email = '' THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('found', false);
  END IF;

  SELECT
    u.id AS user_id,
    LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) AS email,
    LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.raw_app_meta_data->>'rol', ''))) AS app_rol,
    LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) AS perfil_rol,
    LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.tipo_cuenta, ''))) AS perfil_tipo_cuenta,
    (
      (
        u.email_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL
        AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com',
          'admin@kinetic.com'
        )
      )
      OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
    ) AS is_master_admin
  INTO v_row
  FROM auth.users u
  LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
  WHERE u.deleted_at IS NULL
    AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = v_email
  ORDER BY u.created_at ASC
  LIMIT 1;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('found', false);
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'found', true,
    'user_id', v_row.user_id,
    'email', v_row.email,
    'app_rol', v_row.app_rol,
    'perfil_rol', v_row.perfil_rol,
    'perfil_tipo_cuenta', v_row.perfil_tipo_cuenta,
    'is_master_admin', COALESCE(v_row.is_master_admin, false)
  );
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.admin_resolve_auth_user_by_email(TEXT) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_resolve_auth_user_by_email(TEXT) TO service_role;

-- 7) [P1-10] RPC agregada exacta para escala real Admin Hub (cero truncamiento)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_obtener_kpis_globales_v282()
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, extensions, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_atletas_count BIGINT := 0;
  v_suma_mem NUMERIC := 0;
  v_suma_top NUMERIC := 0;
  v_mrr_vip_mxn NUMERIC := 0;
  v_vip_count BIGINT := 0;
  v_escrow_kc NUMERIC := 0;
  v_res_activas BIGINT := 0;
  v_liquidez_kc NUMERIC := 0;
  v_checkins BIGINT := 0;
  v_comision_20 NUMERIC := 0;
  v_neto_80 NUMERIC := 0;
  v_af_totales BIGINT := 0;
  v_af_activos BIGINT := 0;
  v_ugc_pendientes BIGINT := 0;
BEGIN
  IF NOT COALESCE((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()), false) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'FORBIDDEN_ADMIN_ONLY';
  END IF;

  WITH atletas_reales AS (
    SELECT
      p.id,
      GREATEST(0, COALESCE(p.kc_membresia, 0)) AS kc_mem,
      GREATEST(0, COALESCE(p.kc_topup, 0)) AS kc_top,
      LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.nivel, ''))) AS nivel_norm,
      COALESCE((to_jsonb(p)->>'membresia_pagada_activa')::boolean, false) AS paid_flag,
      NULLIF(TRIM(COALESCE(to_jsonb(p)->>'stripe_subscription_id', '')), '') AS sub_id
    FROM public.perfiles p
    WHERE COALESCE((to_jsonb(p)->>'es_cuenta_sandbox')::boolean, false) = false
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.email, ''))) NOT IN (
        'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
        'administrador@kinetic.com',
        'kinetic@kinetic.com',
        'admin@kinetic.com',
        'apple.tester@kineticapp.com.mx',
        'google.tester@kineticapp.com.mx',
        'reviewer@kineticapp.com.mx',
        'sandbox@kineticapp.com.mx'
      )
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.email, ''))) NOT LIKE 'club@%'
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.email, ''))) NOT LIKE 'especialista@%'
      AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) NOT IN ('admin', 'fundador', 'ceo', 'sandbox', 'demo', 'test', 'qa', 'tester')
      AND NOT public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p.rol)
      AND NOT public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p.tipo_cuenta)
      AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM public.afiliados af
        WHERE af.propietario_id = p.id
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(af.estado, 'activo'))) NOT IN ('eliminado', 'rechazado', 'inactivo', 'suspendido')
      )
  )
  SELECT
    COUNT(*),
    COALESCE(SUM(kc_mem), 0),
    COALESCE(SUM(kc_top), 0),
    COALESCE(SUM(
      CASE
        WHEN (kc_mem > 0 OR paid_flag OR sub_id IS NOT NULL) THEN
          CASE
            WHEN nivel_norm LIKE '%plat%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%leyenda%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%black%' THEN 3600
            WHEN nivel_norm LIKE '%gold%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%oro%' THEN 2529
            WHEN nivel_norm LIKE '%silver%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%plata%' THEN 1549
            WHEN nivel_norm LIKE '%bronze%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%bronce%' THEN 699
            ELSE 0
          END
        ELSE 0
      END
    ), 0),
    COUNT(*) FILTER (
      WHERE (kc_mem > 0 OR paid_flag OR sub_id IS NOT NULL)
        AND (
          nivel_norm LIKE '%plat%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%leyenda%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%black%'
          OR nivel_norm LIKE '%gold%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%oro%'
          OR nivel_norm LIKE '%silver%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%plata%'
          OR nivel_norm LIKE '%bronze%' OR nivel_norm LIKE '%bronce%'
        )
    )
  INTO v_atletas_count, v_suma_mem, v_suma_top, v_mrr_vip_mxn, v_vip_count
  FROM atletas_reales;

  WITH res_filtradas AS (
    SELECT
      LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(r.estado, ''))) AS est,
      GREATEST(
        COALESCE(r.costo_total, 0),
        COALESCE(r.precio_kc, 0),
        COALESCE(r.monto_escrow, 0) + COALESCE(r.monto_puerta, 0)
      ) AS total_kc,
      COALESCE(
        NULLIF(r.monto_escrow, 0),
        ROUND(GREATEST(COALESCE(r.costo_total, 0), COALESCE(r.precio_kc, 0)) * 0.40)
      ) AS esc_kc
    FROM public.reservas r
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = r.usuario_id
    LEFT JOIN public.servicios sv ON sv.id = r.servicio_id
    WHERE COALESCE(r.es_pase_maestro, false) = false
      AND COALESCE((to_jsonb(r)->>'es_cuenta_sandbox')::boolean, false) = false
      AND COALESCE((to_jsonb(p)->>'es_cuenta_sandbox')::boolean, false) = false
      AND (
        r.servicio_id IS NULL
        OR (
          sv.id IS NOT NULL
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(sv.estado, 'aprobado'))) NOT IN ('eliminado', 'eliminada', 'borrado', 'cancelado', 'cancelada', 'rechazado')
        )
      )
      AND (
        p.id IS NULL
        OR (
          LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.email, ''))) NOT IN (
            'administrador@kinetic.com.mx', 'administrador@kinetic.com', 'kinetic@kinetic.com', 'admin@kinetic.com',
            'apple.tester@kineticapp.com.mx', 'google.tester@kineticapp.com.mx', 'reviewer@kineticapp.com.mx', 'sandbox@kineticapp.com.mx'
          )
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.email, ''))) NOT LIKE 'club@%'
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.email, ''))) NOT LIKE 'especialista@%'
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) NOT IN ('admin', 'fundador', 'ceo', 'sandbox', 'demo', 'test', 'qa', 'tester')
          AND NOT public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p.rol)
          AND NOT public.is_kinetic_b2b_role_text(p.tipo_cuenta)
        )
      )
      AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM public.afiliados af
        WHERE af.propietario_id = r.usuario_id
          AND LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(af.estado, 'activo'))) NOT IN ('eliminado', 'rechazado', 'inactivo', 'suspendido')
      )
  )
  SELECT
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN total_kc > 0 AND est IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada') THEN esc_kc ELSE 0 END), 0),
    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE total_kc > 0 AND est IN ('pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada')),
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN total_kc > 0 AND est = 'completada' THEN total_kc WHEN total_kc > 0 AND est IN ('no_show', 'penalizada') THEN esc_kc ELSE 0 END), 0),
    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE total_kc > 0 AND est = 'completada')
  INTO v_escrow_kc, v_res_activas, v_liquidez_kc, v_checkins
  FROM res_filtradas;

  v_comision_20 := ROUND(v_liquidez_kc * 0.20, 2);
  v_neto_80 := ROUND(v_liquidez_kc - v_comision_20, 2);

  SELECT
    COUNT(*),
    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.estado, ''))) = 'activo')
  INTO v_af_totales, v_af_activos
  FROM public.afiliados a
  WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(a.estado, 'activo'))) NOT IN ('eliminado', 'rechazado');

  IF to_regclass('public.comunidad_reportes_ugc') IS NOT NULL THEN
    EXECUTE 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.comunidad_reportes_ugc WHERE LOWER(COALESCE(estado, ''pendiente'')) = ''pendiente'''
      INTO v_ugc_pendientes;
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'atletas_reales_count', v_atletas_count,
    'suma_membresia_kc', v_suma_mem,
    'suma_topup_kc', v_suma_top,
    'masa_monetaria_disponible_kc', v_suma_mem + v_suma_top,
    'masa_monetaria_total_kc', v_suma_mem + v_suma_top + v_escrow_kc,
    'mrr_vip_mxn', v_mrr_vip_mxn,
    'suscriptores_vip_activos', v_vip_count,
    'escrow_activo_kc', v_escrow_kc,
    'reservas_activas_count', v_res_activas,
    'liquidez_capturada_kc', v_liquidez_kc,
    'comision_kinetic_20_kc', v_comision_20,
    'neto_socios_80_kc', v_neto_80,
    'checkins_totales', v_checkins,
    'afiliados_totales_count', v_af_totales,
    'afiliados_activos_count', v_af_activos,
    'ugc_pendientes_count', v_ugc_pendientes
  );
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.admin_obtener_kpis_globales_v282() FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_obtener_kpis_globales_v282() TO authenticated, service_role;

-- 8) Indices B-Tree compuestos de alta frecuencia para consultas Admin Hub y resolucion por email
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perfiles_lower_email_v282
  ON public.perfiles (LOWER(TRIM(email)));
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'perfiles' AND column_name = 'es_cuenta_sandbox'
  ) THEN
    EXECUTE 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perfiles_rol_sandbox_v282 ON public.perfiles (rol, es_cuenta_sandbox)';
  END IF;
END $$;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reservas_estado_created_v282
  ON public.reservas (estado, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reservas_negocio_created_v282
  ON public.reservas (negocio_nombre, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_afiliados_propietario_estado_v282
  ON public.afiliados (propietario_id, estado);

COMMIT;

    FILE: supabase/migrations/migration_v280_ugc_admin_rls_and_indexes.sql

    -- === supabase/migrations/migration_v280_ugc_admin_rls_and_indexes.sql === --
-- KINETIC OS v2.7.9 — Reportes UGC en vivo (comunidad_reportes_ugc):
-- 1) Índices B-Tree para FK (reporter_id, target_user_id) e índice compuesto (estado, created_at DESC).
-- 2) Políticas RLS optimizadas con (SELECT auth.uid()) y (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()).
-- 3) RPC SECURITY DEFINER admin_resolver_reporte_ugc con validación de rol Admin Maestro y estados permitidos.

DO $mig$
BEGIN
  IF to_regclass('public.comunidad_reportes_ugc') IS NULL THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_comunidad_reportes_ugc_reporter_id
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc (reporter_id)
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_comunidad_reportes_ugc_target_user_id
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc (target_user_id)
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_comunidad_reportes_ugc_estado_created
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc (estado, created_at DESC)
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE 'ALTER TABLE public.comunidad_reportes_ugc ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY';

  EXECUTE 'DROP POLICY IF EXISTS kinetic_reportes_select_own_or_admin ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc';
  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE POLICY kinetic_reportes_select_own_or_admin
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc
      FOR SELECT
      TO authenticated
      USING (
        reporter_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
        OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin())
      )
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE 'DROP POLICY IF EXISTS kinetic_reportes_insert_own ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc';
  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE POLICY kinetic_reportes_insert_own
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc
      FOR INSERT
      TO authenticated
      WITH CHECK (
        reporter_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
        AND LOWER(COALESCE(estado, 'pendiente')) = 'pendiente'
      )
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE 'DROP POLICY IF EXISTS kinetic_reportes_update_admin ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc';
  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE POLICY kinetic_reportes_update_admin
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc
      FOR UPDATE
      TO authenticated
      USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
      WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE 'DROP POLICY IF EXISTS kinetic_reportes_delete_admin ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc';
  EXECUTE $sql$
    CREATE POLICY kinetic_reportes_delete_admin
      ON public.comunidad_reportes_ugc
      FOR DELETE
      TO authenticated
      USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
  $sql$;

  EXECUTE $ddl$
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_resolver_reporte_ugc(
      p_reporte_id UUID,
      p_nuevo_estado TEXT
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path = public
    AS $fn$
    DECLARE
      v_estado TEXT := LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p_nuevo_estado, '')));
      v_updated public.comunidad_reportes_ugc%ROWTYPE;
    BEGIN
      IF (SELECT auth.uid()) IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'AUTH_REQUIRED';
      END IF;

      IF NOT (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'FORBIDDEN_ADMIN_ONLY';
      END IF;

      IF p_reporte_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_REPORT_ID';
      END IF;

      IF v_estado NOT IN ('pendiente', 'revisado', 'accion_tomada', 'descartado') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'INVALID_UGC_REPORT_STATUS';
      END IF;

      UPDATE public.comunidad_reportes_ugc
      SET estado = v_estado
      WHERE id = p_reporte_id
      RETURNING * INTO v_updated;

      IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'UGC_REPORT_NOT_FOUND';
      END IF;

      RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'ok', TRUE,
        'id', v_updated.id,
        'estado', v_updated.estado
      );
    END;
    $fn$
  $ddl$;

  EXECUTE 'REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.admin_resolver_reporte_ugc(UUID, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC';
  EXECUTE 'GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_resolver_reporte_ugc(UUID, TEXT) TO authenticated';
END $mig$;
